# EFDC Ver 12 이론・Confluence・EFDC+ 코드 대조

## 1 요약

대조 기준일은 2026-10-08이다. 대조 대상 코드는 [EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/)의 현재 원문이다. T12는 [EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf)를 뜻한다.

지정한 장의 번호 식 378개를 판정했다. 식 번호별 판정 수는 아래 표에서 집계했다. Confluence 설명 36건을 별도 대조했다. 계수와 선택 스위치 27건을 별도 대조했다. 코드에만 있는 처리 25건을 정리했다. 현재 노트 대조와 수정안 40건을 작성했다.

| 장 | 번호 범위 | 식 수 | 일치 | 불일치 | 코드에서 찾지 못함 | 문서에만 |
|---|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| 2장 수리역학・좌표계・난류・파랑・이산화・근거리 방류 | 2.1–2.181 | 181 | 122 | 52 | 2 | 5 |
| 3장 수송 | 3.1–3.11 | 11 | 9 | 0 | 1 | 1 |
| 4장 염료 수송과 물의 나이 | 4.1–4.5 | 5 | 1 | 3 | 1 | 0 |
| 5장 열・수온・얼음・증발 | 5.1–5.33 | 33 | 16 | 16 | 0 | 1 |
| 6장 퇴적물 | 6.1–6.148 | 148 | 81 | 26 | 18 | 23 |
| 합계 | 아래 식 대조 표 | 378 | 229 | 97 | 22 | 30 |

주요 불일치는 다음과 같다.

- 문서의 파랑 에너지 계수는 1/8이다. 코드의 유의파고 에너지 계수는 1/16이다. [2.49](#eq-2-49).
- 문서의 장파 구름 인자는 1+0.8C이다. 코드의 인자는 1−0.8C이다. [5.2](#eq-5-2).
- 문서의 평형 온도 이차항은 음수이다. 코드의 이차항은 양수이다. [5.12](#eq-5-12).
- 문서의 혼합 바닥층 압밀은 부분 간극비・투수계수・유효응력을 사용한다. 현재 CALBED에서 해당 해법을 찾지 못했다. [6.94](#eq-6-94) · [6.102](#eq-6-102) · [6.110](#eq-6-110).
- 물의 나이 부호는 Confluence의 −1 플래그로 연결할 수 있다. PDF의 K 단위는 영차 변화율의 차원과 맞지 않는다. 이 판단은 차원 해석이다. [4.5](#eq-4-5).
- Confluence는 SEDZLJ 퇴적 제한값 0을 전량 퇴적으로 설명한다. 코드는 그 값에서 퇴적 질량 상한을 0으로 만든다. [Confluence 대조](#confluence-29).

각 코드 인용은 `nl -ba`로 연 원문 줄이다. 판독 기록은 식과 루틴을 찾는 색인으로 사용했다. PDF 식은 쪽별 판독 기록과 추출본을 대조했다. 식이 있는 92개 PDF 쪽의 이미지를 직접 확인했다. PDF 쪽은 파일 첫 쪽을 1로 센다. 인쇄 쪽은 쪽 하단의 번호이다.

‘일치’는 행에 적은 선택과 단위 변환에서 식의 구조가 대응한다는 뜻이다. ‘불일치’는 원문 부호・계수・첨자・조건의 차이를 포함한다. ‘코드에서 찾지 못함’은 해당 EFDC 원문 검색과 관련 실행 경로 판독의 결과이다. ‘문서에만’은 유도 관계・정의・UI 설명처럼 별도 실행식으로 직접 대응하지 않는 항목이다. 독립 구현식이 없는 유도 중간식을 구현 부재와 구분했다.

이유 열의 원문 기호와 갱신문은 확인 사실이다. 식의 대수 정리・차원 검사・오류 가능성은 해석이다. 코드 실행 결과와 정적 대조 결과를 구분했다. 이 보고서는 모델 실행으로 수치 영향과 성능을 검증하지 않았다.

코드의 `AV`와 `AB`는 물리 점성・확산계수를 수심으로 나눈 저장값을 사용한다. `DML`은 길이 척도를 수심으로 나눈 저장값이다. 이 정규화를 반영해 식을 대조했다. 여러 시간 단계와 선택 스위치의 구현을 같은 분기로 합치지 않았다.

파일 인용에서 코드의 상대 경로 기준은 `models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/`이다. 연결은 저장소 루트 아래의 실제 원문 파일을 가리킨다. PDF 판독 근거는 [쪽별 판독 기록](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf)과 [PDF 추출본](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/extract/EFDC/pdf-md/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.md)이다.

## 2 식 대조 표

식 열은 문서의 LaTeX 원문을 보존한다. 인쇄 오류로 판단한 기호도 원문 열에서 고치지 않았다. ‘코드에서 찾지 못함’과 ‘문서에만’ 행의 코드 열은 판독한 가장 가까운 경로이다. 그 경로를 해당 식의 구현으로 확정하지 않았다.

BELV=−h로 놓으면 z*+h=Z·H이다. |

C3=0은 이 계산의 해석 조건이다. |
| <a id="eq-2-20"></a>[T12・PDF 27쪽・인쇄 14쪽・식 2.20](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=27)<br>`A_b=\rho_KK_0ql`<br>[판독 기록:24](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p019-036.md:24) | [calavb.f90:154](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calavb.f90:154) `            SFAB = SFAB0/(1.+SFAB1*RIQ)`<br>[calavb.f90:155](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calavb.f90:155) `            AB(L,K) = SFAB*DML(L,K)*HP(L)*QQSQR(L,K) + AVBXY(L)`<br>[calavb.f90:156](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calavb.f90:156) `            AV(L,K) = SFAV*DML(L,K)*HP(L)*QQSQR(L,K) + AVOXY(L)`<br>[calavb.f90:157](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calavb.f90:157) `            AV(L,K) = AV(L,K)*HPI(L)  `<br>[calavb.f90:158](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calavb.f90:158) `            AB(L,K) = AB(L,K)*HPI(L)` | 불일치 | 문서 식은 rho_K를 쓰지만 바로 아래 설명은 phi_K를 정의한다. 코드의 확산 안정도 함수는 SFAB0/(1+SFAB1*RIQ)이다. Table 2.1의 R3 역수와 SFAB1은 다르다. 식 2.21에서 이 분모 차이를 대조했다. |
| <a id="eq-2-21"></a>[T12・PDF 27쪽・인쇄 14쪽・식 2.21](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=27)<br>`\phi_K=\frac{1}{(1+R_q/R_3)}`<br>[판독 기록:24](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p019-036.md:24) | [calavb.f90:48](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calavb.f90:48) `  SFAV3= 6.127200`<br>[calavb.f90:49](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calavb.f90:49) `  SFAB0= 0.493928`<br>[calavb.f90:50](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calavb.f90:50) `  SFAB1 = 34.676440`<br>[calavb.f90:154](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calavb.f90:154) `            SFAB = SFAB0/(1.+SFAB1*RIQ)` | 불일치 | 문서 Table 2.1의 Galperin R3 역수는 6.127200이다. 코드의 스칼라 확산 분모 계수 SFAB1은 34.676440이다. 코드의 SFAB1은 Table 2.1의 R2 역수와 같다. 식 2.19의 대수 정의를 R3에 적용하면 코드와 같은 계수가 되므로 문서 내부의 이름 충돌도 존재한다. |
| <a id="eq-2-22"></a>[T12・PDF 27쪽・인쇄 14쪽・식 2.22](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=27)<br>`K_0=A_2\left(1-\frac{6A_1}{B_1}\right)`<br>[판독 기록:24](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p019-036.md:24) | [calavb.f90:49](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calavb.f90:49) `  SFAB0= 0.493928`<br>[calavb.f90:70](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calavb.f90:70) `    SFAB0= 0.490025` | 일치 | MY1982 상수를 대입한 K0는 0.493928이다. 기본·Kantha-Clayson 코드는 이 값을 쓴다. Kantha 2003 분기는 0.490025를 쓴다. 문서 Table 2.1도 이 선택 차이를 적었다. |
| <a id="eq-2-23"></a>[T12・PDF 27쪽・인쇄 14쪽・식 2.23](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=27)<br>`R_q=\frac{gH}{q^2}\frac{l^2}{H^2}\frac{\partial b}{\partial z}`<br>[판독 기록:24](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p019-036.md:24) | [calavb.f90:150](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calavb.f90:150) `            RIQ = -GP*HP(L)*DML(L,K)*DML(L,K)*DZIG(L,K)*(B(L,K+1)-B(L,K))/QQ(L,K)  `<br>[calavb.f90:151](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calavb.f90:151) `            RIQ = max(RIQ,RIQMIN)  `<br>[calavb.f90:152](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calavb.f90:152) `            RIQ = min(RIQ,RIQMAXX)` | 불일치 | 문서 식은 밀도 편차의 수직 기울기에 양 부호를 붙인다. 코드는 B(k+1)-B(k)에 음 부호를 붙인다. 밀도가 위로 감소하는 안정 성층에서 문서 식은 음수가 되지만 코드 RIQ는 양수가 된다. 코드는 RIQ의 상한과 하한도 적용한다. |
| <a id="eq-2-24"></a>[T12・PDF 28쪽・인쇄 15쪽・식 2.24](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=28)<br>`\begin{aligned}&\frac{\partial}{\partial t}(mHq^2)+\frac{\partial}{\partial x}(Pq^2)+\frac{\partial}{\partial y}(Qq^2)+\frac{\partial}{\partial z}(mwq^2)\\&=\frac{\partial}{\partial z}\left(m\frac{A_q}{H}\frac{\partial q^2}{\partial z}\right)2m\frac{A_v}{H}\left[\left(\frac{\partial u}{\partial z}\right)^2+\left(\frac{\partial v}{\partial z}\right)^2\right]+2mgA_b\frac{\partial b}{\partial z}-2m\frac{Hq^3}{B_1l}+S_b\end{aligned}`<br>[판독 기록:25](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p019-036.md:25) | [calqq2t.f90:274](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calqq2t.f90:274) `          PQQB = AB(L,K)*GP*HP(L)*DZIG(L,K)*(B(L,K+1) - B(L,K))`<br>[calqq2t.f90:276](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calqq2t.f90:276) `          PQQU = AV(L,K)*DZIGSD4U(L,K)*(U(LE,K+1)-U(LE,K) + U(L,K+1)-U(L,K))**2  `<br>[calqq2t.f90:277](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calqq2t.f90:277) `          PQQV = AV(L,K)*DZIGSD4V(L,K)*(V(LN,K+1)-V(LN,K) + V(L,K+1)-V(L,K))**2  `<br>[calqq2t.f90:278](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calqq2t.f90:278) `          PQQ  = DELT*( PQQB+PQQU+PQQV+PQQVEGE(L,K)+PQQMHKE(L,K)+PQQSUPE(L,K) )`<br>[calqq2t.f90:279](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calqq2t.f90:279) `          UUU(L,K) = UUU(L,K) + 2.*PQQ`<br>[calqq2t.f90:280](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calqq2t.f90:280) `          PQQL = DELT*HP(L)*(CTE3TMP*PQQB + CTE1*(PQQU+PQQV) + CE4VEG*PQQVEGE(L,K) + CE4MHK*PQQMHKE(L,K) + CE4SUP*PQQSUPE(L,K))`<br>[calqq2t.f90:281](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calqq2t.f90:281) `          VVV(L,K) = VVV(L,K) + DML(L,K)*PQQL`<br>[calqq2t.f90:353](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calqq2t.f90:353) `        CMQTMP  = 1. - CLQTMP-CUQTMP+2.*DELT*QQSQR(L,1)/(CTURBB1(L,1)*DML(L,1)*HP(L)) &`<br>[calqq2t.f90:354](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calqq2t.f90:354) `                  + DELT*HPI(L)*(PQQVEGI(L,1)+PQQMHKI(L,1)+PQQSUPI(L,1))`<br>[calavb.f90:314](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calavb.f90:314) `  if( ISTOPT(0) <= 2 .or. ISSQL == 0 )then`<br>[calavb.f90:320](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calavb.f90:320) `          AQ(L,K) = 0.205*(AV(L,K-1)+AV(L,K))  `<br>[calavb.f90:336](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calavb.f90:336) `  if( ISTOPT(0) == 3 .or. ISSQL == 1 )then`<br>[calavb.f90:352](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calavb.f90:352) `            AQ(L,K) = 0.314*( DML(L,K-1)*QQSQR(L,K-1)+DML(L,K)*QQSQR(L,K) ) + AVOXYHPI(L)`<br>[calqq2t.f90:99](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calqq2t.f90:99) `  SQLDSQ = 1.0`<br>[calqq2t.f90:100](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calqq2t.f90:100) `  if( ISTOPT(0) == 3 )SQLDSQ = 0.377/0.628` | 불일치 | 이미지의 확산항과 전단 생성항 사이에는 더하기 기호가 없다. 코드는 확산과 2배 생성항을 별도로 더한다. 코드는 소산을 행렬 대각에 음해적으로 넣는다. 문서 본문의 Aq=0.2ql과 달리 코드는 0.205배 점성 합 또는 0.314배 ql 합을 사용한다. ISTOPT(0)=3은 q²l 확산에 0.377/0.628을 추가로 곱한다. |
| <a id="eq-2-25"></a>[T12・PDF 28쪽・인쇄 15쪽・식 2.25](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=28)<br>`\begin{aligned}&\frac{\partial}{\partial t}(mHq^2l)+\frac{\partial}{\partial x}(Pq^2l)+\frac{\partial}{\partial y}(Qq^2l)+\frac{\partial}{\partial z}(mwq^2l)\\&=\frac{\partial}{\partial z}\left[m\frac{A_{ql}}{H}\frac{\partial}{\partial z}(q^2l)\right]+mlE_1\left\{\frac{A_v}{H}\left[\left(\frac{\partial u}{\partial z}\right)^2+\left(\frac{\partial v}{\partial z}\right)^2\right]+E_3gA_b\frac{\partial b}{\partial z}\right\}\\&-mE_2\frac{Hq^3}{B_1}\left[1+E_4\left(\frac{l}{\kappa Hz}\right)^2+E_5\left(\frac{l}{\kappa H(1-z)}\right)^2\right]+S_l\end{aligned}`<br>[판독 기록:25](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p019-036.md:25) | [calqq2t.f90:267](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calqq2t.f90:267) `          DELB = B(L,K) - B(L,K+1)`<br>[calqq2t.f90:268](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calqq2t.f90:268) `          CTE3TMP = CTE3`<br>[calqq2t.f90:269](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calqq2t.f90:269) `          if( DELB < 0.0 ) CTE3TMP = CTE1`<br>[calqq2t.f90:271](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calqq2t.f90:271) `          LN = LNC(L)`<br>[calqq2t.f90:272](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calqq2t.f90:272) `          LE = LEC(L)`<br>[calqq2t.f90:274](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calqq2t.f90:274) `          PQQB = AB(L,K)*GP*HP(L)*DZIG(L,K)*(B(L,K+1) - B(L,K))`<br>[calqq2t.f90:276](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calqq2t.f90:276) `          PQQU = AV(L,K)*DZIGSD4U(L,K)*(U(LE,K+1)-U(LE,K) + U(L,K+1)-U(L,K))**2  `<br>[calqq2t.f90:277](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calqq2t.f90:277) `          PQQV = AV(L,K)*DZIGSD4V(L,K)*(V(LN,K+1)-V(LN,K) + V(L,K+1)-V(L,K))**2  `<br>[calqq2t.f90:278](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calqq2t.f90:278) `          PQQ  = DELT*( PQQB+PQQU+PQQV+PQQVEGE(L,K)+PQQMHKE(L,K)+PQQSUPE(L,K) )`<br>[calqq2t.f90:279](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calqq2t.f90:279) `          UUU(L,K) = UUU(L,K) + 2.*PQQ`<br>[calqq2t.f90:280](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calqq2t.f90:280) `          PQQL = DELT*HP(L)*(CTE3TMP*PQQB + CTE1*(PQQU+PQQV) + CE4VEG*PQQVEGE(L,K) + CE4MHK*PQQMHKE(L,K) + CE4SUP*PQQSUPE(L,K))`<br>[calqq2t.f90:281](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calqq2t.f90:281) `          VVV(L,K) = VVV(L,K) + DML(L,K)*PQQL`<br>[calqq2t.f90:355](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calqq2t.f90:355) `        CMQLTMP = 1. - CLQLTMP-CUQLTMP+DELT*(QQSQR(L,1)/(CTURBB1(L,1)*DML(L,1)*HP(L)))*(1.+CTE4*DML(L,1)*DML(L,1)*FPROX(L,1)) &`<br>[calqq2t.f90:356](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calqq2t.f90:356) `                  + DELT*HPI(L)*(CE4VEG*PQQVEGI(L,1)+CE4MHK*PQQMHKI(L,1)+CE4SUP*PQQSUPI(L,1))`<br>[aaefdc.f90:1523](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/aaefdc.f90:1523) `        if( IFPROX == 1 ) FPROX(L,K) = 1./(VKC*Z(L,K))**2 + 1./(VKC*(1. - Z(L,K)))**2`<br>[aaefdc.f90:1524](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/aaefdc.f90:1524) `        ! Blumberg et al., 1992 (correction for open channel flow)`<br>[aaefdc.f90:1525](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/aaefdc.f90:1525) `        if( IFPROX == 2 ) FPROX(L,K) = (1./(VKC*Z(L,K))**2) + CTE5*(1./(VKC*(1.-Z(L,K)))**2)/(CTE4+0.00001)`<br>[aaefdc.f90:1526](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/aaefdc.f90:1526) `        ! *** Burchard et al., 1998 (symmetric linear shape)`<br>[aaefdc.f90:1527](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/aaefdc.f90:1527) `        if( IFPROX == 3 ) FPROX(L,K) = 1./(VKC*MIN(Z(L,K),(1. - Z(L,K))))**2`<br>[aaefdc.f90:1528](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/aaefdc.f90:1528) `        ! *** Burchard et al., 2001 (simulation of an infinitely deep basin)`<br>[aaefdc.f90:1529](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/aaefdc.f90:1529) `        if( IFPROX == 4 ) FPROX(L,K) = 1./(VKC*(1. - Z(L,K)))**2`<br>[input.f90:734](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/input.f90:734) `    ! *** PMC - CTE2 NOT USED`<br>[input.f90:735](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/input.f90:735) `    read(1,*,IOSTAT = ISO) VKC, CTURB, CTURB2B, CTE1, CTE2, CTE3, CTE4, CTE5, RIQMAX, QQMIN, QQLMIN, DMLMIN` | 불일치 | 문서 식은 E1을 전단항과 E3 부력항의 괄호 전체에 곱한다. 코드는 전단항에 CTE1을 곱하고 부력항에 CTE3TMP를 독립적으로 곱한다. 코드는 불안정 성층에서 CTE3TMP를 CTE1로 바꾼다. CTE2는 입력 주석에서 미사용이다. 문서의 E4·E5 벽 항은 IFPROX=2일 때 별도 가중 형태로 대응한다. |
| <a id="eq-2-26"></a>[T12・PDF 28쪽・인쇄 15쪽・식 2.26](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=28)<br>`\frac{1}{L}=\frac{1}{H}\left(\frac{1}{z}+\frac{1}{1-z}\right),`<br>[판독 기록:25](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p019-036.md:25) | [aaefdc.f90:1523](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/aaefdc.f90:1523) `        if( IFPROX == 1 ) FPROX(L,K) = 1./(VKC*Z(L,K))**2 + 1./(VKC*(1. - Z(L,K)))**2`<br>[aaefdc.f90:1524](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/aaefdc.f90:1524) `        ! Blumberg et al., 1992 (correction for open channel flow)`<br>[aaefdc.f90:1525](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/aaefdc.f90:1525) `        if( IFPROX == 2 ) FPROX(L,K) = (1./(VKC*Z(L,K))**2) + CTE5*(1./(VKC*(1.-Z(L,K)))**2)/(CTE4+0.00001)`<br>[aaefdc.f90:1526](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/aaefdc.f90:1526) `        ! *** Burchard et al., 1998 (symmetric linear shape)`<br>[aaefdc.f90:1527](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/aaefdc.f90:1527) `        if( IFPROX == 3 ) FPROX(L,K) = 1./(VKC*MIN(Z(L,K),(1. - Z(L,K))))**2`<br>[aaefdc.f90:1528](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/aaefdc.f90:1528) `        ! *** Burchard et al., 2001 (simulation of an infinitely deep basin)`<br>[aaefdc.f90:1529](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/aaefdc.f90:1529) `        if( IFPROX == 4 ) FPROX(L,K) = 1./(VKC*(1. - Z(L,K)))**2` | 문서에만 | 문서는 보조 벽 거리 L의 역수를 정의한다. 확인한 코드 경로는 이 L을 계산하지 않고 FPROX에 역거리 제곱을 직접 저장한다. 두 기호를 같은 저장 변수로 대응시키지 않았다. |
| <a id="eq-2-27"></a>[T12・PDF 29쪽・인쇄 16쪽・식 2.27](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=29)<br>`A_H=C_s\Delta x\Delta y\sqrt{\left(\frac{\partial u}{\partial x}\right)^2+\left(\frac{\partial v}{\partial y}\right)^2+\frac{1}{2}\left(\frac{\partial u}{\partial y}+\frac{\partial v}{\partial x}\right)^2},`<br>[판독 기록:26](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p019-036.md:26) | [calhdmf.f90:230](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calhdmf.f90:230) `          TMPVAL = AHDXY(L)*DXP(L)*DYP(L)`<br>[calhdmf.f90:231](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calhdmf.f90:231) `          DSQR = DXU1(L,K)*DXU1(L,K) + DYV1(L,K)*DYV1(L,K) + 0.5*SXY(L,K)*SXY(L,K)`<br>[calhdmf.f90:232](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calhdmf.f90:232) `          AH(L,K) = AHOXY(L) + TMPVAL*SQRT(DSQR)` | 일치 | 코드의 AHDXY가 Cs에 대응한다. 코드의 DSQR는 두 정상 변형률 제곱과 교차 전단 제곱의 절반을 더한다. 코드는 배경 점성 AHOXY를 추가한다. |
| <a id="eq-2-28"></a>[T12・PDF 29쪽・인쇄 16쪽・식 2.28](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=29)<br>`q^2=B_1^{2/3}\sqrt{t_{sx}^2+t_{sy}^2},\quad l=0,\ \text{at }z=1`<br>[판독 기록:26](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p019-036.md:26) | [hdmt2t.f90:892](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/hdmt2t.f90:892) `        TMP = ( RSSBCE(L)*TVAR3W(L) + RSSBCW(L)*TSX(L) )**2 + ( RSSBCN(L)*TVAR3S(L) + RSSBCS(L)*TSY(L) )**2`<br>[hdmt2t.f90:893](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/hdmt2t.f90:893) `        QQ(L,KC) = 0.5*CTURB2*SQRT(TMP)` | 일치 | 코드는 두 면의 수면 응력을 중심에 가중 평균한 뒤 CTURB2를 곱한다. 균일한 면 응력에서는 0.5와 두 면 합의 2가 상쇄된다. CTURB2의 지수는 코드에서 0.667로 근사한다. 이 판정은 원래 난류 폐쇄의 경계 조건에 한정한다. |
| <a id="eq-2-29"></a>[T12・PDF 29쪽・인쇄 16쪽・식 2.29](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=29)<br>`q^2=B_1^{2/3}\sqrt{t_{bx}^2+t_{by}^2},\quad l=0,\ \text{at }z=0`<br>[판독 기록:26](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p019-036.md:26) | [hdmt2t.f90:881](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/hdmt2t.f90:881) `  if( ISWAVE == 0 .or. LSEDZLJ )then`<br>[hdmt2t.f90:882](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/hdmt2t.f90:882) `    if( ISCORTBC == 0 )then`<br>[hdmt2t.f90:883](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/hdmt2t.f90:883) `      TVAR3W(:) = TSX(LEC(:))`<br>[hdmt2t.f90:884](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/hdmt2t.f90:884) `      TVAR3S(:) = TSY(LNC(:))`<br>[hdmt2t.f90:885](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/hdmt2t.f90:885) `      TVAR3E(:) = TBX(LEC(:))`<br>[hdmt2t.f90:886](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/hdmt2t.f90:886) `      TVAR3N(:) = TBY(LNC(:))`<br>[hdmt2t.f90:888](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/hdmt2t.f90:888) `      do L = 2,LA`<br>[hdmt2t.f90:889](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/hdmt2t.f90:889) `        TMP = ( RSSBCE(L)*TVAR3E(L) + RSSBCW(L)*TBX(L) )**2 + ( RSSBCN(L)*TVAR3N(L) + RSSBCS(L)*TBY(L) )**2`<br>[hdmt2t.f90:890](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/hdmt2t.f90:890) `        QQ(L,0)  = 0.5*CTURB2*SQRT(TMP)` | 일치 | 파랑을 쓰지 않는 원래 난류 폐쇄의 경계값은 바닥 응력 크기에 CTURB2를 곱한다. 면 가중과 모서리 보정은 문서의 단일 점 응력식에 추가된 이산 처리이다. 파랑 경로는 별도 경계값을 쓴다. |
| <a id="eq-2-30"></a>[T12・PDF 29쪽・인쇄 16쪽・식 2.30](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=29)<br>`\frac{1}{\rho_w}\begin{bmatrix}\tau_{bx}\\\tau_{by}\end{bmatrix}=C_b\sqrt{u_1^2+v_1^2}\begin{bmatrix}u_1\\v_1\end{bmatrix}`<br>[판독 기록:26](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p019-036.md:26) | [caltbxy.f90:530](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caltbxy.f90:530) `          UMAGTMP = SQRT( VU(L)*VU(L) + U(L,KSZU(L))*U(L,KSZU(L)) + 1.E-12 )  `<br>[caltbxy.f90:531](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caltbxy.f90:531) `          VMAGTMP = SQRT( UV(L)*UV(L) + V(L,KSZV(L))*V(L,KSZV(L)) + 1.E-12 )  `<br>[caltbxy.f90:532](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caltbxy.f90:532) `          CDMAXU = CDLIMIT*HU(L)/( DELT*UMAGTMP )  `<br>[caltbxy.f90:533](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caltbxy.f90:533) `          CDMAXV = CDLIMIT*HV(L)/( DELT*VMAGTMP )  `<br>[caluvw.f90:733](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caluvw.f90:733) `          W(L,K) = W(L,K-1) - 0.5*DXYIP(L)  &`<br>[caluvw.f90:734](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caluvw.f90:734) `                   *( UHDY(LE,K) - UHDY(L,K) + UHDYEK(L,K) + UHDY1(LE,K)-UHDY1(L,K) + UHDY1EK(L,K)   &`<br>[caluvw.f90:735](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caluvw.f90:735) `                    + VHDX(LN,K) - VHDX(L,K) + VHDXEK(L,K) + VHDX1(LN,K)-VHDX1(L,K) + VHDX1EK(L,K) ) &`<br>[caluvw.f90:736](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caluvw.f90:736) `                    + ( QSUM(L,K) - DZC(L,K)*QSUME(L) )*DXYIP(L)` | 일치 | 저항 조립은 최하층 면 유속과 교차 면 유속으로 속력의 제곱근을 구한다. 최종 응력 곱과 시간 수준은 식 2.146·2.147에서 대조한다. |
| <a id="eq-2-31"></a>[T12・PDF 30쪽・인쇄 17쪽・식 2.31](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=30)<br>`C_b=\left[\frac{\kappa}{\ln(\Delta_1/2z_0)+(\Pi-1)}\right]^2`<br>[판독 기록:27](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p019-036.md:27) | [caltbxy.f90:541](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caltbxy.f90:541) `          ! *** NEZU & NAKAGAWA (1993) WHERE THE WAKE parameter IS 0.2  FOR TURBULENT CONDITIONS.  -0.8 = WAKE - 1.`<br>[caltbxy.f90:542](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caltbxy.f90:542) `          STBX(L) = (VKC/(LOG( HUDZBR ) - 0.8))**2 `<br>[caltbxy.f90:543](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caltbxy.f90:543) `          STBY(L) = (VKC/(LOG( HVDZBR ) - 0.8))**2  `<br>[caltbxy.f90:545](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caltbxy.f90:545) `          STBX(L) = min(CDMAXU, STBX(L) )`<br>[caltbxy.f90:546](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caltbxy.f90:546) `          STBY(L) = min(CDMAXV, STBY(L) )`<br>[caltbxy.f90:598](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caltbxy.f90:598) `                STBX(L) = STBXO(L)*0.16/( (LOG( HUDZBR ) -1.)**2)  `<br>[caltbxy.f90:599](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caltbxy.f90:599) `                STBY(L) = STBYO(L)*0.16/( (LOG( HVDZBR ) -1.)**2)  `<br>[caltbxy.f90:600](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caltbxy.f90:600) `              else                `<br>[caltbxy.f90:601](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caltbxy.f90:601) `                DZHUDZBR = 1. + SGZUU(L,KSZU(L))*HURTMP/ZBRATU(L)`<br>[caltbxy.f90:602](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caltbxy.f90:602) `                DZHVDZBR = 1. + SGZVV(L,KSZV(L))*HVRTMP/ZBRATV(L)`<br>[caltbxy.f90:603](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caltbxy.f90:603) `                DZHUDZBR = max(DZHUDZBR,7.5)  ! *** APPLY THE SAME LIMIT AS KC = 1`<br>[caltbxy.f90:604](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caltbxy.f90:604) `                DZHVDZBR = max(DZHVDZBR,7.5)  ! *** APPLY THE SAME LIMIT AS KC = 1`<br>[caltbxy.f90:605](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caltbxy.f90:605) `                STBX(L) = STBXO(L)*0.16/((LOG(DZHUDZBR))**2)  `<br>[caltbxy.f90:606](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caltbxy.f90:606) `                STBY(L) = STBYO(L)*0.16/((LOG(DZHVDZBR))**2) ` | 불일치 | 문서 본문은 후류 매개변수 Pi=0을 가정한다. 코드의 해당 Nezu 단층 분기는 Pi=0.2에 해당하는 -0.8을 쓴다. 다층 분기는 log(1+최하층 반두께/조도)를 쓴다. 코드는 수심·시간 간격에 따른 저항 상한도 적용한다. |
| <a id="eq-2-32"></a>[T12・PDF 30쪽・인쇄 17쪽・식 2.32](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=30)<br>`\begin{aligned}&\partial_t(m_xm_yHq^2)+\partial_x(m_yHuq^2)+\partial_y(m_xHvq^2)+\partial_z(m_xm_ywq^2)\\&=\partial_z\left(m_xm_y\frac{A_q}{H}\partial_zq^2\right)-2m_xm_y\frac{Hq^3}{B_1l}\\&+2m_xm_y\left(\frac{A_v}{H}\left((\partial_zu)^2+(\partial_zv)^2\right)+gK_v\partial_zb+\partial_pc_pD_p(u^2+v^2)^{3/2}\right)+Q_q\end{aligned}`<br>[판독 기록:27](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p019-036.md:27) | [calqq2t.f90:278](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calqq2t.f90:278) `          PQQ  = DELT*( PQQB+PQQU+PQQV+PQQVEGE(L,K)+PQQMHKE(L,K)+PQQSUPE(L,K) )`<br>[calqq2t.f90:279](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calqq2t.f90:279) `          UUU(L,K) = UUU(L,K) + 2.*PQQ`<br>[calqq2t.f90:280](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calqq2t.f90:280) `          PQQL = DELT*HP(L)*(CTE3TMP*PQQB + CTE1*(PQQU+PQQV) + CE4VEG*PQQVEGE(L,K) + CE4MHK*PQQMHKE(L,K) + CE4SUP*PQQSUPE(L,K))`<br>[calqq2t.f90:281](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calqq2t.f90:281) `          VVV(L,K) = VVV(L,K) + DML(L,K)*PQQL` | 불일치 | 문서의 식생 생성항 기호는 partial_p이고 식 2.33은 eta_p이다. 코드는 PQQVEGE를 q² 생성항에 더한다. 코드는 q²l 생성항에 CE4VEG를 독립적으로 곱한다. 문서 식의 동일 기호·동일 효율이라는 대응을 확정할 수 없다. |
| <a id="eq-2-33"></a>[T12・PDF 30쪽・인쇄 17쪽・식 2.33](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=30)<br>`\begin{aligned}&\partial_t(m_xm_yHq^2l)+\partial_x(m_yHuq^2l)+\partial_y(m_xHvq^2l)+\partial_z(m_xm_ywq^2l)\\&=\partial_z\left(m_xm_y\frac{A_{ql}}{H}\partial_z(q^2l)\right)-m_xm_yE_2\frac{Hlq^3}{lB_1}\left(1+E_4\left(\frac{l}{\kappa Kz}\right)^2+E_5\left(\frac{l}{\kappa H(1-z)}\right)^2\right)\\&+m_xm_yl\left(E_1\frac{A_v}{H}\left((\partial_zu)^2+(\partial_zv)^2\right)+E_3gK_v\partial_zb+E_1\eta_pc_pD_p(u^2+v^2)^{3/2}\right)+Q_l\end{aligned}`<br>[판독 기록:27](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p019-036.md:27) | [calqq2t.f90:280](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calqq2t.f90:280) `          PQQL = DELT*HP(L)*(CTE3TMP*PQQB + CTE1*(PQQU+PQQV) + CE4VEG*PQQVEGE(L,K) + CE4MHK*PQQMHKE(L,K) + CE4SUP*PQQSUPE(L,K))`<br>[calqq2t.f90:281](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calqq2t.f90:281) `          VVV(L,K) = VVV(L,K) + DML(L,K)*PQQL`<br>[calqq2t.f90:355](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calqq2t.f90:355) `        CMQLTMP = 1. - CLQLTMP-CUQLTMP+DELT*(QQSQR(L,1)/(CTURBB1(L,1)*DML(L,1)*HP(L)))*(1.+CTE4*DML(L,1)*DML(L,1)*FPROX(L,1)) &`<br>[calqq2t.f90:356](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calqq2t.f90:356) `                  + DELT*HPI(L)*(CE4VEG*PQQVEGI(L,1)+CE4MHK*PQQMHKI(L,1)+CE4SUP*PQQSUPI(L,1))`<br>[aaefdc.f90:1525](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/aaefdc.f90:1525) `        if( IFPROX == 2 ) FPROX(L,K) = (1./(VKC*Z(L,K))**2) + CTE5*(1./(VKC*(1.-Z(L,K)))**2)/(CTE4+0.00001)` | 불일치 | 문서 첫 벽 항의 분모는 kappa*K*z이다. 코드의 벽 항은 무차원 층 높이 Z와 수심 정규화 길이 DML을 사용한다. 코드는 CE4VEG를 식생 생성·소산에 쓰며 문서의 E1*eta_p 형태를 직접 계산하지 않는다. |
| <a id="eq-2-34"></a>[T12・PDF 31쪽・인쇄 18쪽・식 2.34](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=31)<br>`\bar C_D=2.0-67ad`<br>[판독 기록:28](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p019-036.md:28) | [caltbxy.f90:750](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caltbxy.f90:750) `            ! *** DRAG COEFFICIENT: U COMPONENT `<br>[caltbxy.f90:751](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caltbxy.f90:751) `            CPVEGU = 1.0`<br>[caltbxy.f90:752](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caltbxy.f90:752) `            if( ISVEGL == 1 ) CPVEGU = CPVEGU + 10.E-6/((BPVEG(MW) + BPVEG(M))*UMAGTMP )  `<br>[caltbxy.f90:753](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caltbxy.f90:753) `            if( CPVEGU > 1.0 )then  `<br>[caltbxy.f90:754](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caltbxy.f90:754) `              ! *** CALCULATE R FOR LAMINAR FLOW  `<br>[caltbxy.f90:755](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caltbxy.f90:755) `              CPVEGU = CPVEGU-0.5  `<br>[caltbxy.f90:756](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caltbxy.f90:756) `            endif`<br>[caltbxy.f90:757](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caltbxy.f90:757) `            CPVEGU = SCVEG(M)*CPVEGU  ` | 코드에서 찾지 못함 | EFDC 폴더의 f90에서 67과 수관 항력식을 검색했다. 코드는 SCVEG와 BPVEG를 사용한 저항을 계산한다. 2.0-67ad를 계산하는 원문 줄은 찾지 못했다. 이 결과를 기능 부재로 단정하지 않는다. |
| <a id="eq-2-35"></a>[T12・PDF 32쪽・인쇄 19쪽・식 2.35](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=32)<br>`C_D(\zeta)=\bar C_D(1.2+0.8\zeta-0.5\zeta^2)`<br>[판독 기록:29](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p019-036.md:29) | [caltbxy.f90:750](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caltbxy.f90:750) `            ! *** DRAG COEFFICIENT: U COMPONENT `<br>[caltbxy.f90:751](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caltbxy.f90:751) `            CPVEGU = 1.0`<br>[caltbxy.f90:752](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caltbxy.f90:752) `            if( ISVEGL == 1 ) CPVEGU = CPVEGU + 10.E-6/((BPVEG(MW) + BPVEG(M))*UMAGTMP )  `<br>[caltbxy.f90:753](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caltbxy.f90:753) `            if( CPVEGU > 1.0 )then  `<br>[caltbxy.f90:754](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caltbxy.f90:754) `              ! *** CALCULATE R FOR LAMINAR FLOW  `<br>[caltbxy.f90:755](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caltbxy.f90:755) `              CPVEGU = CPVEGU-0.5  `<br>[caltbxy.f90:756](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caltbxy.f90:756) `            endif`<br>[caltbxy.f90:757](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caltbxy.f90:757) `            CPVEGU = SCVEG(M)*CPVEGU  ` | 코드에서 찾지 못함 | 수관의 정규화 깊이에 대한 1.2+0.8zeta-0.5zeta² 다항식을 f90에서 찾지 못했다. 확인한 저항 경로는 사용자 입력 SCVEG와 층별 식생 높이를 쓴다. |
| <a id="eq-2-36"></a>[T12・PDF 32쪽・인쇄 19쪽・식 2.36](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=32)<br>`\frac{1}{\rho_w}\begin{bmatrix}\tau_{sx}\\\tau_{sy}\end{bmatrix}=C_D\frac{\rho_a}{\rho_w}W_s\begin{bmatrix}W_{sx}\\W_{sy}\end{bmatrix}`<br>[판독 기록:29](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p019-036.md:29) | [caltsxy.f90:808](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caltsxy.f90:808) `      ! *** 1.225E-3 = RHOa/RHOw (DIMENSIONLESS)`<br>[caltsxy.f90:809](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caltsxy.f90:809) `      TSX(L) = 1.225E-3*CD10*U10*TSEAST             ! *** TSX IS THE WIND SHEAR IN THE U DIRECTION (M2/S2)`<br>[caltsxy.f90:810](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caltsxy.f90:810) `      TSY(L) = 1.225E-3*CD10*U10*TSNORT             ! *** TSY IS THE WIND SHEAR IN THE V DIRECTION (M2/S2)` | 일치 | 코드는 운동학적 응력에 고정 밀도비 1.225E-3을 곱한다. IWDRAG>0일 때 벡터 성분은 물 유속을 뺀 상대 풍속이다. 상대 풍속 크기의 차이는 식 2.42에 적었다. |
| <a id="eq-2-37"></a>[T12・PDF 32쪽・인쇄 19쪽・식 2.37](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=32)<br>`W_s=\sqrt{W_{sx}^2+W_{sy}^2}`<br>[판독 기록:29](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p019-036.md:29) | [caltsxy.f90:770](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caltsxy.f90:770) `    WSPD = SQRT( WNDVELE(L)*WNDVELE(L) + WNDVELN(L)*WNDVELN(L) )    ! *** Compute magnitude of 10m wind components`<br>[caltsxy.f90:771](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caltsxy.f90:771) `    U10 = max(WSPD,0.25)` | 일치 | 코드는 동·북 풍속 성분의 제곱합 제곱근을 계산한다. 응력 계산용 U10에는 0.25 m/s 하한을 적용한다. |
| <a id="eq-2-38"></a>[T12・PDF 32쪽・인쇄 19쪽・식 2.38](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=32)<br>`C_D=\begin{cases}\begin{aligned}&3.83111\times10^{-5}W_s^{-3}-0.000308715W_s^{-2}\\&\quad+0.00116012W_s^{-1}+0.000899602,\end{aligned}&W_s<5\mathrm{m/s}\\\begin{aligned}&-5.37642\times10^{-6}W_s^3+0.000112556W_s^2\\&\quad-0.000721203W_s+0.00259657,\end{aligned}&5\mathrm{m/s}\leq W_s<7\mathrm{m/s}\\\begin{aligned}&-3.99677\times10^{-7}W_s^2+7.32937\times10^{-5}W_s\\&\quad+0.000726716,\end{aligned}&W_s\geq7\mathrm{m/s}\end{cases}`<br>[판독 기록:29](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p019-036.md:29) | [caltsxy.f90:771](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caltsxy.f90:771) `    U10 = max(WSPD,0.25)`<br>[caltsxy.f90:773](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caltsxy.f90:773) `    if( IWDRAG < 2 )then`<br>[caltsxy.f90:774](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caltsxy.f90:774) `      ! *** ORIGINAL EFDC WIND DRAG`<br>[caltsxy.f90:775](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caltsxy.f90:775) `      if( U10 < 5. )then`<br>[caltsxy.f90:776](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caltsxy.f90:776) `        C2 = 1./U10`<br>[caltsxy.f90:777](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caltsxy.f90:777) `        CD10 = 3.83111E-005*(C2**3) - 0.000308715*(C2**2) + 0.00116012*C2 + 0.000899602`<br>[caltsxy.f90:778](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caltsxy.f90:778) `      elseif( U10 >= 5.0 .and. U10 <= 7. )then`<br>[caltsxy.f90:779](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caltsxy.f90:779) `        CD10 = -5.37642E-006*(U10**3) + 0.000112556*(U10**2) - 0.000721203*U10 + 0.00259657`<br>[caltsxy.f90:780](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caltsxy.f90:780) `      elseif( U10 >= 7. )then`<br>[caltsxy.f90:781](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caltsxy.f90:781) `        CD10 = -3.99677E-007*(U10**2) + 7.32937E-005*U10 + 0.000726716` | 불일치 | 세 다항식의 계수는 같다. 문서는 7 m/s를 세 번째 구간에 넣는다. 코드는 7 m/s를 두 번째 구간에 먼저 넣는다. 코드는 0.25 m/s 하한을 적용한다. |
| <a id="eq-2-39"></a>[T12・PDF 32쪽・인쇄 19쪽・식 2.39](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=32)<br>`W_{sx}=W_{sx}-u_s`<br>[판독 기록:29](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p019-036.md:29) | [caltsxy.f90:804](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caltsxy.f90:804) `    if( IWDRAG > 0 )then`<br>[caltsxy.f90:805](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caltsxy.f90:805) `      ! *** COMPUTE WIND SPEED RELATIVE TO WATER`<br>[caltsxy.f90:806](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caltsxy.f90:806) `      TSEAST = WINDSXX(L)*WNDVELE(L) + WINDSXY(L)*WNDVELN(L) - U(L,KC)`<br>[caltsxy.f90:807](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caltsxy.f90:807) `      TSNORT = WINDSYX(L)*WNDVELE(L) + WINDSYY(L)*WNDVELN(L) - V(L,KC)`<br>[caltsxy.f90:808](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caltsxy.f90:808) `      ! *** 1.225E-3 = RHOa/RHOw (DIMENSIONLESS)`<br>[caltsxy.f90:809](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caltsxy.f90:809) `      TSX(L) = 1.225E-3*CD10*U10*TSEAST             ! *** TSX IS THE WIND SHEAR IN THE U DIRECTION (M2/S2)` | 일치 | 코드는 곡선좌표로 변환한 바람의 u 성분에서 수면 U를 뺀다. 이 처리는 IWDRAG>0의 모든 분기에서 적용된다. |
| <a id="eq-2-40"></a>[T12・PDF 32쪽・인쇄 19쪽・식 2.40](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=32)<br>`W_{sy}=W_{sy}-v_s`<br>[판독 기록:29](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p019-036.md:29) | [caltsxy.f90:804](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caltsxy.f90:804) `    if( IWDRAG > 0 )then`<br>[caltsxy.f90:805](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caltsxy.f90:805) `      ! *** COMPUTE WIND SPEED RELATIVE TO WATER`<br>[caltsxy.f90:806](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caltsxy.f90:806) `      TSEAST = WINDSXX(L)*WNDVELE(L) + WINDSXY(L)*WNDVELN(L) - U(L,KC)`<br>[caltsxy.f90:807](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caltsxy.f90:807) `      TSNORT = WINDSYX(L)*WNDVELE(L) + WINDSYY(L)*WNDVELN(L) - V(L,KC)`<br>[caltsxy.f90:808](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caltsxy.f90:808) `      ! *** 1.225E-3 = RHOa/RHOw (DIMENSIONLESS)`<br>[caltsxy.f90:809](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caltsxy.f90:809) `      TSX(L) = 1.225E-3*CD10*U10*TSEAST             ! *** TSX IS THE WIND SHEAR IN THE U DIRECTION (M2/S2)`<br>[caltsxy.f90:810](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caltsxy.f90:810) `      TSY(L) = 1.225E-3*CD10*U10*TSNORT             ! *** TSY IS THE WIND SHEAR IN THE V DIRECTION (M2/S2)` | 일치 | 코드는 곡선좌표로 변환한 바람의 v 성분에서 수면 V를 뺀다. 이 처리는 IWDRAG>0의 모든 분기에서 적용된다. |
| <a id="eq-2-41"></a>[T12・PDF 33쪽・인쇄 20쪽・식 2.41](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=33)<br>`C_D=[c_1+c_2(W_s)^{p_1}]/(W_s)^{p_2}`<br>[판독 기록:30](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p019-036.md:30) | [caltsxy.f90:783](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caltsxy.f90:783) `    elseif( IWDRAG == 2 )then`<br>[caltsxy.f90:784](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caltsxy.f90:784) `      ! *** Use HERSBACH 2011, EUROPEAN CENTRE FOR MEDIUM-RANGE WEATHER FORECASTS (ECMWF)`<br>[caltsxy.f90:785](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caltsxy.f90:785) `      CD10 = (0.00103 + 0.00004 * U10**1.48) / U10**0.21` | 일치 | 계수 0.00103·0.00004와 지수 1.48·0.21이 같다. 문서의 옵션 3은 코드 IWDRAG=2에 대응한다. |
| <a id="eq-2-42"></a>[T12・PDF 33쪽・인쇄 20쪽・식 2.42](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=33)<br>`\tau=\rho_aC_DU_r^2`<br>[판독 기록:30](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p019-036.md:30) | [caltsxy.f90:770](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caltsxy.f90:770) `    WSPD = SQRT( WNDVELE(L)*WNDVELE(L) + WNDVELN(L)*WNDVELN(L) )    ! *** Compute magnitude of 10m wind components`<br>[caltsxy.f90:771](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caltsxy.f90:771) `    U10 = max(WSPD,0.25)`<br>[caltsxy.f90:806](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caltsxy.f90:806) `      TSEAST = WINDSXX(L)*WNDVELE(L) + WINDSXY(L)*WNDVELN(L) - U(L,KC)`<br>[caltsxy.f90:807](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caltsxy.f90:807) `      TSNORT = WINDSYX(L)*WNDVELE(L) + WINDSYY(L)*WNDVELN(L) - V(L,KC)`<br>[caltsxy.f90:808](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caltsxy.f90:808) `      ! *** 1.225E-3 = RHOa/RHOw (DIMENSIONLESS)`<br>[caltsxy.f90:809](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caltsxy.f90:809) `      TSX(L) = 1.225E-3*CD10*U10*TSEAST             ! *** TSX IS THE WIND SHEAR IN THE U DIRECTION (M2/S2)`<br>[caltsxy.f90:810](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caltsxy.f90:810) `      TSY(L) = 1.225E-3*CD10*U10*TSNORT             ! *** TSY IS THE WIND SHEAR IN THE V DIRECTION (M2/S2)`<br>[caltsxy.f90:811](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caltsxy.f90:811) `     if( IWDRAG == 3 .and. ISTOPT(2) == 2 )then`<br>[caltsxy.f90:812](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caltsxy.f90:812) `      TSX(L) = 1.225E-3*CDCOARE(L)*U10*TSEAST `<br>[caltsxy.f90:813](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caltsxy.f90:813) `      TSY(L) = 1.225E-3*CDCOARE(L)*U10*TSNORT` | 불일치 | 문서는 응력 크기에 상대 풍속 Ur의 제곱을 쓴다. 코드는 상대 풍속 벡터에 원래 풍속 크기 U10을 곱한다. 물 유속이 0이 아니면 두 계산은 일반적으로 같지 않다. |
| <a id="eq-2-43"></a>[T12・PDF 33쪽・인쇄 20쪽・식 2.43](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=33)<br>`C_D=\left[\frac{\kappa}{\ln\left(\frac{z}{z_0}\right)}\right]^2`<br>[판독 기록:30](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p019-036.md:30) | [caltsxy.f90:786](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caltsxy.f90:786) `    elseif( IWDRAG == 3 )then`<br>[caltsxy.f90:787](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caltsxy.f90:787) `      ! *** Use COARE3.6 (EDSON, ET.AL. 2013) SIMPLIFICATION AT NEUTRAL BOUYANCY`<br>[caltsxy.f90:788](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caltsxy.f90:788) `      if( U10 > 20. )then`<br>[caltsxy.f90:789](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caltsxy.f90:789) `        CD10 = 8.394E-05*U10 + 8.285E-04`<br>[caltsxy.f90:790](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caltsxy.f90:790) `      else`<br>[caltsxy.f90:791](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caltsxy.f90:791) `        CD10 = 1.376E-08*U10**4 - 9.841E-07*U10**3 + 2.324E-05*U10**2 - 1.012E-04*U10 + 8.971E-04`<br>[caltsxy.f90:792](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caltsxy.f90:792) `      endif` | 불일치 | 바람 응력 전용 중립 COARE 분기는 조도를 반복하여 로그식을 계산하지 않고 풍속 다항식과 20 m/s 이후 선형식을 쓴다. 열교환 COARE를 함께 쓰는 경우에는 CDCOARE로 대체한다. |
| <a id="eq-2-44"></a>[T12・PDF 33쪽・인쇄 20쪽・식 2.44](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=33)<br>`z_0=z_0^{\mathrm{smooth}}+z_0^{\mathrm{rough}}=\gamma\frac{\nu}{u_*}+\alpha\frac{u_*^2}{g}`<br>[판독 기록:30](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p019-036.md:30) | [caltsxy.f90:786](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caltsxy.f90:786) `    elseif( IWDRAG == 3 )then`<br>[caltsxy.f90:787](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caltsxy.f90:787) `      ! *** Use COARE3.6 (EDSON, ET.AL. 2013) SIMPLIFICATION AT NEUTRAL BOUYANCY`<br>[caltsxy.f90:788](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caltsxy.f90:788) `      if( U10 > 20. )then`<br>[caltsxy.f90:789](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caltsxy.f90:789) `        CD10 = 8.394E-05*U10 + 8.285E-04`<br>[caltsxy.f90:790](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caltsxy.f90:790) `      else`<br>[caltsxy.f90:791](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caltsxy.f90:791) `        CD10 = 1.376E-08*U10**4 - 9.841E-07*U10**3 + 2.324E-05*U10**2 - 1.012E-04*U10 + 8.971E-04`<br>[caltsxy.f90:792](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caltsxy.f90:792) `      endif` | 문서에만 | 문서는 중립 바람 항력의 조도 유도식을 제시한다. 바람 응력 전용 분기는 이 조도를 저장하지 않는다. 열교환 COARE 안의 같은 조도식은 식 5.8에서 별도로 대조한다. |
| <a id="eq-2-45"></a>[T12・PDF 33쪽・인쇄 20쪽・식 2.45](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=33)<br>`C_D=\begin{cases}C_1,&W_s\leq W_1\\C_1+\frac{C_2-C_1}{W_2-W_1}(W_s-W_1),&W_1<W_s<W_2\\C_2,&W_s\geq W_2\end{cases}`<br>[판독 기록:30](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p019-036.md:30) | [caltsxy.f90:793](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caltsxy.f90:793) `    elseif( IWDRAG == 4 )then`<br>[caltsxy.f90:794](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caltsxy.f90:794) `      ! *** USER DEFINED WIND DRAG`<br>[caltsxy.f90:795](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caltsxy.f90:795) `      if( U10 <= WDRAG1 )then`<br>[caltsxy.f90:796](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caltsxy.f90:796) `        CD10 = CDRAG1`<br>[caltsxy.f90:797](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caltsxy.f90:797) `      elseif( U10 >= WDRAG2 )then`<br>[caltsxy.f90:798](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caltsxy.f90:798) `        CD10 = CDRAG2`<br>[caltsxy.f90:799](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caltsxy.f90:799) `      else`<br>[caltsxy.f90:800](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caltsxy.f90:800) `        CD10 = CDRAG1 + (CDRAG2 - CDRAG1)/(WDRAG2 - WDRAG1)*(U10 - WDRAG1)`<br>[caltsxy.f90:801](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caltsxy.f90:801) `      endif` | 일치 | 하한·상한과 그 사이 선형 보간이 같다. 문서 옵션 5는 코드 IWDRAG=4에 대응한다. WDRAG1·WDRAG2·CDRAG1·CDRAG2는 입력값이다. |
| <a id="eq-2-46"></a>[T12・PDF 34쪽・인쇄 21쪽・식 2.46](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=34)<br>`S_{xx}=n\cos^2\theta+n-\frac{1}{2}E`<br>[판독 기록:31](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p019-036.md:31) | [Waves/wavesxy.f90:234](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Waves/wavesxy.f90:234) `        WG = WK/SINH(WK)                               ! *** 2KH/SINH(2KH)`<br>[Waves/wavesxy.f90:235](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Waves/wavesxy.f90:235) `        WN = (WG+1)/2                               `<br>[Waves/wavesxy.f90:236](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Waves/wavesxy.f90:236) `        SXXTMP = WE*(WG/2+WN*COS(WV(L).DIR)**2)        ! *** RADIATION SHEAR STRESS [Kg/S2] `<br>[Waves/mod_windwave.f90:745](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Waves/mod_windwave.f90:745) `          WG = WK/SINH(WK)`<br>[Waves/mod_windwave.f90:746](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Waves/mod_windwave.f90:746) `          WN = (WG + 1)/2`<br>[Waves/mod_windwave.f90:747](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Waves/mod_windwave.f90:747) `          WVHUU(L,KC) = WVENEP(L)*(WG/2 + WN*COS(WV(L).DIR)**2)     ! *** SXXTMP (M3/S2)` | 불일치 | 문서 인쇄식은 n*cos²theta+n 전체에 E를 곱하지 않는다. 코드는 E*(n*cos²theta+n-1/2)를 계산한다. 코드의 WG/2는 n-1/2이다. |
| <a id="eq-2-47"></a>[T12・PDF 34쪽・인쇄 21쪽・식 2.47](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=34)<br>`S_{xy}=S_{yx}=(n\cos\theta\sin\theta)E`<br>[판독 기록:31](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p019-036.md:31) | [Waves/wavesxy.f90:237](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Waves/wavesxy.f90:237) `        SXYTMP = WE*WN/2*SIN(2*WV(L).DIR)              ! *** RADIATION SHEAR STRESS [Kg/S2] ` | 일치 | 코드의 sin(2theta)/2는 cos(theta)*sin(theta)와 같다. 에너지 자체의 1/8·1/16 차이는 식 2.49에 적었다. |
| <a id="eq-2-48"></a>[T12・PDF 34쪽・인쇄 21쪽・식 2.48](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=34)<br>`S_{yy}=n\sin^2\theta+n-\frac{1}{2}E`<br>[판독 기록:31](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p019-036.md:31) | [Waves/wavesxy.f90:234](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Waves/wavesxy.f90:234) `        WG = WK/SINH(WK)                               ! *** 2KH/SINH(2KH)`<br>[Waves/wavesxy.f90:235](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Waves/wavesxy.f90:235) `        WN = (WG+1)/2                               `<br>[Waves/wavesxy.f90:236](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Waves/wavesxy.f90:236) `        SXXTMP = WE*(WG/2+WN*COS(WV(L).DIR)**2)        ! *** RADIATION SHEAR STRESS [Kg/S2] `<br>[Waves/wavesxy.f90:237](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Waves/wavesxy.f90:237) `        SXYTMP = WE*WN/2*SIN(2*WV(L).DIR)              ! *** RADIATION SHEAR STRESS [Kg/S2] `<br>[Waves/wavesxy.f90:238](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Waves/wavesxy.f90:238) `        SYYTMP = WE*(WG/2+WN*SIN(WV(L).DIR)**2)        ! *** RADIATION SHEAR STRESS [Kg/S2] ` | 불일치 | 문서 인쇄식은 n*sin²theta+n 전체에 E를 곱하지 않는다. 코드는 E*(n*sin²theta+n-1/2)를 계산한다. |
| <a id="eq-2-49"></a>[T12・PDF 35쪽・인쇄 22쪽・식 2.49](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=35)<br>`E=\frac{1}{8}\rho gH_S^2`<br>[판독 기록:32](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p019-036.md:32) | [Waves/wavesxy.f90:221](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Waves/wavesxy.f90:221) `      WV(L).HEIGHT = min(0.75*HP(L),WV(L).HEISIG)            ! *** INCLUDING BREAKING WAVE`<br>[Waves/wavesxy.f90:222](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Waves/wavesxy.f90:222) `      if( WV(L).HEIGHT >= WHMI .and. HP(L) > HDRYWAV )then`<br>[Waves/wavesxy.f90:223](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Waves/wavesxy.f90:223) `        WDEP1 = HP(L)`<br>[Waves/wavesxy.f90:224](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Waves/wavesxy.f90:224) `        WPRD  = 2.*PI/WV(L).FREQ`<br>[Waves/wavesxy.f90:225](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Waves/wavesxy.f90:225) `        if( WVLCAL == 1 )then`<br>[Waves/wavesxy.f90:226](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Waves/wavesxy.f90:226) `          call BISEC(DISRELATION,WLMIN,WLMAX,EPS,WPRD,WDEP1,0._8,0._8,RRLS)`<br>[Waves/wavesxy.f90:227](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Waves/wavesxy.f90:227) `          WV(L).LENGTH = RRLS`<br>[Waves/wavesxy.f90:228](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Waves/wavesxy.f90:228) `        endif`<br>[Waves/wavesxy.f90:229](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Waves/wavesxy.f90:229) `        WV(L).K   = max( 2.*PI/WV(L).LENGTH, 0.01 ) `<br>[Waves/wavesxy.f90:230](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Waves/wavesxy.f90:230) `        WV(L).KHP = min(WV(L).K*HP(L),SHLIM)`<br>[Waves/wavesxy.f90:231](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Waves/wavesxy.f90:231) `        WK = 2.0*WV(L).KHP                             ! *** 4*PI/WV(L).LENGTH*HP(L) = 2KH`<br>[Waves/wavesxy.f90:232](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Waves/wavesxy.f90:232) `        WE = 9.81*1000.*WV(L).HEIGHT**2 /16.           ! *** TOTAL WAVE ENERGY : KG/S2  `<br>[Waves/wavesxy.f90:233](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Waves/wavesxy.f90:233) `        WVENEP(L) = WE/1000.                           ! *** WAVE ENERGY/RHO: M3/S2`<br>[Waves/mod_windwave.f90:741](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Waves/mod_windwave.f90:741) `          WVENEP(L)= G*WV(L).HEIGHT**2./16.                         ! *** ENERGY/RHO (M3/S2) FOR RANDOM WAVE` | 불일치 | 문서 계수는 1/8이다. 외부 파랑과 내부 SMB 코드의 계수는 모두 1/16이다. 외부 코드의 HEIGHT는 제한을 적용한 HEISIG이다. 밀도 정규화는 이 2배 차이를 없애지 않는다. |
| <a id="eq-2-50"></a>[T12・PDF 35쪽・인쇄 22쪽・식 2.50](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=35)<br>`k=\frac{2\pi}{L}`<br>[판독 기록:32](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p019-036.md:32) | [Waves/mod_windwave.f90:260](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Waves/mod_windwave.f90:260) `            WV(L).K = max( 2.*PI/WV(L).LENGTH, 0.01 )` | 일치 | 코드는 2pi/L를 계산한다. 코드는 파수에 0.01 rad/m 하한을 적용한다. |
| <a id="eq-2-51"></a>[T12・PDF 35쪽・인쇄 22쪽・식 2.51](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=35)<br>`n=\frac{C_g}{C}=\frac{1}{2}\left[1+\frac{2kh}{\sinh(2kh)}\right]`<br>[판독 기록:32](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p019-036.md:32) | [Waves/wavesxy.f90:231](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Waves/wavesxy.f90:231) `        WK = 2.0*WV(L).KHP                             ! *** 4*PI/WV(L).LENGTH*HP(L) = 2KH`<br>[Waves/wavesxy.f90:232](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Waves/wavesxy.f90:232) `        WE = 9.81*1000.*WV(L).HEIGHT**2 /16.           ! *** TOTAL WAVE ENERGY : KG/S2  `<br>[Waves/wavesxy.f90:233](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Waves/wavesxy.f90:233) `        WVENEP(L) = WE/1000.                           ! *** WAVE ENERGY/RHO: M3/S2`<br>[Waves/wavesxy.f90:234](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Waves/wavesxy.f90:234) `        WG = WK/SINH(WK)                               ! *** 2KH/SINH(2KH)`<br>[Waves/wavesxy.f90:235](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Waves/wavesxy.f90:235) `        WN = (WG+1)/2                               ` | 일치 | 코드의 WK는 2kh이다. WN=(WK/sinh(WK)+1)/2의 계수와 부호가 같다. |
| <a id="eq-2-52"></a>[T12・PDF 35쪽・인쇄 22쪽・식 2.52](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=35)<br>`L=\frac{gT^2}{2\pi}\tanh(kh)`<br>[판독 기록:32](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p019-036.md:32) | [Waves/mod_wavelength.f90:28](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Waves/mod_wavelength.f90:28) `    FWL = (RLS/TP-U*COS(PHI))-SQRT(G*RLS/2._8/PI*TANH(2._8*PI*HD/RLS))`<br>[Waves/wavesxy.f90:225](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Waves/wavesxy.f90:225) `        if( WVLCAL == 1 )then`<br>[Waves/wavesxy.f90:226](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Waves/wavesxy.f90:226) `          call BISEC(DISRELATION,WLMIN,WLMAX,EPS,WPRD,WDEP1,0._8,0._8,RRLS)`<br>[Waves/wavesxy.f90:227](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Waves/wavesxy.f90:227) `          WV(L).LENGTH = RRLS` | 일치 | U=0인 DISRELATION의 영점은 문서 분산식과 같다. 외부 파랑에서 WVLCAL=1이면 이분법으로 푼다. 내부 SMB는 식 2.53의 근사식을 쓴다. |
| <a id="eq-2-53"></a>[T12・PDF 35쪽・인쇄 22쪽・식 2.53](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=35)<br>`L\approx T\sqrt{\frac{1}{d}gh},`<br>[판독 기록:32](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p019-036.md:32) | [Waves/mod_windwave.f90:255](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Waves/mod_windwave.f90:255) `            FC1 = WV(L).FREQ**2*HP(L)*GI                                                          ! *** EFDC+ Eq. 2.55`<br>[Waves/mod_windwave.f90:256](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Waves/mod_windwave.f90:256) `            FC2 = FC1 + 1.0/( 1.0 + 0.6522*FC1 + 0.4622*FC1**2 + 0.0864*FC1**4 + 0.0675*FC1**5 )  ! *** EFDC+ Eq. 2.54`<br>[Waves/mod_windwave.f90:257](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Waves/mod_windwave.f90:257) `            WV(L).LENGTH = WV(L).PERIOD*SQRT(G*HP(L)/FC2)                                         ! *** EFDC+ Eq. 2.53` | 일치 | 코드는 T*sqrt(gH/d)를 직접 계산한다. |
| <a id="eq-2-54"></a>[T12・PDF 35쪽・인쇄 22쪽・식 2.54](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=35)<br>`d=\gamma+\frac{1}{(1+0.6522\gamma+0.4622\gamma^2+0.0864\gamma^4+0.0675\gamma^5)},`<br>[판독 기록:32](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p019-036.md:32) | [Waves/mod_windwave.f90:256](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Waves/mod_windwave.f90:256) `            FC2 = FC1 + 1.0/( 1.0 + 0.6522*FC1 + 0.4622*FC1**2 + 0.0864*FC1**4 + 0.0675*FC1**5 )  ! *** EFDC+ Eq. 2.54` | 일치 | 0.6522·0.4622·0.0864·0.0675 계수와 지수 1·2·4·5가 모두 같다. 코드도 gamma³ 항을 넣지 않는다. |
| <a id="eq-2-55"></a>[T12・PDF 35쪽・인쇄 22쪽・식 2.55](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=35)<br>`\gamma=\omega^2\frac{h}{g}`<br>[판독 기록:32](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p019-036.md:32) | [Waves/mod_windwave.f90:255](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Waves/mod_windwave.f90:255) `            FC1 = WV(L).FREQ**2*HP(L)*GI                                                          ! *** EFDC+ Eq. 2.55` | 일치 | 코드는 각주파수 제곱에 수심을 곱하고 중력으로 나눈다. |
| <a id="eq-2-56"></a>[T12・PDF 36쪽・인쇄 23쪽・식 2.56](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=36)<br>`\omega=\frac{2\pi}{T}.`<br>[판독 기록:33](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p019-036.md:33) | [Waves/mod_windwave.f90:240](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Waves/mod_windwave.f90:240) `            WV(L).PERIOD = max(1.D-3, FC1*FC2)`<br>[Waves/mod_windwave.f90:241](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Waves/mod_windwave.f90:241) `            WV(L).FREQ   = 2.0*PI/WV(L).PERIOD` | 일치 | 코드는 2pi/T를 쓴다. 코드의 주기 하한은 0.001 s이다. |
| <a id="eq-2-57"></a>[T12・PDF 36쪽・인쇄 23쪽・식 2.57](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=36)<br>`R_w=\frac{U_bA}{\nu},\quad r=\frac{A}{k_s}`<br>[판독 기록:33](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p019-036.md:33) | [Waves/wavebl.f90:194](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Waves/wavebl.f90:194) `        VISMUDD = 1.36E-6      !DHC: 2010-05-06`<br>[Waves/wavebl.f90:195](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Waves/wavebl.f90:195) `        if( ISMUD >= 1 ) VISMUDD = CSEDVIS(SED(L,KSZ(L),1))`<br>[Waves/wavebl.f90:196](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Waves/wavebl.f90:196) `        REYWAVE = UWORBIT*AEXTMP/VISMUDD`<br>[Waves/wavebl.f90:197](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Waves/wavebl.f90:197) `        RA= AEXTMP/ZBRE(L)       !Relative roughness = A/Ksw` | 일치 | 코드 REYWAVE는 Ub*A/nu이고 RA는 A/ZBRE이다. ISMUD>=1은 퇴적물 농도에 따른 점성으로 nu를 바꾼다. |
| <a id="eq-2-58"></a>[T12・PDF 36쪽・인쇄 23쪽・식 2.58](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=36)<br>`A=\frac{H_s}{2\sinh(kh)}`<br>[판독 기록:33](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p019-036.md:33) | [Waves/wavebl.f90:185](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Waves/wavebl.f90:185) `      if( WV(L).HEIGHT >= WHMI .and. HP(L) > HDRYWAV )then`<br>[Waves/wavebl.f90:186](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Waves/wavebl.f90:186) `        AEXTMP   = 0.5*WV(L).HEIGHT/SINH(WV(L).KHP)`<br>[Waves/wavebl.f90:187](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Waves/wavebl.f90:187) `        UWORBIT  = AEXTMP*WV(L).FREQ` | 일치 | 코드는 Hs/(2*sinh(kh))를 계산한다. 적용 조건은 파고·수심 하한이다. |
| <a id="eq-2-59"></a>[T12・PDF 36쪽・인쇄 23쪽・식 2.59](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=36)<br>`U_b=A\omega=\frac{\omega H_s}{2\sinh(kh)}`<br>[판독 기록:33](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p019-036.md:33) | [Waves/wavebl.f90:186](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Waves/wavebl.f90:186) `        AEXTMP   = 0.5*WV(L).HEIGHT/SINH(WV(L).KHP)`<br>[Waves/wavebl.f90:187](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Waves/wavebl.f90:187) `        UWORBIT  = AEXTMP*WV(L).FREQ`<br>[Waves/wavebl.f90:188](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Waves/wavebl.f90:188) `        UWVSQ(L) = UWORBIT*UWORBIT`<br>[Waves/mod_windwave.f90:262](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Waves/mod_windwave.f90:262) `            WV(L).UDEL = max(1.D-6, PI*WV(L).HEIGHT/( WV(L).PERIOD*SINH(WV(L).KHP) ))              ! *** Orbital velocity    Eq. 2.59` | 일치 | 외부 코드는 A*omega를 계산한다. 내부 SMB의 pi*Hs/(T*sinh(kh))도 같은 식이다. 내부 코드는 궤도 유속에 1E-6 m/s 하한을 적용한다. |
| <a id="eq-2-60"></a>[T12・PDF 36쪽・인쇄 23쪽・식 2.60](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=36)<br>`f_w=\begin{cases}e^{(5.21r^{-0.19}-6.0)}&r>1.57\\0.3r&r=1.57\end{cases}`<br>[판독 기록:33](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p019-036.md:33) | [Waves/wavebl.f90:200](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Waves/wavebl.f90:200) `        if( REYWAVE <= 5D5 )then`<br>[Waves/wavebl.f90:201](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Waves/wavebl.f90:201) `          !LAMINAR`<br>[Waves/wavebl.f90:202](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Waves/wavebl.f90:202) `          FCW  = 2*REYWAVE**(-0.5)`<br>[Waves/wavebl.f90:204](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Waves/wavebl.f90:204) `        elseif( REYWAVE>5D5 .and. RA>1.57 )then`<br>[Waves/wavebl.f90:205](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Waves/wavebl.f90:205) `          !** TURBULENT SMOOTH WAVE BOUNDARY LAYER`<br>[Waves/wavebl.f90:206](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Waves/wavebl.f90:206) `          FCW = 0.09*REYWAVE**(-0.2)`<br>[Waves/wavebl.f90:208](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Waves/wavebl.f90:208) `        elseif( REYWAVE>5D5 .and. RA <= 1.57 )then`<br>[Waves/wavebl.f90:209](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Waves/wavebl.f90:209) `          !** TURBULENT ROUGH WAVE BOUNDARY LAYER`<br>[Waves/wavebl.f90:210](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Waves/wavebl.f90:210) `          FCW = EXP(5.2*RA**(-0.19)-6)    ! *** Baird's paper`<br>[Waves/wavebl.f90:211](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Waves/wavebl.f90:211) `          FCW = min(FCW,0.3)`<br>[Waves/wavebl.f90:212](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Waves/wavebl.f90:212) `        endif`<br>[Waves/wavebl.f90:213](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Waves/wavebl.f90:213) `        CDTMP = 0.5*FCW`<br>[Waves/wavebl.f90:214](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Waves/wavebl.f90:214) `        QQWV1(L) = min(CDTMP*UWORBIT*UWORBIT,QQMAX)`<br>[Waves/mod_windwave.f90:99](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Waves/mod_windwave.f90:99) `            if( REYWAVE  <= 5D5 )then`<br>[Waves/mod_windwave.f90:100](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Waves/mod_windwave.f90:100) `              ! *** LAMINAR`<br>[Waves/mod_windwave.f90:101](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Waves/mod_windwave.f90:101) `              WVFF  = 2*REYWAVE**(-0.5)`<br>[Waves/mod_windwave.f90:102](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Waves/mod_windwave.f90:102) `            elseif( REYWAVE > 5D5 .and. RA > 1.57 )then`<br>[Waves/mod_windwave.f90:103](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Waves/mod_windwave.f90:103) `              ! *** TURBULENT SMOOTH WAVE BOUNDARY LAYER`<br>[Waves/mod_windwave.f90:104](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Waves/mod_windwave.f90:104) `              WVFF = 0.09*REYWAVE**(-0.2)`<br>[Waves/mod_windwave.f90:105](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Waves/mod_windwave.f90:105) `            elseif( REYWAVE > 5D5 .and. RA  <= 1.57 )then`<br>[Waves/mod_windwave.f90:106](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Waves/mod_windwave.f90:106) `              ! *** TURBULENT ROUGH WAVE BOUNDARY LAYER`<br>[Waves/mod_windwave.f90:107](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Waves/mod_windwave.f90:107) `              WVFF = EXP(5.2*RA**(-0.19)-6)    ! *** Baird's paper`<br>[Waves/mod_windwave.f90:108](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Waves/mod_windwave.f90:108) `              WVFF = min(WVFF,0.3)`<br>[Waves/mod_windwave.f90:109](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Waves/mod_windwave.f90:109) `            endif`<br>[Waves/mod_windwave.f90:110](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Waves/mod_windwave.f90:110) `            CDTMP = 0.5*WVFF` | 불일치 | 문서는 r>1.57에 Swart 지수식 5.21을 쓴다. 코드는 Reynolds 수가 5E5보다 크고 r<=1.57일 때 계수 5.2의 지수식을 쓴다. 코드는 그 값을 0.3으로 제한한다. 문서의 둘째 식은 0.3r이며 조건은 r=1.57이다. 코드는 별도의 층류·매끈한 난류 식을 갖는다. |
| <a id="eq-2-61"></a>[T12・PDF 37쪽・인쇄 24쪽・식 2.61](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=37)<br>`\displaystyle H_s=0.283\alpha\frac{W_s^2}{g}\tanh\left(\frac{0.0125}{\alpha}\left(\frac{gF}{W_s^2}\right)^{0.42}\right)`<br>[판독 기록:13](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p037-054.md:13) | [Waves/mod_windwave.f90:214](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Waves/mod_windwave.f90:214) `          AVEDEP = Fetch_Depth(LG,ZONE)              ! *** Get the average depth along the fetch (m)`<br>[Waves/mod_windwave.f90:216](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Waves/mod_windwave.f90:216) `          if( AVEDEP > HDRY .and. HP(L) > HDRY )then`<br>[Waves/mod_windwave.f90:217](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Waves/mod_windwave.f90:217) `            ! *** Wave height (m)`<br>[Waves/mod_windwave.f90:218](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Waves/mod_windwave.f90:218) `            FC0 = (G*AVEDEP/WINDVEL2)**0.75`<br>[Waves/mod_windwave.f90:219](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Waves/mod_windwave.f90:219) `            FC1 = TANH(0.530*FC0)`<br>[Waves/mod_windwave.f90:220](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Waves/mod_windwave.f90:220) `            FC2 = WINDVEL2*GI*0.283*FC1`<br>[Waves/mod_windwave.f90:221](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Waves/mod_windwave.f90:221) `            FC3 = TANH(0.0125*(G*FWDIR(L,ZONE)/WINDVEL2)**0.42/FC1)`<br>[Waves/mod_windwave.f90:222](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Waves/mod_windwave.f90:222) `            WV(L).HEIGHT = min(0.75*HP(L), FC2*FC3)                                      ! *** Wave height (m)` | 불일치 | SMB 기본 식의 0.283·0.0125·0.42는 같다. 코드는 결과를 0.75*국소 수심으로 제한한다. 코드는 alpha에 취송 구간 평균 수심을 쓴다. |
| <a id="eq-2-62"></a>[T12・PDF 37쪽・인쇄 24쪽・식 2.62](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=37)<br>`\displaystyle T_p=7.54\beta\frac{W_s}{g}\tanh\left(\frac{0.077}{\beta}\left(\frac{gF}{W_s^2}\right)^{0.25}\right)`<br>[판독 기록:13](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p037-054.md:13) | [Waves/mod_windwave.f90:237](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Waves/mod_windwave.f90:237) `            FC0 = TANH(0.833*(G*AVEDEP/WINDVEL2)**0.375)`<br>[Waves/mod_windwave.f90:238](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Waves/mod_windwave.f90:238) `            FC1 = 7.54*WINDVEL*GI*FC0`<br>[Waves/mod_windwave.f90:239](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Waves/mod_windwave.f90:239) `            FC2 = TANH(0.077*(G*FWDIR(L,ZONE)/WINDVEL2)**0.25/FC0)`<br>[Waves/mod_windwave.f90:240](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Waves/mod_windwave.f90:240) `            WV(L).PERIOD = max(1.D-3, FC1*FC2)`<br>[Waves/mod_windwave.f90:241](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Waves/mod_windwave.f90:241) `            WV(L).FREQ   = 2.0*PI/WV(L).PERIOD` | 일치 | 7.54·0.077·0.25 계수와 지수가 같다. 코드는 주기에 0.001 s 하한을 적용한다. beta의 수심 대응은 식 2.64에 적었다. |
| <a id="eq-2-63"></a>[T12・PDF 37쪽・인쇄 24쪽・식 2.63](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=37)<br>`\displaystyle \alpha=\tanh\left\{0.53\left(\frac{gH}{W_s^2}\right)^{0.75}\right\}`<br>[판독 기록:13](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p037-054.md:13) | [Waves/mod_windwave.f90:214](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Waves/mod_windwave.f90:214) `          AVEDEP = Fetch_Depth(LG,ZONE)              ! *** Get the average depth along the fetch (m)`<br>[Waves/mod_windwave.f90:216](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Waves/mod_windwave.f90:216) `          if( AVEDEP > HDRY .and. HP(L) > HDRY )then`<br>[Waves/mod_windwave.f90:217](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Waves/mod_windwave.f90:217) `            ! *** Wave height (m)`<br>[Waves/mod_windwave.f90:218](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Waves/mod_windwave.f90:218) `            FC0 = (G*AVEDEP/WINDVEL2)**0.75`<br>[Waves/mod_windwave.f90:219](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Waves/mod_windwave.f90:219) `            FC1 = TANH(0.530*FC0)` | 불일치 | 문서 H는 수주의 총수심이다. 코드는 해당 격자의 HP 대신 취송 구간 평균 수심 AVEDEP를 쓴다. 평균 수심과 국소 수심이 같을 때 식이 같다. |
| <a id="eq-2-64"></a>[T12・PDF 37쪽・인쇄 24쪽・식 2.64](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=37)<br>`\displaystyle \beta=\tanh\left\{0.833\left(\frac{gH}{W_s^2}\right)^{0.375}\right\}`<br>[판독 기록:13](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p037-054.md:13) | [Waves/mod_windwave.f90:214](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Waves/mod_windwave.f90:214) `          AVEDEP = Fetch_Depth(LG,ZONE)              ! *** Get the average depth along the fetch (m)`<br>[Waves/mod_windwave.f90:237](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Waves/mod_windwave.f90:237) `            FC0 = TANH(0.833*(G*AVEDEP/WINDVEL2)**0.375)` | 불일치 | 문서 H와 달리 코드는 취송 구간 평균 수심 AVEDEP를 beta에 쓴다. |
| <a id="eq-2-65"></a>[T12・PDF 38쪽・인쇄 25쪽・식 2.65](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=38)<br>`\displaystyle \zeta(t)=\zeta_0(t)+a_0+\sum_{k=1}^{N}\left[a_k\cos(\omega_k t)+b_k\sin(\omega_k t)\right]`<br>[판독 기록:14](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p037-054.md:14) | [setopenbc.f90:252](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/setopenbc.f90:252) `    FP(L) = PSERT(NPSERS(LL)) + 0.5*PSERZDF(NPSERS(LL)) + PSERST(NPSERS(LL)) + 0.5*PSERZDS(NPSERS(LL))         ! *** m2/s2`<br>[setopenbc.f90:253](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/setopenbc.f90:253) `    if( NPFORT >= 1 .and. NPSERS1(LL) > 0 )then`<br>[setopenbc.f90:254](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/setopenbc.f90:254) `      TMPVAL = PSERT(NPSERS1(LL)) + 0.5*PSERZDF(NPSERS1(LL)) + PSERST(NPSERS1(LL)) + 0.5*PSERZDS(NPSERS1(LL))`<br>[setopenbc.f90:255](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/setopenbc.f90:255) `      FP(L) = FP(L)+TPCOORDS(LL)*(TMPVAL-FP(L))`<br>[setopenbc.f90:256](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/setopenbc.f90:256) `    endif`<br>[setopenbc.f90:257](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/setopenbc.f90:257) `    do M = 1,MTIDE`<br>[setopenbc.f90:258](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/setopenbc.f90:258) `      TC = CCCOS(M)`<br>[setopenbc.f90:259](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/setopenbc.f90:259) `      TS = SSSIN(M)`<br>[setopenbc.f90:260](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/setopenbc.f90:260) `      FP(L) = FP(L)+PCBS(LL,M)*TC+PSBS(LL,M)*TS`<br>[setopenbc.f90:261](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/setopenbc.f90:261) `    enddo`<br>[setopenbc.f90:263](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/setopenbc.f90:263) `    ELEV = FP(L)/G` | 일치 | 코드는 시계열 압력과 조화 성분의 코사인·사인 합을 더한다. FP를 G로 나누어 수위를 만든다. 기준 오프셋은 입력 시계열에 포함될 수 있다. |
| <a id="eq-2-66"></a>[T12・PDF 38쪽・인쇄 25쪽・식 2.66](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=38)<br>`\displaystyle \omega_k=\frac{2\pi}{T_k}`<br>[판독 기록:14](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p037-054.md:14) | [setopenbc.f90:233](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/setopenbc.f90:233) `  do M = 1,MTIDE`<br>[setopenbc.f90:234](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/setopenbc.f90:234) `    TM = MOD(TN,TCP(M))`<br>[setopenbc.f90:235](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/setopenbc.f90:235) `    TM = PI2*TM/TCP(M)`<br>[setopenbc.f90:236](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/setopenbc.f90:236) `    CCCOS(M) = COS(TM)`<br>[setopenbc.f90:237](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/setopenbc.f90:237) `    SSSIN(M) = SIN(TM)` | 일치 | 코드는 주기 TCP로 나눈 2pi*t의 위상을 계산한다. MOD는 큰 시각의 위상을 주기 안으로 줄인다. |
