| <a id="eq-2-67"></a>[T12・PDF 38쪽・인쇄 25쪽・식 2.67](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=38)<br>`\displaystyle \zeta(t)=\zeta_0(t)+a_0+\sum_{k=1}^{N}A_k\cos(\omega_k t-\phi_k)`<br>[판독 기록:14](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p037-054.md:14) | [input.f90:1226](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/input.f90:1226) `            RAD = PI2*PFPH(NPFORE,M)/TCP(M)`<br>[input.f90:1227](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/input.f90:1227) `            AMP = G*PFAM(NPFORE,M)`<br>[input.f90:1228](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/input.f90:1228) `            PCBE_GL(L,M) = AMP*COS(RAD)`<br>[input.f90:1229](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/input.f90:1229) `            PSBE_GL(L,M) = AMP*SIN(RAD)`<br>[setopenbc.f90:260](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/setopenbc.f90:260) `      FP(L) = FP(L)+PCBS(LL,M)*TC+PSBS(LL,M)*TS`<br>[setopenbc.f90:261](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/setopenbc.f90:261) `    enddo`<br>[setopenbc.f90:263](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/setopenbc.f90:263) `    ELEV = FP(L)/G` | 일치 | 코드는 진폭과 시간 지연을 코사인·사인 계수로 바꾼다. 두 계수를 합성하면 cos(omega*t-phi)이다. 입력 위상 PFPH는 각도가 아니라 시간 지연이다. |
| <a id="eq-2-68"></a>[T12・PDF 38쪽・인쇄 25쪽・식 2.68](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=38)<br>`\displaystyle A_k=\sqrt{a_k^2+b_k^2}`<br>[판독 기록:14](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p037-054.md:14) | [input.f90:1227](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/input.f90:1227) `            AMP = G*PFAM(NPFORE,M)`<br>[input.f90:1228](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/input.f90:1228) `            PCBE_GL(L,M) = AMP*COS(RAD)`<br>[input.f90:1229](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/input.f90:1229) `            PSBE_GL(L,M) = AMP*SIN(RAD)` | 문서에만 | 문서는 사인·코사인 계수에서 진폭을 복원한다. 코드의 확인한 입력 경로는 진폭을 읽어 사인·코사인 계수를 만든다. 이 역변환을 계산하는 독립 줄은 없다. |
| <a id="eq-2-69"></a>[T12・PDF 38쪽・인쇄 25쪽・식 2.69](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=38)<br>`\displaystyle f_k=\arctan\left(\frac{b_k}{a_k}\right)`<br>[판독 기록:14](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p037-054.md:14) | [input.f90:1226](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/input.f90:1226) `            RAD = PI2*PFPH(NPFORE,M)/TCP(M)`<br>[input.f90:1227](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/input.f90:1227) `            AMP = G*PFAM(NPFORE,M)`<br>[input.f90:1228](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/input.f90:1228) `            PCBE_GL(L,M) = AMP*COS(RAD)`<br>[input.f90:1229](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/input.f90:1229) `            PSBE_GL(L,M) = AMP*SIN(RAD)` | 문서에만 | 문서 좌변 f_k는 앞 문장의 phi_k와 다르다. 이 식은 계수에서 위상을 복원하는 관계이다. 코드 입력 경로는 시간 지연을 위상으로 바꾸므로 이 arctan 역변환을 사용하지 않는다. 사분면을 보존하려면 atan2(b,a)가 필요한 것은 해석이다. |
| <a id="eq-2-70"></a>[T12・PDF 39쪽・인쇄 26쪽・식 2.70](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=39)<br>`\displaystyle Q=AV=AC\sqrt{RS}=K\sqrt{S}`<br>[판독 기록:15](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p037-054.md:15) | [mod_hydstructure.f90:309](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/mod_hydstructure.f90:309) `        QHS = KCON*SQRT(S)`<br>[mod_hydstructure.f90:959](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/mod_hydstructure.f90:959) `        KCON = FAREA/HS_MANN(IHYD)*HRAD**0.6667  ! *** CONVEYANCE` | 일치 | 수로가 만관인 분기에서 KCON√S를 계산한다. KCON은 A R^(2/3)/n이다. 최소 경사와 유량 제한은 식 밖의 처리이다. |
| <a id="eq-2-71"></a>[T12・PDF 39쪽・인쇄 26쪽・식 2.71](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=39)<br>`\displaystyle C=\frac{1}{n}R^{\frac{1}{6}}`<br>[판독 기록:15](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p037-054.md:15) | [mod_hydstructure.f90:959](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/mod_hydstructure.f90:959) `        KCON = FAREA/HS_MANN(IHYD)*HRAD**0.6667  ! *** CONVEYANCE` | 일치 | Chezy 계수를 별도 변수로 만들지 않고 A·C·√R에서 C=R^(1/6)/n을 소거한다. 코드의 0.6667은 2/3의 소수 근사이다. |
| <a id="eq-2-72"></a>[T12・PDF 40쪽・인쇄 27쪽・식 2.72](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=40)<br>`\displaystyle H_{HW}=y_c+\frac{V_c^2}{2g}`<br>[판독 기록:16](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p037-054.md:16) | [mod_hydstructure.f90:273](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/mod_hydstructure.f90:273) `      HCR =  HUD - 0.5*HRAD               ! *** Critical depth`<br>[mod_hydstructure.f90:274](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/mod_hydstructure.f90:274) `      HCR = min(HCR,DIA)`<br>[mod_hydstructure.f90:275](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/mod_hydstructure.f90:275) `      VCR = SQRT(9.81*HRAD)`<br>[mod_hydstructure.f90:276](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/mod_hydstructure.f90:276) `      QCR = VCR*FAREA` | 불일치 | 코드는 HCR=HUD−HRAD/2와 VCR=√(9.81 HRAD)를 계산한다. HCR을 DIA로 제한한 뒤에는 HUD=HCR+VCR²/(2g)를 보존하지 않는다. |
| <a id="eq-2-73"></a>[T12・PDF 40쪽・인쇄 27쪽・식 2.73](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=40)<br>`\displaystyle \frac{V_c^2}{2g}=\frac{D}{2}`<br>[판독 기록:16](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p037-054.md:16) | [mod_hydstructure.f90:275](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/mod_hydstructure.f90:275) `      VCR = SQRT(9.81*HRAD)`<br>[mod_hydstructure.f90:956](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/mod_hydstructure.f90:956) `    HRAD = FAREA/WETPER                          ! *** HYDRAULIC RADIUS` | 불일치 | 문서의 D는 A/T이다. 코드는 VCR²/(2g)=HRAD/2를 사용한다. HRAD는 A/젖은 둘레이다. |
| <a id="eq-2-74"></a>[T12・PDF 40쪽・인쇄 27쪽・식 2.74](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=40)<br>`\displaystyle D=\frac{A}{T}`<br>[판독 기록:16](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p037-054.md:16) | [mod_hydstructure.f90:956](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/mod_hydstructure.f90:956) `    HRAD = FAREA/WETPER                          ! *** HYDRAULIC RADIUS` | 불일치 | 문서는 수리 수심 A/T를 정의한다. CROSS_SECTION은 HRAD=FAREA/WETPER를 반환한다. 원형 관거의 수면 폭 T와 젖은 둘레 WETPER는 다른 값이다. |
| <a id="eq-2-75"></a>[T12・PDF 40쪽・인쇄 27쪽・식 2.75](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=40)<br>`\displaystyle y_c=H_{HW}-\frac{D}{2}`<br>[판독 기록:16](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p037-054.md:16) | [mod_hydstructure.f90:273](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/mod_hydstructure.f90:273) `      HCR =  HUD - 0.5*HRAD               ! *** Critical depth`<br>[mod_hydstructure.f90:274](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/mod_hydstructure.f90:274) `      HCR = min(HCR,DIA)`<br>[mod_hydstructure.f90:275](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/mod_hydstructure.f90:275) `      VCR = SQRT(9.81*HRAD)` | 불일치 | 코드는 수리 수심 D 대신 수리 반경 HRAD를 뺀다. 코드는 HCR을 관경 이하로 제한한다. |
| <a id="eq-2-76"></a>[T12・PDF 40쪽・인쇄 27쪽・식 2.76](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=40)<br>`\displaystyle Q_{cr}=V_cA`<br>[판독 기록:16](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p037-054.md:16) | [mod_hydstructure.f90:276](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/mod_hydstructure.f90:276) `      QCR = VCR*FAREA` | 일치 | QCR=VCR·FAREA로 임계 유량을 계산한다. VCR을 만드는 수리 반경의 차이는 식 (2.73) 판정에 기록했다. |
| <a id="eq-2-77"></a>[T12・PDF 41쪽・인쇄 28쪽・식 2.77](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=41)<br>`\displaystyle H_{HW}=y_n+\frac{V^2}{2g}`<br>[판독 기록:17](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p037-054.md:17) | [mod_hydstructure.f90:324](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/mod_hydstructure.f90:324) `        YN = HUD - HRAD**1.3333*S/(19.62*HS_MANN(IHYD)**2)` | 일치 | 식의 에너지 관계를 yn에 대해 정리한 형태를 사용한다. 코드의 19.62는 2g이다. 이후 단면 재평가로 바뀐 속도까지 이 관계를 반복해 수렴시키지는 않는다. |
| <a id="eq-2-78"></a>[T12・PDF 41쪽・인쇄 28쪽・식 2.78](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=41)<br>`\displaystyle V=\frac{1}{n}R^{\frac{2}{3}}\sqrt{S}`<br>[판독 기록:17](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p037-054.md:17) | [mod_hydstructure.f90:319](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/mod_hydstructure.f90:319) `        VN = HRAD**0.6667 * S**0.5 / HS_MANN(IHYD)`<br>[mod_hydstructure.f90:320](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/mod_hydstructure.f90:320) `        QHS = VN*FAREA     ! *** Q AT HDD`<br>[mod_hydstructure.f90:326](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/mod_hydstructure.f90:326) `        VN = (FAREA/WETPER)**0.6667*S**0.5/HS_MANN(IHYD)` | 일치 | Manning 유속 R^(2/3)√S/n을 사용한다. 지수 0.6667은 2/3의 소수 근사이다. |
| <a id="eq-2-79"></a>[T12・PDF 41쪽・인쇄 28쪽・식 2.79](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=41)<br>`\displaystyle y_n=H_{HW}-\frac{1}{2g}\frac{1}{n^2}R^{\frac{4}{3}}S`<br>[판독 기록:17](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p037-054.md:17) | [mod_hydstructure.f90:324](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/mod_hydstructure.f90:324) `        YN = HUD - HRAD**1.3333*S/(19.62*HS_MANN(IHYD)**2)`<br>[mod_hydstructure.f90:325](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/mod_hydstructure.f90:325) `        call CROSS_SECTION(IHYD, YN, HOPEN, WOPEN, SOPEN, DIA, FAREA, WETPER, HRAD, TWID)`<br>[mod_hydstructure.f90:326](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/mod_hydstructure.f90:326) `        VN = (FAREA/WETPER)**0.6667*S**0.5/HS_MANN(IHYD)` | 일치 | yn=HUD−R^(4/3)S/(2gn²)의 대입식과 일치한다. 코드의 1.3333은 4/3의 소수 근사이다. 문서 본문의 적응적 반복과 달리 이 분기는 CROSS_SECTION을 한 번 호출한다. |
| <a id="eq-2-80"></a>[T12・PDF 41쪽・인쇄 28쪽・식 2.80](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=41)<br>`\displaystyle Q=C_dW\sqrt{2gH_{HW}^{3}}`<br>[판독 기록:17](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p037-054.md:17) | [mod_hydstructure.f90:463](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/mod_hydstructure.f90:463) `        QHS = CDD*WOPEN*SQRT2G*HUD**1.5`<br>[mod_hydstructure.f90:448](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/mod_hydstructure.f90:448) `        QHS = 2.0D0/3.0D0*CDD*WOPEN*SQRT2G*HUD**1.5`<br>[mod_hydstructure.f90:452](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/mod_hydstructure.f90:452) `        QHS = 8.0D0/15.0D0*CDD*SQRT2G*TAN(0.5*HS_ANGLE(IHYD))*HUD**2.5` | 일치 | 넓은 마루 둑 분기의 Cd·W·√(2g)·HUD^(3/2)와 일치한다. 직사각형 날카로운 마루 분기는 2/3을 별도로 곱한다. 삼각형 분기는 수심 지수 2.5를 사용한다. 문서의 Cd 하나를 모든 형상 입력 계수와 동일시할 수 없다. |
| <a id="eq-2-81"></a>[T12・PDF 41쪽・인쇄 28쪽・식 2.81](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=41)<br>`\displaystyle Q=\left(1-\frac{H_{TW}}{H_{HW}}\right)^{0.385}C_dW\sqrt{2gH_{HW}^{3}}`<br>[판독 기록:17](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p037-054.md:17) | [mod_hydstructure.f90:472](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/mod_hydstructure.f90:472) `      if( ZHD > USINV )then`<br>[mod_hydstructure.f90:473](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/mod_hydstructure.f90:473) `        QHS = QHS*(1.-HDD/HUD)**0.385`<br>[mod_hydstructure.f90:474](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/mod_hydstructure.f90:474) `      endif` | 일치 | 하류 수위가 상류 관저보다 높은 잠김 조건에서 Villemonte 인자 (1−HDD/HUD)^0.385를 곱한다. 기본 자유 유량은 형상 분기로 계산한다. |
| <a id="eq-2-82"></a>[T12・PDF 42쪽・인쇄 29쪽・식 2.82](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=42)<br>`\displaystyle Q=C_1W\sqrt{g\left(\frac{2}{3}H_{HW}\right)^3}`<br>[판독 기록:18](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p037-054.md:18) | [mod_hydstructure.f90:386](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/mod_hydstructure.f90:386) `          Q2 = HS_COEFF(IHYD,1)*W*SQRT(G)*(2./3.*HUD)**1.5     ! *** Super-critical weir flow`<br>[mod_hydstructure.f90:391](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/mod_hydstructure.f90:391) `          QHS = HS_COEFF(IHYD,1)*W*SQRT(G)*(2./3.*HUD)**1.5    ! *** Super-critical weir flow` | 일치 | 초임계 둑 유량 C1·W·√g·(2HUD/3)^1.5와 일치한다. HB<HUD<1.25HB에서는 수문식과 혼합한다. |
| <a id="eq-2-83"></a>[T12・PDF 42쪽・인쇄 29쪽・식 2.83](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=42)<br>`\displaystyle Q=C_2WH_{TW}\sqrt{2g(H_{HW}-H_{TW})}`<br>[판독 기록:18](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p037-054.md:18) | [mod_hydstructure.f90:433](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/mod_hydstructure.f90:433) `          QHS = HS_COEFF(IHYD,2)*W*HDD*SQRT(2.*G*(HUD-HDD))    ! *** Sub-critical weir flow`<br>[mod_hydstructure.f90:407](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/mod_hydstructure.f90:407) `          Q2 = HS_COEFF(IHYD,2)*W*HDD*SQRT(2.*G*(HUD-HDD))     ! *** Sub-critical weir flow` | 일치 | 아임계 둑 유량 C2·W·HDD·√[2g(HUD−HDD)]와 일치한다. 문서가 생략한 0.64≤HDD/HUD≤0.68 전이 구간과 개구부 전이 구간을 코드가 보간한다. |
| <a id="eq-2-84"></a>[T12・PDF 42쪽・인쇄 29쪽・식 2.84](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=42)<br>`\displaystyle Q=C_3A\sqrt{2gH_{HW}}`<br>[판독 기록:18](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p037-054.md:18) | [mod_hydstructure.f90:379](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/mod_hydstructure.f90:379) `        if( HUD >= 1.25*HB )then`<br>[mod_hydstructure.f90:380](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/mod_hydstructure.f90:380) `          QHS = HS_COEFF(IHYD,3)*W*HB*SQRT(2.*G*HUD)           ! *** Free sluice flow` | 일치 | 개구 면적 W·HB와 계수 C3를 사용한다. HUD≥1.25HB와 HDD≤0.64HUD에서 직접 적용한다. |
| <a id="eq-2-85"></a>[T12・PDF 42쪽・인쇄 29쪽・식 2.85](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=42)<br>`\displaystyle Q=C_4A\sqrt{2g(H_{HW}-H_{TW})}`<br>[판독 기록:18](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p037-054.md:18) | [mod_hydstructure.f90:418](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/mod_hydstructure.f90:418) `      elseif( HDD >= 0.68*HUD )then`<br>[mod_hydstructure.f90:422](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/mod_hydstructure.f90:422) `          QHS = HS_COEFF(IHYD,4)*W*HB*SQRT(2.*G*(HUD-HDD))     ! *** Submerged orifice flow` | 일치 | 잠긴 수문에서 C4·W·HB·√[2g(HUD−HDD)]를 사용한다. 코드는 HDD=0.68HUD를 잠긴 분기에 포함한다. |
| <a id="eq-2-86"></a>[T12・PDF 43쪽・인쇄 30쪽・식 2.86](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=43)<br>`\displaystyle Q=C_2A\sqrt{2g(H_{HW}+0.5B)}`<br>[판독 기록:19](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p037-054.md:19) | [mod_hydstructure.f90:494](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/mod_hydstructure.f90:494) `        HUD = ZHU - USINV + 0.5*HB  ! *** HUD based on centerline`<br>[mod_hydstructure.f90:495](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/mod_hydstructure.f90:495) `        QHS = CDD*FAREA*(2.*G*HUD)**0.5`<br>[mod_hydstructure.f90:497](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/mod_hydstructure.f90:497) `        ! *** Account for submergence (Villemonte_1947_Submerged_weir_discharge_studies)`<br>[mod_hydstructure.f90:498](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/mod_hydstructure.f90:498) `        if( ZHD > USINV )then`<br>[mod_hydstructure.f90:499](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/mod_hydstructure.f90:499) `          QHS = QHS*(1.-HDD/HUD)**0.385` | 불일치 | 루트 안의 HUD+0.5HB 형태는 같다. 문서는 H_HW를 관 중심선 위 수두로 정의한다. 코드는 관저 기준 수두에 0.5HB를 더한다. 코드는 부분 잠김에 추가 Villemonte 인자를 적용한다. |
| <a id="eq-2-87"></a>[T12・PDF 43쪽・인쇄 30쪽・식 2.87](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=43)<br>`\displaystyle V_i=V_0\times\cos(\theta_3-\theta_1)`<br>[판독 기록:19](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p037-054.md:19) | [Propwash/Calc_Prop_Erosion.f90:633](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Propwash/Calc_Prop_Erosion.f90:633) `        ang = all_ships(i).heading - 0.5*PI         `<br>[Propwash/Calc_Prop_Erosion.f90:634](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Propwash/Calc_Prop_Erosion.f90:634) `        cos1 = cos(ang)`<br>[Propwash/Calc_Prop_Erosion.f90:635](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Propwash/Calc_Prop_Erosion.f90:635) `        sin1 = sin(ang)`<br>[Propwash/Calc_Prop_Erosion.f90:637](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Propwash/Calc_Prop_Erosion.f90:637) `        UHC = all_ships(i).efflux_vel*cos1`<br>[Propwash/Calc_Prop_Erosion.f90:642](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Propwash/Calc_Prop_Erosion.f90:642) `        UHC1 = CUN(L)*UHC + CUE(L)*VHB` | 일치 | 분출 속도에 cos를 곱한 뒤 격자 회전 계수 CUN·CUE로 i 성분을 만든다. θ3−θ1을 직접 쓰지 않고 두 단계 회전으로 계산한다. |
| <a id="eq-2-88"></a>[T12・PDF 44쪽・인쇄 31쪽・식 2.88](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=44)<br>`\displaystyle V_j=V_0\times\sin(\theta_3-\theta_1)`<br>[판독 기록:20](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p037-054.md:20) | [Propwash/Calc_Prop_Erosion.f90:635](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Propwash/Calc_Prop_Erosion.f90:635) `        sin1 = sin(ang)`<br>[Propwash/Calc_Prop_Erosion.f90:637](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Propwash/Calc_Prop_Erosion.f90:637) `        UHC = all_ships(i).efflux_vel*cos1`<br>[Propwash/Calc_Prop_Erosion.f90:638](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Propwash/Calc_Prop_Erosion.f90:638) `        VHB = all_ships(i).efflux_vel*sin1`<br>[Propwash/Calc_Prop_Erosion.f90:640](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Propwash/Calc_Prop_Erosion.f90:640) `        ! *** Velocity components rotated to grid`<br>[Propwash/Calc_Prop_Erosion.f90:641](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Propwash/Calc_Prop_Erosion.f90:641) `        VHB1 = CVN(L)*UHC + CVE(L)*VHB            ` | 일치 | 분출 속도의 sin 성분과 격자 회전 계수 CVN·CVE를 결합한다. 회전 계수가 직교 격자의 방향 코사인일 때 문서와 같다. |
| <a id="eq-2-89"></a>[T12・PDF 44쪽・인쇄 31쪽・식 2.89](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=44)<br>`\displaystyle \theta_3=\theta_2-\frac{\pi}{2}`<br>[판독 기록:20](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p037-054.md:20) | [Propwash/Calc_Prop_Erosion.f90:633](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Propwash/Calc_Prop_Erosion.f90:633) `        ang = all_ships(i).heading - 0.5*PI         ` | 일치 | 코드의 heading−0.5PI가 문서의 θ2−π/2이다. |
| <a id="eq-2-90"></a>[T12・PDF 44쪽・인쇄 31쪽・식 2.90](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=44)<br>`\displaystyle M_{pi}=\lvert V_i\times A_P\rvert\times V_i\times f_p`<br>[판독 기록:20](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p037-054.md:20) | [Propwash/Calc_Prop_Erosion.f90:645](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Propwash/Calc_Prop_Erosion.f90:645) `        PAREA = all_ships(i).prop_area * FRAC_POWER`<br>[Propwash/Calc_Prop_Erosion.f90:677](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Propwash/Calc_Prop_Erosion.f90:677) `          if( SGZU(L,K) > 0.0 ) PSGZU = 1.0/SGZU(L,K)`<br>[Propwash/Calc_Prop_Erosion.f90:678](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Propwash/Calc_Prop_Erosion.f90:678) `          UHC2  = -RATIO*ABS(UHC1*PAREA)*UHC1`<br>[Propwash/Calc_Prop_Erosion.f90:679](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Propwash/Calc_Prop_Erosion.f90:679) `          if( SUB(L) < 0.5 .or. UHC1 > 0.0 )then`<br>[Propwash/Calc_Prop_Erosion.f90:680](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Propwash/Calc_Prop_Erosion.f90:680) `            ! *** Apply momentum to east face`<br>[Propwash/Calc_Prop_Erosion.f90:681](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Propwash/Calc_Prop_Erosion.f90:681) `            FUHJ(LEC(L),K) = PSGZU*UHC2`<br>[Propwash/Calc_Prop_Erosion.f90:682](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Propwash/Calc_Prop_Erosion.f90:682) `          else`<br>[Propwash/Calc_Prop_Erosion.f90:683](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Propwash/Calc_Prop_Erosion.f90:683) `            ! *** Apply momentum to west face`<br>[Propwash/Calc_Prop_Erosion.f90:684](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Propwash/Calc_Prop_Erosion.f90:684) `            FUHJ(L,K)      = PSGZU*UHC2`<br>[calexp.f90:685](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calexp.f90:685) `        FX(L,K) = FSGZU(L,K)*( FUHU(L,K)-FUHU(LW,K) + FVHU(LN,K)-FVHU(L,K) ) + FUHJ(L,K)   ! ***  M4/S2` | 일치 | 층 비율 RATIO와 격자층 비율 역수 PSGZU를 곱한다. FUHJ에 음의 운동량을 저장한다. FX를 운동량 식에서 빼므로 실제 유속 갱신의 부호는 문서의 양의 Mpi와 같다. FRAC_POWER는 고정 셀별 추가 배분이다. |
| <a id="eq-2-91"></a>[T12・PDF 44쪽・인쇄 31쪽・식 2.91](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=44)<br>`\displaystyle M_{pj}=\lvert V_j\times A_P\rvert\times V_j\times f_p`<br>[판독 기록:20](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p037-054.md:20) | [Propwash/Calc_Prop_Erosion.f90:687](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Propwash/Calc_Prop_Erosion.f90:687) `          PSGZV = 0.0`<br>[Propwash/Calc_Prop_Erosion.f90:688](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Propwash/Calc_Prop_Erosion.f90:688) `          if( SGZV(L,K) > 0.0 ) PSGZV = 1.0/SGZV(L,K)`<br>[Propwash/Calc_Prop_Erosion.f90:689](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Propwash/Calc_Prop_Erosion.f90:689) `          VHB2  = -RATIO*ABS(VHB1*PAREA)*VHB1`<br>[Propwash/Calc_Prop_Erosion.f90:690](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Propwash/Calc_Prop_Erosion.f90:690) `          if( SVB(L) < 0.5 .or. VHB1 > 0.0 )then`<br>[Propwash/Calc_Prop_Erosion.f90:691](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Propwash/Calc_Prop_Erosion.f90:691) `            ! *** Apply momentum to north face`<br>[Propwash/Calc_Prop_Erosion.f90:692](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Propwash/Calc_Prop_Erosion.f90:692) `            FVHJ(LNC(L),K) = PSGZV*VHB2`<br>[Propwash/Calc_Prop_Erosion.f90:693](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Propwash/Calc_Prop_Erosion.f90:693) `          else`<br>[Propwash/Calc_Prop_Erosion.f90:694](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Propwash/Calc_Prop_Erosion.f90:694) `            ! *** Apply momentum to south face`<br>[Propwash/Calc_Prop_Erosion.f90:695](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Propwash/Calc_Prop_Erosion.f90:695) `            FVHJ(L,K)      = PSGZV*VHB2`<br>[calexp.f90:686](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calexp.f90:686) `        FY(L,K) = FSGZV(L,K)*( FVHV(L,K)-FVHV(LS,K) + FUHV(LE,K)-FUHV(L,K) ) + FVHJ(L,K)   ! ***  M4/S2` | 일치 | VHB1에 대해 \|VHB1·PAREA\|VHB1을 계산한다. 음의 저장값을 운동량 갱신에서 빼는 부호 관례를 사용한다. RATIO는 수직 배분 비율이다. |
| <a id="eq-2-92"></a>[T12・PDF 45쪽・인쇄 32쪽・식 2.92](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=45)<br>`\displaystyle \begin{aligned}&\frac{\partial}{\partial t}(m_xm_yHu)+\frac{\partial}{\partial x}(m_yHuu)+\frac{\partial}{\partial y}(m_xHvu)+\frac{\partial}{\partial z}(m_xm_ywu)\\&\quad-\left(v\frac{\partial m_y}{\partial x}-u\frac{\partial m_x}{\partial y}\right)Hv-m_xm_yfHv\\&=-m_yH\frac{\partial p}{\partial x}-m_yHg\frac{\partial\zeta}{\partial x}+m_yHgb\frac{\partial h}{\partial x}-m_yHgbz\frac{\partial H}{\partial x}+\frac{\partial}{\partial z}\left(\frac{m_xm_yA_v}{H}\frac{\partial u}{\partial z}\right)+S_u\end{aligned}`<br>[판독 기록:21](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p037-054.md:21) | [calexp.f90:685](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calexp.f90:685) `        FX(L,K) = FSGZU(L,K)*( FUHU(L,K)-FUHU(LW,K) + FVHU(LN,K)-FVHU(L,K) ) + FUHJ(L,K)   ! ***  M4/S2`<br>[calexp.f90:620](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calexp.f90:620) `          FCAX(L,K) = ROLD*FCAX(L,K) + 0.25*RNEW*SCAX(L)*(CAC(L,K)*(V(LN,K) + V(L,K)) + CAC(LW,K)*(V(LNW,K) + V(LW,K)))`<br>[calexp.f90:1215](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calexp.f90:1215) `            FBBX(L,K) = ROLD*FBBX(L,K) + RNEW*SBX(L)*GP*HU(L)*( HU(L)*( (B(L,K+1)-B(LW,K+1))*DZCK(K+1) + (B(L,K)-B(LW,K))*DZCK(K) ) - (B(L,K+1)-B(L,K)+B(LW,K+1)-B(LW,K))*(BELV(L)-BELV(LW)+Z(L,K)*(HP(L)-HP(LW))) )`<br>[calpuv9c.f90:256](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calpuv9c.f90:256) `      FUHDYE(L) = UHDY1E(L) - DELTD2*SUB(L)*HRUO(L)*HUTMP(L)*(P1(L)-P1(LW)) + SUB(L)*DELT*DXIU(L)*(DXYU(L)*(TSX1(L)-RITB1*TBX1(L)) + FCAXE(L) + FPGXE(L) - SNLT*FXE(L))`<br>[caluvw.f90:409](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caluvw.f90:409) `          DU(L,K)  = (DU(L,K)-RCDZL*DU(L,K-1))*EU` | 일치 | 수평 이류·코리올리·바로클리닉 기울기·수직 응력을 분리해 갱신한다. 자유 수면 기울기는 외부 모드에 남는다. Su에는 수평 응력과 추가 운동량원을 넣는다. 문서의 연속식과 코드의 분할 이산식의 항 구조를 대조한 판정이다. |
| <a id="eq-2-93"></a>[T12・PDF 46쪽・인쇄 33쪽・식 2.93](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=46)<br>`\displaystyle \begin{aligned}&\frac{\partial}{\partial t}(m_xm_yHv)+\frac{\partial}{\partial x}(m_yHuv)+\frac{\partial}{\partial y}(m_xHvv)+\frac{\partial}{\partial z}(m_xm_ywv)\\&\quad+\left(v\frac{\partial m_y}{\partial x}-u\frac{\partial m_x}{\partial y}\right)Hu+m_xm_yfHu\\&=-m_xH\frac{\partial p}{\partial y}-m_xHg\frac{\partial\zeta}{\partial y}+m_xHgb\frac{\partial h}{\partial y}-m_xHgbz\frac{\partial H}{\partial y}+\frac{\partial}{\partial z}\left(\frac{m_xm_yA_v}{H}\frac{\partial v}{\partial z}\right)+S_v\end{aligned}`<br>[판독 기록:22](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p037-054.md:22) | [calexp.f90:686](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calexp.f90:686) `        FY(L,K) = FSGZV(L,K)*( FVHV(L,K)-FVHV(LS,K) + FUHV(LE,K)-FUHV(L,K) ) + FVHJ(L,K)   ! ***  M4/S2`<br>[calexp.f90:621](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calexp.f90:621) `          FCAY(L,K) = ROLD*FCAY(L,K) + 0.25*RNEW*SCAY(L)*(CAC(L,K)*(U(LE,K) + U(L,K)) + CAC(LS,K)*(U(LSE,K) + U(LS,K)))`<br>[calexp.f90:1216](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calexp.f90:1216) `            FBBY(L,K) = ROLD*FBBY(L,K) + RNEW*SBY(L)*GP*HV(L)*( HV(L)*( (B(L,K+1)-B(LS,K+1))*DZCK(K+1) + (B(L,K)-B(LS,K))*DZCK(K) ) - (B(L,K+1)-B(L,K)+B(LS,K+1)-B(LS,K))*(BELV(L)-BELV(LS)+Z(L,K)*(HP(L)-HP(LS))) )`<br>[calpuv9c.f90:257](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calpuv9c.f90:257) `      FVHDXE(L) = VHDX1E(L) - DELTD2*SVB(L)*HRVO(L)*HVTMP(L)*(P1(L)-P1(LS)) + SVB(L)*DELT*DYIV(L)*(DXYV(L)*(TSY1(L)-RITB1*TBY1(L)) - FCAYE(L) + FPGYE(L) - SNLT*FYE(L))`<br>[caluvw.f90:435](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caluvw.f90:435) `          DV(L,K)  = (DV(L,K)-RCDZL*DV(L,K-1))*EV` | 일치 | y 방향 코리올리 힘은 −FCAYE이다. 바로클리닉 힘은 FPGYE이다. 수직 응력은 DV 삼대각 갱신으로 처리한다. Su와 Sv를 서로 바꾸지 않는다. |
| <a id="eq-2-94"></a>[T12・PDF 46쪽・인쇄 33쪽・식 2.94](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=46)<br>`\displaystyle P_k=m_yHu_k,\ Q_k=m_xHv_k`<br>[판독 기록:22](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p037-054.md:22) | [caluvw.f90:540](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caluvw.f90:540) `        UHDYF(L,K) = UHDYF(L,K)*DYU(L)`<br>[caluvw.f90:541](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caluvw.f90:541) `        VHDXF(L,K) = VHDXF(L,K)*DXV(L)`<br>[caluvw.f90:622](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caluvw.f90:622) `        UHDY(L,K)  = UHDYF(L,K)*SGZU(L,K)               ! *** LAYER SPECIFIC FLOWS`<br>[caluvw.f90:624](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caluvw.f90:624) `        VHDX(L,K)  = VHDXF(L,K)*SGZV(L,K)               ! *** LAYER SPECIFIC FLOWS` | 일치 | UHDYF·VHDXF는 층 비율을 곱하기 전 수평 수송량이다. UHDY·VHDX는 SGZU·SGZV를 곱한 실제 층 유량이다. 문서의 Pk·Qk를 실제 층 유량과 곧바로 동일시하면 층 비율을 두 번 곱하게 된다. |
| <a id="eq-2-95"></a>[T12・PDF 46쪽・인쇄 33쪽・식 2.95](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=46)<br>`\displaystyle \begin{aligned}&\frac{\partial}{\partial t}(m_xP_k\Delta_k)+\frac{\partial}{\partial x}(P_ku_k\Delta_k)+\frac{\partial}{\partial y}(Q_ku_k\Delta_k)+m\left[(wu)_k-(wu)_{k-1}\right]\\&\quad-\left(v_k\frac{\partial m_y}{\partial x}-u_k\frac{\partial m_x}{\partial y}\right)Hv_k\Delta_k-m_yfQ_k\Delta_k\\&=-\frac{1}{2}m_yH\Delta_k\frac{\partial}{\partial x}(p_k+p_{k-1})-m_yH\Delta_kg\frac{\partial\zeta}{\partial x}+m_yH\Delta_kgb_k\frac{\partial h}{\partial x}\\&\quad-0.5m_yH\Delta_kgb_k(z_k+z_{k-1})\frac{\partial H}{\partial x}+m\left[(\tau_{xz})_k-(\tau_{xz})_{k-1}\right]+(S_u\Delta)_k\end{aligned}`<br>[판독 기록:22](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p037-054.md:22) | [calexp.f90:685](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calexp.f90:685) `        FX(L,K) = FSGZU(L,K)*( FUHU(L,K)-FUHU(LW,K) + FVHU(LN,K)-FVHU(L,K) ) + FUHJ(L,K)   ! ***  M4/S2`<br>[calexp.f90:243](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calexp.f90:243) `            WUU = 0.25*DXYU(L)*(W2(L,K) + W2(LW,K))`<br>[calexp.f90:244](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calexp.f90:244) `            WVV = 0.25*DXYV(L)*(W2(L,K) + W2(LS,K))`<br>[calexp.f90:245](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calexp.f90:245) `            FWU(L,K) = max(WUU,0.)*U1(L,K) + min(WUU,0.)*U1(L,K+1)`<br>[calexp.f90:1215](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calexp.f90:1215) `            FBBX(L,K) = ROLD*FBBX(L,K) + RNEW*SBX(L)*GP*HU(L)*( HU(L)*( (B(L,K+1)-B(LW,K+1))*DZCK(K+1) + (B(L,K)-B(LW,K))*DZCK(K) ) - (B(L,K+1)-B(L,K)+B(LW,K+1)-B(LW,K))*(BELV(L)-BELV(LW)+Z(L,K)*(HP(L)-HP(LW))) )`<br>[calexp.f90:1309](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calexp.f90:1309) `          DU(L,K) = CDZFU(L,K)*( H1U(L)*(U1(L,K+1)-U1(L,K))*DELTI + DXYIU(L)*(FCAX(L,K+1)-FCAX(L,K)   + FBBX(L,K) + SNLT*(FX(L,K)-FX(L,K+1))) )` | 일치 | 층 수평·수직 운동량 플럭스의 발산을 FX에 모은다. 상하층 차를 취해 DU를 만든다. σ 부력항의 부호는 BELV=−h로 바꾸면 같다. |
| <a id="eq-2-96"></a>[T12・PDF 47쪽・인쇄 34쪽・식 2.96](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=47)<br>`\displaystyle \begin{aligned}&\frac{\partial}{\partial t}(m_yQ_k\Delta_k)+\frac{\partial}{\partial x}(P_kv_k\Delta_k)+\frac{\partial}{\partial y}(Q_kv_k\Delta_k)\\&\quad+m\left[(wv)_k-(wv)_{k-1}\right]+\left(v_k\frac{\partial m_y}{\partial x}-u_k\frac{\partial m_x}{\partial y}\right)Hu_k\Delta_k+m_xfP_k\Delta_k\\&=-\frac{1}{2}m_xH\Delta_k\frac{\partial}{\partial y}(p_k+p_{k-1})-m_xH\Delta_kg\frac{\partial\zeta}{\partial y}+m_xH\Delta_kgb_k\frac{\partial h}{\partial y}\\&\quad-0.5m_xH\Delta_kgb_k(z_k+z_{k-1})\frac{\partial H}{\partial y}+m\left[(t_{yz})_k-m(t_{yz})_{k-1}\right]+(S_v\Delta)_k\end{aligned}`<br>[판독 기록:23](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p037-054.md:23) | [calexp.f90:1310](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calexp.f90:1310) `          DV(L,K) = CDZFV(L,K)*( H1V(L)*(V1(L,K+1)-V1(L,K))*DELTI + DXYIV(L)*(FCAY(L,K)  -FCAY(L,K+1) + FBBY(L,K) + SNLT*(FY(L,K)-FY(L,K+1))) )`<br>[caluvw.f90:435](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caluvw.f90:435) `          DV(L,K)  = (DV(L,K)-RCDZL*DV(L,K-1))*EV` | 불일치 | 문서는 m[(tyz)k−m(tyz)k−1]로 하부 응력에 m을 한 번 더 곱한다. 코드는 인접 응력의 차에 같은 계수를 적용한다. PDF 이미지에서도 안쪽 m을 확인했다. |
| <a id="eq-2-97"></a>[T12・PDF 47쪽・인쇄 34쪽・식 2.97](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=47)<br>`\displaystyle (\tau_{xz})_k=\frac{2}{H}(A_v)_k\frac{u_{k+1}-u_k}{\Delta_{k+1}+\Delta_k}`<br>[판독 기록:23](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p037-054.md:23) | [aaefdc.f90:2460](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/aaefdc.f90:2460) `      DZGU(L,K) = 0.5*(SGZU(L,K)+SGZU(L,K+1))`<br>[caluvw.f90:495](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caluvw.f90:495) `        DU(L,K) = SUB3D(L,K)*DZGU(L,K)*HU(L)*AVUI(L,K)*( DU(L,K) - AAU(L)*UUU(L,K) )` | 일치 | DZGU는 인접층 비율의 산술 평균이다. AVUI를 사용한 응력 풀이를 DU=Δz·H·AVUI·응력으로 역변환한다. 문서의 2/(Δk+1+Δk)와 같은 간격을 사용한다. |
| <a id="eq-2-98"></a>[T12・PDF 47쪽・인쇄 34쪽・식 2.98](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=47)<br>`\displaystyle (t_{yz})_k=\frac{2}{H}(A_v)_k\frac{v_{k+1}-v_k}{\Delta_{k+1}+\Delta_k}`<br>[판독 기록:23](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p037-054.md:23) | [aaefdc.f90:2468](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/aaefdc.f90:2468) `      DZGV(L,K) = 0.5*(SGZV(L,K)+SGZV(L,K+1))`<br>[caluvw.f90:496](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caluvw.f90:496) `        DV(L,K) = SVB3D(L,K)*DZGV(L,K)*HV(L)*AVVI(L,K)*( DV(L,K) - AAV(L)*VVV(L,K) )` | 일치 | DZGV와 AVVI를 사용하는 y 응력 풀이가 x 방향과 대칭이다. 문서의 tyz 표기를 τyz와 같은 응력으로 해석했다. |
| <a id="eq-2-99"></a>[T12・PDF 47쪽・인쇄 34쪽・식 2.99](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=47)<br>`\displaystyle p_k=gH\left(\sum_{j=k}^{K}b_j\Delta_j-b_k\Delta_k\right)+p_s`<br>[판독 기록:23](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p037-054.md:23) | [calebi.f90:113](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calebi.f90:113) `        DBK = DBK + DZCB(K,ND)       `<br>[calebi.f90:114](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calebi.f90:114) `        BK(K,ND) = DBK - 0.5*DZCB(K,ND) `<br>[calpuv9c.f90:217](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calpuv9c.f90:217) `          FPGXE(L) = ROLD*FPGXE(L) + RNEW*(-SBX(L)*SUBD(L)*HU(L)*GP*( (BI2W(L)+BI2W(LW))*(HP(L)-HP(LW)) + 2.0*HU(L)*(BI1W(L)-BI1W(LW)) + (BEW(L)+BEW(LW))*(BELV(L)-BELV(LW)) ) )` | 문서에만 | 인터페이스 압력 pk 자체를 이 식으로 저장하는 문장을 찾지 못했다. 코드는 층 중심의 BK와 깊이 적분 BI1·BI2를 계산해 압력 기울기를 구성한다. BK의 1/2층 항을 인터페이스 식의 −1층 항과 비교해 오류로 판정하지 않았다. |
| <a id="eq-2-100"></a>[T12・PDF 47쪽・인쇄 34쪽・식 2.100](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=47)<br>`\displaystyle \frac{\partial}{\partial t}(m\zeta\Delta_k)+\frac{\partial}{\partial x}(P_k\Delta_k)+\frac{\partial}{\partial y}(Q_k\Delta_k)+m(w_k+w_{k-1})=S_h`<br>[판독 기록:23](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p037-054.md:23) | [caluvw.f90:733](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caluvw.f90:733) `          W(L,K) = W(L,K-1) - 0.5*DXYIP(L)  &`<br>[caluvw.f90:734](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caluvw.f90:734) `                   *( UHDY(LE,K) - UHDY(L,K) + UHDYEK(L,K) + UHDY1(LE,K)-UHDY1(L,K) + UHDY1EK(L,K)   &`<br>[caluvw.f90:735](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caluvw.f90:735) `                    + VHDX(LN,K) - VHDX(L,K) + VHDXEK(L,K) + VHDX1(LN,K)-VHDX1(L,K) + VHDX1EK(L,K) ) &`<br>[caluvw.f90:736](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caluvw.f90:736) `                    + ( QSUM(L,K) - DZC(L,K)*QSUME(L) )*DXYIP(L)` | 불일치 | 문서는 w_k+w_k−1을 쓴다. 코드는 Wk−Wk−1에 수평 유량 발산과 층 원천을 연결한다. PDF 이미지에서 더하기 부호를 확인했다. |
| <a id="eq-2-101"></a>[T12・PDF 47쪽・인쇄 34쪽・식 2.101](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=47)<br>`\displaystyle \begin{aligned}&\frac{\partial}{\partial t}(m_x\hat P)+\sum_{k=1}^{K}\left[\frac{\partial}{\partial x}(P_ku_k\Delta_k)+\frac{\partial}{\partial y}(Q_ku_k\Delta_k)-\left(v_k\frac{\partial m_y}{\partial x}-u_k\frac{\partial m_x}{\partial y}\right)Hv_k\Delta_k-m_yfQ_k\Delta_k\right]\\&=-m_yHg\frac{\partial\zeta}{\partial x}-m_yH\frac{\partial p_s}{\partial x}+m_yHg\hat b\frac{\partial h}{\partial x}-m_yHg\left[\sum_{k=1}^{K}\left(\beta_k\Delta_k+\frac{1}{2}(z_k+z_{k-1})b_k\Delta_k\right)\right]\frac{\partial H}{\partial x}\\&\quad-m_ygH^2\frac{\partial}{\partial x}\left(\sum_{k=1}^{K}\beta_k\Delta_k\right)+m\left[(\tau_{xz})_K-(\tau_{xz})_0\right]+\hat S_u\end{aligned}`<br>[판독 기록:23](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p037-054.md:23) |
