DZCB(K,ND)`<br>[calebi.f90:122](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calebi.f90:122) `        DZCBK = DZCC(K,L)*BK(K,ND)`<br>[calebi.f90:123](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calebi.f90:123) `        BI1C  = BI1C + DZCBK  `<br>[calebi.f90:124](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calebi.f90:124) `        BI2C  = BI2C + DZCBK + ZZC(K,L)*DZCB(K,ND) `<br>[calexp.f90:1133](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calexp.f90:1133) `        FCAXE(L) = FCAXE(L) + FCAX(L,K)*SGZU(L,K)`<br>[calexp.f90:1134](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calexp.f90:1134) `        FCAYE(L) = FCAYE(L) + FCAY(L,K)*SGZV(L,K)`<br>[calexp.f90:1135](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calexp.f90:1135) `        FXE(L) = FXE(L) + FX(L,K)*SGZU(L,K)`<br>[calexp.f90:1136](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calexp.f90:1136) `        FYE(L) = FYE(L) + FY(L,K)*SGZV(L,K)`<br>[calpuv9c.f90:217](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calpuv9c.f90:217) `          FPGXE(L) = ROLD*FPGXE(L) + RNEW*(-SBX(L)*SUBD(L)*HU(L)*GP*( (BI2W(L)+BI2W(LW))*(HP(L)-HP(LW)) + 2.0*HU(L)*(BI1W(L)-BI1W(LW)) + (BEW(L)+BEW(LW))*(BELV(L)-BELV(LW)) ) )`<br>[calpuv9c.f90:256](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calpuv9c.f90:256) `      FUHDYE(L) = UHDY1E(L) - DELTD2*SUB(L)*HRUO(L)*HUTMP(L)*(P1(L)-P1(LW)) + SUB(L)*DELT*DXIU(L)*(DXYU(L)*(TSX1(L)-RITB1*TBX1(L)) + FCAXE(L) + FPGXE(L) - SNLT*FXE(L))` | 일치 | 층 비율로 적분한 이류·코리올리 힘과 BI1·BI2·BE의 부력 기울기를 외부 유량에 적용한다. 표면·바닥 응력은 TSX−TBX이다. |
| <a id="eq-2-102"></a>[T12・PDF 48쪽・인쇄 35쪽・식 2.102](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=48)<br>`\displaystyle \begin{aligned}&\frac{\partial}{\partial t}(m_x\hat Q)+\sum_{k=1}^{K}\left[\frac{\partial}{\partial x}(P_kv_k\Delta_k)+\frac{\partial}{\partial y}(Q_kv_k\Delta_k)-\left(v_k\frac{\partial m_y}{\partial x}-u_k\frac{\partial m_x}{\partial y}\right)Hu_k\Delta_k-m_xfP_k\Delta_k\right]\\&=-m_xHg\frac{\partial\zeta}{\partial y}-m_xH\frac{\partial p_s}{\partial y}+m_xHg\hat b\frac{\partial h}{\partial y}-m_xHg\left[\sum_{k=1}^{K}\left(\beta_k\Delta_k+\frac{1}{2}(z_k+z_{k-1})b_k\Delta_k\right)\right]\frac{\partial H}{\partial y}\\&\quad-m_xgH^2\frac{\partial}{\partial y}\left(\sum_{k=1}^{K}\beta_k\Delta_k\right)+m\left[(\tau_{yz})_K-(\tau_{yz})_0\right]+\hat S_u\end{aligned}`<br>[판독 기록:24](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p037-054.md:24) | [calpuv9c.f90:257](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calpuv9c.f90:257) `      FVHDXE(L) = VHDX1E(L) - DELTD2*SVB(L)*HRVO(L)*HVTMP(L)*(P1(L)-P1(LS)) + SVB(L)*DELT*DYIV(L)*(DXYV(L)*(TSY1(L)-RITB1*TBY1(L)) - FCAYE(L) + FPGYE(L) - SNLT*FYE(L))`<br>[calexp.f90:1134](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calexp.f90:1134) `        FCAYE(L) = FCAYE(L) + FCAY(L,K)*SGZV(L,K)` | 불일치 | 문서는 시간항에 mx를 쓴다. Q̂의 시간항에는 my가 필요하다. 문서는 코리올리·곡률항을 좌변에서 빼지만 코드는 우변에서 −FCAYE를 적용한다. 문서의 마지막 원천은 Ŝu이고 코드는 y 원천을 사용한다. |
| <a id="eq-2-103"></a>[T12・PDF 48쪽・인쇄 35쪽・식 2.103](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=48)<br>`\displaystyle \frac{\partial}{\partial t}(m\zeta)+\frac{\partial}{\partial x}\bar P+\frac{\partial}{\partial y}\bar Q=S_h`<br>[판독 기록:24](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p037-054.md:24) | [calpuv9c.f90:784](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calpuv9c.f90:784) `        HP(L) = H1P(L) + DELT*DXYIP(L)*( QSUME(L) - 0.5*( UHDYE(LE) + UHDY1E(LE) - UHDYE(L) - UHDY1E(L) &`<br>[calpuv9c.f90:785](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calpuv9c.f90:785) `          + VHDXE(LN) + VHDX1E(LN) - VHDXE(L) - VHDX1E(L)) )` | 일치 | 전체 유량의 동서·남북 발산과 QSUME로 수심을 갱신한다. 문서의 P̄·Q̄는 이 문맥에서 깊이 적분 수송량이다. |
| <a id="eq-2-104"></a>[T12・PDF 48쪽・인쇄 35쪽・식 2.104](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=48)<br>`\displaystyle \hat P=m_yH\hat u,\ \hat Q=m_xH\hat v`<br>[판독 기록:24](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p037-054.md:24) | [caluvw.f90:663](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caluvw.f90:663) `          UHE(L) = UHE(L) + UHDYF(L,K)*SGZU(L,K)`<br>[caluvw.f90:664](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caluvw.f90:664) `          VHE(L) = VHE(L) + VHDXF(L,K)*SGZV(L,K)`<br>[calpuv9c.f90:730](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calpuv9c.f90:730) `      UHE(L) = UHDYE(L)*DYIU(L)`<br>[calpuv9c.f90:731](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calpuv9c.f90:731) `      VHE(L) = VHDXE(L)*DXIV(L)` | 일치 | 층 유량을 합산해 전체 수송량을 만든다. UHE·VHE는 폭당 수송량이고 속도 자체가 아니다. 깊이로 나눈 값이 û·v̂이다. |
| <a id="eq-2-105"></a>[T12・PDF 48쪽・인쇄 35쪽・식 2.105](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=48)<br>`\displaystyle \beta_k=\sum_{j=k}^{K}b_j\Delta_j-\frac{1}{2}b_k\Delta_k`<br>[판독 기록:24](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p037-054.md:24) | [calebi.f90:108](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calebi.f90:108) `        DZCB(K,ND) = DZCC(K,L)*B(L,K)`<br>[calebi.f90:113](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calebi.f90:113) `        DBK = DBK + DZCB(K,ND)       `<br>[calebi.f90:114](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calebi.f90:114) `        BK(K,ND) = DBK - 0.5*DZCB(K,ND) ` | 일치 | 위층부터 DBK에 bΔ를 누적한 뒤 현재층의 0.5bΔ를 뺀다. |
| <a id="eq-2-106"></a>[T12・PDF 48쪽・인쇄 35쪽・식 2.106](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=48)<br>`\displaystyle \sum_{k=1}^{K}u_k\Delta_k=\hat u`<br>[판독 기록:24](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p037-054.md:24) | [caluvw.f90:613](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caluvw.f90:613) `      TVARE(L) = TVARE(L) - UHDYE(L)`<br>[caluvw.f90:614](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caluvw.f90:614) `      TVARN(L) = TVARN(L) - VHDXE(L)`<br>[caluvw.f90:621](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caluvw.f90:621) `        UHDYF(L,K) = UHDYF(L,K) - TVARE(L)*CERRU(L,K)`<br>[caluvw.f90:622](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caluvw.f90:622) `        UHDY(L,K)  = UHDYF(L,K)*SGZU(L,K)               ! *** LAYER SPECIFIC FLOWS` | 일치 | 층 유량 합과 외부 유량의 차 ERRU를 층에 보정한다. 공통 면 수심과 폭으로 나누면 ΣukΔk=û가 된다. SGZ에서는 면별 SGZU를 사용한다. |
| <a id="eq-2-107"></a>[T12・PDF 48쪽・인쇄 35쪽・식 2.107](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=48)<br>`\displaystyle \sum_{k=1}^{K}v_k\Delta_k=\hat v`<br>[판독 기록:24](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p037-054.md:24) | [caluvw.f90:613](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caluvw.f90:613) `      TVARE(L) = TVARE(L) - UHDYE(L)`<br>[caluvw.f90:614](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caluvw.f90:614) `      TVARN(L) = TVARN(L) - VHDXE(L)`<br>[caluvw.f90:623](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caluvw.f90:623) `        VHDXF(L,K) = VHDXF(L,K) - TVARN(L)*CERRV(L,K)`<br>[caluvw.f90:624](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caluvw.f90:624) `        VHDX(L,K)  = VHDXF(L,K)*SGZV(L,K)               ! *** LAYER SPECIFIC FLOWS` | 일치 | y 층 유량 합을 외부 VHDXE에 맞춘다. 면별 SGZV를 사용한다. |
| <a id="eq-2-108"></a>[T12・PDF 49쪽・인쇄 36쪽・식 2.108](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=49)<br>`\displaystyle \begin{aligned}&\frac{\partial}{\partial t}\left(m_x\frac{P_{k+1}-P_k}{\Delta_{k+1,k}}\right)+\frac{\partial}{\partial x}\left(\frac{P_{k+1}u_{k+1}-P_ku_k}{\Delta_{k+1,k}}\right)+\frac{\partial}{\partial y}\left(\frac{Q_{k+1}u_{k+1}-Q_ku_k}{\Delta_{k+1,k}}\right)\\&\quad+\frac{m}{\Delta_{k+1,k}}\left[\frac{(wu)_{k+1}-(wu)_k}{\Delta_{k+1}}-\frac{(wu)_k-(wu)_{k-1}}{\Delta_k}\right]-m_yf\frac{Q_{k+1}-Q_k}{\Delta_{k+1,k}}\\&\quad-\frac{1}{\Delta_{k+1,k}}\left[\left(v_{k+1}\frac{\partial m_y}{\partial x}-u_{k+1}\frac{\partial m_x}{\partial y}\right)Hv_{k+1}-\left(v_k\frac{\partial m_y}{\partial x}-u_k\frac{\partial m_x}{\partial y}\right)Hv_k\right]\\&=m_yH\frac{b_{k+1}-b_k}{\Delta_{k+1,k}}g\left(\frac{\partial h}{\partial x}-z_k\frac{\partial H}{\partial x}\right)+\frac{1}{2}\frac{m_yH^2}{\Delta_{k+1,k}}g\left(\Delta_{k+1}\frac{\partial b_{k+1}}{\partial x}+\Delta_k\frac{\partial b_k}{\partial x}\right)\\&\quad+\frac{m}{\Delta_{k+1,k}}\left[\frac{(\tau_{xz})_{k+1}-(\tau_{xz})_k}{\Delta_{k+1}}-\frac{(\tau_{xz})_k-(\tau_{xz})_{k-1}}{\Delta_k}\right]+\frac{(S_u)_{k+1}-(S_u)_k}{\Delta_{k+1,k}}\end{aligned}`<br>[판독 기록:25](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p037-054.md:25) | [calexp.f90:1309](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calexp.f90:1309) `          DU(L,K) = CDZFU(L,K)*( H1U(L)*(U1(L,K+1)-U1(L,K))*DELTI + DXYIU(L)*(FCAX(L,K+1)-FCAX(L,K)   + FBBX(L,K) + SNLT*(FX(L,K)-FX(L,K+1))) )`<br>[calexp.f90:1215](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calexp.f90:1215) `            FBBX(L,K) = ROLD*FBBX(L,K) + RNEW*SBX(L)*GP*HU(L)*( HU(L)*( (B(L,K+1)-B(LW,K+1))*DZCK(K+1) + (B(L,K)-B(LW,K))*DZCK(K) ) - (B(L,K+1)-B(L,K)+B(LW,K+1)-B(LW,K))*(BELV(L)-BELV(LW)+Z(L,K)*(HP(L)-HP(LW))) )`<br>[caluvw.f90:406](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caluvw.f90:406) `          CMU = 1.+RCDZM*HU(L)*AVUI(L,K)`<br>[caluvw.f90:407](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caluvw.f90:407) `          EU  = 1./(CMU-RCDZL*CU1(L,K-1))`<br>[caluvw.f90:408](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caluvw.f90:408) `          CU1(L,K) = RCDZU*EU`<br>[caluvw.f90:409](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caluvw.f90:409) `          DU(L,K)  = (DU(L,K)-RCDZL*DU(L,K-1))*EU` | 일치 | 층간 유속 차의 시간항과 인접층 힘의 차를 DU로 만든다. 부력항의 지형·수심·수평 밀도 기울기를 FBBX에서 계산한다. 수직 응력은 다음 암시적 단계에서 적용한다. |
| <a id="eq-2-109"></a>[T12・PDF 49쪽・인쇄 36쪽・식 2.109](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=49)<br>`\displaystyle \begin{aligned}&\frac{\partial}{\partial t}\left(m_y\frac{Q_{k+1}-Q_k}{\Delta_{k+1,k}}\right)+\frac{\partial}{\partial x}\left(\frac{P_{k+1}v_{k+1}-P_kv_k}{\Delta_{k+1,k}}\right)+\frac{\partial}{\partial y}\left(\frac{Q_{k+1}v_{k+1}-Q_kv_k}{\Delta_{k+1,k}}\right)\\&\quad+\frac{m}{\Delta_{k+1,k}}\left[\frac{(wv)_{k+1}-(wv)_k}{\Delta_{k+1}}-\frac{(wv)_k-(wv)_{k-1}}{\Delta_k}\right]+m_xf\frac{P_{k+1}-P_k}{\Delta_{k+1,k}}\\&\quad+\frac{1}{\Delta_{k+1,k}}\left[\left(v_{k+1}\frac{\partial m_y}{\partial x}-u_{k+1}\frac{\partial m_x}{\partial y}\right)Hu_{k+1}-\left(v_k\frac{\partial m_y}{\partial x}-u_k\frac{\partial m_x}{\partial y}\right)Hu_k\right]\\&=m_xH\frac{b_{k+1}-b_k}{\Delta_{k+1,k}}g\left(\frac{\partial h}{\partial y}-z_k\frac{\partial H}{\partial y}\right)+\frac{1}{2}\frac{m_xH^2}{\Delta_{k+1,k}}g\left(\Delta_{k+1}\frac{\partial b_{k+1}}{\partial y}+\Delta_k\frac{\partial b_k}{\partial y}\right)\\&\quad+\frac{m}{\Delta_{k+1,k}}\left[\frac{(\tau_{yz})_{k+1}-(\tau_{yz})_k}{\Delta_{k+1}}-\frac{(\tau_{yz})_k-(\tau_{yz})_{k-1}}{\Delta_k}\right]+\frac{(S_v)_{k+1}-(S_v)_k}{\Delta_{k+1,k}}\end{aligned}`<br>[판독 기록:25](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p037-054.md:25) | [calexp.f90:1310](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calexp.f90:1310) `          DV(L,K) = CDZFV(L,K)*( H1V(L)*(V1(L,K+1)-V1(L,K))*DELTI + DXYIV(L)*(FCAY(L,K)  -FCAY(L,K+1) + FBBY(L,K) + SNLT*(FY(L,K)-FY(L,K+1))) )`<br>[calexp.f90:1216](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calexp.f90:1216) `            FBBY(L,K) = ROLD*FBBY(L,K) + RNEW*SBY(L)*GP*HV(L)*( HV(L)*( (B(L,K+1)-B(LS,K+1))*DZCK(K+1) + (B(L,K)-B(LS,K))*DZCK(K) ) - (B(L,K+1)-B(L,K)+B(LS,K+1)-B(LS,K))*(BELV(L)-BELV(LS)+Z(L,K)*(HP(L)-HP(LS))) )`<br>[caluvw.f90:432](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caluvw.f90:432) `          CMV = 1.+RCDZM*HV(L)*AVVI(L,K)`<br>[caluvw.f90:433](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caluvw.f90:433) `          EV  = 1./(CMV-RCDZL*CU2(L,K-1))`<br>[caluvw.f90:434](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caluvw.f90:434) `          CU2(L,K) = RCDZU*EV`<br>[caluvw.f90:435](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caluvw.f90:435) `          DV(L,K)  = (DV(L,K)-RCDZL*DV(L,K-1))*EV` | 일치 | DV는 y 방향 층간 차이다. −FCAY와 FBBY를 사용한다. 수직 응력은 y 삼대각계에서 적용한다. |
| <a id="eq-2-110"></a>[T12・PDF 49쪽・인쇄 36쪽・식 2.110](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=49)<br>`\displaystyle \Delta_{k+1,k}=\frac{1}{2}(\Delta_{k+1}+\Delta_k)`<br>[판독 기록:25](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p037-054.md:25) | [aaefdc.f90:1500](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/aaefdc.f90:1500) `      DZG(L,K)  = 0.5*(DZC(L,K)+DZC(L,K+1))`<br>[aaefdc.f90:2460](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/aaefdc.f90:2460) `      DZGU(L,K) = 0.5*(SGZU(L,K)+SGZU(L,K+1))`<br>[aaefdc.f90:2468](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/aaefdc.f90:2468) `      DZGV(L,K) = 0.5*(SGZV(L,K)+SGZV(L,K+1))` | 일치 | 셀 중심 DZG와 면 DZGU·DZGV가 인접층 두께의 1/2 합이다. |
| <a id="eq-2-111"></a>[T12・PDF 49쪽・인쇄 36쪽・식 2.111](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=49)<br>`\displaystyle w_k=w_{k-1}-\frac{\Delta_k}{m}\left[\frac{\partial}{\partial x}(P_k-\hat P)+\frac{\partial}{\partial y}(Q_k-\hat Q)\right]`<br>[판독 기록:25](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p037-054.md:25) | [caluvw.f90:733](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caluvw.f90:733) `          W(L,K) = W(L,K-1) - 0.5*DXYIP(L)  &`<br>[caluvw.f90:734](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caluvw.f90:734) `                   *( UHDY(LE,K) - UHDY(L,K) + UHDYEK(L,K) + UHDY1(LE,K)-UHDY1(L,K) + UHDY1EK(L,K)   &`<br>[caluvw.f90:735](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caluvw.f90:735) `                    + VHDX(LN,K) - VHDX(L,K) + VHDXEK(L,K) + VHDX1(LN,K)-VHDX1(L,K) + VHDX1EK(L,K) ) &`<br>[caluvw.f90:736](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caluvw.f90:736) `                    + ( QSUM(L,K) - DZC(L,K)*QSUME(L) )*DXYIP(L)` | 일치 | 원천이 없을 때 층 유량과 깊이 적분 유량의 차에서 W를 재구성한다. 원천이 있으면 코드가 QSUM−DZC·QSUME를 추가한다. 문서는 이 원천항을 생략한다. |
| <a id="eq-2-112"></a>[T12・PDF 50쪽・인쇄 37쪽・식 2.112](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=50)<br>`\displaystyle \begin{aligned}\frac{\partial\hat P}{\partial t}&=-\frac{m_y}{m_x}Hg\frac{\partial\zeta}{\partial x}-\frac{m_y}{m_x}H\frac{\partial p_s}{\partial x}+\frac{m_y}{m_x}Hg\left(\hat b\frac{\partial h}{\partial x}-\hat B\frac{\partial H}{\partial x}-H\frac{\partial\hat\beta}{\partial x}\right)\\&\quad-\frac{1}{m_x}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\Delta_k\left(\frac{\partial}{\partial x}(P_ku_k)+\frac{\partial}{\partial y}(Q_ku_k)\right)+\frac{1}{m_x}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\Delta_k\left[\left(v_k\frac{\partial m_y}{\partial x}-u_k\frac{\partial m_x}{\partial y}\right)Hv_k+m_yfQ_k\right]\\&\quad+\frac{1}{m_x}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\left[\frac{\partial}{\partial x}\left(\frac{m_y}{m_x}HA_{Hk}\Delta_k\frac{\partial u_k}{\partial x}\right)+\frac{\partial}{\partial y}\left(\frac{m_x}{m_y}HA_{Hk}\Delta_k\frac{\partial u_k}{\partial y}\right)\right]\\&\quad+m_y(\tau_{xz})_K-m_y(\tau_{xz})_0+\frac{1}{m_x}\hat S_u\end{aligned}`<br>[판독 기록:26](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p037-054.md:26) | [calhdmf3.f90:267](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calhdmf3.f90:267) `        FMDUX0(L,K) = 2.0* DYP(L)*H1P(L)*AH(L,K)*DXU1(L,K)`<br>[calhdmf3.f90:268](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calhdmf3.f90:268) `        FMDUY0(L,K) = 0.5*( DXU(L) + DXU(LS) )*H1C(L)*AHC(L,K)*( DYU1(L,K) + DXV1(L,K) )`<br>[calhdmf3.f90:269](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calhdmf3.f90:269) `        FMDVY0(L,K) = 2.0* DXP(L)*H1P(L)*AH(L,K)*DYV1(L,K)`<br>[calhdmf3.f90:270](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calhdmf3.f90:270) `        FMDVX0(L,K) = 0.5*( DYV(L) + DYV(LW) )*H1C(L)*AHC(L,K)*( DYU1(L,K) + DXV1(L,K) )`<br>[calexp.f90:953](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calexp.f90:953) `            FX(L,K) = FX(L,K) - SUB3D(L,K)*SDX(L)*( FMDUX(L,K) - FMDUX(LW,K) + FMDUY(LN,K) - FMDUY(L,K) )`<br>[calpuv9c.f90:256](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calpuv9c.f90:256) `      FUHDYE(L) = UHDY1E(L) - DELTD2*SUB(L)*HRUO(L)*HUTMP(L)*(P1(L)-P1(LW)) + SUB(L)*DELT*DXIU(L)*(DXYU(L)*(TSX1(L)-RITB1*TBX1(L)) + FCAXE(L) + FPGXE(L) - SNLT*FXE(L))` | 불일치 | 자유 수면·부력·이류·코리올리의 갱신 구조는 같다. 문서의 수평 확산은 u의 두 방향 기울기만 사용한다. 3시각 코드는 xx 응력 계수 2와 xy 응력의 ∂v/∂x를 사용한다. 코드의 수평 응력 전체는 식 (2.112)의 스칼라 확산과 다르다. |
| <a id="eq-2-113"></a>[T12・PDF 50쪽・인쇄 37쪽・식 2.113](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=50)<br>`\displaystyle \begin{aligned}\frac{\partial\hat Q}{\partial t}&=-\frac{m_x}{m_y}Hg\frac{\partial\zeta}{\partial y}-\frac{m_x}{m_y}H\frac{\partial p_s}{\partial y}+\frac{m_x}{m_y}Hg\left(\hat b\frac{\partial h}{\partial y}-\hat B\frac{\partial H}{\partial y}-H\frac{\partial\hat\beta}{\partial y}\right)\\&\quad-\frac{1}{m_y}\sum_{k=1}^{K}k\left(\frac{\partial}{\partial x}(P_kv_k)+\frac{\partial}{\partial y}(Q_kv_k)\right)+\frac{1}{m_y}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\Delta_k\left[\left(v_k\frac{\partial m_y}{\partial x}-u_k\frac{\partial m_x}{\partial y}\right)Hu_k+m_xfP_k\right]\\&\quad+\frac{1}{m_y}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\left[\frac{\partial}{\partial x}\left(\frac{m_y}{m_x}HA_{Hk}\Delta_k\frac{\partial v_k}{\partial x}\right)+\frac{\partial}{\partial y}\left(\frac{m_x}{m_y}HA_{Hk}\Delta_k\frac{\partial v_k}{\partial y}\right)\right]\\&\quad+m_x(\tau_{yz})_K-m_x(\tau_{yz})_0+\frac{1}{m_y}\hat S_v\end{aligned}`<br>[판독 기록:26](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p037-054.md:26) | [calpuv9c.f90:257](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calpuv9c.f90:257) `      FVHDXE(L) = VHDX1E(L) - DELTD2*SVB(L)*HRVO(L)*HVTMP(L)*(P1(L)-P1(LS)) + SVB(L)*DELT*DYIV(L)*(DXYV(L)*(TSY1(L)-RITB1*TBY1(L)) - FCAYE(L) + FPGYE(L) - SNLT*FYE(L))`<br>[calexp.f90:1134](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calexp.f90:1134) `        FCAYE(L) = FCAYE(L) + FCAY(L,K)*SGZV(L,K)`<br>[calhdmf3.f90:269](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calhdmf3.f90:269) `        FMDVY0(L,K) = 2.0* DXP(L)*H1P(L)*AH(L,K)*DYV1(L,K)`<br>[calhdmf3.f90:270](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calhdmf3.f90:270) `        FMDVX0(L,K) = 0.5*( DYV(L) + DYV(LW) )*H1C(L)*AHC(L,K)*( DYU1(L,K) + DXV1(L,K) )` | 불일치 | 문서의 우변 코리올리·곡률 부호는 양수이다. 코드는 −FCAYE이다. PDF 이미지에서 이류 가중치가 Δ_k 대신 k로 인쇄된 것을 확인했다. 수평 응력은 문서의 v 단독 확산과 다르다. |
| <a id="eq-2-114"></a>[T12・PDF 50쪽・인쇄 37쪽・식 2.114](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=50)<br>`\displaystyle \frac{\partial\zeta}{\partial t}+\frac{1}{m}\left(\frac{\partial\hat P}{\partial x}+\frac{\partial\hat Q}{\partial y}\right)=S_h`<br>[판독 기록:26](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p037-054.md:26) | [calpuv9c.f90:784](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calpuv9c.f90:784) `        HP(L) = H1P(L) + DELT*DXYIP(L)*( QSUME(L) - 0.5*( UHDYE(LE) + UHDY1E(LE) - UHDYE(L) - UHDY1E(L) &`<br>[calpuv9c.f90:785](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calpuv9c.f90:785) `          + VHDXE(LN) + VHDX1E(LN) - VHDXE(L) - VHDX1E(L)) )` | 일치 | 셀 면 유량 발산을 셀 면적의 역수 DXYIP로 나눈다. 문서가 Sh를 면적당 원천으로 재정의한 경우에 일치한다. 식 (2.103)의 체적 원천을 그대로 쓴다면 Sh에도 1/m이 필요하다. |
| <a id="eq-2-115"></a>[T12・PDF 50쪽・인쇄 37쪽・식 2.115](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=50)<br>`\displaystyle \hat\beta=\sum_{k=1}^{K}\beta_k\Delta_k`<br>[판독 기록:26](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p037-054.md:26) | [calebi.f90:122](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calebi.f90:122) `        DZCBK = DZCC(K,L)*BK(K,ND)`<br>[calebi.f90:123](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calebi.f90:123) `        BI1C  = BI1C + DZCBK  ` | 일치 | BI1C에 BK·Δz를 합산한다. |
| <a id="eq-2-116"></a>[T12・PDF 50쪽・인쇄 37쪽・식 2.116](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=50)<br>`\displaystyle \hat\beta=\sum_{k=1}^{K}\left[\beta_k\Delta_k+\frac{1}{2}(z_k+z_{k-1})b_k\Delta_k\right]`<br>[판독 기록:26](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p037-054.md:26) | [calebi.f90:123](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calebi.f90:123) `        BI1C  = BI1C + DZCBK  `<br>[calebi.f90:124](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calebi.f90:124) `        BI2C  = BI2C + DZCBK + ZZC(K,L)*DZCB(K,ND) ` | 불일치 | 문서는 식 (2.115)와 다른 합을 같은 β̂로 정의한다. 코드는 BI1C와 BI2C를 분리한다. 식 (2.112)의 B̂에 해당하는 합이 BI2C이다. |
| <a id="eq-2-117"></a>[T12・PDF 51쪽・인쇄 38쪽・식 2.117](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=51)<br>`\displaystyle \begin{aligned}\hat P^{n+1}&=\hat P^{n-1}-\Delta t\left(\frac{m_y}{m_x}H\right)^u g d_x^u(\zeta^{n+1}+\zeta^{n-1})-2\Delta t\left(\frac{m_y}{m_x}H\right)^u d_x^u p_s\\&\quad+2\Delta t\left(\frac{m_y}{m_x}H\right)^u g\left(\hat b^u d_x^u h-\hat B^u d_x^u H-H^u d_x^u\hat\beta\right)\\&\quad-2\Delta t\left(\frac{1}{m_x}\right)^u\sum_{k=1}^{K}\Delta_k\left[d_x^u(P_ku_k)+d_y^u(Q_ku_k)\right]\\&\quad+2\Delta t\left(\frac{1}{m_x}\right)^u\sum_{k=1}^{K}\Delta_k\left[\left(v_k\frac{\partial m_y}{\partial x}-u_k\frac{\partial m_x}{\partial y}\right)Hv_k+m_yfQ_k\right]^u\\&\quad+2\Delta t m_y^u\left[(t_{xz}^{n-1})_K-(t_{xz}^{n-1})_0\right]^u\\&\quad+2\Delta t\left(\frac{1}{m_x}\right)^u\sum_{k=1}^{K}\Delta_k\left[\frac{\partial}{\partial x}(m_yHt_{xx}^{n-1})+\frac{\partial}{\partial y}(m_xHt_{xy}^{n-1})\right.\\&\qquad\left.+\frac{\partial}{\partial y}(m_xHt_{xy}^{n-1})-\frac{\partial}{\partial x}(m_yHt_{yy}^{n-1})\right]_k^u\end{aligned}`<br>[판독 기록:27](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p037-054.md:27) | [calpuv9c.f90:130](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calpuv9c.f90:130) `  DELT = DT2`<br>[calpuv9c.f90:131](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calpuv9c.f90:131) `  DELTD2 = DT`<br>[calpuv9c.f90:132](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calpuv9c.f90:132) `  if( ISTL == 2 )then`<br>[calpuv9c.f90:133](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calpuv9c.f90:133) `    DELT = DT`<br>[calpuv9c.f90:134](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calpuv9c.f90:134) `    DELTD2 = 0.5*DT`<br>[calpuv9c.f90:135](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calpuv9c.f90:135) `  endif`<br>[calpuv9c.f90:256](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calpuv9c.f90:256) `      FUHDYE(L) = UHDY1E(L) - DELTD2*SUB(L)*HRUO(L)*HUTMP(L)*(P1(L)-P1(LW)) + SUB(L)*DELT*DXIU(L)*(DXYU(L)*(TSX1(L)-RITB1*TBX1(L)) + FCAXE(L) + FPGXE(L) - SNLT*FXE(L))`<br>[calhdmf3.f90:267](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calhdmf3.f90:267) `        FMDUX0(L,K) = 2.0* DYP(L)*H1P(L)*AH(L,K)*DXU1(L,K)`<br>[calhdmf3.f90:268](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calhdmf3.f90:268) `        FMDUY0(L,K) = 0.5*( DXU(L) + DXU(LS) )*H1C(L)*AHC(L,K)*( DYU1(L,K) + DXV1(L,K) )`<br>[calexp.f90:953](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calexp.f90:953) `            FX(L,K) = FX(L,K) - SUB3D(L,K)*SDX(L)*( FMDUX(L,K) - FMDUX(LW,K) + FMDUY(LN,K) - FMDUY(L,K) )` | 불일치 | DELT=2DT와 DELTD2=DT는 문서의 2Δt와 Δt에 대응한다. 문서는 xy 응력의 y 미분을 중복한다. 문서는 −∂x(Hτyy)를 추가한다. 코드는 xx 발산과 xy 발산을 각각 한 번 사용한다. |
| <a id="eq-2-118"></a>[T12・PDF 52쪽・인쇄 39쪽・식 2.118](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=52)<br>`\displaystyle \begin{aligned}\hat Q^{n+1}&=\hat Q^{n-1}-\Delta t\left(\frac{m_x}{m_y}H\right)^v g\delta_y^u(\zeta^{n+1}+\zeta^{n-1})-2\Delta t\left(\frac{m_x}{m_y}H\right)^v\delta_y^u p_s\\&\quad+2\Delta t\left(\frac{m_x}{m_y}H\right)^v g\left(\hat b^v d_y^v h-\hat B^v\delta_y^v H-H^v\delta_y^v\hat\beta\right)\\&\quad-2\Delta t\left(\frac{1}{m_y}\right)^u\sum_{k=1}^{K}\Delta_k\left[d_x^v(P_kv_k)+d_y^v(Q_kv_k)\right]\\&\quad-2\Delta t\left(\frac{1}{m_y}\right)^v\sum_{k=1}^{K}\Delta_k\left[\left(v_k\frac{\partial m_y}{\partial x}-u_k\frac{\partial m_x}{\partial y}\right)Hu_k+m_xfP_k\right]^v\\&\quad+2\Delta t m_x^v\left[(\tau_{yz}^{n-1})_K-(\tau_{yz}^{n-1})_0\right]^v\\&\quad+2\Delta t\left(\frac{1}{m_y}\right)^v\sum_{k=1}^{K}\Delta_k\left[\frac{\partial}{\partial x}(m_yHt_{yx}^{n-1})+\frac{\partial}{\partial y}(m_xHt_{yy}^{n-1})\right.\\&\qquad\left.\frac{\partial}{\partial y}(m_xHt_{xx}^{n-1})-\frac{\partial}{\partial x}(m_yHt_{yx}^{n-1})\right]_k^v\end{aligned}`<br>[판독 기록:28](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p037-054.md:28) | [calpuv9c.f90:257](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calpuv9c.f90:257) `      FVHDXE(L) = VHDX1E(L) - DELTD2*SVB(L)*HRVO(L)*HVTMP(L)*(P1(L)-P1(LS)) + SVB(L)*DELT*DYIV(L)*(DXYV(L)*(TSY1(L)-RITB1*TBY1(L)) - FCAYE(L) + FPGYE(L) - SNLT*FYE(L))`<br>[calexp.f90:963](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calexp.f90:963) `            FY(L,K) = FY(L,K) - SVB3D(L,K)*SDY(L)*( FMDVY(L,K) - FMDVY(LS,K) + FMDVX(LE,K) - FMDVX(L,K) )` | 불일치 | 문서의 수면·압력 미분은 y 방향인데 u 위치 첨자를 쓴다. 이류 앞 1/my도 u 위치를 쓴다. 문서의 수평 응력 마지막 줄에는 연결 연산자가 없다. 코드는 y 면에서 yy와 yx의 발산을 한 번씩 계산한다. |
| <a id="eq-2-119"></a>[T12・PDF 52쪽・인쇄 39쪽・식 2.119](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=52)<br>`\displaystyle \zeta^{n+1}-\zeta^{n-1}+\Delta t\left(\frac{1}{m}\right)^\zeta\left[\delta_x^\zeta(\hat P^{n+1}+\hat P^{n-1})+\delta_y^\zeta(\hat Q^{n+1}+\hat Q^{n-1})\right]=S_h\Delta t`<br>[판독 기록:28](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p037-054.md:28) | [calpuv9c.f90:130](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calpuv9c.f90:130) `  DELT = DT2`<br>[calpuv9c.f90:131](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calpuv9c.f90:131) `  DELTD2 = DT`<br>[calpuv9c.f90:132](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calpuv9c.f90:132) `  if( ISTL == 2 )then`<br>[calpuv9c.f90:133](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calpuv9c.f90:133) `    DELT = DT`<br>[calpuv9c.f90:134](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calpuv9c.f90:134) `    DELTD2 = 0.5*DT`<br>[calpuv9c.f90:135](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calpuv9c.f90:135) `  endif`<br>[calpuv9c.f90:784](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calpuv9c.f90:784) `        HP(L) = H1P(L) + DELT*DXYIP(L)*( QSUME(L) - 0.5*( UHDYE(LE) + UHDY1E(LE) - UHDYE(L) - UHDY1E(L) &`<br>[calpuv9c.f90:785](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calpuv9c.f90:785) `          + VHDXE(LN) + VHDX1E(LN) - VHDXE(L) - VHDX1E(L)) )` | 불일치 | 3시각 코드의 원천 증분은 DELT·QSUME/면적=2DT·QSUME/면적이다. 문서는 n−1부터 n+1의 연속식 원천을 Sh·Δt로만 쓴다. 원천이 0인 경우에는 이 차이가 사라진다. |
| <a id="eq-2-120"></a>[T12・PDF 52쪽・인쇄 39쪽・식 2.120](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=52)<br>`\displaystyle \delta_x(\phi_{i,j,k})=\frac{1}{\Delta x}\left(\phi_{i+\frac{1}{2},j,k}-\phi_{i-\frac{1}{2},j,k}\right)`<br>[판독 기록:28](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p037-054.md:28) | [calexp.f90:685](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calexp.f90:685) `        FX(L,K) = FSGZU(L,K)*( FUHU(L,K)-FUHU(LW,K) + FVHU(LN,K)-FVHU(L,K) ) + FUHJ(L,K)   ! ***  M4/S2`<br>[caluvw.f90:733](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caluvw.f90:733) `          W(L,K) = W(L,K-1) - 0.5*DXYIP(L)  &` | 일치 | 동쪽 면과 서쪽 면 플럭스의 차를 취한다. 코드의 DXYIP와 면 폭이 1/Δx에 해당하는 격자 길이 계수를 구성한다. |
| <a id="eq-2-121"></a>[T12・PDF 52쪽・인쇄 39쪽・식 2.121](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=52)<br>`\displaystyle \delta_y(\phi_{i,j,k})=\frac{1}{\Delta y}\left(\phi_{i,j+\frac{1}{2},k}-\phi_{i,j-\frac{1}{2},k}\right)`<br>[판독 기록:28](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p037-054.md:28) | [calexp.f90:686](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calexp.f90:686) `        FY(L,K) = FSGZV(L,K)*( FVHV(L,K)-FVHV(LS,K) + FUHV(LE,K)-FUHV(L,K) ) + FVHJ(L,K)   ! ***  M4/S2`<br>[caluvw.f90:734](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caluvw.f90:734) `                   *( UHDY(LE,K) - UHDY(L,K) + UHDYEK(L,K) + UHDY1(LE,K)-UHDY1(L,K) + UHDY1EK(L,K)   &` | 일치 | 북쪽 면과 남쪽 면 플럭스의 차를 취한다. |
| <a id="eq-2-122"></a>[T12・PDF 52쪽・인쇄 39쪽・식 2.122](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=52)<br>`\displaystyle \delta_x^u(P_{i,j,k}u_{i,j,k})=\frac{1}{\Delta x}\left(P_{i+\frac{1}{2},j,k}u_{i+\frac{1}{2},j,k}-P_{i-\frac{1}{2},j,k}u_{i-\frac{1}{2},j,k}\right)`<br>[판독 기록:28](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p037-054.md:28) | [calexp.f90:685](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calexp.f90:685) `        FX(L,K) = FSGZU(L,K)*( FUHU(L,K)-FUHU(LW,K) + FVHU(LN,K)-FVHU(L,K) ) + FUHJ(L,K)   ! ***  M4/S2` | 일치 | FX는 FUHU(L)−FUHU(LW)의 차를 사용한다. 셀 적분 플럭스의 길이·면적 계수는 유속 갱신 단계에서 적용한다. |
| <a id="eq-2-123"></a>[T12・PDF 52쪽・인쇄 39쪽・식 2.123](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=52)<br>`\displaystyle \delta_x^u(P_{i,j,k}u_{i,j,k})=\frac{1}{\Delta x}\left(\frac{P_{i+1,j,k}+P_{i,j,k}}{2}\frac{u_{i+1,j,k}+u_{i,j,k}}{2}-\frac{P_{i,j,k}+P_{i-1,j,k}}{2}\frac{u_{i,j,k}+u_{i-1,j,k}}{2}\right)`<br>[판독 기록:28](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p037-054.md:28) | [calexp.f90:333](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calexp.f90:333) `          FUHU(L,K) = 0.25*(UHDY(L,K) + UHDY(LE,K))*(U(L,K)+U(LE,K))`<br>[calexp.f90:685](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calexp.f90:685) `        FX(L,K) = FSGZU(L,K)*( FUHU(L,K)-FUHU(LW,K) + FVHU(LN,K)-FVHU(L,K) ) + FUHJ(L,K)   ! ***  M4/S2` | 일치 | 수송량과 유속을 각각 두 점 평균한 곱이 0.25 계수이다. 동서 플럭스의 차를 취한다. |
| <a id="eq-2-124"></a>[T12・PDF 53쪽・인쇄 40쪽・식 2.124](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=53)<br>`\displaystyle \begin{aligned}\delta_x^u(P_{i,j,k}u_{i,j,k})&=\frac{1}{\Delta x}\left[\max\left(\frac{P_{i+1,j,k}+P_{i,j,k}}{2},0\right)u_{i,j,k}^{n-1}-\max\left(\frac{P_{i,j,k}+P_{i-1,j,k}}{2},0\right)u_{i-1,j,k}^{n-1}\right]\\&\quad+\frac{1}{\Delta x}\left[\min\left(\frac{P_{i+1,j,k}+P_{i,j,k}}{2},0\right)u_{i+1,j,k}^{n-1}-\min\left(\frac{P_{i,j,k}+P_{i-1,j,k}}{2},0\right)u_{i,j,k}^{n-1}\right]\end{aligned}`<br>[판독 기록:29](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p037-054.md:29) | [calexp.f90:272](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calexp.f90:272) `          UHB = 0.5*(UHDY(L,K) + UHDY(LE,K))`<br>[calexp.f90:276](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calexp.f90:276) `          FUHU(L,K) = (MAX(UHB,0.)*U1(L,K)  + min(UHB,0.)*U1(LE,K))  `<br>[calexp.f90:685](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calexp.f90:685) `        FX(L,K) = FSGZU(L,K)*( FUHU(L,K)-FUHU(LW,K) + FVHU(LN,K)-FVHU(L,K) ) + FUHJ(L,K)   ! ***  M4/S2` | 일치 | 수송량을 0.5 합으로 평균한다. 양의 수송량은 U1(L), 음의 수송량은 U1(LE)의 유속을 사용한다. U1은 후방 시각 유속이다. |
| <a id="eq-2-125"></a>[T12・PDF 53쪽・인쇄 40쪽・식 2.125](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=53)<br>`\displaystyle \left[m_yfQ_k+\left(v_k\frac{\partial m_y}{\partial x}-u_k\frac{\partial m_x}{\partial y}\right)Hv_k\right]^u=\frac{1}{2}\left[(RH)_{i+\frac{1}{2},j}^\zeta v_{i+\frac{1}{2},j,k}^\zeta+(RH)_{i-\frac{1}{2},j}^\zeta v_{i-\frac{1}{2},j,k}^\zeta\right]`<br>[판독 기록:29](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p037-054.md:29) | [calexp.f90:620](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calexp.f90:620) `          FCAX(L,K) = ROLD*FCAX(L,K) + 0.25*RNEW*SCAX(L)*(CAC(L,K)*(V(LN,K) + V(L,K)) + CAC(LW,K)*(V(LNW,K) + V(LW,K)))` | 일치 | CAC 두 셀과 V 두 면을 각각 평균한 0.25 곱을 사용한다. |
| <a id="eq-2-126"></a>[T12・PDF 53쪽・인쇄 40쪽・식 2.126](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=53)<br>`\displaystyle R_{i+\frac{1}{2},j}^\zeta=(mf)_{i+\frac{1}{2},j}+\frac{(m_y)_{i+1,j}-(m_y)_{i,j}}{\Delta x}v_{i+\frac{1}{2},j,k}^\zeta-\frac{(m_x)_{i+\frac{1}{2},j+\frac{1}{2}}-(m_x)_{i+\frac{1}{2},j-\frac{1}{2}}}{\Delta y}u_{i+\frac{1}{2},j,k}^\zeta`<br>[판독 기록:29](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p037-054.md:29) | [calexp.f90:571](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calexp.f90:571) `          CAC(L,K) = ( FCORC(L)*DXYP(L) + 0.5*SNLT*(V(LN,K) + V(L,K))*DYDI(L) - 0.5*SNLT*(U(LE,K) + U(L,K))*DXDJ(L) )*HP(L)` | 일치 | FCORC·DXYP와 V·DYDI/2−U·DXDJ/2를 합산한 뒤 HP를 곱한다. SNLT는 곡률을 포함한 비선형 항 선택값이다. |
| <a id="eq-2-127"></a>[T12・PDF 53쪽・인쇄 40쪽・식 2.127](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=53)<br>`\displaystyle v_{i+\frac{1}{2},j,k}^\zeta=\frac{1}{2}\left(v_{i+\frac{1}{2},j+\frac{1}{2},k}+v_{i+\frac{1}{2},j-\frac{1}{2},k}\right)`<br>[판독 기록:29](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p037-054.md:29) | [calexp.f90:571](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calexp.f90:571) `          CAC(L,K) = ( FCORC(L)*DXYP(L) + 0.5*SNLT*(V(LN,K) + V(L,K))*DYDI(L) - 0.5*SNLT*(U(LE,K) + U(L,K))*DXDJ(L) )*HP(L)`<br>[calexp.f90:620](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calexp.f90:620) `          FCAX(L,K) = ROLD*FCAX(L,K) + 0.25*RNEW*SCAX(L)*(CAC(L,K)*(V(LN,K) + V(L,K)) + CAC(LW,K)*(V(LNW,K) + V(LW,K)))` | 일치 | 남북 V의 합에 0.5 또는 두 평균의 곱 0.25를 적용한다. |
| <a id="eq-2-128"></a>[T12・PDF 53쪽・인쇄 40쪽・식 2.128](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=53)<br>`\displaystyle u_{i+\frac{1}{2},j,k}^\zeta=\frac{1}{2}\left(u_{i+1,j,k}+u_{i,j,k}\right)`<br>[판독 기록:29](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p037-054.md:29) | [calexp.f90:571](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calexp.f90:571) `          CAC(L,K) = ( FCORC(L)*DXYP(L) + 0.5*SNLT*(V(LN,K) + V(L,K))*DYDI(L) - 0.5*SNLT*(U(LE,K) + U(L,K))*DXDJ(L) )*HP(L)`<br>[calexp.f90:621](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calexp.f90:621) `          FCAY(L,K) = ROLD*FCAY(L,K) + 0.25*RNEW*SCAY(L)*(CAC(L,K)*(U(LE,K) + U(L,K)) + CAC(LS,K)*(U(LSE,K) + U(LS,K)))` | 일치 | 동서 U의 합에 같은 산술 평균을 적용한다. |
| <a id="eq-2-129"></a>[T12・PDF 53쪽・인쇄 40쪽・식 2.129](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=53)<br>`\displaystyle (\tau_{xx})_k=2A_H\frac{1}{m_x}\frac{\partial u_k}{\partial x}`<br>[판독 기록:29](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p037-054.md:29) | [calhdmf3.f90:267](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calhdmf3.f90:267) `        FMDUX0(L,K) = 2.0* DYP(L)*H1P(L)*AH(L,K)*DXU1(L,K)`<br>[calhdmf.f90:116](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calhdmf.f90:116) `        DXU1(L,K) = ( U(LE,K) - U(L,K) )/DXP(L)`<br>[calhdmf.f90:310](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calhdmf.f90:310) `          FMDUX0(L,K) = ( DYP(L) *HP(L) *AH(L,K) *DXU1(L,K) - DYP(LW)*HP(LW)*AH(LW,K)*DXU1(LW,K) )*SUB(L)`<br>[hdmt.f90:234](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/hdmt.f90:234) `  if( ISHDMF >= 1 ) CALL CALHDMF3`<br>[hdmt2t.f90:827](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/hdmt2t.f90:827) `    call CALHDMF` | 일치 | 이 식이 속한 3시각 분기 CALHDMF3는 2AH·∂u/∂x를 계산한다. 2시각 CALHDMF는 계수 1을 사용한다. 이 행의 일치 판정은 문서가 지정한 3시각 경로에 한정한다. |
| <a id="eq-2-130"></a>[T12・PDF 53쪽・인쇄 40쪽・식 2.130](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=53)<br>`\displaystyle (\tau_{xy})_k=(t_{yx})_k=2A_H\left(\frac{1}{m_x}\frac{\partial v_k}{\partial x}+\frac{1}{m_y}\frac{\partial u_k}{\partial y}\right)`<br>[판독 기록:29](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p037-054.md:29) | [calhdmf3.f90:268](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calhdmf3.f90:268) `        FMDUY0(L,K) = 0.5*( DXU(L) + DXU(LS) )*H1C(L)*AHC(L,K)*( DYU1(L,K) + DXV1(L,K) )`<br>[calhdmf.f90:216](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calhdmf.f90:216) `        SXY(L,K) = DYU1(L,K) + DXV1(L,K)`<br>[calhdmf.f90:311](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calhdmf.f90:311) `          FMDUY0(L,K) = ( DXU(LN)*HU(LN)*AH(LN,K)*SXY(LN,K) - DXU(L) *HU(L) *AH(L,K) *SXY(L,K)   )*SUB(L)*SVB(L)*SVB(LN)` | 불일치 | 문서는 AH·(∂v/∂x+∂u/∂y)에 2를 곱한다. 두 코드 경로는 방향 길이 평균 외에 응력 계수 2를 곱하지 않는다. |
| <a id="eq-2-131"></a>[T12・PDF 54쪽・인쇄 41쪽・식 2.131](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=54)<br>`\displaystyle (t_{yy})_k=2A_H\frac{1}{m_y}\frac{\partial v_k}{\partial y}`<br>[판독 기록:30](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p037-054.md:30) | [calhdmf3.f90:269](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calhdmf3.f90:269) `        FMDVY0(L,K) = 2.0* DXP(L)*H1P(L)*AH(L,K)*DYV1(L,K)`<br>[calhdmf.f90:118](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calhdmf.f90:118) `        DYV1(L,K) = ( V(LN,K) - V(L,K) )/DYP(L)`<br>[calhdmf.f90:313](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calhdmf.f90:313) `          FMDVY0(L,K) = ( DXP(L) *HP(L) *AH(L,K) *DYV1(L,K) - DXP(LS)*HP(LS)*AH(LS,K)*DYV1(LS,K) )*SVB(L)` | 일치 | 문서가 지정한 3시각 분기는 2AH·∂v/∂y를 계산한다. 2시각 분기의 정상 응력 계수는 1이다. 2시각 경로를 이 식과 동일하게 설명할 수 없다. |
| <a id="eq-2-132"></a>[T12・PDF 54쪽・인쇄 41쪽・식 2.132](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=54)<br>`\displaystyle \zeta^{n+1}-g\Delta t^2\left(\frac{1}{m}\right)^\zeta\left[\delta_x^\zeta\left(H\frac{m_y}{m_x}\right)^u\delta_x^u\zeta^{n+1}+\delta_x^\zeta\left(H\frac{m_x}{m_y}\right)^v\delta_y^v\zeta^{n+1}\right]-\phi=0`<br>[판독 기록:30](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p037-054.md:30) | [calpuv9c.f90:623](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calpuv9c.f90:623) `      CS(L) = C1*SVB(L) *HRVO(L) *RCY(L) *HVTMP(L)`<br>[calpuv9c.f90:624](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calpuv9c.f90:624) `      CW(L) = C1*SUB(L) *HRUO(L) *RCX(L) *HUTMP(L)`<br>[calpuv9c.f90:625](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calpuv9c.f90:625) `      CE(L) = C1*SUB(LE)*HRUO(LE)*RCX(LE)*HUTMP(LE)`<br>[calpuv9c.f90:626](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calpuv9c.f90:626) `      CN(L) = C1*SVB(LN)*HRVO(LN)*RCY(LN)*HVTMP(LN)` | 불일치 | 문서는 y 기울기 항의 바깥 발산도 δx로 쓴다. 코드는 CS·CN으로 남북 y 방향 연결을 만든다. PDF 이미지에서 두 번째 바깥 δx를 확인했다. |
| <a id="eq-2-133"></a>[T12・PDF 56쪽・인쇄 43쪽・식 2.133](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=56)<br>`\displaystyle \begin{aligned}(P_{k+1}-P_k)^{**}&=(P_{k+1}-P_k)^{n-1}\\&-2\Delta t\left(\frac{1}{m_x}\right)^u\left[\delta_x^u(P_{k+1}u_{k+1}-P_ku_k)+\delta_y^u(Q_{k+1}u_{k+1}-Q_ku_k)\right]\\&-2\Delta t\left(\frac{1}{m_x}\right)^u\left[\frac{(Wu)_{k+1}-(Wu)_k}{\Delta_{k+1}}-\frac{(Wu)_k-(Wu)_{k-1}}{\Delta_k}\right]^u\\&+2\Delta t\left(\frac{1}{m_x}\right)^u[m_yfQ_{k+1}-m_yfQ_k]^u\\&+2\Delta t\left(\frac{1}{m_x}\right)^u\left[\left(v_{k+1}\frac{\partial m_y}{\partial x}-u_{k+1}\frac{\partial m_x}{\partial y}\right)Hv_{k+1}-\left(v_k\frac{\partial m_y}{\partial x}-u_k\frac{\partial m_x}{\partial y}\right)Hv_k\right]^u\\&+2\Delta t\left(\frac{m_y}{m_x}H\right)^ug\left[(b_{k+1}-b_k)^u\delta_x^u(h-z_kH)+\frac{1}{2}H^u\delta_x^u(b_{k+1}\Delta_{k+1}+b_k\Delta_k)\right]\\&+2\Delta t\left(\frac{1}{m_x}\right)^u[(S_u)_{k+1}-(S_u)_k]^u\end{aligned}`<br>[판독 기록:14](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p055-072.md:14) | [calexp.f90:1309](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calexp.f90:1309) `          DU(L,K) = CDZFU(L,K)*( H1U(L)*(U1(L,K+1)-U1(L,K))*DELTI + DXYIU(L)*(FCAX(L,K+1)-FCAX(L,K)   + FBBX(L,K) + SNLT*(FX(L,K)-FX(L,K+1))) )`<br>[calexp.f90:1215](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calexp.f90:1215) `            FBBX(L,K) = ROLD*FBBX(L,K) + RNEW*SBX(L)*GP*HU(L)*( HU(L)*( (B(L,K+1)-B(LW,K+1))*DZCK(K+1) + (B(L,K)-B(LW,K))*DZCK(K) ) - (B(L,K+1)-B(L,K)+B(LW,K+1)-B(LW,K))*(BELV(L)-BELV(LW)+Z(L,K)*(HP(L)-HP(LW))) )`<br>[calpuv9c.f90:130](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calpuv9c.f90:130) `  DELT = DT2` | 일치 | DU에는 후방 층간 유속 차와 2DT 동안의 이류·수직 이류·코리올리·부력 차를 넣는다. 수직 응력은 이후 단계에서 넣는다. |
| <a id="eq-2-134"></a>[T12・PDF 56쪽・인쇄 43쪽・식 2.134](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=56)<br>`\displaystyle \begin{aligned}(Q_{k+1}-Q_k)^{**}&=(Q_{k+1}-Q_k)^{n-1}\\&-2\Delta t\left(\frac{1}{m_y}\right)^v\left[\delta_y^v(P_{k+1}v_{k+1}-P_kv_k)+\delta_y^v(Q_{k+1}v_{k+1}-Q_kv_k)\right]\\&-2\Delta t\left(\frac{1}{m_y}\right)^v\left[\frac{(Wv)_{k+1}-(Wv)_k}{\Delta_{k+1}}-\frac{(Wv)_k-(Wv)_{k-1}}{\Delta_k}\right]^v\\&-2\Delta t\left(\frac{1}{m_y}\right)^v[m_xfP_{k+1}-m_xfP_k]^v\\&-2\Delta t\left(\frac{1}{m_y}\right)^v\left[\left(v_{k+1}\frac{\partial m_y}{\partial x}-u_{k+1}\frac{\partial m_x}{\partial y}\right)Hu_{k+1}-\left(v_k\frac{\partial m_y}{\partial x}-u_k\frac{\partial m_x}{\partial y}\right)Hu_k\right]^v\\&+2\Delta t\left(\frac{m_x}{m_y}H\right)^vg\left[(b_{k+1}-b_k)^v\delta_y^v(h-z_kH)+\frac{1}{2}H^v\delta_y^v(\Delta_{k+1}b_{k+1}+\Delta_kb_k)\right]\\&+2\Delta t\left(\frac{1}{m_y}\right)^v[(S_v)_{k+1}-(S_v)_k]^v\end{aligned}`<br>[판독 기록:14](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p055-072.md:14) | [calexp.f90:686](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calexp.f90:686) `        FY(L,K) = FSGZV(L,K)*( FVHV(L,K)-FVHV(LS,K) + FUHV(LE,K)-FUHV(L,K) ) + FVHJ(L,K)   ! ***  M4/S2`<br>[calexp.f90:1310](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calexp.f90:1310) `          DV(L,K) = CDZFV(L,K)*( H1V(L)*(V1(L,K+1)-V1(L,K))*DELTI + DXYIV(L)*(FCAY(L,K)  -FCAY(L,K+1) + FBBY(L,K) + SNLT*(FY(L,K)-FY(L,K+1))) )` | 불일치 | 문서는 Pv 항의 x 미분도 δy로 쓴다. 코드는 FUHV(LE)−FUHV(L)의 x 발산과 FVHV(L)−FVHV(LS)의 y 발산을 분리한다. |
| <a id="eq-2-135"></a>[T12・PDF 56쪽・인쇄 43쪽・식 2.135](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=56)<br>`\displaystyle W=m_xm_yw=mw,`<br>[판독 기록:14](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p055-072.md:14) | [calexp.f90:243](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calexp.f90:243) `            WUU = 0.25*DXYU(L)*(W2(L,K) + W2(LW,K))`<br>[calexp.f90:348](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calexp.f90:348) `            FWU(L,K) = 0.25*DXYU(L)*(W(L,K)+W(LW,K))*(U(L,K+1)+U(L,K))` | 일치 | 코드는 W 유속에 수평 셀 면적 DXYU를 곱해 수직 체적 수송을 구성한다. 별도의 대문자 W 배열은 만들지 않는다. |
| <a id="eq-2-136"></a>[T12・PDF 56쪽・인쇄 43쪽・식 2.136](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=56)<br>`\displaystyle (Wu)^u_{i,j,k}=\frac{W_{i-\frac{1}{2},j,k}+W_{i+\frac{1}{2},j,k}}{2}\frac{u_{i,j,k}+u_{i,j,k+1}}{2}`<br>[판독 기록:14](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p055-072.md:14) | [calexp.f90:348](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calexp.f90:348) `            FWU(L,K) = 0.25*DXYU(L)*(W(L,K)+W(LW,K))*(U(L,K+1)+U(L,K))` | 일치 | 두 셀 W와 두 층 U를 평균한 곱이 0.25·DXYU이다. |
| <a id="eq-2-137"></a>[T12・PDF 56쪽・인쇄 43쪽・식 2.137](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=56)<br>`\displaystyle (Wu)^u_{i,j,k}=\max\left(\frac{W_{i-\frac{1}{2},j,k}+W_{i+\frac{1}{2},j,k}}{2},0\right)u^{n-1}_{i,j,k}+\min\left(\frac{W_{i-\frac{1}{2},j,k}+W_{i+\frac{1}{2},j,k}}{2},0\right)u^{n-1}_{i,j,k+1}`<br>[판독 기록:14](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p055-072.md:14) | [calexp.f90:304](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calexp.f90:304) `            WUU = 0.5*DXYU(L)*(W(L,K)+W(LW,K))`<br>[calexp.f90:305](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calexp.f90:305) `            WVV = 0.5*DXYV(L)*(W(L,K)+W(LS,K))`<br>[calexp.f90:306](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calexp.f90:306) `            FWU(L,K) = max(WUU,0.)*U1(L,K) + min(WUU,0.)*U1(L,K+1)`<br>[calexp.f90:307](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calexp.f90:307) `            FWV(L,K) = max(WVV,0.)*V1(L,K) + min(WVV,0.)*V1(L,K+1)`<br>[calexp.f90:243](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calexp.f90:243) `            WUU = 0.25*DXYU(L)*(W2(L,K) + W2(LW,K))`<br>[calexp.f90:244](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calexp.f90:244) `            WVV = 0.25*DXYV(L)*(W2(L,K) + W2(LS,K))`<br>[calexp.f90:245](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calexp.f90:245) `            FWU(L,K) = max(WUU,0.)*U1(L,K) + min(WUU,0.)*U1(L,K+1)` | 일치 | 문서의 3시각 풍상차분은 ISCDMA=0·ISTL≠2 경로의 0.5·면적·(WL+WLW)와 일치한다. 다른 시간 단계와 이류 옵션은 W2 또는 계수 0.25를 사용한다. 일치 판정은 지정한 3시각 풍상차분에 한정한다. |
