| <a id="eq-2-140"></a>[T12・PDF 57쪽・인쇄 44쪽・식 2.140](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=57)<br>`\displaystyle (\tau_{xz})_k^{n+1}=\left(\frac{A_v^u}{H^u}\right)_k^n\left(\frac{1}{m_y^uH^u}\frac{P_{k+1}-P_k}{\Delta_{k+1,k}}\right)^{n+1}`<br>[판독 기록:15](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p055-072.md:15) | [caluvw.f90:495](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caluvw.f90:495) `        DU(L,K) = SUB3D(L,K)*DZGU(L,K)*HU(L)*AVUI(L,K)*( DU(L,K) - AAU(L)*UUU(L,K) )`<br>[aaefdc.f90:2460](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/aaefdc.f90:2460) `      DZGU(L,K) = 0.5*(SGZU(L,K)+SGZU(L,K+1))` | 일치 | 새 시각의 층간 수송 차와 기존 AVUI를 연결한다. 코드는 응력으로부터 수송 차를 구하는 역관계를 사용한다. |
| <a id="eq-2-141"></a>[T12・PDF 57쪽・인쇄 44쪽・식 2.141](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=57)<br>`\displaystyle (t_{yz})_k^{n+1}=\left(\frac{A_v^v}{H^v}\right)_k^n\left(\frac{1}{m_x^vH^v}\frac{Q_{k+1}-Q_k}{\Delta_{k+1,k}}\right)^{n+1}`<br>[판독 기록:15](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p055-072.md:15) | [caluvw.f90:496](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caluvw.f90:496) `        DV(L,K) = SVB3D(L,K)*DZGV(L,K)*HV(L)*AVVI(L,K)*( DV(L,K) - AAV(L)*VVV(L,K) )`<br>[aaefdc.f90:2468](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/aaefdc.f90:2468) `      DZGV(L,K) = 0.5*(SGZV(L,K)+SGZV(L,K+1))` | 일치 | AVVI를 사용한 y 응력의 역관계이다. |
| <a id="eq-2-142"></a>[T12・PDF 57쪽・인쇄 44쪽・식 2.142](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=57)<br>`\displaystyle \begin{aligned}-\frac{1}{\Delta_k\Delta_{k+1,k}}(\tau_{xz})_{k-1}^{n+1}&+\left[\frac{1}{\Delta_k\Delta_{k+1,k}}+\frac{(H^u)^{n+1}}{2\Delta t}\left(\frac{H^u}{A_v^u}\right)_k^n+\frac{1}{\Delta_{k+1}\Delta_{k+1,k}}\right](\tau_{xz})_k^{n+1}\\&-\frac{1}{\Delta_{k+1}\Delta_{k+1,k}}(\tau_{xz})_{k+1}^{n+1}=\frac{1}{2\Delta t\,m_y^u}\frac{(P_{k+1}-P_k)^{**}}{\Delta_{k+1,k}}\end{aligned}`<br>[판독 기록:15](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p055-072.md:15) | [aaefdc.f90:2462](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/aaefdc.f90:2462) `        CDZFU(L,K) = SGZU(L,K)*SGZU(L,K+1)/(SGZU(L,K)+SGZU(L,K+1))`<br>[aaefdc.f90:2463](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/aaefdc.f90:2463) `        CDZUU(L,K) = -SGZU(L,  K)/(SGZU(L,K)+SGZU(L,K+1))`<br>[aaefdc.f90:2464](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/aaefdc.f90:2464) `        CDZLU(L,K) = -SGZU(L,K+1)/(SGZU(L,K)+SGZU(L,K+1))`<br>[caluvw.f90:406](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caluvw.f90:406) `          CMU = 1.+RCDZM*HU(L)*AVUI(L,K)`<br>[caluvw.f90:407](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caluvw.f90:407) `          EU  = 1./(CMU-RCDZL*CU1(L,K-1))`<br>[caluvw.f90:408](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caluvw.f90:408) `          CU1(L,K) = RCDZU*EU`<br>[caluvw.f90:409](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caluvw.f90:409) `          DU(L,K)  = (DU(L,K)-RCDZL*DU(L,K-1))*EU`<br>[caluvw.f90:459](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caluvw.f90:459) `        DU(L,K) = DU(L,K)-CU1(L,K)*DU(L,K+1)` | 일치 | ΔkΔk+1/(Δk+Δk+1)로 행을 배율 조정한 삼대각계를 푼다. 위아래 계수는 음수이다. 시간항은 DELTI·HU·AVUI이다. |
| <a id="eq-2-143"></a>[T12・PDF 57쪽・인쇄 44쪽・식 2.143](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=57)<br>`\displaystyle \begin{aligned}-\frac{1}{\Delta_k\Delta_{k+1,k}}(t_{yz})_{k-1}^{n+1}&+\left[\frac{1}{\Delta_k\Delta_{k+1,k}}+\frac{(H^v)^{n+1}}{2\Delta t}\left(\frac{H^v}{A_v^v}\right)_k^n+\frac{1}{\Delta_{k+1}\Delta_{k+1,k}}\right](t_{yz})_k^{n+1}\\&-\frac{1}{\Delta_{k+1}\Delta_{k+1,k}}(t_{yz})_{k+1}^{n+1}=\frac{1}{2\Delta t\,m_x^v}\frac{(Q_{k+1}-Q_k)^{**}}{\Delta_{k+1,k}}\end{aligned}`<br>[판독 기록:15](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p055-072.md:15) | [aaefdc.f90:2470](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/aaefdc.f90:2470) `        CDZFV(L,K) = SGZV(L,K)*SGZV(L,K+1)/(SGZV(L,K)+SGZV(L,K+1))`<br>[caluvw.f90:432](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caluvw.f90:432) `          CMV = 1.+RCDZM*HV(L)*AVVI(L,K)`<br>[caluvw.f90:433](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caluvw.f90:433) `          EV  = 1./(CMV-RCDZL*CU2(L,K-1))`<br>[caluvw.f90:434](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caluvw.f90:434) `          CU2(L,K) = RCDZU*EV`<br>[caluvw.f90:435](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caluvw.f90:435) `          DV(L,K)  = (DV(L,K)-RCDZL*DV(L,K-1))*EV`<br>[caluvw.f90:460](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caluvw.f90:460) `        DV(L,K) = DV(L,K)-CU2(L,K)*DV(L,K+1)` | 일치 | x 식과 대칭인 HV·AVVI의 삼대각계를 푼다. 시간 간격은 DELT=2DT이다. |
| <a id="eq-2-144"></a>[T12・PDF 58쪽・인쇄 45쪽・식 2.144](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=58)<br>`\displaystyle P_k=\hat{P}+\sum_{k=1}^{K-1}\left(\sum_{j=1}^{k}\Delta_j\right)(P_{k+1}-P_k).`<br>[판독 기록:16](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p055-072.md:16) | [caluvw.f90:517](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caluvw.f90:517) `        UHE(L) = UHE(L) + CDZDU(L,K)*DU(L,K)`<br>[caluvw.f90:525](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caluvw.f90:525) `      UHDYF(L,KC) = UHE(L)*SUB(L)`<br>[caluvw.f90:531](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caluvw.f90:531) `        UHDYF(L,K) = SUB3D(L,K)*(UHDYF(L,K+1) - DU(L,K))` | 불일치 | 우변은 표층 P_K를 재구성하는 식이다. 문서 좌변은 자유 첨자 P_k로 인쇄되어 있다. 코드는 KC 층을 먼저 만든 뒤 아래층을 재구성한다. |
| <a id="eq-2-145"></a>[T12・PDF 58쪽・인쇄 45쪽・식 2.145](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=58)<br>`\displaystyle P_1=\hat{P}-\sum_{k=1}^{K-1}\left(1-\sum_{j=1}^{k}\Delta_j\right)(P_{k+1}-P_k),`<br>[판독 기록:16](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p055-072.md:16) | [caluvw.f90:517](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caluvw.f90:517) `        UHE(L) = UHE(L) + CDZDU(L,K)*DU(L,K)`<br>[caluvw.f90:531](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caluvw.f90:531) `        UHDYF(L,K) = SUB3D(L,K)*(UHDYF(L,K+1) - DU(L,K))` | 일치 | 표층에 누적 가중 차를 더한 뒤 DU를 순차적으로 빼면 바닥층에서 1−누적 Δ 가중치가 된다. 이 행의 일치는 합과 하향 재귀식의 대수적 동치이다. |
| <a id="eq-2-146"></a>[T12・PDF 58쪽・인쇄 45쪽・식 2.146](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=58)<br>`\displaystyle (\tau_{xz})_0^{n+1}=C_b\left(\sqrt{(u_1)^2+(v_1^u)^2}\right)^n\left(\frac{P_1}{m_y^uH^u}\right)^{n+1}`<br>[판독 기록:16](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p055-072.md:16) | [caluvw.f90:311](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caluvw.f90:311) `        RCXX(L) = STBX(L)*SQRT( V1U(L)*V1U(L) + U1(L,KSZU(L))*U1(L,KSZU(L)) )`<br>[caluvw.f90:419](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caluvw.f90:419) `          DU(L,K)  = (DU(L,K)-RCDZL*RCXX(L)*UHE(L)*HUI(L))*EU` | 일치 | STBX·기존 유속 크기와 새 바닥 유속의 곱을 바닥 응력으로 사용한다. AVCON 상수 점성 경로와 파랑 경로는 이 단순식을 대체한다. |
| <a id="eq-2-147"></a>[T12・PDF 58쪽・인쇄 45쪽・식 2.147](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=58)<br>`\displaystyle (t_{yz})_0^{n+1}=C_b\left(\sqrt{(u_1^v)^2+(v_1)^2}\right)^n\left(\frac{Q_1}{m_x^vH^v}\right)^{n+1}`<br>[판독 기록:16](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p055-072.md:16) | [caluvw.f90:312](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caluvw.f90:312) `        RCYY(L) = STBY(L)*SQRT( U1V(L)*U1V(L) + V1(L,KSZV(L))*V1(L,KSZV(L)) )`<br>[caluvw.f90:442](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caluvw.f90:442) `          CMV = 1.+RCDZM*HV(L)*AVVI(L,K)` | 일치 | y 바닥 응력 계수 RCYY와 새 바닥 유속을 결합한다. |
| <a id="eq-2-148"></a>[T12・PDF 58쪽・인쇄 45쪽・식 2.148](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=58)<br>`\displaystyle C_b=\frac{\kappa^2}{\left[\ln\left(\frac{\Delta_1H}{2z_0^*}\right)\right]^2}`<br>[판독 기록:16](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p055-072.md:16) | [caltbxy.f90:594](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caltbxy.f90:594) `                HUDZBR = 0.5*HURTMP/ZBRATU(L)  `<br>[caltbxy.f90:595](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caltbxy.f90:595) `                HUDZBR = max(HUDZBR,7.5)`<br>[caltbxy.f90:596](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caltbxy.f90:596) `                HVDZBR = 0.5*HVRTMP/ZBRATV(L)  `<br>[caltbxy.f90:597](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caltbxy.f90:597) `                HVDZBR = max(HVDZBR,7.5)`<br>[caltbxy.f90:598](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caltbxy.f90:598) `                STBX(L) = STBXO(L)*0.16/( (LOG( HUDZBR ) -1.)**2)  `<br>[caltbxy.f90:599](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caltbxy.f90:599) `                STBY(L) = STBYO(L)*0.16/( (LOG( HVDZBR ) -1.)**2)  `<br>[caltbxy.f90:601](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caltbxy.f90:601) `                DZHUDZBR = 1. + SGZUU(L,KSZU(L))*HURTMP/ZBRATU(L)`<br>[caltbxy.f90:602](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caltbxy.f90:602) `                DZHVDZBR = 1. + SGZVV(L,KSZV(L))*HVRTMP/ZBRATV(L)`<br>[caltbxy.f90:603](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caltbxy.f90:603) `                DZHUDZBR = max(DZHUDZBR,7.5)  ! *** APPLY THE SAME LIMIT AS KC = 1`<br>[caltbxy.f90:604](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caltbxy.f90:604) `                DZHVDZBR = max(DZHVDZBR,7.5)  ! *** APPLY THE SAME LIMIT AS KC = 1`<br>[caltbxy.f90:605](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caltbxy.f90:605) `                STBX(L) = STBXO(L)*0.16/((LOG(DZHUDZBR))**2)  `<br>[caltbxy.f90:606](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caltbxy.f90:606) `                STBY(L) = STBYO(L)*0.16/((LOG(DZHVDZBR))**2) ` | 불일치 | 문서는 ln(Δ1H/(2z0))를 사용한다. 다층 코드의 로그 인수는 1+0.5·max(DZC,SGZU)·H/z0이다. 단층 코드는 최소 7.5와 로그에서 −1을 적용한다. 코드는 STBXO·0.16과 시간 안정성 상한을 사용한다. |
| <a id="eq-2-149"></a>[T12・PDF 59쪽・인쇄 46쪽・식 2.149](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=59)<br>`\displaystyle (\tau_{xz})_0^{n+1}=C_b\left(\sqrt{(u_1)^2+(v_1^u)^2}\right)^n\left[\left(\frac{\hat{P}}{m_y^uH^u}\right)^{n+1}-\sum_{k=1}^{K-1}\left(1-\sum_{j=1}^{k}\Delta_j\right)\frac{\Delta_{k+1,k}(\tau_{xz})_k^{n+1}}{\left(\frac{A_v^u}{H^u}\right)_k^n}\right],`<br>[판독 기록:17](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p055-072.md:17) | [caluvw.f90:478](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caluvw.f90:478) `        CRU = CDZRU(L,K)*RCXX(L)*AVUI(L,K)`<br>[caluvw.f90:479](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caluvw.f90:479) `        AAU(L) = AAU(L) + CRU*DU(L,K)`<br>[caluvw.f90:480](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caluvw.f90:480) `        BBU(L) = BBU(L) + CRU*UUU(L,K)`<br>[caluvw.f90:488](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caluvw.f90:488) `      AAU(L) = AAU(L)/BBU(L)`<br>[caluvw.f90:495](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caluvw.f90:495) `        DU(L,K) = SUB3D(L,K)*DZGU(L,K)*HU(L)*AVUI(L,K)*( DU(L,K) - AAU(L)*UUU(L,K) )` | 일치 | 전체 수송과 층간 응력을 연결한 바닥 조건을 AAU·BBU의 Sherman–Morrison 보정으로 적용한다. 단순 이차 마찰 경로에 대한 일치이다. |
| <a id="eq-2-150"></a>[T12・PDF 59쪽・인쇄 46쪽・식 2.150](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=59)<br>`\displaystyle w_k=w_{k-1}-\frac{\Delta_k}{m^\zeta}\left[\delta_x^\zeta(P_k-\hat{P})+\delta_y^\zeta(Q_k-\hat{Q})\right]`<br>[판독 기록:17](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p055-072.md:17) | [caluvw.f90:733](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caluvw.f90:733) `          W(L,K) = W(L,K-1) - 0.5*DXYIP(L)  &`<br>[caluvw.f90:734](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caluvw.f90:734) `                   *( UHDY(LE,K) - UHDY(L,K) + UHDYEK(L,K) + UHDY1(LE,K)-UHDY1(L,K) + UHDY1EK(L,K)   &`<br>[caluvw.f90:735](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caluvw.f90:735) `                    + VHDX(LN,K) - VHDX(L,K) + VHDXEK(L,K) + VHDX1(LN,K)-VHDX1(L,K) + VHDX1EK(L,K) ) &`<br>[caluvw.f90:736](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caluvw.f90:736) `                    + ( QSUM(L,K) - DZC(L,K)*QSUME(L) )*DXYIP(L)` | 일치 | 원천이 없는 2시각 평균 플럭스 경로에서 층간 W를 재구성한다. 코드는 원천과 SGZ 면 높이 차 보정도 포함한다. 문서는 이를 생략한다. |
| <a id="eq-2-151"></a>[T12・PDF 61쪽・인쇄 48쪽・식 2.151](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=61)<br>`\displaystyle \sum_{k=n}^{KC}\Delta z_k=1,`<br>[판독 기록:19](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p055-072.md:19) | [aaefdc.f90:1357](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/aaefdc.f90:1357) `            DZPC = SUM(R1D_Global(:))                 ! *** Ensure the DZC's sum to 1.0`<br>[aaefdc.f90:1358](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/aaefdc.f90:1358) `            if( DZPC <= 0.0 ) DZPC = 1.0`<br>[aaefdc.f90:1359](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/aaefdc.f90:1359) `            DZC_Global(LG,:) = R1D_Global(:)/DZPC     ! *** Ensure the DZC's sum to 1.0` | 일치 | 활성층 두께 합 DZPC로 각 DZC를 나눠 합을 1로 만든다. 활성층 시작 번호는 KSZ이다. |
| <a id="eq-2-152"></a>[T12・PDF 61쪽・인쇄 48쪽・식 2.152](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=61)<br>`\displaystyle pd[C_{i,j,k}]x=\frac{C_{i,j,k}-C_{i-1,j,k}}{\Delta x}.`<br>[판독 기록:19](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p055-072.md:19) | [calexp.f90:1215](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calexp.f90:1215) `            FBBX(L,K) = ROLD*FBBX(L,K) + RNEW*SBX(L)*GP*HU(L)*( HU(L)*( (B(L,K+1)-B(LW,K+1))*DZCK(K+1) + (B(L,K)-B(LW,K))*DZCK(K) ) - (B(L,K+1)-B(L,K)+B(LW,K+1)-B(LW,K))*(BELV(L)-BELV(LW)+Z(L,K)*(HP(L)-HP(LW))) )` | 일치 | B(L)−B(LW)의 서쪽 차분을 바로클리닉 기울기에 사용한다. SGZ는 면별 층 비율과 지형 보정을 추가한다. |
| <a id="eq-2-153"></a>[T12・PDF 61쪽・인쇄 48쪽・식 2.153](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=61)<br>`\displaystyle M_0=\rho_0\pi b_0^2h_0,`<br>[판독 기록:19](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p055-072.md:19) | [Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:670](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:670) `      RHOJ(1) = FUNDEN(SALJET,SEDJETT,TEMJET)         ! *** Jet density`<br>[Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:671](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:671) `      RMAJP(1) = PI*RHOJ(1)*RADJ(1)*RADJ(1)*RLEJ(1)   ! *** Jet mass` | 일치 | 초기 분출 밀도에 π·반지름²·요소 길이를 곱한다. |
| <a id="eq-2-154"></a>[T12・PDF 62쪽・인쇄 49쪽・식 2.154](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=62)<br>`\displaystyle h_0=V_0\Delta t`<br>[판독 기록:20](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p055-072.md:20) | [Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:632](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:632) `      RLEJ(1) = 0.1*RJET             ! *** 0.1*Radius = Element length of the plume - Element 1`<br>[Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:659](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:659) `      DTJP = RLEJ(1)/VJ(1)                    ! *** Initialize time step (s)` | 일치 | 요소 길이를 반지름의 0.1로 정한 뒤 DTJP=RLEJ/VJ로 시간 간격을 계산한다. 역관계 h0=V0Δt와 같다. |
| <a id="eq-2-155"></a>[T12・PDF 62쪽・인쇄 49쪽・식 2.155](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=62)<br>`\displaystyle \Delta M_n=\Delta M_s+\Delta M_f`<br>[판독 기록:20](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p055-072.md:20) | [Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:1008](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:1008) `        if( JET_PLM(NJP).ISENT == 0 )then`<br>[Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:1009](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:1009) `          ! ***  TAKE MAX OF SHEAR AND FORCED`<br>[Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:1010](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:1010) `          DRMAJ = max(DRMAJSA,DRMAJFA)`<br>[Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:1011](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:1011) `        else`<br>[Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:1012](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:1012) `          ! ***  TAKE SUM OF SHEAR AND FORCED`<br>[Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:1013](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:1013) `          DRMAJ = DRMAJSA + DRMAJFA` | 불일치 | ISENT=0은 두 연행량의 최댓값을 사용한다. ISENT≠0만 합을 사용한다. 두 연행량은 전후 요소 값의 평균이다. |
| <a id="eq-2-156"></a>[T12・PDF 62쪽・인쇄 49쪽・식 2.156](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=62)<br>`\displaystyle \Delta M_s=\rho_a2\pi b_nh_nE\lvert V_n-u_a\cos\phi_n\cos\theta_n\rvert\Delta t.`<br>[판독 기록:20](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p055-072.md:20) | [Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:971](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:971) `        DVEL = ABS(VJ(N2) - RDQA(N2))                ! *** Velocity shear (m/s):  VJ (3D centerline velocity)  RDQA (difference between jet and ambient velocities)`<br>[Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:986](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:986) `        DRMAJS(N2) = 2.*DTJP*PI*RHOA(N2)*ENTS*DVEL*RADJ(N2)*RLEJ(N2)     ! *** Shear at the end of the current element time step (DTJP) (kg)`<br>[Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:987](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:987) `        DRMAJSA = 0.5*(DRMAJS(N2) + DRMAJS(N1))                          ! *** New average shear between N2 and N1`<br>[Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:1170](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:1170) `        RDQA(N2) = COS(0.0175*PHJ(N2))*COS(0.0175*THJL(N2))*VAH               ! *** Projection of ambient on jet` | 일치 | 2πρa·E·상대 속도·반지름·길이·DTJP로 전단 연행을 계산한다. 주변 유속은 분출축 방향 투영 RDQA이다. 실제 질량 갱신은 이 값을 전후 평균한다. |
| <a id="eq-2-157"></a>[T12・PDF 63쪽・인쇄 50쪽・식 2.157](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=63)<br>`\displaystyle E=\sqrt{2}\frac{0.057-0.554\frac{\sin\theta_n}{F_1^2}}{1+5\frac{u_a\cos\phi_n\cos\theta_n}{\lvert V_n-u_a\cos\phi_n\cos\theta_n\rvert}},`<br>[판독 기록:21](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p055-072.md:21) | [Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:979](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:979) `          FRD2I = FTOP/(ALPH2*DVEL*DVEL)`<br>[Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:980](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:980) `          EBOT = 1. + 5.*RDQA(1)/DVEL `<br>[Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:981](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:981) `          ETOP = 0.057 + 0.554*SINPHJ*FRD2I`<br>[Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:982](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:982) `          ENTS = 1.414*ETOP/EBOT` | 불일치 | 문서는 −0.554·sinθ/F1²이다. 코드는 +0.554·SINPHJ·FRD2I이다. SINPHJ는 수직 각의 sin이다. 분모 코드는 현재 RDQA(N2) 대신 초기 RDQA(1)를 사용한다. √2는 1.414로 근사한다. |
| <a id="eq-2-158"></a>[T12・PDF 63쪽・인쇄 50쪽・식 2.158](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=63)<br>`\displaystyle F_1=\alpha\frac{\lvert V_n-u_a\cos\phi_n\cos\theta_n\rvert}{\sqrt{g\frac{\Delta\rho_n}{\rho_a}b_n}},`<br>[판독 기록:21](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p055-072.md:21) | [Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:973](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:973) `        FTOP = G*ABS(DRHO)*RADJ(N2)                  ! *** Density momentum`<br>[Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:979](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:979) `          FRD2I = FTOP/(ALPH2*DVEL*DVEL)` | 일치 | 코드는 F1 자체 대신 1/F1²=G·\|Δρ/ρa\|·b/(α²·상대 속도²)를 계산한다. 절댓값으로 음의 밀도 차에도 실수를 유지한다. |
| <a id="eq-2-159"></a>[T12・PDF 63쪽・인쇄 50쪽・식 2.159](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=63)<br>`\displaystyle \Delta M_f=\rho_au_a\left[2b\Delta s\sqrt{1-\cos^2\phi\cos^2\theta}+\pi b\Delta b\cos\phi\cos\theta+\frac{1}{2}\pi b^2\Delta(\cos\phi\cos\theta)\right]`<br>[판독 기록:21](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p055-072.md:21) | [Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:992](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:992) `        ENTF1 = 2.*SQRT( 1. - COSPHJ*COSPHJ*COSTHJL*COSTHJL )`<br>[Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:993](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:993) `        DELSIG = SIG(N2) - SIG(N1)`<br>[Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:994](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:994) `        ENTF2 = 0.`<br>[Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:995](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:995) `        ENTF3 = 0.`<br>[Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:996](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:996) `        if( DELSIG > 0. )then`<br>[Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:997](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:997) `          ENTF2 = PI*COSPHJ*COSTHJL*(RADJ(N2) - RADJ(N1))/(DELSIG)`<br>[Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:998](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:998) `          ENTF3 = 0.5*PI*RADJ(N2)*(COSPHJ*COSTHJL - COSPHJM*COSTHJLM)/(DELSIG)`<br>[Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:999](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:999) `        endif`<br>[Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:1000](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:1000) `        ENTF = ENTF1 + ENTF2 + ENTF3`<br>[Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:1001](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:1001) `        ENTF = max(ENTF,0.)`<br>[Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:1002](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:1002) `        DRMAJF(N2) = DTJP*RHOA(N2)*RADJ(N2)*RLEJ(N2)*VAH*ENTF` | 일치 | 반지름과 방향 코사인의 변화를 호 길이 차 DELSIG로 나눈 형태를 사용한다. 요소 길이·DTJP를 곱한 시간 형식은 식 (2.160)에 대응한다. ENTF는 0 이상으로 제한한다. |
| <a id="eq-2-160"></a>[T12・PDF 63쪽・인쇄 50쪽・식 2.160](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=63)<br>`\displaystyle \begin{aligned}\Delta M_f=\rho_au_ah_nb_n\Bigg[&2\sqrt{(\sin^2\phi+\sin^2\theta-\sin^2\phi\sin^2\theta)_n}\\&+\pi\left(\frac{\Delta b}{\Delta s}\cos\phi\cos\theta\right)_n+\frac{\pi b_n}{2}\frac{(\cos\phi\cos\theta)_n-(\cos\phi\cos\theta)_{n-1}}{\Delta s_n}\Bigg]\Delta t\end{aligned}`<br>[판독 기록:21](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p055-072.md:21) | [Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:992](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:992) `        ENTF1 = 2.*SQRT( 1. - COSPHJ*COSPHJ*COSTHJL*COSTHJL )`<br>[Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:993](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:993) `        DELSIG = SIG(N2) - SIG(N1)`<br>[Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:994](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:994) `        ENTF2 = 0.`<br>[Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:995](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:995) `        ENTF3 = 0.`<br>[Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:996](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:996) `        if( DELSIG > 0. )then`<br>[Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:997](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:997) `          ENTF2 = PI*COSPHJ*COSTHJL*(RADJ(N2) - RADJ(N1))/(DELSIG)`<br>[Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:998](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:998) `          ENTF3 = 0.5*PI*RADJ(N2)*(COSPHJ*COSTHJL - COSPHJM*COSTHJLM)/(DELSIG)`<br>[Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:999](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:999) `        endif`<br>[Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:1000](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:1000) `        ENTF = ENTF1 + ENTF2 + ENTF3`<br>[Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:1001](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:1001) `        ENTF = max(ENTF,0.)`<br>[Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:1002](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:1002) `        DRMAJF(N2) = DTJP*RHOA(N2)*RADJ(N2)*RLEJ(N2)*VAH*ENTF` | 일치 | √(1−cos²φcos²θ)와 문서의 sin² 조합은 항등적으로 같다. 나머지 반지름·방향 변화 계수도 같다. 첫 요소 연행은 추가로 0을 적용한다. |
| <a id="eq-2-161"></a>[T12・PDF 64쪽・인쇄 51쪽・식 2.161](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=64)<br>`\displaystyle M_n=\rho_n\pi b_n^2h_n`<br>[판독 기록:22](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p055-072.md:22) | [Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:1174](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:1174) `        RADJ(N2) = SQRT( RMAJP(N2)/(PI*RHOJ(N2)*RLEJ(N2)) )                   ! *** New plume radius (RMAJP - Total mass, RHOJ - Plume density, RLEJ - Element length)` | 일치 | 반지름 계산을 역으로 풀면 M=ρπb²h이다. 질량은 별도 보존 갱신으로 유지한다. |
| <a id="eq-2-162"></a>[T12・PDF 64쪽・인쇄 51쪽・식 2.162](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=64)<br>`\displaystyle M_{n+1}=M_n+\Delta M_n`<br>[판독 기록:22](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p055-072.md:22) | [Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:1019](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:1019) `        RMAJP(N2) = RMAJP(N1) + DRMAJ       ! *** Total volume at the end of the current time step`<br>[Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:1020](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:1020) `        RMAJI = 1./RMAJP(N2)` | 일치 | 기존 질량과 연행 질량을 더한 뒤 새 질량의 역수를 만든다. |
| <a id="eq-2-163"></a>[T12・PDF 64쪽・인쇄 51쪽・식 2.163](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=64)<br>`\displaystyle M_{n+1}=\rho_{n+1}\pi b_{n+1}^2h_{n+1}`<br>[판독 기록:22](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p055-072.md:22) | [Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:1174](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:1174) `        RADJ(N2) = SQRT( RMAJP(N2)/(PI*RHOJ(N2)*RLEJ(N2)) )                   ! *** New plume radius (RMAJP - Total mass, RHOJ - Plume density, RLEJ - Element length)` | 일치 | 새 밀도와 새 길이를 사용해 새 반지름을 구한다. 새 질량의 원통 체적 관계와 같다. |
| <a id="eq-2-164"></a>[T12・PDF 64쪽・인쇄 51쪽・식 2.164](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=64)<br>`\displaystyle C_{n+1}=\frac{M_nC_n+\Delta M_nC_a}{M_{n+1}}`<br>[판독 기록:22](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p055-072.md:22) | [Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:1026](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:1026) `          DYEJ(N2,MD) = RMAJI*( RMAJP(N1)*DYEJ(N1,MD) + DRMAJ*DYEA(MD) )` | 일치 | 보존성 DYE의 농도는 기존 질량 농도와 연행 질량 농도의 합을 새 질량으로 나눈다. 비보존성 물질의 추가 반응까지 이 식의 일치로 판단하지 않았다. |
| <a id="eq-2-165"></a>[T12・PDF 64쪽・인쇄 51쪽・식 2.165](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=64)<br>`\displaystyle S_{n+1}=\frac{M_nS_n+\Delta M_nS_a}{M_{n+1}}`<br>[판독 기록:22](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p055-072.md:22) | [Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:1023](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:1023) `        SALJ(N2)      = RMAJI*( RMAJP(N1)*SALJ(N1)    + DRMAJ*SALA )` | 일치 | SALJ에 같은 질량 가중 혼합식을 사용한다. |
| <a id="eq-2-166"></a>[T12・PDF 64쪽・인쇄 51쪽・식 2.166](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=64)<br>`\displaystyle T_{n+1}=\frac{M_nT_n+\Delta M_nT_a}{M_{n+1}}`<br>[판독 기록:22](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p055-072.md:22) | [Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:1024](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:1024) `        TEMJ(N2)      = RMAJI*( RMAJP(N1)*TEMJ(N1)    + DRMAJ*TEMA )` | 일치 | TEMJ에 같은 질량 가중 혼합식을 사용한다. |
| <a id="eq-2-167"></a>[T12・PDF 64쪽・인쇄 51쪽・식 2.167](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=64)<br>`\displaystyle \rho_{n+1}=\rho(S_{n+1},T_{n+1})`<br>[판독 기록:22](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p055-072.md:22) | [Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:1101](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:1101) `        RHOJ(N2) = FUNDEN(SALJ(N2),SEDJETT,TEMJ(N2))`<br>[Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:1656](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:1656) `  RHO = RHTMP + SSTMP*RHOCA + SQRT(SSTMP)*SSTMP*RHOCB + SSTMP*SSTMP*RHOCC`<br>[Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:1659](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:1659) `  RHO = RHO*( (1. - SDEN*SEDIN) + (SSG - 1.)*SDEN*SEDIN )` | 불일치 | 문서 함수 인자는 염분·온도 두 개이다. 코드는 SEDJETT를 추가한 FUNDEN(S,TSS,T)을 사용한다. TSS=0일 때만 두 인자 관계로 줄어든다. |
| <a id="eq-2-168"></a>[T12・PDF 64쪽・인쇄 51쪽・식 2.168](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=64)<br>`\displaystyle u_{n+1}=\frac{M_nu_n+\Delta M_nu_a}{M_{n+1}}`<br>[판독 기록:22](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p055-072.md:22) | [Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:1106](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:1106) `        UJG(N2) = RMAJI*( RMAJP(N1)*UJG(N1) + DRMAJ*UAG )                   ! ***` | 일치 | x 속도에 주변 x 운동량 DRMAJ·UAG를 혼합한다. 문서의 x축을 주변 수평 흐름축으로 놓으면 ua에 대응한다. |
| <a id="eq-2-169"></a>[T12・PDF 64쪽・인쇄 51쪽・식 2.169](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=64)<br>`\displaystyle v_{n+1}=\frac{M_nv_n}{M_{n+1}}`<br>[판독 기록:22](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p055-072.md:22) | [Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:1105](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:1105) `        VJG(N2) = RMAJI*( RMAJP(N1)*VJG(N1) + DRMAJ*VAG )                   ! *** ` | 불일치 | 문서는 v의 주변 운동량을 0으로 둔다. 코드는 DRMAJ·VAG를 포함한다. 주변 흐름축으로 좌표를 회전해 VAG=0으로 놓은 경우에만 같다. |
| <a id="eq-2-170"></a>[T12・PDF 64쪽・인쇄 51쪽・식 2.170](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=64)<br>`\displaystyle w_{n+1}=\frac{M_nw_n+\Delta M_{n+1}\left(\frac{\Delta\rho}{\rho}\right)_{n+1}g\Delta t}{M_{n+1}}`<br>[판독 기록:22](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p055-072.md:22) | [Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:1102](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:1102) `        DRHO = (RHOA(N2) - RHOJ(N2))/RHOA(N2)`<br>[Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:1107](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:1107) `        WJG(N2) = RMAJI*( RMAJP(N1)*WJG(N1) + DRMAJ*WAG ) + G*DRHO*DTJP     ! ***` | 불일치 | 문서는 연행 질량 ΔM_(n+1)에 부력을 곱해 새 총질량으로 나눈다. 코드는 총질량당 G·DRHO·DTJP를 직접 더한다. 코드에는 주변 수직 속도 DRMAJ·WAG의 혼합도 있다. |
| <a id="eq-2-171"></a>[T12・PDF 65쪽・인쇄 52쪽・식 2.171](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=65)<br>`\displaystyle (Mw)_{n+1}=(Mw)_n+(\Delta\rho V)_{n+1}g\Delta t`<br>[판독 기록:23](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p055-072.md:23) | [Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:1102](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:1102) `        DRHO = (RHOA(N2) - RHOJ(N2))/RHOA(N2)`<br>[Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:1107](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:1107) `        WJG(N2) = RMAJI*( RMAJP(N1)*WJG(N1) + DRMAJ*WAG ) + G*DRHO*DTJP     ! ***` | 일치 | 주변 수직 유속이 0이면 코드의 총질량·부력 가속도는 (ρa−ρj)·분출 체적·g와 같다. 식 (2.170)의 연행 질량 표기와 달리 이 총체적 부력식은 코드와 연결된다. |
| <a id="eq-2-172"></a>[T12・PDF 65쪽・인쇄 52쪽・식 2.172](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=65)<br>`\displaystyle V_{n+1}=\sqrt{u_{n+1}^2+v_{n+1}^2+w_{n+1}^2},\ \text{and}`<br>[판독 기록:23](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p055-072.md:23) | [Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:1157](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:1157) `        VJ(N2)  = SQRT(UJG(N2)*UJG(N2) + VJG(N2)*VJG(N2) + WJG(N2)*WJG(N2))   ! *** 3D jet velocity` | 일치 | 3차원 속도 크기의 제곱합 제곱근을 구한다. |
| <a id="eq-2-173"></a>[T12・PDF 65쪽・인쇄 52쪽・식 2.173](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=65)<br>`\displaystyle U_{n+1}=\sqrt{u_{n+1}^2+v_{n+1}^2}`<br>[판독 기록:23](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p055-072.md:23) | [Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:1158](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:1158) `        VJH(N2) = SQRT(UJG(N2)*UJG(N2) + VJG(N2)*VJG(N2))                     ! *** 2DH jet velocity` | 일치 | 수평 두 속도 성분의 제곱합 제곱근을 구한다. |
| <a id="eq-2-174"></a>[T12・PDF 65쪽・인쇄 52쪽・식 2.174](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=65)<br>`\displaystyle h_{n+1}=\frac{V_{n+1}}{V_n}h_n`<br>[판독 기록:23](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p055-072.md:23) | [Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:1173](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:1173) `        RLEJ(N2) = VJ(N2)*RLEJ(N1)/VJ(N1)                                     ! *** New element length` | 일치 | Vnew/Vold로 기존 요소 길이를 늘린다. |
| <a id="eq-2-175"></a>[T12・PDF 65쪽・인쇄 52쪽・식 2.175](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=65)<br>`\displaystyle b_{n+1}=\sqrt{\frac{M_{n+1}}{\pi\rho_{n+1}h_{n+1}}}`<br>[판독 기록:23](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p055-072.md:23) | [Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:1174](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:1174) `        RADJ(N2) = SQRT( RMAJP(N2)/(PI*RHOJ(N2)*RLEJ(N2)) )                   ! *** New plume radius (RMAJP - Total mass, RHOJ - Plume density, RLEJ - Element length)` | 일치 | 새 질량/(π·새 밀도·새 길이)의 제곱근을 반지름으로 사용한다. |
| <a id="eq-2-176"></a>[T12・PDF 65쪽・인쇄 52쪽・식 2.176](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=65)<br>`\displaystyle \phi_{n+1}=\arctan\left(\frac{w_{n+1}}{U_{n+1}}\right)`<br>[판독 기록:23](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p055-072.md:23) | [Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:1161](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:1161) `        PHJ(N2)  = 57.2958*ATAN2(WJG(N2),VJH(N2))                             ! *** Azimuth or vertical angle (PHI) (degrees)` | 일치 | ATAN2(WJG,VJH)를 사용한다. VJH≥0이므로 유효 수평 속도에서 문서의 arctan 비와 같다. 코드 저장 단위는 도이다. |
| <a id="eq-2-177"></a>[T12・PDF 65쪽・인쇄 52쪽・식 2.177](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=65)<br>`\displaystyle \theta_{n+1}=\arctan\left(\frac{v_{n+1}}{u_{n+1}}\right)`<br>[판독 기록:23](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p055-072.md:23) | [Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:1162](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:1162) `        THJG(N2) = 57.2958*ATAN2(VJG(N2),UJG(N2))                             ! *** Horizontal angle of the jet` | 불일치 | 문서는 arctan(v/u)이다. 코드는 ATAN2(VJG,UJG)로 사분면을 보존한다. 코드 저장 단위는 도이다. |
| <a id="eq-2-178"></a>[T12・PDF 65쪽・인쇄 52쪽・식 2.178](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=65)<br>`\displaystyle x_{n+1}=x_n+u_{n+1}\Delta t`<br>[판독 기록:23](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p055-072.md:23) | [Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:1110](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:1110) `        DXTMP = DTJP*UJG(N2)`<br>[Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:1113](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:1113) `        XJG(N2) = XJG(N1) + DXTMP                             ! *** New X location  delme - can be used to update cell location` | 일치 | 새 x 속도에 DTJP를 곱해 기존 x에 더한다. |
| <a id="eq-2-179"></a>[T12・PDF 65쪽・인쇄 52쪽・식 2.179](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=65)<br>`\displaystyle y_{n+1}=y_n+v_{n+1}\Delta t`<br>[판독 기록:23](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p055-072.md:23) | [Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:1111](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:1111) `        DYTMP = DTJP*VJG(N2)`<br>[Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:1114](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:1114) `        YJG(N2) = YJG(N1) + DYTMP                             ! *** New Y location` | 일치 | 새 y 속도에 DTJP를 곱해 기존 y에 더한다. |
| <a id="eq-2-180"></a>[T12・PDF 65쪽・인쇄 52쪽・식 2.180](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=65)<br>`\displaystyle z_{n+1}=z_n+w_{n+1}\Delta t`<br>[판독 기록:23](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p055-072.md:23) | [Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:1112](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:1112) `        DZTMP = DTJP*WJG(N2)`<br>[Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:1115](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:1115) `        ZJG(N2) = ZJG(N1) + DZTMP                             ! *** New Elevation` | 일치 | 새 수직 속도에 DTJP를 곱해 기존 높이에 더한다. |
| <a id="eq-2-181"></a>[T12・PDF 65쪽・인쇄 52쪽・식 2.181](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=65)<br>`\displaystyle \Delta s_{n+1}=V_{n+1}\Delta t`<br>[판독 기록:23](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p055-072.md:23) | [Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:1110](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:1110) `        DXTMP = DTJP*UJG(N2)`<br>[Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:1111](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:1111) `        DYTMP = DTJP*VJG(N2)`<br>[Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:1112](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:1112) `        DZTMP = DTJP*WJG(N2)`<br>[Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:1116](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:1116) `        DS = SQRT( DXTMP*DXTMP + DYTMP*DYTMP + DZTMP*DZTMP )  ! *** 3D movement`<br>[Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:1117](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_diffuser.f90:1117) `        SIG(N2) = SIG(N1) + DS                                ! *** Cumulative plume movement` | 일치 | 세 이동 성분의 제곱합 제곱근 DS는 Vnew·DTJP이다. SIG에 누적한다. |

z}\left(\frac{m_xm_y}{H}A_b\frac{dC}{dz}\right)+S_C\end{aligned}`<br>[판독 기록:24](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p055-072.md:24) | CCMBTMP - CCLBTMP(L,K)*VCU(L,K-1) )` | 일치 | 이류·원천·선택 수평 확산·암시적 수직 확산의 보존형 구조와 일치한다. 침강은 퇴적물의 별도 분할 단계에서 처리한다. 침강 세부 식은 6장에 대조한다. |
| <a id="eq-3-2"></a>[T12・PDF 67쪽・인쇄 54쪽・식 3.2](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=67)<br>`\displaystyle (FWUU(L,K-1,IW)-FWUU(L,K,IW))  )  `<br>[Transport/calconc.f90:366](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/calconc.f90:366) `          CCLBTMP(L,K) = HPI를 추가로 곱해 문서의 Ab/H²에 대응한다. |
| <a id="eq-3-3"></a>[T12・PDF 68쪽・인쇄 55쪽・식 3.3](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=68)<br>`\displaystyle C_k^*=C_k^{n-1}+\frac{2dt}{mH^{n-1}}(S_C)_k^{n-1}`<br>[판독 기록:26](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p055-072.md:26) | [Transport/caltran.f90:183](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/caltran.f90:183) `        CD(L,K,IT) = CON1(L,K)*H2PK(L,K) + DDELT*( ( FQC(L,K,IT) +                                                                      &`<br>[Transport/caltran.f90:184](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/caltran.f90:184) `                                                     FUHUD(L,K,IW)-FUHUD(LEC(L),K,IW) + FVHUD(L,K,IW)-FVHUD(LNC(L),K,IW) ) * DXYIP(L)   &`<br>[Transport/caltran.f90:185](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/caltran.f90:185) `                                                  + (FWUU(L,K-1,IW)-FWUU(L,K,IW))  )  ` | 문서에만 | 원천만 적용한 별도 C* 배열을 만들지 않는다. 코드는 FQC 원천과 이류 발산을 같은 질량 증분에 더한다. 원천 기여량 2DT·FQC/체적은 이 식과 대응한다. |
| <a id="eq-3-4"></a>[T12・PDF 68쪽・인쇄 55쪽・식 3.4](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=68)<br>`\displaystyle (mH)^{n+1}C_k^{**}=(mH)^{n-1}C_k^*-2dt\left[d_x^d(P_kC_k)+d_y^d(Q_kC_k)+\frac{(WC)_k-(WC)_{k-1}}{d_k}\right]`<br>[판독 기록:26](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p055-072.md:26) | [Transport/caltran.f90:64](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/caltran.f90:64) `    DDELT = DT2  `<br>[Transport/caltran.f90:183](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/caltran.f90:183) `        CD(L,K,IT) = CON1(L,K)*H2PK(L,K) + DDELT*( ( FQC(L,K,IT) +                                                                      &`<br>[Transport/caltran.f90:184](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/caltran.f90:184) `                                                     FUHUD(L,K,IW)-FUHUD(LEC(L),K,IW) + FVHUD(L,K,IW)-FVHUD(LNC(L),K,IW) ) * DXYIP(L)   &`<br>[Transport/caltran.f90:185](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/caltran.f90:185) `                                                  + (FWUU(L,K-1,IW)-FWUU(L,K,IW))  )  ` | 일치 | DDELT=DT2인 3시각 경로에서 이전 HC에 원천과 유입−유출 플럭스를 더한다. 별도 C*를 소거하면 식 (3.3)과 (3.4)의 합과 같다. |
| <a id="eq-3-5"></a>[T12・PDF 68쪽・인쇄 55쪽・식 3.5](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=68)<br>`\displaystyle (HC_k)^{n+1}-2dt\left\{\left[\left(\frac{A_b}{H}\right)_k^n\frac{(C_{k+1}-C_k)^{n+1}}{d_kd_{k+1,k}}\right]-\left[\left(\frac{A_b}{H}\right)_{k-1}^n\frac{(C_k-C_{k-1})^{n+1}}{d_kd_{k,k-1}}\right]\right\}=H^{n+1}C_k^{**}`<br>[판독 기록:26](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p055-072.md:26) | [Transport/calconc.f90:366](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/calconc.f90:366) `          CCLBTMP(L,K) = RCDZKMK*HPI(L)*AB(L,K-1)`<br>[Transport/calconc.f90:367](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/calconc.f90:367) `          CCUBTMP      = RCDZKK*HPI(L)*AB(L,K)`<br>[Transport/calconc.f90:368](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/calconc.f90:368) `          CCMBTMP      = 1._8-CCLBTMP(L,K)-CCUBTMP`<br>[Transport/calconc.f90:369](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/calconc.f90:369) `          EEB(L,K)     = 1._8/( CCMBTMP - CCLBTMP(L,K)*VCU(L,K-1) )`<br>[Transport/calconc.f90:400](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/calconc.f90:400) `            WCV(IW).VAL0(L,K) = ( WCV(IW).VAL0(L,K) - CCLBTMP(L,K)*WCV(IW).VAL0(L,K-1) )*EEB(L,K)` | 일치 | 새 농도의 상하층 차를 암시적으로 푼다. 행을 H로 나눠 대각 1−하부−상부 계수를 구성한다. |
| <a id="eq-3-6"></a>[T12・PDF 68쪽・인쇄 55쪽・식 3.6](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=68)<br>`\displaystyle \begin{aligned}-\frac{2dt}{d_kd_{k,k-1}}\left(\frac{A_b}{H}\right)_{k-1}^nC_{k-1}^{n+1}&+\left[\frac{2dt}{d_kd_{k,k-1}}\left(\frac{A_b}{H}\right)_{k-1}^n+H^{n+1}+\frac{2dt}{d_kd_{k+1,k}}\left(\frac{A_b}{H}\right)_k^n\right]C_k^{n+1}\\&-\frac{2dt}{d_kd_{k+1,k}}\left(\frac{A_b}{H}\right)_k^nC_{k+1}^{n+1}=H^{n+1}C_k^{**}\end{aligned}`<br>[판독 기록:26](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p055-072.md:26) | [Transport/calconc.f90:364](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/calconc.f90:364) `          RCDZKMK      = -DELT*CDZKMK(L,K)`<br>[Transport/calconc.f90:365](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/calconc.f90:365) `          RCDZKK       = -DELT*CDZKK(L,K)`<br>[Transport/calconc.f90:366](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/calconc.f90:366) `          CCLBTMP(L,K) = RCDZKMK*HPI(L)*AB(L,K-1)`<br>[Transport/calconc.f90:367](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/calconc.f90:367) `          CCUBTMP      = RCDZKK*HPI(L)*AB(L,K)`<br>[Transport/calconc.f90:368](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/calconc.f90:368) `          CCMBTMP      = 1._8-CCLBTMP(L,K)-CCUBTMP`<br>[Transport/calconc.f90:369](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/calconc.f90:369) `          EEB(L,K)     = 1._8/( CCMBTMP - CCLBTMP(L,K)*VCU(L,K-1) )`<br>[Transport/calconc.f90:370](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/calconc.f90:370) `          VCU(L,K)     = CCUBTMP*EEB(L,K)`<br>[Transport/calconc.f90:408](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/calconc.f90:408) `            WCV(IW).VAL0(L,K) = WCV(IW).VAL0(L,K) - VCU(L,K)*WCV(IW).VAL0(L,K+1)` | 일치 | 음의 양옆 계수와 양의 대각을 사용한 Thomas 풀이이다. AB=물리 Ab/H이므로 HPI와 결합한 계수가 식의 Ab/H²이다. |
| <a id="eq-3-7"></a>[T12・PDF 69쪽・인쇄 56쪽・식 3.7](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=69)<br>`\displaystyle q_0^2=B_1^{2/3}\sqrt{t_{bx}^2+t_{by}^2},l_0=0,\ \text{at}z=0`<br>[판독 기록:27](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p055-072.md:27) | [aaefdc.f90:781](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/aaefdc.f90:781) `  CTURB2 = CTURB**0.667`<br>[hdmt2t.f90:889](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/hdmt2t.f90:889) `        TMP = ( RSSBCE(L)*TVAR3E(L) + RSSBCW(L)*TBX(L) )**2 + ( RSSBCN(L)*TVAR3N(L) + RSSBCS(L)*TBY(L) )**2`<br>[hdmt2t.f90:890](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/hdmt2t.f90:890) `        QQ(L,0)  = 0.5*CTURB2*SQRT(TMP)`<br>[calqq2t.f90:361](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calqq2t.f90:361) `        UUU(L,1) = (UUU(L,1)-CLQTMP*HP(L)*QQ(L,0)-CUQTMP*HP(L)*QQ(L,KC))*EQ`<br>[calqq2t.f90:362](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calqq2t.f90:362) `        VVV(L,1) = VVV(L,1)*EQL` | 일치 | 단순 바닥 응력 분기에서 B1^0.667과 응력 벡터 크기를 사용한다. 0.667은 2/3의 근사이다. QQL 경계는 추가 원천 없이 처리한다. 파랑·GOTM 경로는 다른 조건을 쓴다. |
| <a id="eq-3-8"></a>[T12・PDF 69쪽・인쇄 56쪽・식 3.8](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=69)<br>`\displaystyle q_K^2=B_1^{2/3}\sqrt{t_{sx}^2+t_{sy}^2},l_K=0,\ \text{at}z=1`<br>[판독 기록:27](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p055-072.md:27) | [hdmt2t.f90:892](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/hdmt2t.f90:892) `        TMP = ( RSSBCE(L)*TVAR3W(L) + RSSBCW(L)*TSX(L) )**2 + ( RSSBCN(L)*TVAR3S(L) + RSSBCS(L)*TSY(L) )**2`<br>[hdmt2t.f90:893](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/hdmt2t.f90:893) `        QQ(L,KC) = 0.5*CTURB2*SQRT(TMP)`<br>[calqq2t.f90:361](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calqq2t.f90:361) `        UUU(L,1) = (UUU(L,1)-CLQTMP*HP(L)*QQ(L,0)-CUQTMP*HP(L)*QQ(L,KC))*EQ`<br>[calqq2t.f90:362](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calqq2t.f90:362) `        VVV(L,1) = VVV(L,1)*EQL` | 일치 | 단순 표면 응력 분기에서 동일 B1 지수를 사용한다. 표면 난류원 선택 시 추가 항을 적용한다. |
| <a id="eq-3-9"></a>[T12・PDF 69쪽・인쇄 56쪽・식 3.9](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=69)<br>`\displaystyle \begin{aligned}(mHC_k)^{n+1}=(mHC_k)^{n-1}&-2\frac{dt}{dx}\left[(PC)_{i+\frac{1}{2},j,k}-(PC)_{i-\frac{1}{2},j,k}\right]\\&-2\frac{dt}{dy}\left[(QC)_{i,j+\frac{1}{2},k}-(QC)_{i,j-\frac{1}{2},k}\right]-2\frac{dt}{d_k}[(WC)_k-(WC)_{k-1}]\end{aligned}`<br>[판독 기록:27](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p055-072.md:27) | [Transport/caltran.f90:183](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/caltran.f90:183) `        CD(L,K,IT) = CON1(L,K)*H2PK(L,K) + DDELT*( ( FQC(L,K,IT) +                                                                      &`<br>[Transport/caltran.f90:184](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/caltran.f90:184) `                                                     FUHUD(L,K,IW)-FUHUD(LEC(L),K,IW) + FVHUD(L,K,IW)-FVHUD(LNC(L),K,IW) ) * DXYIP(L)   &`<br>[Transport/caltran.f90:185](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/caltran.f90:185) `                                                  + (FWUU(L,K-1,IW)-FWUU(L,K,IW))  )  ` | 일치 | FQC=0이면 수평 유입−유출과 수직 하부−상부 플럭스로 HC를 갱신한다. 코드의 실제 층 체적을 사용하면 문서의 mH 식과 같다. |
| <a id="eq-3-10"></a>[T12・PDF 69쪽・인쇄 56쪽・식 3.10](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=69)<br>`\displaystyle \begin{aligned}(mHC_k)^{n+1}=(mHC_k)^{n-1}&-\frac{dt}{dx}\left[\tilde{P}_{i+\frac{1}{2},j,k}(C_{i+1,j,k}+C_{i,j,k})-\tilde{P}_{i-\frac{1}{2},j,k}(C_{i,j,k}+C_{i-1,j,k})\right]\\&-\frac{dt}{dy}\left[\tilde{Q}_{i,j+\frac{1}{2},k}(C_{i,j+1,k}+C_{i,j,k})-\tilde{Q}_{i,j-\frac{1}{2},k}(C_{i,j,k}+C_{i,j-1,k})\right]\\&-\frac{dt}{d_k}\left[\tilde{W}_{i,j,k}(C_{i,j,k+1}+C_{i,j,k})-\tilde{W}_{i,j,k-1}(C_{i,j,k}+C_{i,j,k-1})\right]\end{aligned}`<br>[판독 기록:27](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p055-072.md:27) | 수송 식의 구현으로 판단하지 않았다. |

| <a id="eq-4-5"></a>[T12・PDF 71쪽・인쇄 58쪽・식 4.5](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=71)<br>`\displaystyle \frac{dC}{dt}=-K`<br>[판독 기록:29](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p055-072.md:29) | [Transport/caldye.f90:130](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/caldye.f90:130) `    elseif( DYES(MD).ITYPE == 2 )then `<br>[Transport/caldye.f90:131](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/caldye.f90:131) `      ! *** Age of Water (0th order growth)`<br>[Transport/caldye.f90:132](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/caldye.f90:132) `      DAGE = DELT/86400.`<br>[Transport/caldye.f90:134](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/caldye.f90:134) `      !$OMP PARALLEL DO PRIVATE(ND,K,LP,L)`<br>[Transport/caldye.f90:135](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/caldye.f90:135) `      do ND = 1,NDM  `<br>[Transport/caldye.f90:136](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/caldye.f90:136) `        do K = 1,KC  `<br>[Transport/caldye.f90:137](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/caldye.f90:137) `          do LP = 1,LLWET(K,ND)`<br>[Transport/caldye.f90:138](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/caldye.f90:138) `            L = LKWET(LP,K,ND)  `<br>[Transport/caldye.f90:139](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/caldye.f90:139) `            DYE(L,K,MD) = DYE(L,K,MD) + DAGE` | 불일치 | ITYPE=2는 경과 일수 Δt/86400을 더한다. Confluence는 영차 플래그 −1을 설명하므로 K=−1이면 문서 식의 부호를 코드와 맞출 수 있다. 엔진은 이 갱신에서 KRATE0를 사용하지 않는다. PDF는 C의 단위를 day, t의 단위를 day, K의 단위를 1/day로 정의한다. 영차 dC/dt의 단위는 day/day이므로 PDF의 K 단위와 맞지 않는다. 이 단위 불일치 판정은 차원 해석이다. |

### 2.4 5장 열・수온・얼음・증발

| 문서 식・쪽・LaTeX 원문 | 코드 원문 줄 | 판정 | 이유 |
|---|---|---|---|
| <a id="eq-5-1"></a>[T12・PDF 73쪽・인쇄 60쪽・식 5.1](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=73)<br>`-\frac{\rho c_p A_b}{H}\frac{\partial T}{\partial z}=H_L+H_E+H_C`<br>[판독 기록:13](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p073-090.md:13) | [Transport/mod_heat.f90:642](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:642) `        C2 = -DELT*DZIC(L,KC)`<br>[Transport/mod_heat.f90:651](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:651) `          RADNET(L,KC) = RADNET(L,KC) + C2*(HBLW + HBCV + HBEV)`<br>[Transport/mod_heat.f90:661](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:661) `          TEMP = TEM(L,KC) + HPI(L)*RADNET(L,KC)`<br>[Transport/mod_heat.f90:662](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:662) `          TEM(L,KC) = TEMP` | 일치 | 양의 장파·잠열·현열 손실을 표층 온도에서 뺀다. 온도 기울기 경계값을 직접 지정하는 대신 표층 체적 원천으로 적용한다. 이후 수직 확산을 분리해 계산한다. |
| <a id="eq-5-2"></a>[T12・PDF 73쪽・인쇄 60쪽・식 5.2](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=73)<br>`H_L=\varepsilon\sigma(T_s+273.15)^4(0.39-0.05\sqrt{e_a})(1+B_c C)+4\varepsilon\sigma(T_s+273.15)^3(T_s-T_a)`<br>[판독 기록:13](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p073-090.md:13) | [Transport/mod_heat.f90:646](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:646) `          HBLW = 1.312E-14*((TEM(L,KC)+273.)**4)*(0.39-0.05*SQRT(VPAT(L)))*(1.-.8*CLOUDT(L)) + &`<br>[Transport/mod_heat.f90:647](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:647) `            5.248E-14*((TEM(L,KC)+273.)**3)*(TEM(L,KC)-TATMT(L))` | 불일치 | 문서는 구름 인자 1+0.8C이다. 코드는 1−0.8C이다. 문서는 273.15이고 코드는 273이다. 코드는 ρcp로 나눈 계수 1.312E−14와 5.248E−14를 사용한다. |
| <a id="eq-5-3"></a>[T12・PDF 73쪽・인쇄 60쪽・식 5.3](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=73)<br>`H_E=c_e\rho_a L_E W_s(e_s-e_a)\frac{0.622}{P_a}`<br>[판독 기록:13](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p073-090.md:13) | [Transport/mod_heat.f90:649](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:649) `          HBEV = CLEVAP(L)*0.445*WINDST(L)*(SVPW1-VPAT(L))/PATMT(L)`<br>[caltsxy.f90:513](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caltsxy.f90:513) `              CLEVAPTMP = 1.E-3*(0.8 + 0.065*WINDST(L))  `<br>[caltsxy.f90:514](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caltsxy.f90:514) `              CLEVAP(L) = max(SVREVC(L),CLEVAPTMP)  `<br>[Transport/mod_heat.f90:299](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:299) `    RHOWCP  = 1000.0*CP             ! *** CP-SPECIFIC HEAT (J/KG/degC) * RHO (KG/M3)`<br>[Transport/mod_heat.f90:300](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:300) `    RHOWCPI = 1./RHOWCP` | 일치 | CLEVAP·풍속·수증기압 차/기압을 사용한다. 0.445는 고정 대기 밀도·잠열·0.622와 ρwcp 정규화를 흡수한 계수이다. 이 값은 문서 상수로 환산한 약 0.4467의 근사이다. CLEVAP은 입력 또는 풍속 함수이다. |
| <a id="eq-5-4"></a>[T12・PDF 73쪽・인쇄 60쪽・식 5.4](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=73)<br>`H_C=c_h\rho_a c_{pa}W_s(T_s-T_a)`<br>[판독 기록:13](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p073-090.md:13) | [Transport/mod_heat.f90:648](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:648) `          HBCV = CCNHTT(L)*0.288E-3*WINDST(L)*(TEM(L,KC)-TATMT(L)) `<br>[caltsxy.f90:530](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caltsxy.f90:530) `      ! *** CONDUCTIVE HEAT TRANSFER WIND FUNCTION`<br>[caltsxy.f90:531](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caltsxy.f90:531) `      if( ISTOPT(2) == 1 )then`<br>[Transport/mod_heat.f90:299](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:299) `    RHOWCP  = 1000.0*CP             ! *** CP-SPECIFIC HEAT (J/KG/degC) * RHO (KG/M3)`<br>[Transport/mod_heat.f90:300](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:300) `    RHOWCPI = 1./RHOWCP` | 일치 | CCNHTT·풍속·수온−기온의 곱이다. 0.288E−3은 ρacpa/(ρwcp)의 근사이다. 계수는 공간 입력 또는 풍속 함수로 지정한다. |
| <a id="eq-5-5"></a>[T12・PDF 74쪽・인쇄 61쪽・식 5.5](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=74)<br>`\overline{w'x'}=c_x^{1/2}c_d^{1/2}S\Delta X=C_x S\Delta X`<br>[판독 기록:14](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p073-090.md:14) | [Transport/coare36.f90:404](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/coare36.f90:404) `      cdhf = von/(log(zu/zo)-psiu_26(zu/L))`<br>[Transport/coare36.f90:405](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/coare36.f90:405) `      cqhf = von*fdg/(log(zq/zoq)-psit_26(zq/L))`<br>[Transport/coare36.f90:406](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/coare36.f90:406) `      cthf = von*fdg/(log(zt/zot)-psit_26(zt/L))`<br>[Transport/coare36.f90:407](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/coare36.f90:407) `      usr_no_gust = du*cdhf ! ustar without gustiness -- avoids division by wind speed which could be zero.`<br>[Transport/coare36.f90:408](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/coare36.f90:408) `      usr = ut*cdhf ! ustar with gustiness`<br>[Transport/coare36.f90:409](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/coare36.f90:409) `      qsr = -(dq-wetc*dT_skin*jcool)*cqhf`<br>[Transport/coare36.f90:410](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/coare36.f90:410) `      tsr = -(dT-dT_skin*jcool)*cthf`<br>[Transport/coare36.f90:522](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/coare36.f90:522) `    hsb = -rhoa*cpa*usr*tsr         !% sensible heat flux`<br>[Transport/coare36.f90:523](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/coare36.f90:523) `    hlb = -rhoa*Le*usr*qsr          !% latent heat flux` | 일치 | 마찰속도 usr=풍속·cdhf와 스칼라 스케일 tsr·qsr를 곱한다. cdhf와 cthf·cqhf의 곱은 문서의 총 전달계수이다. 냉각 표피와 돌풍 보정은 추가된다. |
| <a id="eq-5-6"></a>[T12・PDF 74쪽・인쇄 61쪽・식 5.6](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=74)<br>`H_C=\rho_a c_{pa}\overline{w'T'}=\rho_a c_{pa}C_h S(T_s-\theta)`<br>[판독 기록:14](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p073-090.md:14) | [Transport/coare36.f90:408](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/coare36.f90:408) `      usr = ut*cdhf ! ustar with gustiness`<br>[Transport/coare36.f90:410](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/coare36.f90:410) `      tsr = -(dT-dT_skin*jcool)*cthf`<br>[Transport/coare36.f90:522](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/coare36.f90:522) `    hsb = -rhoa*cpa*usr*tsr         !% sensible heat flux`<br>[Transport/mod_heat.f90:730](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:730) `          HBCV = RHOWCPI*hsb    ! *** m*degC/s` | 일치 | hsb=−ρa·cpa·usr·tsr이고 tsr는 음의 온도 차·전달계수이다. 두 음의 부호를 결합하면 문서의 해양→대기 양의 현열이다. |
| <a id="eq-5-7"></a>[T12・PDF 74쪽・인쇄 61쪽・식 5.7](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=74)<br>`H_E=\rho_a L_e\overline{w'q'}=\rho_a L_e C_e S(q_s-q)`<br>[판독 기록:14](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p073-090.md:14) | [Transport/coare36.f90:409](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/coare36.f90:409) `      qsr = -(dq-wetc*dT_skin*jcool)*cqhf`<br>[Transport/coare36.f90:523](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/coare36.f90:523) `    hlb = -rhoa*Le*usr*qsr          !% latent heat flux`<br>[Transport/mod_heat.f90:731](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:731) `          HBEV = RHOWCPI*hlb    ! *** m*degC/s` | 일치 | 수증기 스케일 qsr와 마찰속도의 곱으로 잠열을 계산한다. 해양→대기 양의 부호가 같다. |
| <a id="eq-5-8"></a>[T12・PDF 74쪽・인쇄 61쪽・식 5.8](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=74)<br>`z_0=\frac{\alpha u_*^2}{g}+\frac{0.11\nu}{u_*}`<br>[판독 기록:14](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p073-090.md:14) | [Transport/coare36.f90:393](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/coare36.f90:393) `      zo = charn*usr*usr/grav + 0.11*visa/usr !% surface roughness`<br>[Transport/coare36.f90:527](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/coare36.f90:527) `    zo10  = 0.011*usr*usr/grav + 0.11*visa/(usr+1.e-12)` | 불일치 | Charnock+0.11ν/u* 형태는 같다. 문서는 물의 동점성으로 ν를 설명한다. 코드의 visa는 공기 동점성이다. 냉각 표피 열교환의 zo와 최종 중립 항력의 zo10도 서로 다른 Charnock 계수를 사용한다. |
| <a id="eq-5-9"></a>[T12・PDF 75쪽・인쇄 62쪽・식 5.9](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=75)<br>`H_n=-K_{aw}(T_s-T_e)`<br>[판독 기록:15](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p073-090.md:15) | [Transport/mod_heat.f90:823](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:823) `          TFLUX = CSHE*(ET - TEM(L,KC))/THICK*DELT`<br>[Transport/mod_heat.f90:824](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:824) `          TEM(L,KC) = TEM(L,KC) + TFLUX`<br>[Transport/mod_heat.f90:1599](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:1599) `  CSHE = CSHE*FLUX_BR_TO_FLUX_SI*RHOWCPI   ! *** M/S` | 일치 | CSHE·(ET−Ts)/표층 두께·DELT로 온도를 갱신한다. CSHE=Kaw/(ρcp)이므로 열교환 부호와 같다. |
