| <a id="eq-5-11"></a>[T12・PDF 75쪽・인쇄 62쪽・식 5.11](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=75)<br>`K=23+(\beta_w+0.225)17W_2`<br>[판독 기록:15](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p073-090.md:15) | [Transport/mod_heat.f90:1581](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:1581) `  FW    = W_M2_TO_BTU_FT2_DAY*AFW+BCONV*BFW*WIND_2M**CFW`<br>[Transport/mod_heat.f90:1582](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:1582) `  CSHE  = 15.7+(0.26+BETA)*FW` | 불일치 | 문서는 23+(βw+0.225)17W2이다. 코드는 15.7+(0.26+β)FW이다. FW는 AFW+BFW·W2^CFW의 단위 환산값이다. |
| <a id="eq-5-12"></a>[T12・PDF 76쪽・인쇄 63쪽・식 5.12](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=76)<br>`\beta_w=0.255-0.0085T_w-0.000204T_w^2`<br>[판독 기록:16](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p073-090.md:16) | [Transport/mod_heat.f90:1580](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:1580) `  BETA  = 0.255-(8.5E-3*TSTAR)+(2.04E-4*TSTAR*TSTAR)`<br>[Transport/mod_heat.f90:1588](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:1588) `    TSTAR = (ET+TDEW_F)*0.5`<br>[Transport/mod_heat.f90:1589](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:1589) `    BETA  = 0.255-(8.5E-3*TSTAR)+(2.04E-4*TSTAR*TSTAR)` | 불일치 | 문서의 이차항 부호는 음수이다. 코드의 이차항 부호는 양수이다. 코드는 Tw 대신 평형온도와 이슬점의 평균 TSTAR를 사용한다. |
| <a id="eq-5-13"></a>[T12・PDF 76쪽・인쇄 63쪽・식 5.13](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=76)<br>`I_{sw}=I_0 S_f\min\{\exp[-K_{e,me}(H_{rps}-H)],1\}\min\{\exp[-K_{e,ice}H_{ice}],1\}`<br>[판독 기록:16](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p073-090.md:16) | [Transport/mod_heat.f90:391](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:391) `            SOLSWRT(L) = SOLSWRT(L)*PSHADE(L)`<br>[Transport/mod_heat.f90:1666](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:1666) `    SOLBOT = EXP(-GAMMAI*ICETHICK(L))`<br>[Transport/mod_heat.f90:1667](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:1667) `    BETAI = 1.0 - SOLBOT`<br>[Transport/mod_heat.f90:1668](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:1668) `    REFICE  = (1. - ALBEDOI)/(1.-REFL)*SOLBOT`<br>[Transport/mod_heat.f90:1669](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:1669) `    RADTOP(L,KC) = SOLSWRT(L)*REFICE` | 불일치 | 차광 PSHADE와 얼음 지수 감쇠는 있다. 이 수면 경로에서 줄기 높이 H_rps−H의 별도 min[exp(...),1] 인자를 찾지 못했다. 코드는 얼음 알베도와 수면 반사를 추가한다. PSHADE 입력에 이미 포함한 효과를 별도 줄기 구현으로 판단하지 않았다. |
| <a id="eq-5-14"></a>[T12・PDF 76쪽・인쇄 63쪽・식 5.14](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=76)<br>`I(z)=I_{sw}\exp(-\zeta z)`<br>[판독 기록:16](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p073-090.md:16) | [Transport/mod_heat.f90:600](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:600) `              BOT = max(-WCKESS*HPK(L,K),-40.0)`<br>[Transport/mod_heat.f90:601](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:601) `              FRACLAYER = EXP(BOT)`<br>[Transport/mod_heat.f90:603](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:603) `              RADBOT(L,K) = RADTOP(L,K)*FRACLAYER` | 일치 | 물리 층 두께에 음의 소산계수를 곱한 Beer 감쇠이다. 음의 지수는 −40 이상으로 제한한다. 이 식의 z는 σ가 아니라 수면 아래 거리이다. |
| <a id="eq-5-15"></a>[T12・PDF 77쪽・인쇄 64쪽・식 5.15](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=77)<br>`I(z)=I_{sw}[R\exp(z\zeta_f)+(1-R)\exp(z\zeta_s)]`<br>[판독 기록:17](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p073-090.md:17) | [Transport/mod_heat.f90:515](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:515) `          TFAST  = SWRATNF*(Z(L,KC)  -1.)`<br>[Transport/mod_heat.f90:516](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:516) `          TFAST1 = SWRATNF*(Z(L,KC-1)-1.)`<br>[Transport/mod_heat.f90:517](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:517) `          TSLOW  = SWRATNS*(Z(L,KC)  -1.)`<br>[Transport/mod_heat.f90:518](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:518) `          TSLOW1 = SWRATNS*(Z(L,KC-1)-1.)`<br>[Transport/mod_heat.f90:519](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:519) `          C1 = DELT*DZIC(L,KC)*RHOWCPI`<br>[Transport/mod_heat.f90:520](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:520) `          RSN = RADTOP(L,KC)*( FSWRATF*EXP(TFAST*HP(L))  + (1.-FSWRATF)*EXP(TSLOW*HP(L))        &`<br>[Transport/mod_heat.f90:521](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:521) `                             - FSWRATF*EXP(TFAST1*HP(L)) - (1.-FSWRATF)*EXP(TSLOW1*HP(L)) )` | 불일치 | 문서는 깊이 z≥0에서 두 지수에 양의 부호를 쓴다. 코드는 H·(Z−1)≤0을 사용한다. R=FSWRATF이고 두 소산계수는 SWRATNF·SWRATNS이다. |
| <a id="eq-5-16"></a>[T12・PDF 78쪽・인쇄 65쪽・식 5.16](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=78)<br>`I(z)=(1-\beta)I_{sw}\exp(-K_e z)`<br>[판독 기록:18](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p073-090.md:18) | [Transport/mod_heat.f90:572](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:572) `          BOT = max(-WCKESS*HPK(L,K),-40.0)`<br>[Transport/mod_heat.f90:573](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:573) `          FRACLAYER = EXP(BOT)`<br>[Transport/mod_heat.f90:574](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:574) `          if( FSOLRADMIN > 0.0 )then`<br>[Transport/mod_heat.f90:575](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:575) `            BOT = 1.0 - FRACLAYER`<br>[Transport/mod_heat.f90:576](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:576) `            BOT = max(BOT, FSOLRADMIN)`<br>[Transport/mod_heat.f90:577](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:577) `            FRACLAYER = 1.0 - BOT`<br>[Transport/mod_heat.f90:578](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:578) `          endif`<br>[Transport/mod_heat.f90:580](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:580) `          RADBOT(L,K) = RADTOP(L,K)*FRACLAYER` | 불일치 | 문서는 (1−β)·exp(−Kez)를 곱한다. 코드는 층 흡수율 1−exp(−Ke·층 두께)와 FSOLRADMIN의 최댓값을 사용한다. 투과율은 min[exp(...),1−FSOLRADMIN]이다. 두 방법은 같지 않다. |
| <a id="eq-5-17"></a>[T12・PDF 78쪽・인쇄 65쪽・식 5.17](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=78)<br>`K_{ess}=K_{e,b}+K_{e,TSS}TSS+K_{e,POC}POC+K_{e,DOC}DOC+K_{e,Chl}\sum Chl+K_{e,MAC}MAC`<br>[판독 기록:18](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p073-090.md:18) | [Transport/mod_heat.f90:1620](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:1620) `    WCKESS = WQKEB(IWQZMAP(L,K))                                     ! *** BACKGROUND EXTINCTION (BY WQ ZONE)`<br>[Transport/mod_heat.f90:1621](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:1621) `    WCKESS = WCKESS + WQKETSS*TSS                                    ! *** INORGANIC SOLIDS COMPONENT`<br>[Transport/mod_heat.f90:1622](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:1622) `    WCKESS = WCKESS + WQKEPOC*(WQV(L,K,IROC) + WQV(L,K,ILOC))        ! *** PARTICULATE ORGANIC MATTER`<br>[Transport/mod_heat.f90:1623](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:1623) `    WCKESS = WCKESS + WQKEDOM*WQV(L,K,IDOC)                          ! *** DISSOLVED ORGANIC MATTER`<br>[Transport/mod_heat.f90:1629](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:1629) `          WCKESS = WCKESS + ALGAES(NAL).WQKEMAC*WQV(L,K,19+NAL)      ! *** MACROPHYTE MASS G/M3 TO 1/M`<br>[Transport/mod_heat.f90:1634](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:1634) `    if( WQKECHL < 0.0 )then                                          ! *** CHLOROPHYLL`<br>[Transport/mod_heat.f90:1635](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:1635) `      ! *** Compute Extinction Factor as a fn(Chla)`<br>[Transport/mod_heat.f90:1636](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:1636) `      CHLKE = 0.054*WQCHL(L,K)**0.6667 + 0.0088*WQCHL(L,K)`<br>[Transport/mod_heat.f90:1637](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:1637) `    else`<br>[Transport/mod_heat.f90:1638](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:1638) `      CHLKE = WQKECHL*WQCHL(L,K)**WQKECHLE`<br>[Transport/mod_heat.f90:1639](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:1639) `    endif`<br>[Transport/mod_heat.f90:1640](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:1640) `    WCKESS = WCKESS + CHLKE` | 불일치 | 배경·TSS·POC·DOC·MAC의 합 구조는 같다. CHL은 WQKECHLE 지수 또는 0.054·Chl^0.6667+0.0088·Chl이다. 문서의 선형 Chl 합은 WQKECHLE=1인 경우에만 같다. |
| <a id="eq-5-18"></a>[T12・PDF 79쪽・인쇄 66쪽・식 5.18](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=79)<br>`H_b=-(K_{b,v}U+K_{b,c})(T_w-T_b)`<br>[판독 기록:19](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p073-090.md:19) | [Transport/mod_heat.f90:920](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:920) `          USPD = SQRT( UBED*UBED + VBED*VBED )`<br>[Transport/mod_heat.f90:921](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:921) `          TEMP = max(TEM(L,KSZ(L)), 0.1 )`<br>[Transport/mod_heat.f90:923](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:923) `          !        (nodim) (m/s)   (m/s)      (C)       (C)    (s)`<br>[Transport/mod_heat.f90:924](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:924) `          TFLUX = ( HTBED1*USPD + HTBED2 )*( TEMB(L) - TEMP )*DELT   ! *** C*m` | 일치 | 수주로 들어오는 열의 부호는 Tb−Tw이다. HTBED1·U+HTBED2는 ρwcp로 정규화한 계수이다. 코드는 Tw를 최소 0.1도로 제한한다. |
| <a id="eq-5-19"></a>[T12・PDF 79쪽・인쇄 66쪽・식 5.19](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=79)<br>`U=\sqrt{u_1^2+v_1^2}`<br>[판독 기록:19](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p073-090.md:19) | [Transport/mod_heat.f90:918](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:918) `          UBED = 0.5*( U(L,KSZU(L)) + U(LEC(L),KSZU(LEC(L))) )`<br>[Transport/mod_heat.f90:919](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:919) `          VBED = 0.5*( V(L,KSZV(L)) + V(LNC(L),KSZV(LNC(L))) )`<br>[Transport/mod_heat.f90:920](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:920) `          USPD = SQRT( UBED*UBED + VBED*VBED )` | 일치 | 바닥층 면 유속을 셀 중심으로 평균한 뒤 수평 크기를 계산한다. SGZ에서는 KSZU·KSZV를 사용한다. |
| <a id="eq-5-20"></a>[T12・PDF 79쪽・인쇄 66쪽・식 5.20](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=79)<br>`\frac{\delta(D_b T_b)}{\delta t}=-(K_{b,v}U+K_{b,c})(T_b-T_w)`<br>[판독 기록:19](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p073-090.md:19) | [Transport/mod_heat.f90:924](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:924) `          TFLUX = ( HTBED1*USPD + HTBED2 )*( TEMB(L) - TEMP )*DELT   ! *** C*m`<br>[Transport/mod_heat.f90:978](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:978) `            TEMB(L) = TEMB(L) + ( RHOWCPI*RADBOTT(L) - FLUXTB(L) )/TBEDTHK(L)        ! *** Update bed temperature` | 일치 | 수주로 준 누적 FLUXTB를 저면 두께로 나누어 저면에서 뺀다. 대류·전도 교환의 부호는 같다. 저면에 흡수한 단파와 저면 장파를 추가한다. 누적 온도 변화가 0.001도보다 커질 때 적용한다. |
| <a id="eq-5-21"></a>[T12・PDF 80쪽・인쇄 67쪽・식 5.21](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=80)<br>`\rho_i L_f\frac{dh}{dt}=h_{ai}(T_i-T_e)-h_{wi}(T_w-T_m)`<br>[판독 기록:20](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p073-090.md:20) | [Transport/mod_heat.f90:1334](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:1334) `            RN = RT-RB-RE-RC                                    ! *** W/M2`<br>[Transport/mod_heat.f90:1335](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:1335) `            DEL = RN + ICEK*(TF-ICETEMP(L))/ICETHICK(L)         ! *** W/M2`<br>[Transport/mod_heat.f90:1387](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:1387) `            HICE    = ICEK*(TF-TICEBOT)/ICETHICK(L)         ! *** W/M2`<br>[Transport/mod_heat.f90:1388](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:1388) `            DICETHI = DELTICE*HICE*RHOILHFI`<br>[Transport/mod_heat.f90:1407](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:1407) `          HICE   = -HWI*TEM(L,KC)*RHOILHFI`<br>[Transport/mod_heat.f90:1408](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:1408) `          DICETHW = DELTICE*HICE` | 문서에만 | hai·(Ti−Te)의 단일 선형 공기–얼음식을 직접 평가하는 문장을 찾지 못했다. 코드가 복사·현열·잠열을 합해 얼음 온도를 구한 뒤 성장과 수중 융해를 나누어 갱신한다. |
| <a id="eq-5-22"></a>[T12・PDF 80쪽・인쇄 67쪽・식 5.22](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=80)<br>`\theta_0=-\frac{T_{wn}\rho_w c_{pw}h}{\rho_i L_f}`<br>[판독 기록:20](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p073-090.md:20) | [Transport/mod_heat.f90:1255](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:1255) `            if( TEM(L,K) <= TF )then`<br>[Transport/mod_heat.f90:1256](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:1256) `              WTEMP  = WTEMP  + (TF-TEM(L,K))*DZC(L,K)`<br>[Transport/mod_heat.f90:1257](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:1257) `            endif`<br>[Transport/mod_heat.f90:1258](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:1258) `          enddo`<br>[Transport/mod_heat.f90:1259](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:1259) `          WTEMP = WTEMP*DZIC(L,KC)`<br>[Transport/mod_heat.f90:1278](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:1278) `          TMPVAL  = WTEMP*RHOA*CP*THICKMIN`<br>[Transport/mod_heat.f90:1279](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:1279) `          DICETHW = TMPVAL*RHOILHFI` | 일치 | 빙점 아래 열량을 ρiLf로 나누어 초기 얼음 두께로 환산한다. 담수 TF=0일 때 문서의 −Twn과 같다. 염수에서는 TF−T를 사용한다. 과냉각층 합과 유효 두께 조건을 추가한다. |
| <a id="eq-5-23"></a>[T12・PDF 81쪽・인쇄 68쪽・식 5.23](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=81)<br>`T_s^n=\frac{\theta^{n-1}}{K_i}[H_{sn}^n+H_{an}^n-H_{br}(T_s^n)-H_c(T_s^n)]`<br>[판독 기록:21](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p073-090.md:21) | [Transport/mod_heat.f90:1333](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:1333) `            call SURFACE_TERMS (ICETEMP(L),L,RB,RC,RE)          ! *** OUT: RB,RC,RE`<br>[Transport/mod_heat.f90:1334](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:1334) `            RN = RT-RB-RE-RC                                    ! *** W/M2`<br>[Transport/mod_heat.f90:1335](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:1335) `            DEL = RN + ICEK*(TF-ICETEMP(L))/ICETHICK(L)         ! *** W/M2`<br>[Transport/mod_heat.f90:1336](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:1336) `            TMPVAL = DEL*ICETHICK(L)/10.`<br>[Transport/mod_heat.f90:1337](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:1337) `            if( ABS(TMPVAL) > 1. )then`<br>[Transport/mod_heat.f90:1338](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:1338) `              TMPVAL = SIGN(1.0,TMPVAL)`<br>[Transport/mod_heat.f90:1339](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:1339) `            endif`<br>[Transport/mod_heat.f90:1340](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:1340) `            ICETEMP(L) = ICETEMP(L) + TMPVAL`<br>[Transport/mod_heat.f90:1341](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:1341) `            if( ABS(TMPVAL) < 0.01 ) EXIT` | 불일치 | 문서 식에는 증발 He가 없다. 코드의 RN에는 −RE가 있다. 코드의 전도에는 TF를 포함한다. 코드가 잔차 DEL을 반복해 0으로 만들며 반복 증분을 ±1도로 제한한다. |
| <a id="eq-5-24"></a>[T12・PDF 81쪽・인쇄 68쪽・식 5.24](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=81)<br>`H_{sn}+H_{an}-H_{br}-H_e-H_c+q_i=\rho_i L_f\frac{d\theta_{ai}}{dt},\quad\text{for }T_s=0{}^\circ C`<br>[판독 기록:21](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p073-090.md:21) | [Transport/mod_heat.f90:1334](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:1334) `            RN = RT-RB-RE-RC                                    ! *** W/M2`<br>[Transport/mod_heat.f90:1335](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:1335) `            DEL = RN + ICEK*(TF-ICETEMP(L))/ICETHICK(L)         ! *** W/M2`<br>[Transport/mod_heat.f90:1375](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:1375) `            ICETEMP(L) = min(ICETEMP(L),0.001/ICETHICK(L))`<br>[Transport/mod_heat.f90:1376](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:1376) `            DICETHI = -CP*ICETEMP(L)*ICETHICK(L)*MELTFACTOR/LHF*ICESTEP/DELTICE` | 불일치 | 문서는 Ts=0에서 순 열 잔차를 곧바로 표면 융해율로 바꾼다. 코드는 양의 ICETEMP를 허용한 뒤 CP·ICETEMP·두께·MELTFACTOR/LHF로 융해량을 구한다. 같은 표면 Stefan 갱신문은 찾지 못했다. |
| <a id="eq-5-25"></a>[T12・PDF 81쪽・인쇄 68쪽・식 5.25](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=81)<br>`q_i=K_i\frac{T_f-T_s(t)}{\theta(t)}`<br>[판독 기록:21](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p073-090.md:21) | [Transport/mod_heat.f90:1335](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:1335) `            DEL = RN + ICEK*(TF-ICETEMP(L))/ICETHICK(L)         ! *** W/M2`<br>[Transport/mod_heat.f90:1387](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:1387) `            HICE    = ICEK*(TF-TICEBOT)/ICETHICK(L)         ! *** W/M2` | 일치 | ICEK·(TF−얼음 온도)/얼음 두께로 전도 열을 계산한다. 성장 분기에서는 얼음 온도에 수온을 더한 TICEBOT 보정을 적용한다. |
| <a id="eq-5-26"></a>[T12・PDF 81쪽・인쇄 68쪽・식 5.26](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=81)<br>`T_f=\begin{cases}-0.0545\ TDS,&TDS<35\ \mathrm{ppt}\\-0.3146-0.0417\ TDS-0.000166\ TDS^2,&TDS>35\ \mathrm{ppt}\end{cases}`<br>[판독 기록:21](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p073-090.md:21) | [Transport/mod_heat.f90:1178](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:1178) `        if( SAL(L,KC) < 35. )then`<br>[Transport/mod_heat.f90:1179](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:1179) `          TF = -0.0545*SAL(L,KC)`<br>[Transport/mod_heat.f90:1180](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:1180) `        else`<br>[Transport/mod_heat.f90:1181](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:1181) `          TF = -0.31462-0.04177*SAL(L,KC)-0.000166*SAL(L,KC)*SAL(L,KC)`<br>[Transport/mod_heat.f90:1182](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:1182) `        endif`<br>[Transport/mod_heat.f90:1183](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:1183) `      else`<br>[Transport/mod_heat.f90:1184](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:1184) `        TF = 0.0` | 불일치 | 문서는 TDS를 인자로 쓴다. 코드는 SAL을 쓴다. 고염분 계수는 문서 −0.3146·−0.0417이고 코드 −0.31462·−0.04177이다. SAL=35는 코드의 두 번째 분기에 들어간다. 문서는 35의 등호를 지정하지 않는다. |
| <a id="eq-5-27"></a>[T12・PDF 82쪽・인쇄 69쪽・식 5.27](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=82)<br>`\rho_i c_{pi}\frac{T_s(t)}{2}\theta(t)=\rho_i L_f\Delta\theta_{ai}`<br>[판독 기록:22](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p073-090.md:22) | [Transport/mod_heat.f90:40](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:40) `real, save, PRIVATE :: CP      = 4179.0                  ! *** SPECIFIC HEAT (J/KG/degC)    (Previously EFDC used 4179.0)   4184`<br>[Transport/mod_heat.f90:1375](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:1375) `            ICETEMP(L) = min(ICETEMP(L),0.001/ICETHICK(L))`<br>[Transport/mod_heat.f90:1376](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:1376) `            DICETHI = -CP*ICETEMP(L)*ICETHICK(L)*MELTFACTOR/LHF*ICESTEP/DELTICE` | 불일치 | 문서는 얼음 비열 cpi·Ts·두께/(2Lf)이다. 코드는 물 비열 CP=4179와 MELTFACTOR를 곱한다. 1/2는 코드의 고정 계수가 아니다. 코드 융해량 부호는 음수이다. |
| <a id="eq-5-28"></a>[T12・PDF 82쪽・인쇄 69쪽・식 5.28](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=82)<br>`q_i-q_{iw}=\rho_i L_f\frac{d\theta_{iw}}{dt}`<br>[판독 기록:22](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p073-090.md:22) | [Transport/mod_heat.f90:1386](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:1386) `            TICEBOT = min(ICETEMP(L) + TEM(L,KC),0.0)       ! *** ADJUST ICE TEMPERATURE FOR WATER CONTACT`<br>[Transport/mod_heat.f90:1387](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:1387) `            HICE    = ICEK*(TF-TICEBOT)/ICETHICK(L)         ! *** W/M2`<br>[Transport/mod_heat.f90:1388](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:1388) `            DICETHI = DELTICE*HICE*RHOILHFI`<br>[Transport/mod_heat.f90:1405](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:1405) `        if( TEM(L,KC) > 0.0 )then`<br>[Transport/mod_heat.f90:1406](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:1406) `          ! *** MELT`<br>[Transport/mod_heat.f90:1407](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:1407) `          HICE   = -HWI*TEM(L,KC)*RHOILHFI`<br>[Transport/mod_heat.f90:1408](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:1408) `          DICETHW = DELTICE*HICE` | 불일치 | 전도 성장과 수중 융해를 별도 조건에서 계산한다. 수중 융해는 TEM>0에서 HWI·TEM을 사용한다. 문서의 qi−qiw를 모든 온도에서 동시에 평가하지 않는다. |
| <a id="eq-5-29"></a>[T12・PDF 82쪽・인쇄 69쪽・식 5.29](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=82)<br>`\Delta\theta_{iw}^n=\frac{1}{\rho_i L_f}\left[K_i\frac{T_f-T_s^n}{\theta^{n-1}}-h_{wi}(T_w^n-T_f)\right]`<br>[판독 기록:22](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p073-090.md:22) | [Transport/mod_heat.f90:1387](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:1387) `            HICE    = ICEK*(TF-TICEBOT)/ICETHICK(L)         ! *** W/M2`<br>[Transport/mod_heat.f90:1388](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:1388) `            DICETHI = DELTICE*HICE*RHOILHFI`<br>[Transport/mod_heat.f90:1407](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:1407) `          HICE   = -HWI*TEM(L,KC)*RHOILHFI`<br>[Transport/mod_heat.f90:1408](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:1408) `          DICETHW = DELTICE*HICE` | 불일치 | 문서의 Δθ 식에는 시간 간격이 없다. 코드는 DELTICE를 곱한다. 코드의 수중 융해는 Tw−TF 대신 Tw를 사용한다. 전도 성장의 TICEBOT도 문서 Ts와 다르다. |
| <a id="eq-5-30"></a>[T12・PDF 82쪽・인쇄 69쪽・식 5.30](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=82)<br>`H_{ps}=H_s(1-\alpha_i)(1-\beta_i)\exp[-\gamma_i\theta(t)]`<br>[판독 기록:22](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p073-090.md:22) | [Transport/mod_heat.f90:1666](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:1666) `    SOLBOT = EXP(-GAMMAI*ICETHICK(L))`<br>[Transport/mod_heat.f90:1667](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:1667) `    BETAI = 1.0 - SOLBOT`<br>[Transport/mod_heat.f90:1668](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:1668) `    REFICE  = (1. - ALBEDOI)/(1.-REFL)*SOLBOT`<br>[Transport/mod_heat.f90:1669](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:1669) `    RADTOP(L,KC) = SOLSWRT(L)*REFICE` | 불일치 | 코드의 투과율은 (1−ALBEDOI)·exp(−GAMMAI·두께)이다. 코드는 BETAI=1−exp(−GAMMAI·두께)로 덮어쓴다. 문서의 추가 (1−βi)를 다시 곱하지 않는다. |
| <a id="eq-5-31"></a>[T12・PDF 83쪽・인쇄 70쪽・식 5.31](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=83)<br>`\Delta z=E\Delta t=\frac{H_E}{\rho L_E}\Delta t`<br>[판독 기록:23](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p073-090.md:23) | [Transport/mod_heat.f90:735](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:735) `          EVACOARE(L) = hlb/Le_/1000. !*** evap rate m/s`<br>[Transport/calqvs.f90:1644](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/calqvs.f90:1644) `          CLEVAPTMP  = WINDFA + WINDFB*WINDST(L) + WINDFC*WINDST(L)**2`<br>[Transport/calqvs.f90:1645](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/calqvs.f90:1645) `          EVAPT(L)   = CLEVAPTMP*(SVPW(L)-VPAT(L))`<br>[Transport/calqvs.f90:1646](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/calqvs.f90:1646) `          QSUM(L,KC) = QSUM(L,KC) + DXYP(L)*(RAINT(L)-EVAPT(L))`<br>[input.f90:2717](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/input.f90:2717) `    ! *** Latent Heat of Evaporation = 2259 KJ/KG`<br>[input.f90:2718](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/input.f90:2718) `    ! *** Density of Water           = 1000 kg/m3`<br>[input.f90:2719](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/input.f90:2719) `    WINDFA = WINDFA*4.42674E-10`<br>[input.f90:2720](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/input.f90:2720) `    WINDFB = WINDFB*4.42674E-10` | 일치 | COARE는 hlb/(Le·1000)로 증발 속도를 만든다. 다항 풍속 경로는 1/(1000·2.259E6)으로 계수를 환산한다. 코드의 수심 갱신은 QSUM에서 증발 유량을 뺀다. 서로 다른 잠열을 쓰는 선택 경로를 같은 기본값으로 묶지 않았다. |
| <a id="eq-5-32"></a>[T12・PDF 83쪽・인쇄 70쪽・식 5.32](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=83)<br>`H_E=f(W)(e_s-e_a)`<br>[판독 기록:23](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p073-090.md:23) | [Transport/calqvs.f90:1644](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/calqvs.f90:1644) `          CLEVAPTMP  = WINDFA + WINDFB*WINDST(L) + WINDFC*WINDST(L)**2`<br>[Transport/calqvs.f90:1645](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/calqvs.f90:1645) `          EVAPT(L)   = CLEVAPTMP*(SVPW(L)-VPAT(L))`<br>[Transport/mod_heat.f90:1712](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:1712) `    FW = AFWI+BFWI*WINDST(L)**CFWI`<br>[Transport/mod_heat.f90:1713](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:1713) `  endif`<br>[Transport/mod_heat.f90:1715](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:1715) `  ! *** EVAPORATIVE HEAT LOSS, (W/M2)`<br>[Transport/mod_heat.f90:1716](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:1716) `  RE = FW*(VPS-VPA)` | 일치 | 증발량 계산에는 수증기압 차를 곱한 속도 단위 계수를 사용한다. SURFACE_TERMS에는 열 단위 FW·(VPS−VPA)를 사용한다. |
| <a id="eq-5-33"></a>[T12・PDF 83쪽・인쇄 70쪽・식 5.33](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=83)<br>`f(W)=a+bW+cW^2`<br>[판독 기록:23](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p073-090.md:23) | [Transport/calqvs.f90:1644](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/calqvs.f90:1644) `          CLEVAPTMP  = WINDFA + WINDFB*WINDST(L) + WINDFC*WINDST(L)**2`<br>[input.f90:2693](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/input.f90:2693) `      read(1,*,IOSTAT = ISO) DS_LONG, DS_LAT, COMPUTESOLRAD, USESHADE, IEVAP, WINDFA, WINDFB, WINDFC, PBLZ, TEM_HRZ, COARE_NITS`<br>[input.f90:2719](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/input.f90:2719) `    WINDFA = WINDFA*4.42674E-10`<br>[input.f90:2720](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/input.f90:2720) `    WINDFB = WINDFB*4.42674E-10` | 일치 | IEVAP=3..10에서 상수·풍속·풍속²의 합이다. a·b·c는 C46C 입력이다. IEVAP 번호만 보고 Table 5.2의 계수를 자동 대입하는 코드는 확인하지 못했다. |

(G/M2/S)`<br>[SedTran-Original/calsed.f90:268](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsed.f90:268) `          SEDF(L,K,NS) = 0.` | 일치 | 최상단 침강 플럭스를 0으로 둔다. 수직 확산의 수면 경계와 합치면 총 무플럭스 조건이다. |
| <a id="eq-6-4"></a>[T12・PDF 88쪽・인쇄 75쪽・식 6.4](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=88)<br>`\begin{aligned}H^{n+1}C^*=H^nC^n+\frac{\Delta t}{m_xm_y}(S_s^E)^{n+\frac12}\\-\frac{\Delta t}{m_xm_y}\left[\frac{\partial}{\partial x}\left(m_y(Hu)^{n+\frac12}C^n\right)+\frac{\partial}{\partial y}\left(m_x(Hv)^{n+\frac12}C^n\right)+\frac{\partial}{\partial z}\left(m_xm_yw^{n+\frac12}C^n\right)\right]\end{aligned}`<br>[판독 기록:28](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p073-090.md:28) | [Transport/caltran.f90:169](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/caltran.f90:169) `        CD(L,K,IT) = CON1(L,K)*H1PK(L,K) + DDELT*( ( FQC(L,K,IT) +                                                                      &`<br>[Transport/caltran.f90:170](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/caltran.f90:170) `                                                     FUHUD(L,K,IW)-FUHUD(LEC(L),K,IW) + FVHUD(L,K,IW)-FVHUD(LNC(L),K,IW) ) * DXYIP(L)   &`<br>[Transport/caltran.f90:171](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/caltran.f90:171) `                                                  + (FWUU(L,K-1,IW)-FWUU(L,K,IW)) ) ` | 일치 | 2시각 이류 단계에서 이전 HC에 원천을 더하고 유출 플럭스 발산을 뺀다. 고차 보정은 별도 선택이다. |
| <a id="eq-6-5"></a>[T12・PDF 88쪽・인쇄 75쪽・식 6.5](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=88)<br>`C^{**}=C^*+\frac{\Delta t}{H^{n+1}}\frac{\partial}{\partial z}(w_sC^{**})`<br>[판독 기록:28](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p073-090.md:28) | [SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:401](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:401) `            CLEFT = 1.+WSETA(L,K-1,NS)*WVEL  `<br>[SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:402](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:402) `            CRIGHT = max(SND(L,K,NX),0.)-SNDF(L,K,NX)*WVEL  `<br>[SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:403](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:403) `            SND(L,K,NX) = CRIGHT/CLEFT  `<br>[SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:404](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:404) `            SNDF(L,K-1,NX) = -WSETA(L,K-1,NS)*SND(L,K,NX)  ` | 일치 | 현재 농도와 상층의 이미 갱신한 농도로 침강을 암시적으로 계산한다. 양의 침강속도는 아래쪽 플럭스에 음수로 들어간다. |
| <a id="eq-6-6"></a>[T12・PDF 88쪽・인쇄 75쪽・식 6.6](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=88)<br>`C_{KC}^{**}=C_{KC}^*+\frac{\Delta t}{\Delta_zH^{n+1}}(w_sC^{**})_{KC}`<br>[판독 기록:28](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p073-090.md:28) | [SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:390](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:390) `          CLEFT = 1.+WSETA(L,K-1,NS)*WVEL  `<br>[SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:391](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:391) `          CRIGHT = max(SND(L,K,NX),0.)  `<br>[SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:392](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:392) `          SND(L,K,NX) = CRIGHT/CLEFT  `<br>[SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:393](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:393) `          SNDF(L,K-1,NX) = -WSETA(L,K-1,NS)*SND(L,K,NX)  `<br>[SedTran-Original/calsed.f90:270](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsed.f90:270) `          CLEFT = 1. + WSETA(L,K-1,NS)*WVEL`<br>[SedTran-Original/calsed.f90:271](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsed.f90:271) `          CRIGHT = max(SED(L,K,NS),0.)`<br>[SedTran-Original/calsed.f90:272](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsed.f90:272) `          SED(L,K,NS) = CRIGHT/CLEFT`<br>[SedTran-Original/calsed.f90:273](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsed.f90:273) `          SEDF(L,K-1,NS) = -WSETA(L,K-1,NS)*SED(L,K,NS)` | 불일치 | 문서는 최상층의 양의 침강속도 항을 더한다. 코드는 1+w_s Δt/H_k로 나누어 최상층의 질량을 감소시킨다. PDF 이미지의 더하기 부호를 확인했다. |
| <a id="eq-6-7"></a>[T12・PDF 88쪽・인쇄 75쪽・식 6.7](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=88)<br>`C_k^{**}=C_k^*+\frac{\Delta t}{\Delta_1H^{n+1}}(w_sC^{**})_{k+1}-\frac{\Delta t}{\Delta_kH^{n+1}}(w_sC^{**})_k\quad\text{for }2\leq k\leq KC-1`<br>[판독 기록:28](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p073-090.md:28) | [SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:401](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:401) `            CLEFT = 1.+WSETA(L,K-1,NS)*WVEL  `<br>[SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:402](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:402) `            CRIGHT = max(SND(L,K,NX),0.)-SNDF(L,K,NX)*WVEL  `<br>[SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:403](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:403) `            SND(L,K,NX) = CRIGHT/CLEFT  `<br>[SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:404](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:404) `            SNDF(L,K-1,NX) = -WSETA(L,K-1,NS)*SND(L,K,NX)  ` | 불일치 | 문서는 상층 유입항을 Δ_1 H로 나눈다. 코드는 유입항과 유출항을 모두 현재 층 두께 HPK(L,K)로 나눈다. 비균등 층에서 계수가 달라진다. 코드의 K 인터페이스가 상층 유입에 대응한다. |
| <a id="eq-6-8"></a>[T12・PDF 88쪽・인쇄 75쪽・식 6.8](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=88)<br>`C_1^{**}=C_1^*+\frac{\Delta t}{\Delta_zH^{n+1}}(w_sC^{**})_2`<br>[판독 기록:28](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p073-090.md:28) | [SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:528](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:528) `        CRIGHT = max(SND(L,KSZ(L),NX),0.) + ( WESE-SNDF(L,KSZ(L),NX) )*WVEL  `<br>[SedTran-Original/calsed.f90:420](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsed.f90:420) `        CRIGHT = max(SED(L,KSZ(L),NS),0.) + (WESE-SEDF(L,KSZ(L),NS))*WVEL` | 일치 | 최하층의 상층 유입을 현재 최하층 두께로 나누어 더한다. 코드는 이 항과 바닥 교환항을 같은 암시적 갱신에 합친다. |
| <a id="eq-6-9"></a>[T12・PDF 89쪽・인쇄 76쪽・식 6.9](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=89)<br>`C_1^{***}=C_1^{**}+\frac{\Delta t}{\Delta_zH^{n+1}}L_oJ_o^{***}`<br>[판독 기록:29](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p073-090.md:29) | [SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:527](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:527) `        CLEFT  = 1. + WSETMP*WVEL  `<br>[SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:528](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:528) `        CRIGHT = max(SND(L,KSZ(L),NX),0.) + ( WESE-SNDF(L,KSZ(L),NX) )*WVEL  `<br>[SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:529](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:529) `        SND(L,KSZ(L),NX) = CRIGHT/CLEFT  `<br>[SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:530](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:530) `        SNDF(L,0,NX) = -WSETMP*SND(L,KSZ(L),NX) + WESE  `<br>[SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:563](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:563) `            SNDF(L,0,NX)     = SNDB(L,KBT(L),NX)/DTSED`<br>[SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:564](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:564) `            SND(L,KSZ(L),NX) = SNDS(L,KSZ(L),NX) + (SNDF(L,0,NX) - SNDF(L,KSZ(L),NX))*WVEL  ` | 일치 | 바닥 교환 플럭스를 최하층 질량에 더한다. 가용 바닥 질량을 초과하면 플럭스를 가용 질량/Δt로 제한한다. L_o를 독립 변수로 저장하지 않는다. |
| <a id="eq-6-10"></a>[T12・PDF 89쪽・인쇄 76쪽・식 6.10](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=89)<br>`J_0^{***}=w_s(C_{eq}-C_1^{***})`<br>[판독 기록:29](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p073-090.md:29) | [SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:509](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:509) `        WESE = WSFAC*WSETA(L,0,NS)*SNDEQ(L)  `<br>[SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:512](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:512) `        WSETMP = WSFAC*WSETA(L,0,NS)  `<br>[SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:530](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:530) `        SNDF(L,0,NX) = -WSETMP*SND(L,KSZ(L),NX) + WESE  ` | 일치 | 다층에서 WSFAC=1일 때 w_s(Ceq−C)를 구현한다. 단층에서는 식 (6.63)의 WSFAC을 곱한다. |
| <a id="eq-6-11"></a>[T12・PDF 89쪽・인쇄 76쪽・식 6.11](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=89)<br>`J_0^{***}=-P_dw_sC_1^{***}`<br>[판독 기록:29](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p073-090.md:29) | [SedTran-Original/calsed.f90:415](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsed.f90:415) `          WSETMP = PROBDEP*WSETA(L,0,NS)`<br>[SedTran-Original/calsed.f90:422](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsed.f90:422) `        SEDF(L,0,NS)     = -WSETMP*SED(L,KSZ(L),NS) + WESE` | 일치 | 퇴적항이 −P_d w_s C이다. 양의 WESE 침식항은 별도로 더한다. 프로펠러 세굴 중에는 퇴적을 끈다. |
| <a id="eq-6-12"></a>[T12・PDF 89쪽・인쇄 76쪽・식 6.12](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=89)<br>`C^{n+1}=C^{***}+\Delta t\frac{\partial}{\partial z}\left[\left(\frac{A_b}{H^2}\right)^{n+1}\frac{\partial}{\partial z}C^{n+1}\right]`<br>[판독 기록:29](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p073-090.md:29) | [Transport/calconc.f90:366](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/calconc.f90:366) `          CCLBTMP(L,K) = RCDZKMK*HPI(L)*AB(L,K-1)`<br>[Transport/calconc.f90:367](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/calconc.f90:367) `          CCUBTMP      = RCDZKK*HPI(L)*AB(L,K)`<br>[Transport/calconc.f90:368](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/calconc.f90:368) `          CCMBTMP      = 1._8-CCLBTMP(L,K)-CCUBTMP`<br>[Transport/calconc.f90:369](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/calconc.f90:369) `          EEB(L,K)     = 1._8/( CCMBTMP - CCLBTMP(L,K)*VCU(L,K-1) )` | 일치 | 층 두께와 AB의 변환을 반영한 삼중 대각 계수로 수직 확산을 암시적으로 푼다. |
| <a id="eq-6-13"></a>[T12・PDF 90쪽・인쇄 77쪽・식 6.13](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=90)<br>`w_{sj}=w_{soj}`<br>[판독 기록:30](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p073-090.md:30) | [SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:235](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:235) `            WSETA(L,K,NS) = WSEDO(NS)  ` | 일치 | ISNDVW=0 경로에서 지정한 WSEDO를 사용한다. 농도 의존 옵션을 켜면 식 (6.17)의 처리를 적용한다. |
| <a id="eq-6-14"></a>[T12・PDF 91쪽・인쇄 78쪽・식 6.14](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=91)<br>`w_{soj}=\sqrt{g'd_j}\begin{cases}\dfrac{R_{dj}}{18},&d\leq100\,\mu\mathrm{m}\\\dfrac{10}{R_{dj}}\left(\sqrt{1+0.01R_{dj}^{2}}-1\right),&100\mu\mathrm{m}<d_j\leq1000\,\mu\mathrm{m}\\1.1,&d_j>1000\mu\mathrm{m}\end{cases}`<br>[판독 기록:13](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p091-108.md:13) | [SedTran-Original/setstvel.f90:28](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/setstvel.f90:28) `  if( D < 1.0E-4 )then`<br>[SedTran-Original/setstvel.f90:29](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/setstvel.f90:29) `    WSET = SQGPD*RD/18.`<br>[SedTran-Original/setstvel.f90:30](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/setstvel.f90:30) `  endif`<br>[SedTran-Original/setstvel.f90:31](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/setstvel.f90:31) `  if( D >= 1.0E-4 .and. D < 1.E-3 )then`<br>[SedTran-Original/setstvel.f90:32](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/setstvel.f90:32) `    TMP = SQRT(1.+0.01*RD*RD)-1.`<br>[SedTran-Original/setstvel.f90:33](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/setstvel.f90:33) `    WSET = 10.0*SQGPD*TMP/RD`<br>[SedTran-Original/setstvel.f90:34](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/setstvel.f90:34) `  endif`<br>[SedTran-Original/setstvel.f90:35](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/setstvel.f90:35) `  if( D >= 1.E-3 )then`<br>[SedTran-Original/setstvel.f90:36](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/setstvel.f90:36) `    WSET = 1.1*SQGPD` | 불일치 | 세 분기의 계수는 같다. 문서는 100 μm를 첫 분기에 넣고 1000 μm를 둘째 분기에 넣는다. 코드는 각각 둘째 분기와 셋째 분기에 넣는다. |
| <a id="eq-6-15"></a>[T12・PDF 91쪽・인쇄 78쪽・식 6.15](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=91)<br>`g'=g\left(\dfrac{\rho_{sj}}{\rho_w}-1\right)`<br>[판독 기록:13](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p091-108.md:13) | [SedTran-Original/setstvel.f90:21](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/setstvel.f90:21) `  GP = (SSG-1.)*9.82` | 일치 | SSG=ρ_s/ρ_w로 놓으면 같다. 코드는 g=9.82를 사용한다. |
| <a id="eq-6-16"></a>[T12・PDF 91쪽・인쇄 78쪽・식 6.16](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=91)<br>`R_{dj}=\dfrac{d_j\sqrt{g'd_j}}{\nu}`<br>[판독 기록:13](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p091-108.md:13) | [SedTran-Original/setstvel.f90:20](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/setstvel.f90:20) `  VISC = 1.D-6`<br>[SedTran-Original/setstvel.f90:22](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/setstvel.f90:22) `  GPD = GP*D`<br>[SedTran-Original/setstvel.f90:23](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/setstvel.f90:23) `  SQGPD = SQRT(GPD)`<br>[SedTran-Original/setstvel.f90:24](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/setstvel.f90:24) `  RD = SQGPD*D/VISC` | 일치 | d√(g′d)/ν를 계산한다. 코드는 ν=10^−6 m²/s를 고정한다. |
| <a id="eq-6-17"></a>[T12・PDF 91쪽・인쇄 78쪽・식 6.17](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=91)<br>`w_{sj}=\left(1-\displaystyle\sum_i^I\dfrac{C_i}{\rho_{si}}\right)^n w_{soj}`<br>[판독 기록:13](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p091-108.md:13) | [SedTran-Original/csndset.f90:20](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/csndset.f90:20) `  ROPT = FLOAT(IOPT)`<br>[SedTran-Original/csndset.f90:21](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/csndset.f90:21) `  CSNDSET = (1.-SDEN*SND)**ROPT`<br>[SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:244](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:244) `            WSETA(L,K,NS) = WSEDO(NS)*CSNDSET(SNDT(L,K+1),SDEN(NS),ISNDVW)  ` | 불일치 | 문서는 각 계급의 C_i/ρ_si를 합한다. 코드는 전체 비점착성 농도 SNDT에 현재 계급의 밀도 역수 SDEN(NS)를 곱한다. 계급 밀도가 같으면 두 합이 같다. 지수는 정수 ISNDVW이다. |
| <a id="eq-6-18"></a>[T12・PDF 91쪽・인쇄 78쪽・식 6.18](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=91)<br>`w_{sj}=\left(1-n\displaystyle\sum_i^I\dfrac{C_i}{\rho_{si}}\right)w_{soj}`<br>[판독 기록:13](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p091-108.md:13) | [SedTran-Original/csndset.f90:21](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/csndset.f90:21) `  CSNDSET = (1.-SDEN*SND)**ROPT` | 문서에만 | 식 (6.17)의 선형 근사이다. 코드는 선형 근사를 선택하는 분기를 제공하지 않고 거듭제곱을 계산한다. |
| <a id="eq-6-19"></a>[T12・PDF 91쪽・인쇄 78쪽・식 6.19](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=91)<br>`\theta_{csj}=\dfrac{\tau_{csj}}{g'd_j}=\dfrac{u_{*csj}^{2}}{g'd_j}=f\left(R_{dj}\right)`<br>[판독 기록:13](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p091-108.md:13) | [SedTran-Original/setshld.f90:48](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/setshld.f90:48) `  TSC = GPD*THETA`<br>[SedTran-Original/setshld.f90:49](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/setshld.f90:49) `  USC = SQRT(TSC)` | 일치 | 정규화 임계 응력 TSC=g′d θ와 USC=√TSC가 정의식에 대응한다. 응력은 물 밀도로 정규화한 단위이다. |
| <a id="eq-6-20"></a>[T12・PDF 92쪽・인쇄 79쪽・식 6.20](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=92)<br>`\theta_{csj}=\begin{cases}0.24\left(R_{dj}^{2/3}\right)^{-1}&\text{for }R_{dj}^{2/3}<4\\0.14\left(R_{dj}^{2/3}\right)^{-0.64}&\text{for }4\leq R_{dj}^{2/3}<10\\0.04\left(R_{dj}^{2/3}\right)^{-0.1}&\text{for }10\leq R_{dj}^{2/3}<20\\0.013\left(R_{dj}^{2/3}\right)^{0.29}&\text{for }20\leq R_{dj}^{2/3}<150\\0.055&\text{for }150\leq R_{dj}^{2/3}\end{cases}`<br>[판독 기록:14](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p091-108.md:14) | [SedTran-Original/setshld.f90:33](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/setshld.f90:33) `  if(DSR <= 4.0 )then`<br>[SedTran-Original/setshld.f90:34](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/setshld.f90:34) `    THETA = 0.24/DSR`<br>[SedTran-Original/setshld.f90:35](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/setshld.f90:35) `  endif`<br>[SedTran-Original/setshld.f90:36](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/setshld.f90:36) `  if(DSR > 4.0 .and. DSR <= 10.0 )then`<br>[SedTran-Original/setshld.f90:37](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/setshld.f90:37) `    THETA = 0.14/(DSR**0.64)`<br>[SedTran-Original/setshld.f90:38](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/setshld.f90:38) `  endif`<br>[SedTran-Original/setshld.f90:39](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/setshld.f90:39) `  if(DSR > 10.0 .and. DSR <= 20.0 )then`<br>[SedTran-Original/setshld.f90:40](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/setshld.f90:40) `    THETA = 0.04/(DSR**0.1)`<br>[SedTran-Original/setshld.f90:41](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/setshld.f90:41) `  endif`<br>[SedTran-Original/setshld.f90:42](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/setshld.f90:42) `  if(DSR > 20.0 .and. DSR <= 150.0 )then`<br>[SedTran-Original/setshld.f90:43](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/setshld.f90:43) `    THETA = 0.013*(DSR**0.29)`<br>[SedTran-Original/setshld.f90:44](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/setshld.f90:44) `  endif`<br>[SedTran-Original/setshld.f90:45](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/setshld.f90:45) `  if(DSR > 150.0 )then`<br>[SedTran-Original/setshld.f90:46](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/setshld.f90:46) `    THETA = 0.055` | 불일치 | 계수와 지수는 같다. 코드는 4·10·20·150의 경계값을 낮은 구간에 넣는다. 문서는 높은 구간에 넣는다. 코드의 0.333333은 1/3의 근사이다. |
| <a id="eq-6-21"></a>[T12・PDF 92쪽・인쇄 79쪽・식 6.21](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=92)<br>`u_* =\sqrt{\tau_b}`<br>[판독 기록:14](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p091-108.md:14) | [SedTran-Original/csndeqc.f90:24](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/csndeqc.f90:24) `  !     TAUR   = WATER DENSITY NORMALIZED CRITICAL SHIELDS STRESS`<br>[SedTran-Original/csndeqc.f90:25](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/csndeqc.f90:25) `  !     TAUB   = WATER DENSITY NORMALIZED BED STRESS`<br>[SedTran-Original/csndeqc.f90:36](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/csndeqc.f90:36) `  USTAR = SQRT(TAUB)` | 일치 | 코드는 물 밀도로 정규화한 TAUB의 제곱근을 사용한다. Pa 단위 응력의 제곱근으로 해석하면 안 된다. |
| <a id="eq-6-22"></a>[T12・PDF 92쪽・인쇄 79쪽・식 6.22](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=92)<br>`u_{*csj}=\sqrt{\tau_{csj}}=\sqrt{g'd_j\theta_{csj}}`<br>[판독 기록:14](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p091-108.md:14) | [SedTran-Original/setshld.f90:48](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/setshld.f90:48) `  TSC = GPD*THETA`<br>[SedTran-Original/setshld.f90:49](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/setshld.f90:49) `  USC = SQRT(TSC)` | 일치 | 임계 응력과 임계 마찰속도를 g′d θ 및 그 제곱근으로 계산한다. |
| <a id="eq-6-23"></a>[T12・PDF 92쪽・인쇄 79쪽・식 6.23](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=92)<br>`u_{*csj}<u_*<w_{soj}`<br>[판독 기록:14](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p091-108.md:14) | [SedTran-Original/csndeqc.f90:38](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/csndeqc.f90:38) `  if( USTAR < WS )then`<br>[SedTran-Original/csndeqc.f90:39](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/csndeqc.f90:39) `     CSNDEQC = 0.`<br>[SedTran-Original/bedload.f90:94](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/bedload.f90:94) `            if( SBDLDG1(NX)*SHIELDS > SBDLDG2(NX)*CSHIELDS )then`<br>[SedTran-Original/fsedmode.f90:64](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/fsedmode.f90:64) `  elseif( ISNDM1 == 3 )then`<br>[SedTran-Original/fsedmode.f90:65](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/fsedmode.f90:65) `    ! *** ISNDM1 = 3 use BINARY RELATIONSHIP FOR BEDLOAD AND SUSPENDED LOAD`<br>[SedTran-Original/fsedmode.f90:66](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/fsedmode.f90:66) `    FSEDMODE = 0.`<br>[SedTran-Original/fsedmode.f90:67](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/fsedmode.f90:67) `    if( IMODE == 1 )then`<br>[SedTran-Original/fsedmode.f90:68](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/fsedmode.f90:68) `      if( USDWS < RSNDM ) FSEDMODE = 1.`<br>[SedTran-Original/fsedmode.f90:69](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/fsedmode.f90:69) `    else`<br>[SedTran-Original/fsedmode.f90:70](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/fsedmode.f90:70) `      if( USDWS >= RSNDM ) FSEDMODE = 1.` | 일치 | ISNDM1=3에서 RSNDM=1을 지정하면 u*<w_s일 때 소류사만 허용한다. BEDLOAD의 Shields 임계 검사가 하한 조건을 제공한다. 다른 ISNDM1·RSNDM은 이 분류와 다르다. |
| <a id="eq-6-24"></a>[T12・PDF 93쪽・인쇄 80쪽・식 6.24](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=93)<br>`\dfrac{q_B}{\rho_s d\sqrt{g'd}}=\Phi\left(\theta,\theta_{cs}\right)`<br>[판독 기록:15](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p091-108.md:15) | [SedTran-Original/bedload.f90:90](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/bedload.f90:90) `          BDLDTMP = SQRT(GPDIASED)*DIASED/DSEDGMM`<br>[SedTran-Original/bedload.f90:105](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/bedload.f90:105) `              QSBDLDP(L) = FACBEDL(L)*VFRBED(L,KBT(L),NS)*BDLDTMP*BDLDTMPP*BDLDTMPA*BDLDTMPB     ! *** (G/M/S)` | 일치 | 입자 밀도·입경·√g′d를 곱해 차원 있는 소류사 플럭스를 만든다. FACBEDL과 VFRBED는 수송 분율과 계급 체적 분율이다. |
| <a id="eq-6-25"></a>[T12・PDF 93쪽・인쇄 80쪽・식 6.25](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=93)<br>`\theta=\dfrac{\tau_b}{g'd_j}=\dfrac{u_*^2}{g'd_j}`<br>[판독 기록:15](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p091-108.md:15) | [SedTran-Original/bedload.f90:93](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/bedload.f90:93) `            SHIELDS = TAUBSND(L)/GPDIASED` | 일치 | TAUBSND/(g′d)를 사용한다. TAUBSND는 물 밀도로 정규화한 입자 응력이다. |
| <a id="eq-6-26"></a>[T12・PDF 94쪽・인쇄 81쪽・식 6.26](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=94)<br>`\Phi\left(\theta,\theta_{cs}\right)=\phi\left(\theta-\theta_{cs}\right)^\alpha\left(\sqrt{\theta}-\gamma\sqrt{\theta_{cs}}\right)^\beta`<br>[판독 기록:16](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p091-108.md:16) | [SedTran-Original/bedload.f90:96](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/bedload.f90:96) `                BDLDTMPA = (SBDLDG1(NX)*SHIELDS - SBDLDG2(NX)*CSHIELDS)**SBDLDA(NX)`<br>[SedTran-Original/bedload.f90:101](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/bedload.f90:101) `                BDLDTMPB = (SBDLDG3(NX)*SQRT(SHIELDS)-SBDLDG4(NX)*SQRT(CSHIELDS))**SBDLDB(NX)`<br>[SedTran-Original/bedload.f90:105](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/bedload.f90:105) `              QSBDLDP(L) = FACBEDL(L)*VFRBED(L,KBT(L),NS)*BDLDTMP*BDLDTMPP*BDLDTMPA*BDLDTMPB     ! *** (G/M/S)` | 일치 | ISBDLD=0의 SBDLDG1=G2=G3=1, G4=γ에서 같은 두 거듭제곱을 곱한다. 지수와 φ는 입력값이다. |
| <a id="eq-6-27"></a>[T12・PDF 94쪽・인쇄 81쪽・식 6.27](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=94)<br>`\phi=\dfrac{0.053}{R_d^{1/5}\theta_{cs}^{2.1}}`<br>[판독 기록:16](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p091-108.md:16) | [SedTran-Original/fsbdld.f90:34](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/fsbdld.f90:34) `    RD  = DIASED*SQRT(GPDIASED)*1.E6`<br>[SedTran-Original/fsbdld.f90:35](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/fsbdld.f90:35) `    RD  = (1./RD)**0.2`<br>[SedTran-Original/fsbdld.f90:36](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/fsbdld.f90:36) `    TMP = CSHIELDS**2.1`<br>[SedTran-Original/fsbdld.f90:37](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/fsbdld.f90:37) `    FSBDLD = 0.053*RD/TMP` | 일치 | ISBDLD=1에서 R_d^−0.2와 θ_cs^−2.1 및 0.053을 곱한다. |
| <a id="eq-6-28"></a>[T12・PDF 94쪽・인쇄 81쪽・식 6.28](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=94)<br>`\Phi=\phi\left(\theta-\theta_{cs}\right)^{2.1}`<br>[판독 기록:16](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p091-108.md:16) | [SedTran-Original/bedload.f90:154](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/bedload.f90:154) `          BDLDTMPA = (SBDLDG1(NX)*SHIELDS -SBDLDG2(NX)*CSHIELDSC)**SBDLDA(NX)`<br>[SedTran-Original/bedload.f90:155](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/bedload.f90:155) `          ! ***           (-)       (-)                G/M/S    (-)       (-) `<br>[SedTran-Original/bedload.f90:156](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/bedload.f90:156) `          QSBDLDP(L) = FACBEDL(L)*VFRBED(L,KBT(L),NS)*BDLDTMP*BDLDTMPP*BDLDTMPA                               ! *** G/M/S`<br>[input.f90:2245](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/input.f90:2245) `        read(1,*,IOSTAT = ISO)ICALC_BL,SBDLDA(NX),SBDLDB(NX),SBDLDG1(NX),SBDLDG2(NX),SBDLDG3(NX),SBDLDG4(NX),SBDLDP(NX),ISBLFUC,BLBSNT` | 일치 | ISBDLD=1, SBDLDA=2.1, G1=G2=1에서 일치한다. 코드는 α를 입력으로 받는다. 숨김·노출 보정은 추가 선택이다. |
| <a id="eq-6-29"></a>[T12・PDF 94쪽・인쇄 81쪽・식 6.29](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=94)<br>`\Phi=\phi\left(\theta\right)^{2.1}\left(\sqrt{\theta}\right)^\beta`<br>[판독 기록:16](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p091-108.md:16) | [SedTran-Original/bedload.f90:192](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/bedload.f90:192) `          BDLDTMPA = (SBDLDG1(NX)*SHIELDS)**SBDLDA(NX)`<br>[SedTran-Original/bedload.f90:193](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/bedload.f90:193) `          QSBDLDP(L) = FACBEDL(L)*VFRBED(L,KBT(L),NS)*BDLDTMP*BDLDTMPP*BDLDTMPA` | 불일치 | 문서는 θ^2.1(√θ)^β를 쓴다. ISBDLD=2 코드는 θ^SBDLDA만 쓴다. SBDLDA=2.1+β/2로 지정하면 대수적으로 같은 지수를 얻지만 SBDLDB를 읽어도 이 분기에서 사용하지 않는다. |
| <a id="eq-6-30"></a>[T12・PDF 94쪽・인쇄 81쪽・식 6.30](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=94)<br>`\Phi=\phi\left(\theta-\theta_{cs}\right)^{1.5}`<br>[판독 기록:16](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p091-108.md:16) | [SedTran-Original/bedload.f90:96](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/bedload.f90:96) `                BDLDTMPA = (SBDLDG1(NX)*SHIELDS - SBDLDG2(NX)*CSHIELDS)**SBDLDA(NX)`<br>[SedTran-Original/bedload.f90:105](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/bedload.f90:105) `              QSBDLDP(L) = FACBEDL(L)*VFRBED(L,KBT(L),NS)*BDLDTMP*BDLDTMPP*BDLDTMPA*BDLDTMPB     ! *** (G/M/S)`<br>[input.f90:2245](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/input.f90:2245) `        read(1,*,IOSTAT = ISO)ICALC_BL,SBDLDA(NX),SBDLDB(NX),SBDLDG1(NX),SBDLDG2(NX),SBDLDG3(NX),SBDLDG4(NX),SBDLDP(NX),ISBLFUC,BLBSNT` | 일치 | ISBDLD=0, α=1.5, β=0, G1=G2=1을 지정하면 일치한다. Meyer-Peter–Müller 전용 자동 설정 분기는 없다. |
| <a id="eq-6-31"></a>[T12・PDF 94쪽・인쇄 81쪽・식 6.31](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=94)<br>`\Phi=\phi\left(\theta-\theta_{cs}\right)\left(\sqrt{\theta}\right)`<br>[판독 기록:16](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p091-108.md:16) | [SedTran-Original/bedload.f90:96](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/bedload.f90:96) `                BDLDTMPA = (SBDLDG1(NX)*SHIELDS - SBDLDG2(NX)*CSHIELDS)**SBDLDA(NX)`<br>[SedTran-Original/bedload.f90:101](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/bedload.f90:101) `                BDLDTMPB = (SBDLDG3(NX)*SQRT(SHIELDS)-SBDLDG4(NX)*SQRT(CSHIELDS))**SBDLDB(NX)`<br>[input.f90:2245](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/input.f90:2245) `        read(1,*,IOSTAT = ISO)ICALC_BL,SBDLDA(NX),SBDLDB(NX),SBDLDG1(NX),SBDLDG2(NX),SBDLDG3(NX),SBDLDG4(NX),SBDLDP(NX),ISBLFUC,BLBSNT` | 일치 | ISBDLD=0, α=1, β=1, G1=G2=G3=1, G4=0을 지정하면 일치한다. Bagnold 전용 자동 설정 분기는 없다. |
| <a id="eq-6-32"></a>[T12・PDF 94쪽・인쇄 81쪽・식 6.32](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=94)<br>`\Phi=\phi\left(\theta-\theta_{cs}\right)^{2.2}`<br>[판독 기록:16](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p091-108.md:16) | [SedTran-Original/bedload.f90:225](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/bedload.f90:225) `          BDLDTMPA = (SBDLDG1(NX)*SHIELDS - SBDLDG2(NX)*CSHIELDS)**SBDLDA(NX)`<br>[SedTran-Original/bedload.f90:226](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/bedload.f90:226) `          QSBDLDP(L) = FACBEDL(L)*VFRBED(L,KBT(L),NS)*BDLDTMP*BDLDTMPP*BDLDTMPA`<br>[SedTran-Original/fsbdld.f90:48](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/fsbdld.f90:48) `    TMP1   = 0.03*((PHID/PEXP)**0.6)`<br>[SedTran-Original/fsbdld.f90:49](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/fsbdld.f90:49) `    TMP2   = TMP1**2.2`<br>[SedTran-Original/fsbdld.f90:50](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/fsbdld.f90:50) `    FSBDLD = 0.0053/TMP2` | 일치 | ISBDLD=3, α=2.2, G1=G2=1에서 같은 초과 Shields 거듭제곱이다. φ와 임계 Shields에 숨김·노출 분율을 반영한다. |
| <a id="eq-6-33"></a>[T12・PDF 94쪽・인쇄 81쪽・식 6.33](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=94)<br>`\begin{aligned}q_{bcx}&=\dfrac{u}{\sqrt{u^2+v^2}}q_{bc}\\q_{bcy}&=\dfrac{v}{\sqrt{u^2+v^2}}q_{bc}\end{aligned}`<br>[판독 기록:16](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p091-108.md:16) | [SedTran-Original/ssedtox.f90:849](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/ssedtox.f90:849) `          UCELLCTR(L)  = UCELLCTR(L)/QCELLCTR(L)             ! *** UNIT VECTOR, X COMPONENT TO SPLIT BEDLOAD (DIMENSIONLESS)`<br>[SedTran-Original/ssedtox.f90:850](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/ssedtox.f90:850) `          VCELLCTR(L)  = VCELLCTR(L)/QCELLCTR(L)             ! *** UNIT VECTOR, Y COMPONENT TO SPLIT BEDLOAD (DIMENSIONLESS)`<br>[SedTran-Original/bedload.f90:274](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/bedload.f90:274) `          QSBDLDX(L,NX) = SUB(L)*QSBDLDP(LW)*UCELLCTR(LW)`<br>[SedTran-Original/bedload.f90:280](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/bedload.f90:280) `          QSBDLDY(L,NX) = SVB(L)*QSBDLDP(LS)*VCELLCTR(LS)` | 일치 | 유속을 크기로 나눈 단위 벡터로 소류사 크기를 투영한다. 영 유속은 영 벡터로 처리한다. |
