| <a id="eq-6-35"></a>[T12・PDF 95쪽・인쇄 82쪽・식 6.35](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=95)<br>`m_xm_yJ_b=\left(m_yq_{bfx}\right)_e-\left(m_yq_{bfx}\right)_w+\left(m_xq_{bfy}\right)_n-\left(m_xq_{bfy}\right)_s`<br>[판독 기록:17](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p091-108.md:17) | [SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:712](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:712) `          SNDFBL(L,NX) = DXYIP(L)*( QSBDLDX(LE,NX)-QSBDLDX(L,NX) + QSBDLDY(LN,NX)-QSBDLDY(L,NX) + QSBDLDOT(L,NX)-QSBDLDIN(L,NX) )  `<br>[SedTran-Original/bedload.f90:385](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/bedload.f90:385) `      QSBDLDX(L,NX) = SUB(L)*DYU(L)*QSBDLDX(L,NX)`<br>[SedTran-Original/bedload.f90:386](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/bedload.f90:386) `      QSBDLDY(L,NX) = SVB(L)*DXV(L)*QSBDLDY(L,NX)` | 일치 | 면 너비를 곱한 동−서+북−남 플럭스 발산을 면적으로 나눈다. 외부 소류사 경계 플럭스도 더한다. |
| <a id="eq-6-36"></a>[T12・PDF 95쪽・인쇄 82쪽・식 6.36](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=95)<br>`\dfrac{\partial}{\partial t}\left(HC\right)=\dfrac{\partial}{\partial z}\left(\dfrac{A_b}{H}\dfrac{\partial C}{\partial z}+w_zC\right)`<br>[판독 기록:17](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p091-108.md:17) | [SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:401](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:401) `            CLEFT = 1.+WSETA(L,K-1,NS)*WVEL  `<br>[SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:402](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:402) `            CRIGHT = max(SND(L,K,NX),0.)-SNDF(L,K,NX)*WVEL  `<br>[SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:403](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:403) `            SND(L,K,NX) = CRIGHT/CLEFT  `<br>[SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:404](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:404) `            SNDF(L,K-1,NX) = -WSETA(L,K-1,NS)*SND(L,K,NX)  `<br>[Transport/calconc.f90:366](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/calconc.f90:366) `          CCLBTMP(L,K) = RCDZKMK*HPI(L)*AB(L,K-1)`<br>[Transport/calconc.f90:367](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/calconc.f90:367) `          CCUBTMP      = RCDZKK*HPI(L)*AB(L,K)`<br>[Transport/calconc.f90:368](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/calconc.f90:368) `          CCMBTMP      = 1._8-CCLBTMP(L,K)-CCUBTMP`<br>[Transport/calconc.f90:369](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/calconc.f90:369) `          EEB(L,K)     = 1._8/( CCMBTMP - CCLBTMP(L,K)*VCU(L,K-1) )` | 일치 | 수평 변화와 원천을 제거하면 침강과 수직 확산의 보존형 갱신이 남는다. w_z는 이 문맥의 침강속도 표기이다. |
| <a id="eq-6-37"></a>[T12・PDF 96쪽・인쇄 83쪽・식 6.37](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=96)<br>`\dfrac{\partial}{\partial t}\left(\Delta H\bar C\right)=J_0-J_\Delta`<br>[판독 기록:18](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p091-108.md:18) | [SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:528](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:528) `        CRIGHT = max(SND(L,KSZ(L),NX),0.) + ( WESE-SNDF(L,KSZ(L),NX) )*WVEL  `<br>[SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:529](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:529) `        SND(L,KSZ(L),NX) = CRIGHT/CLEFT  `<br>[SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:530](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:530) `        SNDF(L,0,NX) = -WSETMP*SND(L,KSZ(L),NX) + WESE  ` | 일치 | 최하층 갱신에서 바닥 유입 J_0에서 상단 유출 J_Δ를 뺀다. 코드의 침강 플럭스는 위쪽을 양수로 둔다. |
| <a id="eq-6-38"></a>[T12・PDF 96쪽・인쇄 83쪽・식 6.38](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=96)<br>`\dfrac{\partial}{\partial t}\left(HC'\right)=\dfrac{\partial}{\partial z}\left(\dfrac{A_b}{H}\dfrac{\partial C}{\partial z}+w_zC\right)-\left(\dfrac{J_0-J_\Delta}{\Delta}\right)`<br>[판독 기록:18](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p091-108.md:18) | [SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:494](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:494) `          SNDEQ(L) = SNDEQB(L)*TOP/BOT  `<br>[SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:530](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:530) `        SNDF(L,0,NX) = -WSETMP*SND(L,KSZ(L),NX) + WESE  ` | 문서에만 | 평균 농도를 뺀 C′의 연속식이다. 코드는 C′를 독립적으로 저장하거나 이 식을 적분하지 않는다. |
| <a id="eq-6-39"></a>[T12・PDF 96쪽・인쇄 83쪽・식 6.39](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=96)<br>`\dfrac{\partial}{\partial t}\left(HC'\right)<<\dfrac{\partial}{\partial t}\left(H\bar C\right)`<br>[판독 기록:18](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p091-108.md:18) | [SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:494](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:494) `          SNDEQ(L) = SNDEQB(L)*TOP/BOT  `<br>[SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:530](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:530) `        SNDF(L,0,NX) = -WSETMP*SND(L,KSZ(L),NX) + WESE  ` | 문서에만 | 작은 변동률을 가정하는 유도 조건이다. 코드는 이 부등식을 검사하지 않는다. |
| <a id="eq-6-40"></a>[T12・PDF 96쪽・인쇄 83쪽・식 6.40](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=96)<br>`\dfrac{\partial}{\partial z}\left(\dfrac{A_b}{H}\dfrac{\partial C}{\partial z}+w_zC\right)=\left(\dfrac{J_0-J_\Delta}{\Delta}\right)`<br>[판독 기록:18](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p091-108.md:18) | [SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:494](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:494) `          SNDEQ(L) = SNDEQB(L)*TOP/BOT  `<br>[SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:530](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:530) `        SNDF(L,0,NX) = -WSETMP*SND(L,KSZ(L),NX) + WESE  ` | 문서에만 | 준정상 농도 편차에 대한 유도식이다. 코드는 이 미분방정식을 직접 풀지 않는다. |
| <a id="eq-6-41"></a>[T12・PDF 96쪽・인쇄 83쪽・식 6.41](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=96)<br>`\dfrac{A_b}{H}\dfrac{\partial C}{\partial z}+w_zC=\left(J_0-J_\Delta\right)\dfrac{z}{\Delta}-J_0`<br>[판독 기록:18](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p091-108.md:18) | [SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:494](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:494) `          SNDEQ(L) = SNDEQB(L)*TOP/BOT  `<br>[SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:530](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:530) `        SNDF(L,0,NX) = -WSETMP*SND(L,KSZ(L),NX) + WESE  ` | 문서에만 | 준정상 유도식의 적분형이다. 코드에는 z에 선형인 근저 플럭스 프로파일 계산이 없다. |
| <a id="eq-6-42"></a>[T12・PDF 96쪽・인쇄 83쪽・식 6.42](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=96)<br>`\dfrac{A_b}{H}\dfrac{\partial C}{\partial z}+w_zC=-J_0`<br>[판독 기록:18](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p091-108.md:18) | [SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:494](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:494) `          SNDEQ(L) = SNDEQB(L)*TOP/BOT  `<br>[SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:530](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:530) `        SNDF(L,0,NX) = -WSETMP*SND(L,KSZ(L),NX) + WESE  ` | 문서에만 | 바닥 가까이에서 식 (6.41)을 줄인 유도식이다. 구현은 층 평균 바닥 교환식 (6.51)을 쓴다. |
| <a id="eq-6-43"></a>[T12・PDF 96쪽・인쇄 83쪽・식 6.43](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=96)<br>`\dfrac{A_b}{H}=K_oq\dfrac{l}{H}\cong u_*\kappa z`<br>[판독 기록:18](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p091-108.md:18) | [SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:494](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:494) `          SNDEQ(L) = SNDEQB(L)*TOP/BOT  `<br>[SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:530](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:530) `        SNDF(L,0,NX) = -WSETMP*SND(L,KSZ(L),NX) + WESE  ` | 문서에만 | 중립 근저 확산계수의 선형 근사이다. 코드는 이 프로파일을 직접 지정하지 않고 Rouse 수와 층 평균 변환에 사용한다. |
| <a id="eq-6-44"></a>[T12・PDF 96쪽・인쇄 83쪽・식 6.44](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=96)<br>`\dfrac{\partial C}{\partial z}+\dfrac{R}{z}C=-\dfrac{R}{z}\dfrac{J_o}{w_s}`<br>[판독 기록:18](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p091-108.md:18) | [SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:494](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:494) `          SNDEQ(L) = SNDEQB(L)*TOP/BOT  `<br>[SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:530](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:530) `        SNDF(L,0,NX) = -WSETMP*SND(L,KSZ(L),NX) + WESE  ` | 문서에만 | Rouse 해를 구하기 위한 미분방정식이다. 코드에는 C(z)의 직접 적분기가 없다. |
| <a id="eq-6-45"></a>[T12・PDF 96쪽・인쇄 83쪽・식 6.45](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=96)<br>`R=\dfrac{w_s}{u_*\kappa}`<br>[판독 기록:18](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p091-108.md:18) | [SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:363](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:363) `            ROUSE(L) = WSETA(L,0,NS)/(VKC*USTAR(L))  `<br>[SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:374](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:374) `          else  ` | 일치 | 침강속도를 VKC와 선택한 마찰속도의 곱으로 나눈다. IROUSE는 전체 응력과 입자 응력 사이를 선택한다. |
| <a id="eq-6-46"></a>[T12・PDF 97쪽・인쇄 84쪽・식 6.46](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=97)<br>`C=-\dfrac{J_o}{w_s}+\dfrac{C_0}{z^R}`<br>[판독 기록:19](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p091-108.md:19) | [SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:494](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:494) `          SNDEQ(L) = SNDEQB(L)*TOP/BOT  `<br>[SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:530](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:530) `        SNDF(L,0,NX) = -WSETMP*SND(L,KSZ(L),NX) + WESE  ` | 문서에만 | Rouse 해의 적분상수 표현이다. 코드는 C_0를 계산하지 않는다. |
| <a id="eq-6-47"></a>[T12・PDF 97쪽・인쇄 84쪽・식 6.47](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=97)<br>`C=C_{eq}\quad\text{at }z=z_{eq}\quad\text{and }J_o=0`<br>[판독 기록:19](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p091-108.md:19) | [SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:494](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:494) `          SNDEQ(L) = SNDEQB(L)*TOP/BOT  `<br>[SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:530](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:530) `        SNDF(L,0,NX) = -WSETMP*SND(L,KSZ(L),NX) + WESE  ` | 문서에만 | 평형 기준높이에서 적분상수를 정하는 조건이다. 코드는 CSNDZEQ와 CSNDEQC를 계산하지만 이 점 경계값 문제를 직접 풀지 않는다. |
| <a id="eq-6-48"></a>[T12・PDF 97쪽・인쇄 84쪽・식 6.48](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=97)<br>`C=\left(\dfrac{z_{eq}}{z}\right)^R C_{eq}-\dfrac{J_o}{w_s}`<br>[판독 기록:19](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p091-108.md:19) | [SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:494](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:494) `          SNDEQ(L) = SNDEQB(L)*TOP/BOT  `<br>[SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:530](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:530) `        SNDF(L,0,NX) = -WSETMP*SND(L,KSZ(L),NX) + WESE  ` | 문서에만 | 연속 수직 농도 프로파일의 해이다. 구현은 이 해를 적분한 식 (6.52)의 층 평균 계수를 계산한다. |
| <a id="eq-6-49"></a>[T12・PDF 97쪽・인쇄 84쪽・식 6.49](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=97)<br>`J_o=w_s\left(C_{eq}-C_{ne}\right)`<br>[판독 기록:19](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p091-108.md:19) | [SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:494](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:494) `          SNDEQ(L) = SNDEQB(L)*TOP/BOT  `<br>[SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:530](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:530) `        SNDF(L,0,NX) = -WSETMP*SND(L,KSZ(L),NX) + WESE  ` | 문서에만 | 기준높이의 실제 농도 C_ne를 쓰는 유도식이다. 코드는 실제 층 평균 농도와 변환한 평형 농도의 차를 쓴다. |
| <a id="eq-6-50"></a>[T12・PDF 97쪽・인쇄 84쪽・식 6.50](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=97)<br>`J_o=-w_sC_{ne}\left(1-\dfrac{C_{eq}}{C_{ne}}\right)`<br>[판독 기록:19](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p091-108.md:19) | [SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:494](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:494) `          SNDEQ(L) = SNDEQB(L)*TOP/BOT  `<br>[SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:530](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:530) `        SNDF(L,0,NX) = -WSETMP*SND(L,KSZ(L),NX) + WESE  ` | 문서에만 | 식 (6.49)의 대수적 재작성이다. 코드에는 C_eq/C_ne 퇴적계수를 따로 계산하는 분기가 없다. |
| <a id="eq-6-51"></a>[T12・PDF 97쪽・인쇄 84쪽・식 6.51](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=97)<br>`J_o=w_s\left(\bar C_{eq}-\bar C\right)`<br>[판독 기록:19](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p091-108.md:19) | [SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:509](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:509) `        WESE = WSFAC*WSETA(L,0,NS)*SNDEQ(L)  `<br>[SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:512](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:512) `        WSETMP = WSFAC*WSETA(L,0,NS)  `<br>[SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:530](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:530) `        SNDF(L,0,NX) = -WSETMP*SND(L,KSZ(L),NX) + WESE  ` | 일치 | 다층에서 WSFAC=1로 두고 w_s(층 평균 평형농도−층 평균 실제농도)를 계산한다. |
| <a id="eq-6-52"></a>[T12・PDF 97쪽・인쇄 84쪽・식 6.52](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=97)<br>`\begin{aligned}\bar C_{eq}&=\dfrac{\ln\left(\Delta z_{eq}^{-1}\right)}{\left(\Delta z_{eq}^{-1}-1\right)}C_{eq},\quad R=1\\\bar C_{eq}&=\dfrac{\left(\Delta z_{eq}^{-1}\right)^{1-R}-1}{\left(1-R\right)\left(\Delta z_{eq}^{-1}-1\right)}C_{eq},\quad R\neq1\end{aligned}`<br>[판독 기록:19](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p091-108.md:19) | [SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:492](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:492) `          TOP = (ZEQD(L)**(ROUSE(L)-1.))-1.  `<br>[SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:493](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:493) `          BOT = (1.-ROUSE(L))*(ZEQDI(L)-1.)  `<br>[SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:494](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:494) `          SNDEQ(L) = SNDEQB(L)*TOP/BOT  `<br>[SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:495](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:495) `          SNDEQ(L) = FACSUSL(L)*VFRBED(L,KBT(L),NS)*MAX(SNDEQ(L),0.)  `<br>[SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:496](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:496) `          SNDEQSAV(L,NX) = SNDEQ(L)  `<br>[SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:497](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:497) `        else  `<br>[SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:498](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:498) `          TOP = LOG(ZEQDI(L))  `<br>[SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:499](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:499) `          BOT = (ZEQDI(L)-1.)  `<br>[SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:500](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:500) `          SNDEQ(L) = SNDEQB(L)*TOP/BOT  ` | 일치 | ZEQD=z_eq/Δ를 사용하면 거듭제곱 분자와 분모가 같다. 코드는 0.999≤R≤1.001에서 로그 분기를 사용한다. 그 밖에서는 거듭제곱 분기를 사용한다. |
| <a id="eq-6-53"></a>[T12・PDF 98쪽・인쇄 85쪽・식 6.53](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=98)<br>`\begin{aligned}w_rC_r&=w_s\bar C_{eq}\\P_dw_s&=w_s\end{aligned}`<br>[판독 기록:20](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p091-108.md:20) | [SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:509](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:509) `        WESE = WSFAC*WSETA(L,0,NS)*SNDEQ(L)  `<br>[SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:512](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:512) `        WSETMP = WSFAC*WSETA(L,0,NS)  ` | 일치 | 다층에서 WESE=w_s Cbar_eq와 WSETMP=w_s를 설정한다. |
| <a id="eq-6-54"></a>[T12・PDF 98쪽・인쇄 85쪽・식 6.54](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=98)<br>`\begin{aligned}\bar C_{eq}&=\dfrac{\ln\left(M\Delta z_{eq}^{-1}\right)}{\left(M\Delta z_{eq}^{-1}-1\right)}C_{eq},\quad R=1\\\bar C_{eq}&=\dfrac{\left(M\Delta z_{eq}^{-1}\right)^{1-R}-1}{\left(1-R\right)\left(M\Delta z_{eq}^{-1}-1\right)}C_{eq},\quad R\neq1\end{aligned}`<br>[판독 기록:20](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p091-108.md:20) | [SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:448](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:448) `          ZEQMAX   = 0.5*DZC(L,KSZ(L))  `<br>[SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:449](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:449) `          ZEQ(L)   = min(ZEQ(L),ZEQMAX)  `<br>[SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:494](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:494) `          SNDEQ(L) = SNDEQB(L)*TOP/BOT  ` | 불일치 | 문서는 z_eq가 최하층을 넘으면 M개 층으로 평균 범위를 확대한다. 코드는 z_eq를 최하층 두께의 0.5 이하로 제한한다. M개 층 평균 처리를 찾지 못했다. |
| <a id="eq-6-55"></a>[T12・PDF 98쪽・인쇄 85쪽・식 6.55](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=98)<br>`\dfrac{A_b}{H}\dfrac{\partial C}{\partial z}+w_sC=-J_o\left(1-z\right)`<br>[판독 기록:20](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p091-108.md:20) | [SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:507](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:507) `          WSFAC = 2.*(1.+ROUSE(L))/(2.+ROUSE(L)*(1.-ZEQ(L)))  ` | 문서에만 | 단층의 해석적 수직 프로파일을 유도하는 방정식이다. 코드는 층 평균 플럭스 계수만 계산한다. |
| <a id="eq-6-56"></a>[T12・PDF 98쪽・인쇄 85쪽・식 6.56](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=98)<br>`\dfrac{A_b}{H}=K_oq\dfrac{1}{H}\cong u_*\kappa z\left(1-z\right)^\lambda`<br>[판독 기록:20](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p091-108.md:20) | [SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:507](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:507) `          WSFAC = 2.*(1.+ROUSE(L))/(2.+ROUSE(L)*(1.-ZEQ(L)))  ` | 문서에만 | 단층 수직 확산 프로파일의 유도 가정이다. PDF는 l 대신 숫자 1을 인쇄한다. 코드는 λ에 따른 이 프로파일을 계산하지 않는다. |
| <a id="eq-6-57"></a>[T12・PDF 98쪽・인쇄 85쪽・식 6.57](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=98)<br>`\dfrac{\partial C}{\partial z}+\dfrac{R}{z\left(1-z\right)^\lambda}C=-\dfrac{R\left(1-z\right)^{1-\lambda}}{z}\dfrac{J_o}{w_s}`<br>[판독 기록:20](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p091-108.md:20) | [SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:507](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:507) `          WSFAC = 2.*(1.+ROUSE(L))/(2.+ROUSE(L)*(1.-ZEQ(L)))  ` | 문서에만 | λ에 따른 연속 농도 미분방정식이다. 코드에는 λ별 직접 해석 경로가 없다. |
| <a id="eq-6-58"></a>[T12・PDF 98쪽・인쇄 85쪽・식 6.58](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=98)<br>`C=-\left(1-\dfrac{Rz}{\left(1+R\right)}\right)\dfrac{J_o}{w_s}+\dfrac{C_0}{z^R}`<br>[판독 기록:20](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p091-108.md:20) | [SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:507](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:507) `          WSFAC = 2.*(1.+ROUSE(L))/(2.+ROUSE(L)*(1.-ZEQ(L)))  ` | 문서에만 | λ=0에서의 연속 농도 해이다. 코드는 C_0와 C(z)를 계산하지 않는다. |
| <a id="eq-6-59"></a>[T12・PDF 98쪽・인쇄 85쪽・식 6.59](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=98)<br>`C=\left(\dfrac{z_{eq}}{z}\right)^R C_{eq}-\left(1-\dfrac{Rz}{\left(1+R\right)}\right)\dfrac{J_o}{w_s}`<br>[판독 기록:20](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p091-108.md:20) | [SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:507](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:507) `          WSFAC = 2.*(1.+ROUSE(L))/(2.+ROUSE(L)*(1.-ZEQ(L)))  ` | 문서에만 | 평형 기준높이를 적용한 연속 농도 해이다. 코드는 이 해를 적분한 층 평균 교환 계수를 쓴다. |
| <a id="eq-6-60"></a>[T12・PDF 99쪽・인쇄 86쪽・식 6.60](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=99)<br>`J_o=w_s\left(\dfrac{1+R}{1+R\left(1-z_{eq}\right)}\right)\left(C_{eq}-C_{ne}\right)`<br>[판독 기록:21](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p091-108.md:21) | [SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:507](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:507) `          WSFAC = 2.*(1.+ROUSE(L))/(2.+ROUSE(L)*(1.-ZEQ(L)))  ` | 문서에만 | 기준높이의 농도 차에 곱하는 (1+R)/(1+R(1−z_eq))이다. 코드는 기준높이 농도를 저장하지 않고 식 (6.61)의 층 평균 계수를 쓴다. |
| <a id="eq-6-61"></a>[T12・PDF 99쪽・인쇄 86쪽・식 6.61](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=99)<br>`J_o=w_s\left(\dfrac{2\left(1+R\right)}{2+R\left(1-z_{eq}\right)}\right)\left(\bar C_{eq}-\bar C\right)`<br>[판독 기록:21](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p091-108.md:21) | [SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:507](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:507) `          WSFAC = 2.*(1.+ROUSE(L))/(2.+ROUSE(L)*(1.-ZEQ(L)))  `<br>[SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:509](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:509) `        WESE = WSFAC*WSETA(L,0,NS)*SNDEQ(L)  `<br>[SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:530](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:530) `        SNDF(L,0,NX) = -WSETMP*SND(L,KSZ(L),NX) + WESE  ` | 일치 | 최하 활성층이 유일한 층일 때 2(1+R)/(2+R(1−z_eq))를 침식과 퇴적에 같이 곱한다. |
| <a id="eq-6-62"></a>[T12・PDF 99쪽・인쇄 86쪽・식 6.62](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=99)<br>`\begin{aligned}\bar C_{eq}&=\dfrac{\ln\left(z_{eq}^{-1}\right)}{\left(z_{eq}^{-1}-1\right)}C_{eq},\quad R=1\\\bar C_{eq}&=\dfrac{\left(z_{eq}^{R-1}-1\right)}{\left(1-R\right)\left(z_{eq}^{-1}-1\right)}C_{eq},\quad R\neq1\end{aligned}`<br>[판독 기록:21](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p091-108.md:21) | [SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:492](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:492) `          TOP = (ZEQD(L)**(ROUSE(L)-1.))-1.  `<br>[SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:493](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:493) `          BOT = (1.-ROUSE(L))*(ZEQDI(L)-1.)  `<br>[SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:494](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:494) `          SNDEQ(L) = SNDEQB(L)*TOP/BOT  `<br>[SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:495](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:495) `          SNDEQ(L) = FACSUSL(L)*VFRBED(L,KBT(L),NS)*MAX(SNDEQ(L),0.)  `<br>[SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:496](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:496) `          SNDEQSAV(L,NX) = SNDEQ(L)  `<br>[SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:497](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:497) `        else  `<br>[SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:498](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:498) `          TOP = LOG(ZEQDI(L))  `<br>[SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:499](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:499) `          BOT = (ZEQDI(L)-1.)  `<br>[SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:500](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:500) `          SNDEQ(L) = SNDEQB(L)*TOP/BOT  ` | 일치 | 단층에서는 Δ=1이므로 식 (6.52)와 같은 층 평균 계수를 쓴다. R≈1 처리의 허용 폭은 식 (6.52) 행과 같다. |
| <a id="eq-6-63"></a>[T12・PDF 99쪽・인쇄 86쪽・식 6.63](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=99)<br>`\begin{aligned}w_rs_r&=w_s\left(\dfrac{2\left(1+R\right)}{2+R\left(1-z_{eq}\right)}\right)\bar C_{eq}\\P_dw_s&=\left(\dfrac{2\left(1+R\right)}{2+R\left(1-z_{eq}\right)}\right)w_s\end{aligned}`<br>[판독 기록:21](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p091-108.md:21) | [SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:507](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:507) `          WSFAC = 2.*(1.+ROUSE(L))/(2.+ROUSE(L)*(1.-ZEQ(L)))  `<br>[SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:508](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:508) `        endif`<br>[SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:509](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:509) `        WESE = WSFAC*WSETA(L,0,NS)*SNDEQ(L)  `<br>[SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:511](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:511) `        ! *** SET DEPOSITION VELOCITY  `<br>[SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:512](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:512) `        WSETMP = WSFAC*WSETA(L,0,NS)  ` | 일치 | WESE와 WSETMP에 같은 WSFAC을 곱한다. 첫 식의 s_r는 앞 문맥의 C_r에 대응한다. |
| <a id="eq-6-64"></a>[T12・PDF 99쪽・인쇄 86쪽・식 6.64](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=99)<br>`C_{eq}=C_{eq}\left(d,\rho_s,\rho_w,w_s,u_*,v\right)`<br>[판독 기록:21](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p091-108.md:21) | [SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:455](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsnd.f90:455) `          SNDEQB(L) = CSNDEQC(ISNDEQ(NS),DIASED,SSG(NS),WSETA(L,0,NS),TAUR(NS),TAUBSND(L),SEDDIA50(L,KBT(L)),SIGP,ZEQ(L),VDRBED(L,KBT(L)),ISNDAL)      !  `<br>[SedTran-Original/csndeqc.f90:48](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/csndeqc.f90:48) `      REY = 1.E6*SNDDIA*SQRT( 9.8*(SSG-1.)*SNDDIA )   !EQ 42`<br>[SedTran-Original/csndeqc.f90:79](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/csndeqc.f90:79) `      REY = 1.E4*SNDDIA*( (9.8*(SSG-1.))**0.333 )  ` | 일치 | 선택 함수에 입경·비중·침강속도·응력·입도 통계를 전달한다. 점성은 Reynolds 수의 고정 변환계수에 들어간다. |
| <a id="eq-6-65"></a>[T12・PDF 100쪽・인쇄 87쪽・식 6.65](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=100)<br>`C_{eq}=\rho_s\dfrac{0.65\gamma_oT}{1+\gamma_oT}`<br>[판독 기록:22](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p091-108.md:22) | [SedTran-Original/csndeqc.f90:67](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/csndeqc.f90:67) `    VAL = 2.4E-3*( (TAUB/TAUR)-1. )  `<br>[SedTran-Original/csndeqc.f90:68](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/csndeqc.f90:68) `    VAL = max(VAL,0.)  `<br>[SedTran-Original/csndeqc.f90:69](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/csndeqc.f90:69) `    TMP = 0.65*VAL/(1.+VAL)  `<br>[SedTran-Original/csndeqc.f90:70](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/csndeqc.f90:70) `    CSNDEQC = 1.E6*SSG*TMP  ` | 일치 | IOPT=2에서 γ_0=2.4×10^−3와 0.65를 사용한다. 음의 초과 응력은 0으로 제한한다. 질량 농도로 변환하기 위해 10^6 SSG를 곱한다. |
| <a id="eq-6-66"></a>[T12・PDF 100쪽・인쇄 87쪽・식 6.66](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=100)<br>`T=\dfrac{\tau_b-\tau_{cs}}{\tau_{cs}}=\dfrac{u_*^2-u_{*cs}^2}{u_{*cs}^2}`<br>[판독 기록:22](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p091-108.md:22) | [SedTran-Original/csndeqc.f90:67](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/csndeqc.f90:67) `    VAL = 2.4E-3*( (TAUB/TAUR)-1. )  ` | 일치 | Smith–McLean 분기에서 TAUB/TAUR−1을 사용한다. 물 밀도 정규화가 분자와 분모에서 상쇄된다. |

`<br>[SedTran-Original/csndeqc.f90:80](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/csndeqc.f90:80) `      if( REY <= 10. ) TAURS = (4.*WS/REY)**2 1984`<br>[SedTran-Original/csndeqc.f90:82](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/csndeqc.f90:82) `      REY3 = REY**0.3  `<br>[SedTran-Original/csndeqc.f90:83](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/csndeqc.f90:83) `      VAL = (TAUB/TAURS)-1.  `<br>[SedTran-Original/csndeqc.f90:84](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/csndeqc.f90:84) `      VAL = max(VAL,0.)  `<br>[SedTran-Original/csndeqc.f90:85](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/csndeqc.f90:85) `      VAL = VAL**1.5  `<br>[SedTran-Original/csndeqc.f90:86](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/csndeqc.f90:86) `      RATIO = SNDDIA/(3.*SNDDMX)  `<br>[SedTran-Original/csndeqc.f90:87](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/csndeqc.f90:87) `      TMP = 0.015*RATIO*VAL/REY3 `<br>[SedTran-Original/csndzeq.f90:55](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/csndzeq.f90:55) `      VAL = (TAUB/TAURS)-1.  `<br>[SedTran-Original/csndzeq.f90:56](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/csndzeq.f90:56) `      VAL = min(MAX(VAL,0.),100.)  `<br>[SedTran-Original/csndzeq.f90:57](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/csndzeq.f90:57) `      VAL1 = 1.-EXP(-0.5*VAL) `<br>[SedTran-Original/csndzeq.f90:61](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/csndzeq.f90:61) `      CSNDZEQ = max(ZEQ1,0.01) ` | 불일치 | 0.015와 T^1.5 및 R_d^−0.2는 대응한다. 코드는 T의 임계 응력을 TAUR 대신 침강 기반 TAURS로 계산한다. 농도식의 기준높이는 3×SNDDMX를 고정한다. CSNDZEQ가 계산한 수심 의존 기준높이와 같은 값이라고 보장하지 않는다. |
| <a id="eq-6-68"></a>[T12・PDF 100쪽・인쇄 87쪽・식 6.68](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=100)<br>`C_{jeq}=\rho_s\dfrac{A\left(\lambda Z_j\right)^5}{\left(1+3.33A\left(\lambda Z\right)^5\right)}`<br>[판독 기록:22](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p091-108.md:22) | [SedTran-Original/csndeqc.f90:53](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/csndeqc.f90:53) `      VAL = DFAC*RLAM*REY*USTAR/WS                    !Z IN EQ 43`<br>[SedTran-Original/csndeqc.f90:54](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/csndeqc.f90:54) `      VAL = 1.3E-7*(VAL**5)                           !TOP OF EQ 45`<br>[SedTran-Original/csndeqc.f90:55](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/csndeqc.f90:55) `      TMP = VAL/(1+3.33*VAL)                          !EQ 45`<br>[SedTran-Original/csndeqc.f90:56](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/csndeqc.f90:56) `      CSNDEQC = 1.E6*SSG*TMP                          !CONVERT TO MASS CONC` | 일치 | A=1.3×10^−7과 분모 1+3.33A(λZ_j)^5를 사용한다. 문서 분모의 Z에는 j 첨자가 없지만 같은 계급의 Z로 해석하면 같다. |
| <a id="eq-6-69"></a>[T12・PDF 100쪽・인쇄 87쪽・식 6.69](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=100)<br>`Z_j=\dfrac{u_*}{w_{sj}}R_{dj}^{3/5}F_H`<br>[판독 기록:22](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p091-108.md:22) | [SedTran-Original/csndeqc.f90:48](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/csndeqc.f90:48) `      REY = 1.E6*SNDDIA*SQRT( 9.8*(SSG-1.)*SNDDIA )   !EQ 42`<br>[SedTran-Original/csndeqc.f90:49](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/csndeqc.f90:49) `      REY = REY**0.6                                  !SEE EQ 43`<br>[SedTran-Original/csndeqc.f90:50](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/csndeqc.f90:50) `      DFAC = 1.`<br>[SedTran-Original/csndeqc.f90:51](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/csndeqc.f90:51) `      if( ISNDAL >= 1) DFAC = (SNDDIA/D50)**0.2       !SEE EQ 43`<br>[SedTran-Original/csndeqc.f90:52](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/csndeqc.f90:52) `      RLAM = 1.-0.29*SIGPHI                           !EQ 51`<br>[SedTran-Original/csndeqc.f90:53](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/csndeqc.f90:53) `      VAL = DFAC*RLAM*REY*USTAR/WS                    !Z IN EQ 43`<br>[SedTran-Original/csndeqc.f90:54](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/csndeqc.f90:54) `      VAL = 1.3E-7*(VAL**5)                           !TOP OF EQ 45` | 일치 | IOPT=1에서 R_d^0.6, u*/w_s, F_H를 곱한다. w_s≤0이면 농도를 0으로 둔다. |
| <a id="eq-6-70"></a>[T12・PDF 100쪽・인쇄 87쪽・식 6.70](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=100)<br>`F_H=\left(\dfrac{d_j}{d_{50}}\right)^{1/5}`<br>[판독 기록:22](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p091-108.md:22) | [SedTran-Original/csndeqc.f90:50](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/csndeqc.f90:50) `      DFAC = 1.`<br>[SedTran-Original/csndeqc.f90:51](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/csndeqc.f90:51) `      if( ISNDAL >= 1) DFAC = (SNDDIA/D50)**0.2       !SEE EQ 43` | 일치 | ISNDAL≥1에서 (d_j/d_50)^0.2를 사용한다. 보정을 끄면 F_H=1이다. |
| <a id="eq-6-71"></a>[T12・PDF 100쪽・인쇄 87쪽・식 6.71](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=100)<br>`\lambda=1+\dfrac{\sigma_\phi}{\sigma_{\phi o}}\left(\lambda_o-1\right)`<br>[판독 기록:22](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p091-108.md:22) | [SedTran-Original/csndeqc.f90:52](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/csndeqc.f90:52) `      RLAM = 1.-0.29*SIGPHI                           !EQ 51` | 일치 | 코드는 λ=1−0.29σ_φ를 고정한다. 문서 일반식에서 (λ_0−1)/σ_φ0=−0.29인 경우와 같다. λ_0·σ_φ0는 별도 입력으로 받지 않는다. |
| <a id="eq-6-72"></a>[T12・PDF 101쪽・인쇄 88쪽・식 6.72](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=101)<br>`w_{se}=w_{se}\left(d,C,\dfrac{du}{dz},q\right)`<br>[판독 기록:23](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p091-108.md:23) | [SedTran-Original/calsed.f90:125](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsed.f90:125) `              WSETA(L,K,NS) = CSEDSET(SEDT(L,K+1),SHEAR,ISEDVW)`<br>[SedTran-Original/csedset.f90:37](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/csedset.f90:37) `  if( IOPT == 1 )then`<br>[SedTran-Original/csedset.f90:50](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/csedset.f90:50) `  if( IOPT == 2 )then`<br>[SedTran-Original/csedset.f90:70](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/csedset.f90:70) `  if( IOPT == 3 )then` | 일치 | 농도와 전단에 의존하는 침강속도 함수를 선택한다. 일반 함수가 모든 인자에 동시에 의존한다는 뜻은 아니다. 함수의 실제 인자는 SED·SHEAR·IOPT이다. |
| <a id="eq-6-73"></a>[T12・PDF 101쪽・인쇄 88쪽・식 6.73](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=101)<br>`w_s=\dfrac{aC''}{\left(C^2+b^2\right)^m}`<br>[판독 기록:23](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p091-108.md:23) | [SedTran-Original/csedset.f90:38](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/csedset.f90:38) `    TMPSED = SED/2000.`<br>[SedTran-Original/csedset.f90:39](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/csedset.f90:39) `    TMP = LOG10(TMPSED)`<br>[SedTran-Original/csedset.f90:40](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/csedset.f90:40) `    TMP = -16.*TMP*TMP/9.`<br>[SedTran-Original/csedset.f90:41](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/csedset.f90:41) `    TMP = 10.**TMP`<br>[SedTran-Original/csedset.f90:42](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/csedset.f90:42) `    CSEDSET = 8.E-4*TMP` | 불일치 | 문서는 aC″/(C²+b²)^m을 인쇄한다. IOPT=1 코드는 8×10^−4×10^{−16[log10(C/2000)]²/9}를 계산한다. 유리함수의 계수 a·b·m·n을 사용하지 않는다. |
| <a id="eq-6-74"></a>[T12・PDF 102쪽・인쇄 89쪽・식 6.74](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=102)<br>`cw_s=C^\alpha\exp\left(-4.21+0.147G\right)`<br>[판독 기록:24](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p091-108.md:24) | [SedTran-Original/csedset.f90:58](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/csedset.f90:58) `    TMPSED  = 1.E-3*SED`<br>[SedTran-Original/csedset.f90:59](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/csedset.f90:59) `    RNG     = 1.11075 + 0.0386*SHEAR`<br>[SedTran-Original/csedset.f90:60](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/csedset.f90:60) `    BG      = EXP( -4.20706 + 0.1465*SHEAR )`<br>[SedTran-Original/csedset.f90:61](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/csedset.f90:61) `    WTMP    = BG*TMPSED**RNG`<br>[SedTran-Original/csedset.f90:62](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/csedset.f90:62) `    CSEDSET = WTMP/3600.` | 불일치 | PDF 좌변에 c w_s를 인쇄한다. 코드는 C^α exp(−4.20706+0.1465G)/3600을 침강속도로 설정한다. 문서의 −4.21·0.147은 반올림 값이다. 코드는 농도를 g/L로 바꾸고 속도를 시간당 값에서 초당 값으로 바꾼다. |
| <a id="eq-6-75"></a>[T12・PDF 102쪽・인쇄 89쪽・식 6.75](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=102)<br>`\alpha=1.11075+0.0386G`<br>[판독 기록:24](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p091-108.md:24) | [SedTran-Original/csedset.f90:59](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/csedset.f90:59) `    RNG     = 1.11075 + 0.0386*SHEAR` | 일치 | 1.11075+0.0386G의 두 계수가 같다. |
| <a id="eq-6-76"></a>[T12・PDF 102쪽・인쇄 89쪽・식 6.76](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=102)<br>`G=\sqrt{\left(\dfrac{\partial u}{\partial z}\right)^2+\left(\dfrac{\partial v}{\partial z}\right)^2}`<br>[판독 기록:24](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p091-108.md:24) | [SedTran-Original/calsed.f90:123](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsed.f90:123) `              STRESS = QQ(L,0)/CTURB2`<br>[SedTran-Original/calsed.f90:124](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsed.f90:124) `              SHEAR = 2.*HPKI(L,K+1)*SQRT(STRESS)/VKC`<br>[SedTran-Original/calsed.f90:125](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsed.f90:125) `              WSETA(L,K,NS) = CSEDSET(SEDT(L,K+1),SHEAR,ISEDVW)`<br>[SedTran-Original/calsed.f90:154](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsed.f90:154) `                  SHEAR = HPI(L)*SQRT( DZIGSD4U(L,K) )*SQRT( (U(LEC(L),K+1)-U(LEC(L),K)+U(L,K+1)-U(L,K))**2  &`<br>[SedTran-Original/calsed.f90:155](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsed.f90:155) `                                                           + (V(LNC(L),K+1)-V(LNC(L),K)+V(L,K+1)-V(L,K))**2 )`<br>[SedTran-Original/calsed.f90:156](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsed.f90:156) `                  WSETA(L,K,NS) = CSEDSET(SEDT(L,K+1),SHEAR,ISEDVW)` | 불일치 | 파랑 영향이 있는 내부 인터페이스에서만 두 유속 성분의 수직 차분 크기를 사용한다. 파랑을 끄거나 파랑 마스크 밖이면 G≈2u*/(κH_k)로 대체한다. 모든 셀에서 문서의 직접 유속 미분을 계산하지 않는다. |
| <a id="eq-6-77"></a>[T12・PDF 102쪽・인쇄 89쪽・식 6.77](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=102)<br>`w_s=ad_f^b`<br>[판독 기록:24](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p091-108.md:24) | [SedTran-Original/csedset.f90:75](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/csedset.f90:75) `    BD2     = -0.4 - 0.25*LOG10(CG - 7.5E-6)`<br>[SedTran-Original/csedset.f90:76](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/csedset.f90:76) `    CON     = 9.6E-4*(1.E-8)**BD2`<br>[SedTran-Original/csedset.f90:77](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/csedset.f90:77) `    VAL     = CG**(-0.85-BD2)`<br>[SedTran-Original/csedset.f90:78](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/csedset.f90:78) `    CSEDSET = 0.01*CON*VAL` | 일치 | IOPT=3에서 식 (6.78)~(6.80)을 소거한 w_s=B_1 α_f^{b/2}(Cτ)^{−0.85−b/2}를 계산한다. 마지막 0.01은 cm/s에서 m/s로 바꾸는 계수이다. |
| <a id="eq-6-78"></a>[T12・PDF 102쪽・인쇄 89쪽・식 6.78](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=102)<br>`d_f=\sqrt{\dfrac{\alpha_f}{C\sqrt{\tau_{xz}^2+\tau_{yz}^2}}}`<br>[판독 기록:24](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p091-108.md:24) | [SedTran-Original/csedset.f90:74](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/csedset.f90:74) `    CG      = max(CG,7.51E-6)`<br>[SedTran-Original/csedset.f90:75](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/csedset.f90:75) `    BD2     = -0.4 - 0.25*LOG10(CG - 7.5E-6)`<br>[SedTran-Original/csedset.f90:76](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/csedset.f90:76) `    CON     = 9.6E-4*(1.E-8)**BD2`<br>[SedTran-Original/csedset.f90:77](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/csedset.f90:77) `    VAL     = CG**(-0.85-BD2)`<br>[SedTran-Original/csedset.f90:78](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/csedset.f90:78) `    CSEDSET = 0.01*CON*VAL` | 일치 | df를 독립 변수로 저장하지 않는다. α_f=10^−8을 식 (6.77)에 대입하여 (10^−8)^{b/2}로 계산한다. Cτ를 7.51×10^−6 이상으로 제한한다. |
| <a id="eq-6-79"></a>[T12・PDF 102쪽・인쇄 89쪽・식 6.79](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=102)<br>`a=B_1\left(C\sqrt{\tau_{xz}^2+\tau_{yz}^2}\right)^{-0.85}`<br>[판독 기록:24](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p091-108.md:24) | [SedTran-Original/csedset.f90:76](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/csedset.f90:76) `    CON     = 9.6E-4*(1.E-8)**BD2`<br>[SedTran-Original/csedset.f90:77](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/csedset.f90:77) `    VAL     = CG**(-0.85-BD2)`<br>[SedTran-Original/csedset.f90:78](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/csedset.f90:78) `    CSEDSET = 0.01*CON*VAL` | 일치 | B_1=9.6×10^−4와 (Cτ)^−0.85가 소거된 최종식에 들어간다. C와 τ를 각각 g/cm³와 cm²/s²로 변환한다. |
| <a id="eq-6-80"></a>[T12・PDF 103쪽・인쇄 90쪽・식 6.80](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=103)<br>`b=-0.8-0.5\log\left(C\sqrt{\tau_{xz}^2+\tau_{yz}^2}-B_2\right)`<br>[판독 기록:25](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p091-108.md:25) | [SedTran-Original/csedset.f90:75](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/csedset.f90:75) `    BD2     = -0.4 - 0.25*LOG10(CG - 7.5E-6)` | 일치 | BD2=b/2를 사용하므로 −0.4−0.25log10(Cτ−7.5×10^−6)가 문서의 절반 지수에 대응한다. 문서 log를 상용로그로 해석한다. |
| <a id="eq-6-81"></a>[T12・PDF 103쪽・인쇄 90쪽・식 6.81](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=103)<br>`w_s=\begin{cases}1.510\times10^{-5}\left(C'\right)^{0.45},&C'<40\\8\times10^{-5},&40\leq C'\leq400\\0.893\times10^{-6}\left(C'\right)^{0.75},&C'>400\end{cases}`<br>[판독 기록:25](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p091-108.md:25) | [SedTran-Original/csedset.f90:85](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/csedset.f90:85) `    CSEDSET = 8.E-5`<br>[SedTran-Original/csedset.f90:86](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/csedset.f90:86) `    if( TMPSED < 40.0  ) CSEDSET = 1.510E-5*(TMPSED**0.45)`<br>[SedTran-Original/csedset.f90:87](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/csedset.f90:87) `    if( TMPSED > 400.0 ) CSEDSET = 0.893E-6*(TMPSED**0.75)` | 일치 | IOPT=4의 세 계수·지수 및 40·400 경계 포함 조건이 같다. |
| <a id="eq-6-82"></a>[T12・PDF 103쪽・인쇄 90쪽・식 6.82](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=103)<br>`C'=\tau C`<br>[판독 기록:25](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p091-108.md:25) | [SedTran-Original/csedset.f90:83](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/csedset.f90:83) `    GG      = 1.E4*SHEAR       ! *** CONVERT FROM M^2/S^2 TO CM^2/S^2`<br>[SedTran-Original/csedset.f90:84](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/csedset.f90:84) `    TMPSED  = GG*SED` | 일치 | τ를 m²/s²에서 cm²/s²로 변환한 뒤 농도와 곱한다. |
| <a id="eq-6-83"></a>[T12・PDF 103쪽・인쇄 90쪽・식 6.83](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=103)<br>`J_o^d=\begin{cases}-w_sC_d\left(\dfrac{\tau_{cd}-\tau_b}{\tau_{cd}}\right)=-w_sP_dC_d,&\tau_b<\tau_{cd}\\0,&\tau_b\geq\tau_{cd}\end{cases}`<br>[판독 기록:25](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p091-108.md:25) | [SedTran-Original/calsed.f90:382](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsed.f90:382) `        if( ISPROBDEP(NS) == 0 )then`<br>[SedTran-Original/calsed.f90:383](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsed.f90:383) `          ! *** KRONE PROBABILITY OF DEPOSITION USING COHESIVE GRAIN STRESS`<br>[SedTran-Original/calsed.f90:384](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsed.f90:384) `          if( TAUBSED(L) < TAUDSS) PROBDEP = (TAUDSS-TAUBSED(L))/TAUDSS`<br>[SedTran-Original/calsed.f90:385](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsed.f90:385) `        elseif( ISPROBDEP(NS) == 1 )then`<br>[SedTran-Original/calsed.f90:386](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsed.f90:386) `          ! *** KRONE PROBABILITY OF DEPOSITION USING TOTAL BED STRESS`<br>[SedTran-Original/calsed.f90:387](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsed.f90:387) `          if( TAUBHYDRO < TAUDSS) PROBDEP = (TAUDSS-TAUBHYDRO)/TAUDSS`<br>[SedTran-Original/calsed.f90:415](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsed.f90:415) `          WSETMP = PROBDEP*WSETA(L,0,NS)`<br>[SedTran-Original/calsed.f90:422](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsed.f90:422) `        SEDF(L,0,NS)     = -WSETMP*SED(L,KSZ(L),NS) + WESE` | 일치 | ISPROBDEP=0·1에서 임계값 미만에만 선형 Krone 확률을 사용한다. 응력 성분 선택과 고농도 예외는 코드에 추가되어 있다. |
| <a id="eq-6-84"></a>[T12・PDF 104쪽・인쇄 91쪽・식 6.84](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=104)<br>`J_o^r=w_rC_r=\dfrac{dm_e}{dt}\left(\dfrac{\tau_b-\tau_{ce}}{\tau_{ce}}\right)^\alpha,\quad\tau_b\geq\tau_{ce}`<br>[판독 기록:26](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p091-108.md:26) | [SedTran-Original/calsed.f90:334](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsed.f90:334) `          if( TAUBSED(L) > TAURS(L,KBT(L)) )then`<br>[SedTran-Original/calsed.f90:335](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsed.f90:335) `            ! *** SURFACE EROSION`<br>[SedTran-Original/calsed.f90:336](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsed.f90:336) `            WESE = WRSPS(L,KBT(L))*VFRBED(L,KBT(L),NS)`<br>[SedTran-Original/calsed.f90:338](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsed.f90:338) `            ! *** SET NORMALIZING SHEAR BASED ON INPUT OPTION`<br>[SedTran-Original/calsed.f90:339](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsed.f90:339) `            if( IWRSP(1) >= 99 )then`<br>[SedTran-Original/calsed.f90:340](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsed.f90:340) `              ! *** SITE SPECIFIC/SPACIALLY VARIABLE TAU FROM SSCOHSEDPMAP`<br>[SedTran-Original/calsed.f90:341](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsed.f90:341) `              TAURTMP = TAUNS(L,KBT(L))`<br>[SedTran-Original/calsed.f90:342](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsed.f90:342) `            elseif( (IWRSP(1) >= 2) .and. (IWRSP(1) < 4) )then`<br>[SedTran-Original/calsed.f90:343](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsed.f90:343) `              ! *** 1 HWANG AND METHA - LAKE OKEECHOBEE`<br>[SedTran-Original/calsed.f90:344](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsed.f90:344) `              ! *** 2 HAMRICK'S MODIFICATION OF SANFORD AND MAA`<br>[SedTran-Original/calsed.f90:345](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsed.f90:345) `              ! *** 3 SAME AS 2 EXCEPT VOID RATIO OF COHESIVE SEDIMENT FRACTION IS USED`<br>[SedTran-Original/calsed.f90:346](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsed.f90:346) `              TAURTMP = TAUR(1)`<br>[SedTran-Original/calsed.f90:347](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsed.f90:347) `            else`<br>[SedTran-Original/calsed.f90:348](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsed.f90:348) `              ! *** Use DIRECTLY INPUT PROPERTIES`<br>[SedTran-Original/calsed.f90:349](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsed.f90:349) `              TAURTMP = TAURS(L,KBT(L))`<br>[SedTran-Original/calsed.f90:350](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsed.f90:350) `            endif`<br>[SedTran-Original/calsed.f90:352](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsed.f90:352) `            TAUE = (TAUBSED(L)-TMPSTR*TAURS(L,KBT(L)))/TAURTMP`<br>[SedTran-Original/calsed.f90:353](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsed.f90:353) `            TAUE = max(TAUE,0.0)`<br>[SedTran-Original/calsed.f90:362](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsed.f90:362) `              WESE = TMPSEDHID*WESE*( TAUE**TEXP(NS) )` | 일치 | IWRSP=0 등 직접 입력 분기에서 표면 침식률에 양의 정규화 초과 응력의 TEXP 거듭제곱을 곱한다. IWRSP=2·3은 분모에 초기 TAUR를 사용한다. IWRSP=4는 임계 응력 차감항을 끈다. |
| <a id="eq-6-85"></a>[T12・PDF 104쪽・인쇄 91쪽・식 6.85](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=104)<br>`J_o^r=w_rC_r=\dfrac{dm_e}{dt}\exp\left(-\beta\left(\dfrac{\tau_b-\tau_{ce}}{\tau_{ce}}\right)^\gamma\right),\quad\tau_b\geq\tau_{ce}`<br>[판독 기록:26](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p091-108.md:26) | [SedTran-Original/calsed.f90:362](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calsed.f90:362) `              WESE = TMPSEDHID*WESE*( TAUE**TEXP(NS) )`<br>[SedTran-Original/csedress.f90:49](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/csedress.f90:49) `    TMPVAL = (1.+VDRO)/(1.+VDR)`<br>[SedTran-Original/csedress.f90:50](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/csedress.f90:50) `    FACTOR = EXP(-TMPVAL)`<br>[SedTran-Original/csedress.f90:51](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/csedress.f90:51) `    CSEDRESS = FACTOR*WRSPO*(1.+VDRO)/(1.+VDR)`<br>[SedTran-Original/csedress.f90:52](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/csedress.f90:52) `  elseif( IOPT == 5 )then`<br>[SedTran-Original/csedress.f90:53](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/csedress.f90:53) `    TMPVAL = (1.+VDRO)/(1.+VDRC)`<br>[SedTran-Original/csedress.f90:54](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/csedress.f90:54) `    FACTOR = EXP(-TMPVAL)`<br>[SedTran-Original/csedress.f90:55](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/csedress.f90:55) `    CSEDRESS = FACTOR*WRSPO*(1.+VDRO)/(1.+VDRC)` | 코드에서 찾지 못함 | 초과 바닥 응력을 음의 β 지수함수에 넣는 침식률 분기를 찾지 못했다. CSEDRESS의 EXP(−TMPVAL)은 간극비 보정이며 초과 응력의 지수함수가 아니다. |
| <a id="eq-6-86"></a>[T12・PDF 104쪽・인쇄 91쪽・식 6.86](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=104)<br>`\tau_{ce}=c\rho_s^d`<br>[판독 기록:26](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p091-108.md:26) | [SedTran-Original/csedtaus.f90:34](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/csedtaus.f90:34) `      TMP = (BULKDEN - 1.065)**0.2`<br>[SedTran-Original/csedtaus.f90:35](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/csedtaus.f90:35) `      CSEDTAUS = 0.001*(0.883*TMP + 0.05)` | 문서에만 | 문서는 관측 기반 일반 관계 τ_ce=cρ_s^d를 소개한다. 구현의 Hwang–Mehta 관계는 습윤 벌크밀도 임계값을 차감한 거듭제곱과 상수를 사용한다. |
| <a id="eq-6-87"></a>[T12・PDF 105쪽・인쇄 92쪽・식 6.87](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=105)<br>`\tau_{ce}=\begin{cases}a\left(\rho_b-\rho_l\right)^b+c,&\rho_b>1.065\\0,&\rho_b\leq1.065\end{cases}`<br>[판독 기록:27](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p091-108.md:27) | [SedTran-Original/csedtaus.f90:30](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/csedtaus.f90:30) `    BULKDEN = 0.001*DENBULK  ! *** TO PREVENT CORRUPTING THE DENBULK VARIABLE`<br>[SedTran-Original/csedtaus.f90:31](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/csedtaus.f90:31) `    if( BULKDEN <= 1.065 )then`<br>[SedTran-Original/csedtaus.f90:32](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/csedtaus.f90:32) `      CSEDTAUS = 1.0E-12`<br>[SedTran-Original/csedtaus.f90:33](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/csedtaus.f90:33) `    else`<br>[SedTran-Original/csedtaus.f90:34](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/csedtaus.f90:34) `      TMP = (BULKDEN - 1.065)**0.2`<br>[SedTran-Original/csedtaus.f90:35](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/csedtaus.f90:35) `      CSEDTAUS = 0.001*(0.883*TMP + 0.05)` | 불일치 | 임계값 위에서는 a=0.883, ρ_b1=1.065, b=0.2, c=0.05와 응력 단위 변환이 대응한다. 임계값 이하에서 문서는 0을 지정한다. 코드는 10^−12를 지정한다. |
| <a id="eq-6-88"></a>[T12・PDF 105쪽・인쇄 92쪽・식 6.88](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=105)<br>`\tau_{ce}=\tau_{ci}\dfrac{\left(1+\varepsilon_r\right)}{\left(1+\varepsilon_b\right)}`<br>[판독 기록:27](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p091-108.md:27) | [SedTran-Original/csedtaus.f90:42](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/csedtaus.f90:42) `    CSEDTAUS = TAUCO*(1. + VDRO)/(1. + VDR)`<br>[SedTran-Original/csedtaus.f90:46](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/csedtaus.f90:46) `    CSEDTAUS = TAUCO*(1. + VDRO)/(1. + VDRC)` | 일치 | IOPT=2·3은 초기 간극비와 현재 간극비의 1+ε 비를 임계 응력에 곱한다. 호출부는 두 현재 간극비에 같은 VDRBED를 전달한다. |
| <a id="eq-6-89"></a>[T12・PDF 105쪽・인쇄 92쪽・식 6.89](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=105)<br>`\varepsilon=\dfrac{\phi}{1-\phi}`<br>[판독 기록:27](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p091-108.md:27) | [SedTran-Original/calbed.f90:53](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calbed.f90:53) `      VOIDCON1 = BEDPORC/(1.-BEDPORC)  `<br>[SedTran-Original/calbed.f90:169](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calbed.f90:169) `              PORBED(L,K)  = VDRBED(L,K) /(1. + VDRBED(L,K))  ` | 일치 | ε=φ/(1−φ)와 역변환 φ=ε/(1+ε)를 사용한다. |
| <a id="eq-6-90"></a>[T12・PDF 105쪽・인쇄 92쪽・식 6.90](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=105)<br>`\tau_{ce}=\tau_{ci}`<br>[판독 기록:27](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p091-108.md:27) | [SedTran-Original/csedtaus.f90:49](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/csedtaus.f90:49) `  elseif( IOPT == 4 )then`<br>[SedTran-Original/csedtaus.f90:50](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/csedtaus.f90:50) `    CSEDTAUS = TAUCO`<br>[SedTran-Original/csedtaus.f90:52](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/csedtaus.f90:52) `  elseif( IOPT == 5 )then`<br>[SedTran-Original/csedtaus.f90:53](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/csedtaus.f90:53) `    CSEDTAUS = TAUCO` | 일치 | IOPT=4·5에서 TAUCO를 그대로 반환한다. |
| <a id="eq-6-91"></a>[T12・PDF 105쪽・인쇄 92쪽・식 6.91](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=105)<br>`\left(\dfrac{\varepsilon H_{bed}}{1+\varepsilon}\right)=\left(\psi_{wc}+\psi_{wn}\right)H_{bed}`<br>[판독 기록:27](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p091-108.md:27) | [SedTran-Original/calbed.f90:65](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calbed.f90:65) `          TMPVALO      = VDRBED(L,K)*HBED(L,K)/(1. + VDRBED(L,K))  `<br>[SedTran-Original/calbed.f90:66](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calbed.f90:66) `          TMPVALN      = VOIDCON1*HBEDTMP/(1. + VOIDCON1)  ` | 일치 | 좌변 εH_bed/(1+ε)는 간극수의 단위 면적당 체적이다. 코드는 전체 간극수 체적을 계산한다. 점착성·비점착성 간극수를 별도 배열로 분할하지 않는다. |
| <a id="eq-6-92"></a>[T12・PDF 106쪽・인쇄 93쪽・식 6.92](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=106)<br>`\left(\dfrac{H_{bed}}{1+\varepsilon}\right)=\left(\psi_{sc}+\psi_{sn}\right)H_{bed}`<br>[판독 기록:28](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p091-108.md:28) | [SedTran-Original/bedinit.f90:274](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/bedinit.f90:274) `              HBED(L,K) = HBED(L,K) + SDEN(NS)*SEDB(L,K,NS)`<br>[SedTran-Original/bedinit.f90:293](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/bedinit.f90:293) `          HBED(L,K) = (1. + VDRBED(L,K))*HBED(L,K)` | 일치 | 각 계급 질량에 밀도 역수를 곱해 고체 체적을 합한 뒤 1+ε를 곱해 바닥 두께를 만든다. |
| <a id="eq-6-93"></a>[T12・PDF 106쪽・인쇄 93쪽・식 6.93](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=106)<br>`\dfrac{\partial}{\partial}\left(H_{bed}\psi_{sc}\right)=\dfrac{\partial}{\partial}\left(H_{bed}\psi_{sn}\right)=0`<br>[판독 기록:28](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p091-108.md:28) | [SedTran-Original/calbed.f90:103](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calbed.f90:103) `                VDRBED(L,K)  = SEDVDRM + (VDRBED1(L,K)-SEDVDRM)*TMPEXP  `<br>[SedTran-Original/calbed.f90:104](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calbed.f90:104) `                TMPTOP = 1. + VDRBED(L,K)  `<br>[SedTran-Original/calbed.f90:105](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calbed.f90:105) `                TMPBOT = 1. + VDRBED1(L,K)  `<br>[SedTran-Original/calbed.f90:106](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calbed.f90:106) `                HBED(L,K) = TMPTOP*HBED1(L,K)/TMPBOT  ` | 일치 | 단순 압밀 단계는 고체 질량을 바꾸지 않고 H_bed/(1+ε)를 유지한다. 문서의 ∂/∂에는 미분 변수 t가 빠져 있다. 시간 미분으로 해석하면 고체 체적 보존과 같다. |
| <a id="eq-6-94"></a>[T12・PDF 106쪽・인쇄 93쪽・식 6.94](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=106)<br>`\varepsilon_c=\dfrac{\psi_{wc}}{\psi_{sc}}`<br>[판독 기록:28](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p091-108.md:28) | [SedTran-Original/calbed.f90:96](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calbed.f90:96) `          TMPEXP = EXP(-DTSED/SEDVRDT)  `<br>[SedTran-Original/calbed.f90:97](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calbed.f90:97) `          do K = 1,KB  `<br>[SedTran-Original/calbed.f90:98](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calbed.f90:98) `            do LP = LF,LL  `<br>[SedTran-Original/calbed.f90:99](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calbed.f90:99) `              L = | <a id="eq-6-108"></a>[T12・PDF 108쪽・인쇄 95쪽・식 6.108](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=108)<br>`H_{bed,k}^{n+1}=H_{bed,k}^*+\Delta t\left(q_{w:k-}-q_{w:k+}\right)`<br>[판독 기록:30](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p091-108.md:30) | [SedTran-Original/calbed.f90:106](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calbed.f90:106) `                HBED(L,K) = TMPTOP*HBED1(L,K)/TMPBOT  `<br>[SedTran-Original/calbed.f90:184](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calbed.f90:184) `              TMPVAL = HBED(L,K)/(1. + VDRBED(L,K))  `<br>[SedTran-Original/calbed.f90:185](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calbed.f90:185) `              QWTRBED(L,K) = QWTRBED(L,K-1) - DELTI*TMPVAL*(VDRBED(L,K)-VDRBED1(L,K))  ` | 일치 | 간극비에서 바닥 두께를 먼저 갱신하고 간극수 유량을 역산한다. 순서를 소거하면 ΔH=Δt(q하단−q상단)과 같다. 식 (6.102)의 Darcy 해법을 구현했다는 뜻은 아니다. |
| <a id="eq-6-109"></a>[T12・PDF 108쪽・인쇄 95쪽・식 6.109](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=108)<br>`\left(\dfrac{H_{bed}}{1+\varepsilon}\right)_k^{n+1}=\left(\dfrac{H_{bed}}{1+\varepsilon}\right)_k^*`<br>[판독 기록:30](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p091-108.md:30) | [SedTran-Original/calbed.f90:104](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calbed.f90:104) `                TMPTOP = 1. + VDRBED(L,K)  `<br>[SedTran-Original/calbed.f90:105](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calbed.f90:105) `                TMPBOT = 1. + VDRBED1(L,K)  `<br>[SedTran-Original/calbed.f90:106](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calbed.f90:106) `                HBED(L,K) = TMPTOP*HBED1(L,K)/TMPBOT  ` | 일치 | H새=H이전(1+ε새)/(1+ε이전)이므로 고체 체적 H/(1+ε)를 보존한다. |
