| <a id="eq-6-111"></a>[T12・PDF 109쪽・인쇄 96쪽・식 6.111](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=109)<br>`E-Pw_sC=0`<br>[판독 기록:13](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p109-126.md:13) | [SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:689](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:689) `      QBFLUX(NS)    = PSUS(L,NS)*ETOT(NS) - DEPTSS(NS)      ! *** Flux in/out of bed for suspended suspended load  (g/cm^2)` | 문서에만 | 평형에서 침식과 퇴적이 같다는 설명용 조건이다. 코드는 이 값이 0이 되도록 강제하지 않고 비정상 순 플럭스를 계산한다. |
| <a id="eq-6-112"></a>[T12・PDF 109쪽・인쇄 96쪽・식 6.112](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=109)<br>`C=\dfrac{E}{Pw_s}`<br>[판독 기록:13](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p109-126.md:13) | [SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:158](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:158) `    DEPTSS(NS) = CTB(NS)*PROB(NS)*(DWS(NS)*DTSEDJ)                                  ! *** Deposition of a size class is equal to the probability of deposition times the settling rate times the time step times the sediment concentration`<br>[SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:689](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:689) `      QBFLUX(NS)    = PSUS(L,NS)*ETOT(NS) - DEPTSS(NS)      ! *** Flux in/out of bed for suspended suspended load  (g/cm^2)` | 문서에만 | 침식·퇴적 매개변수의 비식별성을 설명하는 평형식이다. 코드는 E/(Pw_s)를 수층 농도에 강제 대입하지 않는다. |
| <a id="eq-6-113"></a>[T12・PDF 110쪽・인쇄 97쪽・식 6.113](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=110)<br>`\tau_b=\rho_wc_fV^2`<br>[판독 기록:14](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p109-126.md:14) | [SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_shear.f90:266](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_shear.f90:266) `          SHEAR = FC*VELMAG**2`<br>[SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_shear.f90:315](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_shear.f90:315) `        if( SHEAR > TAU(L)*(1.+GROWTH) )then`<br>[SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_shear.f90:316](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_shear.f90:316) `          TAU(L) = TAU(L) + GROWTH*(SHEAR-TAU(L))`<br>[SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_shear.f90:317](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_shear.f90:317) `          if( QQWV3(L) > 0.0 )then`<br>[SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_shear.f90:318](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_shear.f90:318) `            QQWV3(L) = TAU(L) - SHEARC `<br>[SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_shear.f90:319](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_shear.f90:319) `          endif`<br>[SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_shear.f90:320](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_shear.f90:320) `        else`<br>[SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_shear.f90:321](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_shear.f90:321) `          TAU(L) = SHEAR` | 불일치 | 유동만의 목표 응력은 c_f V²이다. WATERDENS를 곱하지 않으므로 ρ_w=1 g/cm³일 때에만 문서와 같다. 응력 증가를 완화하면 저장한 TAU도 순간 목표값과 달라진다. |
| <a id="eq-6-114"></a>[T12・PDF 110쪽・인쇄 97쪽・식 6.114](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=110)<br>`c_f=\begin{cases}\dfrac{\kappa^2}{\left(\ln\dfrac{11H}{2z_b}\right)^2}&\text{for }H\geq H_{min}\\0.0&\text{for }H<H_{min}\end{cases}`<br>[판독 기록:14](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p109-126.md:14) | [SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_shear.f90:249](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_shear.f90:249) `        if( HP(L) < HPMIN )then`<br>[SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_shear.f90:250](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_shear.f90:250) `          CYCLE    ! *** Leave the shear unchanged from the last time HP >= HPMIN`<br>[SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_shear.f90:261](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_shear.f90:261) `        FC = ( 0.42/LOG(11.*HP(L)/(2.0*ZBTEMP(L))) )**2                             ! *** Friction factor for current only (dimensionless)` | 불일치 | κ=0.42와 로그 계수는 같다. H<HPMIN이면 문서는 0을 지정한다. 코드는 이전 응력을 유지한다. ZBSKIN>0은 지정 입경 거칠기를 사용한다. |
| <a id="eq-6-115"></a>[T12・PDF 111쪽・인쇄 98쪽・식 6.115](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=111)<br>`\rho=\rho_sx_s+\rho_wx_w=\rho_sx_s+\rho_w(1-x_s)`<br>[판독 기록:15](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p109-126.md:15) | [SedTran-Original/calbed.f90:242](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calbed.f90:242) `            BDENBED(L,K) = 1000.*PORBED(L,K) + 0.001*(SEDBT(L,K) + SNDBT(L,K))/HBED(L,K)` | 일치 | 포화 바닥의 물 체적과 고체 질량을 합한 밀도 정의는 같다. 현재 SEDZLJ의 BULKDENS는 건조밀도 입력으로 사용하므로 습윤 벌크밀도와 구분해야 한다. |
| <a id="eq-6-116"></a>[T12・PDF 112쪽・인쇄 99쪽・식 6.116](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=112)<br>`x_s\rho_s=\dfrac{\rho_s(\rho-\rho_w)}{\rho_s-\rho_w}=\dfrac{2.6}{1.6}(\rho-1)`<br>[판독 기록:16](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p109-126.md:16) | [SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedic.f90:258](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedic.f90:258) `      read(10,*) (BDEN(CORE,K),K = 1,KB)             ! *** read in the bulk density of the core `<br>[SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedic.f90:259](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedic.f90:259) `      read(10,'(A80)') STR_LINE      `<br>[SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedic.f90:260](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedic.f90:260) `      read(10,*) WATERDENS, SEDDENS(CORE)          ! *** read in the water density and sediment solid's density`<br>[SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedic.f90:367](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedic.f90:367) `        TSED(K,L)  = TSED0S(K,NCORENO(IL(L),JL(L)))*BULKDENS(K,L)  ! *** TSED  in units of (g/cm^2).`<br>[SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:499](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:499) `                   + SN10*EXP(SN11*LOG(ERATE(K+1,L,NTAU1))+SN01*LOG(ERATE(K,L,NTAU1))) )*BULKDENS(K,L)*SQRT(ONE/SH_SCALE(L))` | 불일치 | 문서는 습윤 벌크밀도에서 ρ_s=2.6·ρ_w=1을 가정해 건조밀도로 변환한다. 코드의 SEDZLJ 질량·침식률 계산은 입력 BULKDENS를 건조밀도로 직접 곱한다. 해당 2.6/1.6 변환을 찾지 못했다. |
| <a id="eq-6-117"></a>[T12・PDF 112쪽・인쇄 99쪽・식 6.117](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=112)<br>`E(\tau)=\left(\dfrac{\tau_{i+1}-\tau}{\tau_{i+1}-\tau_i}\right)E_i+\left(\dfrac{\tau-\tau_i}{\tau_{i+1}-\tau_i}\right)E_{i+1}`<br>[판독 기록:16](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p109-126.md:16) | [SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:492](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:492) `        SN00 = (TAUDD(2)-TAU(L))/(TAUDD(2)-TAUDD(1)) ! *** weighting factor 1 for interpolation`<br>[SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:493](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:493) `        SN10 = (TAUDD(1)-TAU(L))/(TAUDD(1)-TAUDD(2)) ! *** weighting factor 2`<br>[SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:494](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:494) `        SN01 = TSED(K,L)/TSED0(K,L)                  ! *** weighting factor 3`<br>[SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:495](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:495) `        SN11 = (TSED0(K,L)-TSED(K,L))/TSED0(K,L)     ! *** weighting factor 4`<br>[SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:497](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:497) `        if( K+1 <= KB )then  ! *** Modeled erosion rate`<br>[SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:498](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:498) `          ERATEMOD = ( SN00*EXP(SN11*LOG(ERATE(K+1,L,NTAU0))+SN01*LOG(ERATE(K,L,NTAU0))) &`<br>[SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:499](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:499) `                   + SN10*EXP(SN11*LOG(ERATE(K+1,L,NTAU1))+SN01*LOG(ERATE(K,L,NTAU1))) )*BULKDENS(K,L)*SQRT(ONE/SH_SCALE(L))` | 일치 | NSEDFLUME=1에서 응력 구간의 두 침식률을 선형 가중한다. 깊이 방향은 로그 보간이다. 밀도와 SH_SCALE 보정은 이후에 곱한다. |
| <a id="eq-6-118"></a>[T12・PDF 112쪽・인쇄 99쪽・식 6.118](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=112)<br>`\ln[E(T)]=\left(\dfrac{T_0-T}{T_0}\right)\ln(E^j)+\dfrac{T}{T_0}\ln\left(E^{j+1}\right)`<br>[판독 기록:16](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p109-126.md:16) | [SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:494](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:494) `        SN01 = TSED(K,L)/TSED0(K,L)                  ! *** weighting factor 3`<br>[SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:495](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:495) `        SN11 = (TSED0(K,L)-TSED(K,L))/TSED0(K,L)     ! *** weighting factor 4`<br>[SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:497](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:497) `        if( K+1 <= KB )then  ! *** Modeled erosion rate`<br>[SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:498](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:498) `          ERATEMOD = ( SN00*EXP(SN11*LOG(ERATE(K+1,L,NTAU0))+SN01*LOG(ERATE(K,L,NTAU0))) &`<br>[SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:499](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:499) `                   + SN10*EXP(SN11*LOG(ERATE(K+1,L,NTAU1))+SN01*LOG(ERATE(K,L,NTAU1))) )*BULKDENS(K,L)*SQRT(ONE/SH_SCALE(L))` | 불일치 | 문서는 T를 현재 층 두께로 정의하면서 위쪽 E^j에 1−T/T0를 곱한다. 코드는 현재 잔여 질량 비 TSED/TSED0를 위쪽 ERATE(K)에 곱한다. 밀도가 일정하면 현재 두께 비와 잔여 질량 비가 같으므로 문서의 가중치가 반대이다. |
| <a id="eq-6-119"></a>[T12・PDF 113쪽・인쇄 100쪽・식 6.119](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=113)<br>`\tau_{ce}=\rho gd\theta=\rho gd\left\{\dfrac{0.3}{1+1.2d_*}+0.055[1-\exp(-0.02d_*)]\right\}`<br>[판독 기록:17](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p109-126.md:17) | [SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedic.f90:88](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedic.f90:88) `    read(30,*) (TCRE(K),K = 1,NSEDS) `<br>[SedTran-Original/setshld.f90:33](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/setshld.f90:33) `  if(DSR <= 4.0 )then`<br>[SedTran-Original/setshld.f90:34](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/setshld.f90:34) `    THETA = 0.24/DSR`<br>[SedTran-Original/setshld.f90:35](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/setshld.f90:35) `  endif`<br>[SedTran-Original/setshld.f90:36](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/setshld.f90:36) `  if(DSR > 4.0 .and. DSR <= 10.0 )then`<br>[SedTran-Original/setshld.f90:37](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/setshld.f90:37) `    THETA = 0.14/(DSR**0.64)`<br>[SedTran-Original/setshld.f90:38](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/setshld.f90:38) `  endif`<br>[SedTran-Original/setshld.f90:39](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/setshld.f90:39) `  if(DSR > 10.0 .and. DSR <= 20.0 )then`<br>[SedTran-Original/setshld.f90:40](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/setshld.f90:40) `    THETA = 0.04/(DSR**0.1)`<br>[SedTran-Original/setshld.f90:41](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/setshld.f90:41) `  endif`<br>[SedTran-Original/setshld.f90:42](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/setshld.f90:42) `  if(DSR > 20.0 .and. DSR <= 150.0 )then`<br>[SedTran-Original/setshld.f90:43](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/setshld.f90:43) `    THETA = 0.013*(DSR**0.29)`<br>[SedTran-Original/setshld.f90:44](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/setshld.f90:44) `  endif`<br>[SedTran-Original/setshld.f90:45](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/setshld.f90:45) `  if(DSR > 150.0 )then`<br>[SedTran-Original/setshld.f90:46](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/setshld.f90:46) `    THETA = 0.055` | 코드에서 찾지 못함 | SEDZLJ는 계급 임계 침식응력 TCRE를 입력으로 읽는다. Soulsby의 0.3/(1+1.2d*)+0.055[1−exp(−0.02d*)] 계산을 찾지 못했다. 기존 SETSHLD는 다른 구간 함수를 쓴다. |
| <a id="eq-6-120"></a>[T12・PDF 113쪽・인쇄 100쪽・식 6.120](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=113)<br>`d_*=d\left[(\rho_{sd}/\rho_w-1)g/v^2\right]^{1/3}`<br>[판독 기록:17](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p109-126.md:17) | [SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedic.f90:439](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedic.f90:439) `    DISTAR(K) = D50(K)/10000.0*(((SEDDENS(1)/WATERDENS)-1.0)*980.0/0.01**2)**(1.0/3.0)` | 일치 | DISTAR는 d[(ρ_s/ρ_w−1)g/ν²]^(1/3)이다. 코드는 cm 단위에서 g=980, ν=0.01을 사용한다. 입자밀도는 첫 코어의 SEDDENS(1)를 쓴다. |
| <a id="eq-6-121"></a>[T12・PDF 114쪽・인쇄 101쪽・식 6.121](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=114)<br>`\tau_{cs}=\begin{cases}\dfrac{1}{\rho_w}\left(\dfrac{4w_s}{d_*}\right)^2,&\text{for }d\leq400\ \mu\mathrm{m}\\\dfrac{1}{\rho_w}(0.4w_s)^2,&\text{for }d>400\ \mu\mathrm{m}\end{cases}`<br>[판독 기록:18](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p109-126.md:18) | [SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedic.f90:92](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedic.f90:92) `    read(30,*) (TCRSUS(K),K = 1,NSEDS)`<br>[SedTran-Original/csndeqc.f90:79](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/csndeqc.f90:79) `      REY = 1.E4*SNDDIA*( (9.8*(SSG-1.))**0.333 )  `<br>[SedTran-Original/csndeqc.f90:80](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/csndeqc.f90:80) `      if( REY <= 10. ) TAURS = (4.*WS/REY)**2  `<br>[SedTran-Original/csndeqc.f90:81](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/csndeqc.f90:81) `      if( REY  > 10. ) TAURS = 0.16*WS*WS                      ! *** Corrected 2021-06 from 0.016.  0.16 = 0.4^2 from VanRijn 1984` | 코드에서 찾지 못함 | SEDZLJ는 TCRSUS를 입력으로 읽는다. 기존 van Rijn 함수는 d*≤10 경계로 정규화 응력을 계산한다. 문서의 400 μm 경계와 1/ρ_w 계수를 SEDZLJ 자동 임계값 계산에서 찾지 못했다. |
| <a id="eq-6-122"></a>[T12・PDF 115쪽・인쇄 102쪽・식 6.122](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=115)<br>`w_s=\dfrac{v}{d}\left(\sqrt{25+1.2d_*^2}-5\right)^{1.5}`<br>[판독 기록:19](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p109-126.md:19) | [SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedic.f90:439](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedic.f90:439) `    DISTAR(K) = D50(K)/10000.0*(((SEDDENS(1)/WATERDENS)-1.0)*980.0/0.01**2)**(1.0/3.0)`<br>[SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedic.f90:441](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedic.f90:441) `    ! Settling speed (DWS) calculated from D50 (micron) if DWS is < 0`<br>[SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedic.f90:442](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedic.f90:442) `    if( DWSIN(K) == -1 )then`<br>[SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedic.f90:443](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedic.f90:443) `      ! input diameter using Cheng's model (1998).  Settling speed in cm/s`<br>[SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedic.f90:444](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedic.f90:444) `      DWS(K) = 0.01/(D50(K)*0.0001)*(SQRT(25.0+1.2*DISTAR(K)**2)-5.0)**1.5` | 일치 | DWSIN=−1에서 Cheng 식을 계산한다. d는 μm 입력에서 cm로 바꾼다. ν=0.01 cm²/s를 고정한다. |
| <a id="eq-6-123"></a>[T12・PDF 115쪽・인쇄 102쪽・식 6.123](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=115)<br>`w_s=\begin{cases}\dfrac{1}{18}\left[\dfrac{(s-1)gD_s^2}{\nu}\right],&D_s<100\ \mu\mathrm{m}\\10\dfrac{\nu}{D_s}\left\{\left[1+\dfrac{0.01(s-1)gD_s^3}{\nu^2}\right]^{0.5}-1\right\},&100\ \mu\mathrm{m}\leq D_s<1000\ \mu\mathrm{m}\\1.1[(s-1)gD_s]^{0.5},&D_s\geq1000\ \mu\mathrm{m}\end{cases}`<br>[판독 기록:19](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p109-126.md:19) | [SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedic.f90:445](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedic.f90:445) `    !ELSEIF( DWSIN(K) == -2 )then`<br>[SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedic.f90:446](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedic.f90:446) `      ! TBD`<br>[SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedic.f90:447](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedic.f90:447) `    else`<br>[SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedic.f90:448](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedic.f90:448) `       DWS(K) = DWSIN(K)`<br>[SedTran-Original/setstvel.f90:28](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/setstvel.f90:28) `  if( D < 1.0E-4 )then`<br>[SedTran-Original/setstvel.f90:29](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/setstvel.f90:29) `    WSET = SQGPD*RD/18.`<br>[SedTran-Original/setstvel.f90:30](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/setstvel.f90:30) `  endif`<br>[SedTran-Original/setstvel.f90:31](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/setstvel.f90:31) `  if( D >= 1.0E-4 .and. D < 1.E-3 )then`<br>[SedTran-Original/setstvel.f90:32](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/setstvel.f90:32) `    TMP = SQRT(1.+0.01*RD*RD)-1.`<br>[SedTran-Original/setstvel.f90:33](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/setstvel.f90:33) `    WSET = 10.0*SQGPD*TMP/RD`<br>[SedTran-Original/setstvel.f90:34](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/setstvel.f90:34) `  endif`<br>[SedTran-Original/setstvel.f90:35](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/setstvel.f90:35) `  if( D >= 1.E-3 )then`<br>[SedTran-Original/setstvel.f90:36](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/setstvel.f90:36) `    WSET = 1.1*SQGPD` | 코드에서 찾지 못함 | 기존 SETSTVEL에는 계수와 경계가 같은 van Rijn 식이 있다. SEDZLJ의 자동 van Rijn 침강 선택 DWSIN=−2는 주석으로 남아 있다. SEDZLJ는 그 값도 지정 속도로 대입한다. |
| <a id="eq-6-124"></a>[T12・PDF 115쪽・인쇄 102쪽・식 6.124](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=115)<br>`\dfrac{q_s}{q_t}=\begin{cases}0,&\tau^b<\tau_{cs}\\\dfrac{\ln(u_*/w_s)-\ln\left(\sqrt{\tau_{cs}/\rho_w}/w_s\right)}{\ln(4)-\ln\left(\sqrt{\tau_{cs}/\rho_w}/w_s\right)},&\tau^b>\tau_{cs}\ \text{and }\dfrac{u_*}{w_s}<4\\1,&\dfrac{u_*}{w_s}>4\end{cases}`<br>[판독 기록:19](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p109-126.md:19) | [SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_bedload.f90:120](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_bedload.f90:120) `        if( USW(L,NS) < 4.0 )then                                 ! *** "USW(L,NS) < 4.0" is an out of range check`<br>[SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_bedload.f90:121](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_bedload.f90:121) `          if( TAU(L) <= TCRE(NS) .or. USW(L,NS) <= 0.0 )then`<br>[SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_bedload.f90:122](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_bedload.f90:122) `            ! *** Shear is too small to erode anything for current class`<br>[SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_bedload.f90:123](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_bedload.f90:123) `            PSUS(L,NS) = 0.0`<br>[SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_bedload.f90:124](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_bedload.f90:124) `          else      `<br>[SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_bedload.f90:125](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_bedload.f90:125) `            ISKIP(NS) = 0`<br>[SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_bedload.f90:126](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_bedload.f90:126) `            PSUS(L,NS) = max((LOG(USW(L,NS))-LOG(SQRT(TCRSUS(NS))/DWS(NS)))/(LOG(4.0)-LOG(SQRT(TCRSUS(NS))/DWS(NS))),0.0)`<br>[SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_bedload.f90:127](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_bedload.f90:127) `          endif`<br>[SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_bedload.f90:128](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_bedload.f90:128) `        else`<br>[SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_bedload.f90:129](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_bedload.f90:129) `          ! *** Shear is high enough to move all eroded material to suspension`<br>[SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_bedload.f90:130](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_bedload.f90:130) `          PSUS(L,NS) = 1.0` | 불일치 | 로그 비와 4의 상한은 대응한다. 코드의 영 부유분율 조건은 τ≤TCRE이다. 문서의 영 분율 조건은 τ<τ_cs이다. TCRE와 TCRSUS는 독립 입력값이다. 코드는 음의 로그 비를 0으로 제한한다. |
| <a id="eq-6-125"></a>[T12・PDF 116쪽・인쇄 103쪽・식 6.125](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=116)<br>`E_{s,j}=\begin{cases}0,&\tau^b<\tau_{ce}\\\dfrac{q_s}{q_t}f_jE,&r\tau^b\geq\tau_{ce}\end{cases}`<br>[판독 기록:20](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p109-126.md:20) | [SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:572](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:572) `      WHERE( TAU(L) >= TCRE(1:NSEDS) )`<br>[SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:573](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:573) `        ELAY(1:NSEDS)  = PERSED(1:NSEDS,K,L)*EBD`<br>[SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:574](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:574) `        ETOT(1:NSEDS)  = ETOT(1:NSEDS) + ELAY(1:NSEDS)`<br>[SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:575](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:575) `        TTEMP(1:NSEDS) = PERSED(1:NSEDS,K,L)*TSED(K,L) - ELAY(1:NSEDS)               ! *** Remaining mass in layer for each size class`<br>[SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:576](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:576) `      elseWHERE`<br>[SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:577](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:577) `        ETOT(1:NSEDS)  = 0.0`<br>[SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:578](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:578) `        ELAY(1:NSEDS)  = 0.0`<br>[SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:579](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:579) `        TTEMP(1:NSEDS) = PERSED(1:NSEDS,K,L)*TSED(K,L)`<br>[SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:580](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:580) `      ENDWHERE`<br>[SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:689](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:689) `      QBFLUX(NS)    = PSUS(L,NS)*ETOT(NS) - DEPTSS(NS)      ! *** Flux in/out of bed for suspended suspended load  (g/cm^2)` | 일치 | 계급 임계응력 이상에서 f_j E를 침식 질량에 더하고 PSUS를 곱해 부유사로 배분한다. 문서 둘째 조건의 rτ^b는 τ^b로 해석한다. 가용 질량 제한은 추가된다. |
| <a id="eq-6-126"></a>[T12・PDF 116쪽・인쇄 103쪽・식 6.126](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=116)<br>`E_{b,j}=\begin{cases}0,&\tau^b<\tau_{ce}\\\left(1-\dfrac{q_s}{q_t}\right)f_jE,&\tau^b\geq\tau_{ce}\end{cases}`<br>[판독 기록:20](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p109-126.md:20) | [SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:572](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:572) `      WHERE( TAU(L) >= TCRE(1:NSEDS) )`<br>[SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:573](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:573) `        ELAY(1:NSEDS)  = PERSED(1:NSEDS,K,L)*EBD`<br>[SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:574](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:574) `        ETOT(1:NSEDS)  = ETOT(1:NSEDS) + ELAY(1:NSEDS)`<br>[SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:575](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:575) `        TTEMP(1:NSEDS) = PERSED(1:NSEDS,K,L)*TSED(K,L) - ELAY(1:NSEDS)               ! *** Remaining mass in layer for each size class`<br>[SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:576](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:576) `      elseWHERE`<br>[SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:577](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:577) `        ETOT(1:NSEDS)  = 0.0`<br>[SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:578](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:578) `        ELAY(1:NSEDS)  = 0.0`<br>[SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:579](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:579) `        TTEMP(1:NSEDS) = PERSED(1:NSEDS,K,L)*TSED(K,L)`<br>[SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:580](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:580) `      ENDWHERE`<br>[SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:683](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:683) `      TEMP = (ONE-PSUS(L,NS))*ETOT(NS)                      ! *** Calculate erosion into bedload             (g/cm^2)` | 일치 | 총 계급 침식 질량의 1−PSUS를 소류사로 보낸다. 임계응력 미만은 계급 침식을 0으로 둔다. |
| <a id="eq-6-127"></a>[T12・PDF 117쪽・인쇄 104쪽・식 6.127](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=117)<br>`Q_{s,j}=E_{s,j}-D_{s,j}`<br>[판독 기록:21](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p109-126.md:21) | [SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:689](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:689) `      QBFLUX(NS)    = PSUS(L,NS)*ETOT(NS) - DEPTSS(NS)      ! *** Flux in/out of bed for suspended suspended load  (g/cm^2)`<br>[SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:701](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:701) `  SEDF(L,0,1:NSEDS2)     = QBFLUX(1:NSEDS2)*10000._8/DTSEDJ                                              ! *** SEDF-Suspended Sediment flux (g/m^2/s), QBFLUX (g/cm^2)` | 일치 | 부유 침식에서 퇴적을 빼어 QBFLUX를 만든다. QBFLUX는 한 단계 적분 질량이므로 DTSEDJ로 나누어 플럭스로 바꾼다. |
| <a id="eq-6-128"></a>[T12・PDF 117쪽・인쇄 104쪽・식 6.128](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=117)<br>`Q_s=\displaystyle\sum_jQ_{s,j}`<br>[판독 기록:21](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p109-126.md:21) | [SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:689](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:689) `      QBFLUX(NS)    = PSUS(L,NS)*ETOT(NS) - DEPTSS(NS)      ! *** Flux in/out of bed for suspended suspended load  (g/cm^2)`<br>[SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:690](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:690) `    enddo`<br>[SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:691](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:691) `  else`<br>[SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:692](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:692) `    QBFLUX(1:NSEDS2) = ETOT(1:NSEDS2) - DEPTSS(1:NSEDS2)       ! *** Net erosion (+) / Deposition (-) from TSS  (g/cm^2)` | 일치 | 계급별 순 부유사 질량을 배열로 계산한다. 전체 플럭스는 이 배열의 합이다. 별도 총량 변수의 저장을 요구하는 식은 아니다. |
| <a id="eq-6-129"></a>[T12・PDF 117쪽・인쇄 104쪽・식 6.129](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=117)<br>`D_{sj}=P_jw_{sj}C_{sj}`<br>[판독 기록:21](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p109-126.md:21) | [SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:158](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:158) `    DEPTSS(NS) = CTB(NS)*PROB(NS)*(DWS(NS)*DTSEDJ)                                  ! *** Deposition of a size class is equal to the probability of deposition times the settling rate times the time step times the sediment concentration` | 일치 | P_j w_sj C_sj에 DTSEDJ를 곱해 단계 퇴적 질량을 만든다. 단층에서는 바닥 농도 보정을 먼저 적용한다. |
| <a id="eq-6-130"></a>[T12・PDF 117쪽・인쇄 104쪽・식 6.130](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=117)<br>`P_j=\begin{cases}0&\text{for }\tau^b\tau_{cs,j}\\\left(1-\dfrac{\tau^b}{\tau_{cs,j}}\right)&\text{for }\tau^b>\tau_{cs,j}\end{cases}`<br>[판독 기록:21](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p109-126.md:21) | [SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:150](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:150) `    elseif( TAU(L) <= TCRSUS(NS) )then`<br>[SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:151](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:151) `      PROB(NS) = ONE - TAU(L)/(TCRSUS(NS))                          ! *** Krones deposition probability is calculated`<br>[SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:152](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:152) `    else`<br>[SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:153](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:153) `      PROB(NS) = 0.0                                                ! *** Zero probability when TAU > TCRSUS` | 불일치 | PDF 첫 조건은 비교 연산자를 빠뜨린다. PDF 둘째 조건은 τ^b>τ_cs이다. 코드는 τ≤τ_cs일 때 1−τ/τ_cs를 적용한다. 초과 조건에서는 0을 쓴다. |
| <a id="eq-6-131"></a>[T12・PDF 117쪽・인쇄 104쪽・식 6.131](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=117)<br>`P_j(Y)=\operatorname{erf}\left(\dfrac{Y}{2}\right)=\dfrac{2}{\sqrt{\pi}}\displaystyle\int_0^{Y/2}\exp(-\xi^2)\,\mathrm{d}\xi`<br>[판독 기록:21](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p109-126.md:21) | [SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:140](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:140) `      if( PY >= 0.0 )then`<br>[SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:141](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:141) `        PFY = SQR2PI*EXP(-0.5d0*PY*PY)`<br>[SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:142](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:142) `        PX  = ONE/(ONE + 0.33267*PY)`<br>[SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:143](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:143) `        PROB(NS) = ONE - PFY*(0.43618*PX - 0.12016*PX*PX + 0.93729*PX**3)`<br>[SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:144](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:144) `      else`<br>[SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:145](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:145) `        PY  = DABS(PY)`<br>[SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:146](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:146) `        PFY = SQR2PI*EXP(-0.5d0*PY*PY)`<br>[SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:147](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:147) `        PX  = ONE/(ONE + 0.33267*PY)`<br>[SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:148](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:148) `        PROB(NS) = PFY*(0.43618*PX - 0.12016*PX*PX + 0.93729*PX**3)` | 불일치 | 문서는 erf(Y/2)를 쓴다. 코드는 표준 정규 누적분포의 다항 근사를 사용한다. Y=0에서 문서 식은 0이고 코드 근사는 약 0.5이다. 두 함수가 같지 않다. |
| <a id="eq-6-132"></a>[T12・PDF 117쪽・인쇄 104쪽・식 6.132](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=117)<br>`Y=\dfrac{1}{\sigma}\left(\dfrac{\tau_{cs,j}}{\tau^b}-1\right)`<br>[판독 기록:21](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p109-126.md:21) | [SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:139](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:139) `      PY  = 1.7544*(TCRSUS(NS)/(TAU(L) + 1.0D-18)-ONE)              ! *** 1.7544 = 1/sigma =  1./0.57 from Gessler` | 일치 | Y=(τ_cs/τ−1)/σ를 사용한다. 코드는 1/σ=1.7544와 분모의 10^−18을 사용한다. |
| <a id="eq-6-133"></a>[T12・PDF 118쪽・인쇄 105쪽・식 6.133](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=118)<br>`P_j=1-F(Y)(0.4632X-0.1202X^2+0.9373X^3)`<br>[판독 기록:22](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p109-126.md:22) | [SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:143](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:143) `        PROB(NS) = ONE - PFY*(0.43618*PX - 0.12016*PX*PX + 0.93729*PX**3)`<br>[SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:148](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:148) `        PROB(NS) = PFY*(0.43618*PX - 0.12016*PX*PX + 0.93729*PX**3)` | 불일치 | 문서 다항 계수는 0.4632·−0.1202·0.9373이다. SEDZLJ 코드는 0.43618·−0.12016·0.93729를 쓴다. 첫 계수의 숫자 순서가 다르다. |
| <a id="eq-6-134"></a>[T12・PDF 118쪽・인쇄 105쪽・식 6.134](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=118)<br>`F(Y)=\dfrac{1}{(2\pi)^{1/2}}e^{-\frac{1}{2}Y^2}`<br>[판독 기록:22](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p109-126.md:22) | [SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:105](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:105) `  SQR2PI = 1. / SQRT(2.*PI)`<br>[SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:141](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:141) `        PFY = SQR2PI*EXP(-0.5d0*PY*PY)` | 일치 | (2π)^−1/2 exp(−Y²/2)를 계산한다. |
| <a id="eq-6-135"></a>[T12・PDF 118쪽・인쇄 105쪽・식 6.135](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=118)<br>`X=\dfrac{1}{(1+0.33267Y)}`<br>[판독 기록:22](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p109-126.md:22) | [SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:142](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:142) `        PX  = ONE/(ONE + 0.33267*PY)` | 일치 | 0.33267 계수가 같다. 음의 Y에서는 절댓값으로 바꾼 뒤 X를 계산한다. |
| <a id="eq-6-136"></a>[T12・PDF 118쪽・인쇄 105쪽・식 6.136](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=118)<br>`P_j=1-P(\lvert Y\rvert)`<br>[판독 기록:22](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p109-126.md:22) | [SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:145](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:145) `        PY  = DABS(PY)`<br>[SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:146](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:146) `        PFY = SQR2PI*EXP(-0.5d0*PY*PY)`<br>[SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:147](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:147) `        PX  = ONE/(ONE + 0.33267*PY)`<br>[SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:148](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:148) `        PROB(NS) = PFY*(0.43618*PX - 0.12016*PX*PX + 0.93729*PX**3)` | 일치 | 음의 Y에서 양의 절댓값 분포의 보완값을 사용한다. 다항 계수 차이는 식 (6.133)에 기록했다. |
| <a id="eq-6-137"></a>[T12・PDF 118쪽・인쇄 105쪽・식 6.137](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=118)<br>`\dfrac{\partial(mC_b)}{\partial t}=\dfrac{\partial(mq_{bx})}{\partial x}+\dfrac{\partial(mq_{by})}{\partial y}+Q_b`<br>[판독 기록:22](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p109-126.md:22) | [SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_bedload.f90:284](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_bedload.f90:284) `        CBL(L,NS) = CBL(L,NS) + ( DXYIPCM(L)*DTSEDJ*( QSBDLDX(L,NS)-QSBDLDX(LE,NS) + QSBDLDY(L,NS)-QSBDLDY(LN,NS) ) + QBLFLUX(L,NS) )` | 불일치 | 문서는 플럭스 발산을 우변에서 더한다. 코드는 서−동+남−북의 유입−유출을 더한다. 이류 발산의 부호가 반대이다. |
| <a id="eq-6-138"></a>[T12・PDF 119쪽・인쇄 106쪽・식 6.138](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=119)<br>`q_b=u_bC_b`<br>[판독 기록:23](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p109-126.md:23) | [SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_bedload.f90:234](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_bedload.f90:234) `            QSBDLDX(L,NS) = SUB(L)*DXUCM(LW)*CBL(LW,NS)*UBL(L,NS)    ! *** FLOWING EAST`<br>[SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_bedload.f90:243](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_bedload.f90:243) `            QSBDLDY(L,NS) = SVB(L)*DYVCM(LS)*CBL(LS,NS)*VBL(L,NS)    ! *** FLOWING NORTH` | 일치 | 소류사 면 속도에 상류 단위 면적당 질량 CBL과 면 너비를 곱한다. CBL은 수층 체적 농도와 다른 단위이다. |
| <a id="eq-6-139"></a>[T12・PDF 119쪽・인쇄 106쪽・식 6.139](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=119)<br>`u_b=1.5T^{0.6}[(\rho_s-1)gd]^{0.5}`<br>[판독 기록:23](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p109-126.md:23) | [SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_bedload.f90:150](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_bedload.f90:150) `          BLVEL(L,NS) = 1.5*TRANS(L,NS)**0.6*SQRT(((SEDDENS(NCORENO(IL(L),JL(L)))/WATERDENS) -1.0)*980.0*D50(NS)/10000.0)    ! *** eqn. 20a  (cm/s)` | 일치 | 1.5와 T^0.6 및 √((ρ_s/ρ_w−1)gd)가 대응한다. 문서 ρ_s−1은 비중 표기일 때에만 같다. 코드는 코어별 입자밀도를 사용한다. |
| <a id="eq-6-140"></a>[T12・PDF 119쪽・인쇄 106쪽・식 6.140](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=119)<br>`h_b=3dd_*^{0.6}T^{0.9}`<br>[판독 기록:23](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p109-126.md:23) | [SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_bedload.f90:148](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_bedload.f90:148) `          DZBL(L,NS)  = D50(NS)/10000.0*0.3*DISTAR(NS)**0.7*SQRT(TRANS(L,NS))   ! *** eqn. 20b`<br>[SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_bedload.f90:149](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_bedload.f90:149) `          DZBL(L,NS)  = min(DZBL(L,NS), HPCM(L))                                ! *** Don't allow bedload height to exceed water column depth` | 불일치 | 문서는 3d d*^0.6 T^0.9를 쓴다. 코드는 0.3d d*^0.7 T^0.5를 쓴다. 계수가 1/10이고 두 지수가 다르다. 코드는 수심 상한도 적용한다. |
| <a id="eq-6-141"></a>[T12・PDF 119쪽・인쇄 106쪽・식 6.141](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=119)<br>`T=\dfrac{\tau^b-\tau_{ce}}{\tau_{ce}}`<br>[판독 기록:23](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p109-126.md:23) | [SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_bedload.f90:147](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_bedload.f90:147) `          TRANS(L,NS) = max((TAU(L)-TCRE(NS))/TCRE(NS),0.0)                     ! *** eqn. 21` | 일치 | 정규화 초과 응력 (τ−τ_ce)/τ_ce를 사용한다. 음의 값은 0으로 제한한다. |
| <a id="eq-6-142"></a>[T12・PDF 119쪽・인쇄 106쪽・식 6.142](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=119)<br>`Q_b=E_b-D_b`<br>[판독 기록:23](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p109-126.md:23) | [SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:683](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:683) `      TEMP = (ONE-PSUS(L,NS))*ETOT(NS)                      ! *** Calculate erosion into bedload             (g/cm^2)`<br>[SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:684](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:684) `      EBL(L,NS)   = TEMP*10000.                             ! *** Save erosion into bedload                  (g/m^2)`<br>[SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:685](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:685) `      DBL(L,NS)   = DEPBL(NS)*10000.                        ! *** Save deposition from bedload               (g/m^2)`<br>[SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:686](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:686) `      QBLFLUX(L,NS) = TEMP - DEPBL(NS)                      ! *** Flux in/out of bed for bedload             (g/cm^2)` | 일치 | 소류사 침식 질량에서 소류사 퇴적 질량을 뺀다. |
| <a id="eq-6-143"></a>[T12・PDF 119쪽・인쇄 106쪽・식 6.143](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=119)<br>`D_b=Pw_sC_b`<br>[판독 기록:23](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p109-126.md:23) | [SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:199](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:199) `      DEPBL(NS) = PROBVR(NS)*CBL(L,NS)*(DWS(NS)*DTSEDJ)       ! *** Calculate Bedload Deposition (DEPBL)  (g/cm^2)` | 일치 | P w_s C_b에 시간 간격을 곱해 단계 퇴적 질량을 계산한다. 가용 질량은 이후에 제한한다. |
| <a id="eq-6-144"></a>[T12・PDF 119쪽・인쇄 106쪽・식 6.144](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=119)<br>`E_b=Pw_sC_e`<br>[판독 기록:23](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p109-126.md:23) | [SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:190](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:190) `      CSEDSS = SMASS(NS)/(DWS(NS)*DTSEDJ)                     ! *** Concentration eroded into bedload from the last time step`<br>[SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:191](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:191) `      CSEDVR(NS) = (0.18*2.65*TRANS(L,NS)/DISTAR(NS)*0.65)    ! *** van Rijn's (1981, Eq. 21) equilibrium bedload concentration (here TRANS is the transport parameter for bedload calculations)`<br>[SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:192](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:192) `      if( CSEDVR(NS) <= 0.0 )then                             ! *** If there is no equilibrium bedload available`<br>[SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:193](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:193) `        PROBVR(NS) = 1.0                                      ! *** then deposition probability is unity`<br>[SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:194](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:194) `      else`<br>[SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:195](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:195) `        PROBVR(NS) = min(CSEDSS/CSEDVR(NS),1.0)               ! *** van Rijn probability of deposition from bedload`<br>[SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:683](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:683) `      TEMP = (ONE-PSUS(L,NS))*ETOT(NS)                      ! *** Calculate erosion into bedload             (g/cm^2)` | 문서에만 | 평형 소류사 침식률을 퇴적계수로 표현한 관계이다. 현재 침식 E_b는 SEDFlume 질량과 PSUS에서 계산한다. E_b=P w_s C_e를 독립 침식식으로 계산하지 않는다. |
| <a id="eq-6-145"></a>[T12・PDF 119쪽・인쇄 106쪽・식 6.145](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=119)<br>`P=\dfrac{E_b}{w_sC_e}`<br>[판독 기록:23](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p109-126.md:23) | [SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:190](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:190) `      CSEDSS = SMASS(NS)/(DWS(NS)*DTSEDJ)                     ! *** Concentration eroded into bedload from the last time step`<br>[SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:191](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:191) `      CSEDVR(NS) = (0.18*2.65*TRANS(L,NS)/DISTAR(NS)*0.65)    ! *** van Rijn's (1981, Eq. 21) equilibrium bedload concentration (here TRANS is the transport parameter for bedload calculations)`<br>[SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:192](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:192) `      if( CSEDVR(NS) <= 0.0 )then                             ! *** If there is no equilibrium bedload available`<br>[SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:193](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:193) `        PROBVR(NS) = 1.0                                      ! *** then deposition probability is unity`<br>[SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:194](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:194) `      else`<br>[SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:195](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:195) `        PROBVR(NS) = min(CSEDSS/CSEDVR(NS),1.0)               ! *** van Rijn probability of deposition from bedload` | 불일치 | 문서는 침식률 E_b로 P를 계산한다. 코드는 이전 소류사 가용 질량 CBL/Δt에서 CSEDSS를 계산한다. P를 1 이하로 제한한다. 침식 질량과 기존 소류사 가용 질량이 다르면 결과가 다르다. |
| <a id="eq-6-146"></a>[T12・PDF 120쪽・인쇄 107쪽・식 6.146](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=120)<br>`C_e=0.117\dfrac{\rho_sT}{d_*}`<br>[판독 기록:24](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p109-126.md:24) | [SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:191](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:191) `      CSEDVR(NS) = (0.18*2.65*TRANS(L,NS)/DISTAR(NS)*0.65)    ! *** van Rijn's (1981, Eq. 21) equilibrium bedload concentration (here TRANS is the transport parameter for bedload calculations)` | 불일치 | 0.18×0.65=0.117은 같다. 문서는 가변 ρ_s를 사용한다. 코드는 2.65를 고정한다. 코어의 SEDDENS를 이 농도식에 사용하지 않는다. |
| <a id="eq-6-147"></a>[T12・PDF 120쪽・인쇄 107쪽・식 6.147](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=120)<br>`P_j=\dfrac{f_jE_b}{w_{sj}f_jC_{ej}}=\dfrac{E_b}{w_{sj}C_{ej}}`<br>[판독 기록:24](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p109-126.md:24) | [SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:190](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:190) `      CSEDSS = SMASS(NS)/(DWS(NS)*DTSEDJ)                     ! *** Concentration eroded into bedload from the last time step`<br>[SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:191](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:191) `      CSEDVR(NS) = (0.18*2.65*TRANS(L,NS)/DISTAR(NS)*0.65)    ! *** van Rijn's (1981, Eq. 21) equilibrium bedload concentration (here TRANS is the transport parameter for bedload calculations)`<br>[SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:192](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:192) `      if( CSEDVR(NS) <= 0.0 )then                             ! *** If there is no equilibrium bedload available`<br>[SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:193](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:193) `        PROBVR(NS) = 1.0                                      ! *** then deposition probability is unity`<br>[SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:194](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:194) `      else`<br>[SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:195](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:195) `        PROBVR(NS) = min(CSEDSS/CSEDVR(NS),1.0)               ! *** van Rijn probability of deposition from bedload` | 불일치 | 문서는 f_j E_b를 f_j w_sj C_ej로 나눈다. 코드는 계급 CBL/Δt를 계급 w_sj C_ej로 나눈다. 기존 소류사 질량을 분자로 사용하는 차이는 식 (6.145)와 같다. |
| <a id="eq-6-148"></a>[T12・PDF 120쪽・인쇄 107쪽・식 6.148](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=120)<br>`T_a=2d_{50}\dfrac{\tau^b}{\tau_{ce}}`<br>[판독 기록:24](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p109-126.md:24) | [SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedic.f90:113](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedic.f90:113) `    read(10,*) TACTM !read in active layer multiplier`<br>[SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:273](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:273) `  if( TAU(L)/TAUCRIT < 1.0 )then`<br>[SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:274](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:274) `    TACT = TACTM*D50AVG(L)*(BULKDENS(1,L)/10000.0)`<br>[SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:275](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:275) `  else`<br>[SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:276](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedzlj.f90:276) `    TACT = TACTM*D50AVG(L)*(TAU(L)/TAUCRIT)*(BULKDENS(1,L)/10000.0)` | 불일치 | 코드는 계수 2 대신 입력 TACTM을 사용한다. τ/τ_ce가 1 미만이면 비를 1로 제한한다. TACT는 건조밀도를 곱한 활성층 질량 기준량이다. 같은 길이로 변환하면 TACTM·d50·max(1,τ/τ_ce)이다. |

### 2.6 계수・기본값・선택 스위치

문서의 추천값과 카드 예시를 코드의 고정 기본값과 구분했다. 입력으로 읽는 계수에는 입력 지점을 인용했다. 실행 조건이 다른 계수는 같은 기본값으로 묶지 않았다.

| 항목 | 문서 원문・출처 | 코드 원문 줄 | 판정 | 계수・선택 조건의 대조 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| K01 Galperin 기본 계수 | [T12・PDF 28쪽・인쇄 15쪽・Table 2.1](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=28)<br>`Galperin et al. (1988)` | [calavb.f90:44](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calavb.f90:44) `  ! ***  GALPERIN (DEFAULT)`<br>[calavb.f90:45](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calavb.f90:45) `  SFAV0= 0.392010`<br>[calavb.f90:46](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calavb.f90:46) `  SFAV1= 7.760050`<br>[calavb.f90:47](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calavb.f90:47) `  SFAV2 = 34.676440`<br>[calavb.f90:48](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calavb.f90:48) `  SFAV3= 6.127200`<br>[calavb.f90:49](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calavb.f90:49) `  SFAB0= 0.493928`<br>[calavb.f90:50](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calavb.f90:50) `  SFAB1 = 34.676440`<br>[calavb.f90:51](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calavb.f90:51) `  RIQMIN = -0.999/SFAB1` | 일치 | 표의 K0·R1⁻¹·R2⁻¹·R3⁻¹는 0.493928·7.760050·34.676440·6.127200이다. B(L,K+1)`<br>[calqq2t.f90:268](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calqq2t.f90:268) `          CTE3TMP = CTE3`<br>[calqq2t.f90:269](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calqq2t.f90:269) `          if( DELB < 0.0 ) CTE3TMP = CTE1`<br>[calqq2t.f90:271](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calqq2t.f90:271) `          LN = LNC(L)`<br>[calqq2t.f90:272](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calqq2t.f90:272) `          LE = LEC(L)`<br>[calqq2t.f90:274](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calqq2t.f90:274) `          PQQB = AB(L,K)*GP*HP(L)*DZIG(L,K)*(B(L,K+1) - B(L,K))`<br>[calqq2t.f90:276](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calqq2t.f90:276) `          PQQU = AV(L,K)*DZIGSD4U(L,K)*(U(LE,K+1)-U(LE,K) + U(L,K+1)-U(L,K))**2  `<br>[calqq2t.f90:277](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calqq2t.f90:277) `          PQQV = AV(L,K)*DZIGSD4V(L,K)*(V(LN,K+1)-V(LN,K) + V(L,K+1)-V(L,K))**2  `<br>[calqq2t.f90:278](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calqq2t.f90:278) `          PQQ  = DELT*( PQQB+PQQU+PQQV+PQQVEGE(L,K)+PQQMHKE(L,K)+PQQSUPE(L,K) )`<br>[calqq2t.f90:279](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calqq2t.f90:279) `          UUU(L,K) = UUU(L,K) + 2.*PQQ`<br>[calqq2t.f90:280](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calqq2t.f90:280) `          PQQL = DELT*HP(L)*(CTE3TMP*PQQB + CTE1*(PQQU+PQQV) + CE4VEG*PQQVEGE(L,K) + CE4MHK*PQQMHKE(L,K) + CE4SUP*PQQSUPE(L,K))` | 불일치 | 카드 예시의 VKC=0.4·B1=16.6·B2=10.1·E1=1.8·E2=1·E3=1.8·E4=1.33·E5=0.25는 입력값이다. 코드가 이 값을 공통 기본값으로 강제하지 않는다. CTE2는 미사용이다. CTE3TMP는 성층에 따라 CTE1로 바뀐다. |

| K07 길이·RIQ·벽 선택 | [models/EFDC/raw/manuals/confluence/spaces/EK/pages/EFDC_Explorer_12_Knowledge_Base/EFDC_Explorer_12_User_Guide/Model_Control_Form/Modules/Hydrodynamics_Module/Turbulence.md:42](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/confluence/spaces/EK/pages/EFDC_Explorer_12_Knowledge_Base/EFDC_Explorer_12_User_Guide/Model_Control_Form/Modules/Hydrodynamics_Module/Turbulence.md:42) `The *Vertical Turbulence Limiting Options* provide a drop down menu that allows the user to choose from three options: no length scale and RIQMAX limitation; to limit RIQMAX in the stability function only; and finally, to limit both the length scale and RIQMAX. When using multi-layer models and vertical turbulence is limiting, the *Limit Length Scale and Limit RIQMAX* option provides runs with less likelihood of model crashes.` | [input.f90:669](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/input.f90:669) `    read(1,*,IOSTAT = ISO) ISTOPT(0), ISSQL, ISAVBMX, ISFAVB, ISINWV, ISLLIM, IFPROX, XYRATIO, BC_EDGEFACTOR`<br>[calavb.f90:75](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calavb.f90:75) `  RIQMAXX = 1.E32`<br>[calavb.f90:76](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calavb.f90:76) `  if( ISLLIM >= 1 ) RIQMAXX = RIQMAX`<br>[calqq2t.f90:458](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calqq2t.f90:458) `        if( DELB > 1.0E-12 .and. ISLLIM == 2 )then`<br>[calqq2t.f90:459](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calqq2t.f90:459) `          DMLMAX = SQRT(RIQMAX)*SQRT(QQ(L,K)/(G*HP(L)*DZIG(L,K)*DELB))`<br>[calqq2t.f90:460](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calqq2t.f90:460) `          DML(L,K) = min(DMLMAX,DMLTMP)`<br>[calqq2t.f90:461](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calqq2t.f90:461) `          QQL(L,K) = QQ(L,K)*DML(L,K)`<br>[aaefdc.f90:1520](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/aaefdc.f90:1520) `      if( IFPROX == 0 ) FPROX(L,K) = 0.`<br>[aaefdc.f90:1521](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/aaefdc.f90:1521) `      if( K /= KC )then`<br>[aaefdc.f90:1522](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/aaefdc.f90:1522) `        ! *** Mellor Yamada, 1982 (parabolic shape)`<br>[aaefdc.f90:1523](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/aaefdc.f90:1523) `        if( IFPROX == 1 ) FPROX(L,K) = 1./(VKC*Z(L,K))**2 + 1./(VKC*(1. - Z(L,K)))**2`<br>[aaefdc.f90:1524](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/aaefdc.f90:1524) `        ! Blumberg et al., 1992 (correction for open channel flow)`<br>[aaefdc.f90:1525](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/aaefdc.f90:1525) `        if( IFPROX == 2 ) FPROX(L,K) = (1./(VKC*Z(L,K))**2) + CTE5*(1./(VKC*(1.-Z(L,K)))**2)/(CTE4+0.00001)`<br>[aaefdc.f90:1526](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/aaefdc.f90:1526) `        ! *** Burchard et al., 1998 (symmetric linear shape)`<br>[aaefdc.f90:1527](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/aaefdc.f90:1527) `        if( IFPROX == 3 ) FPROX(L,K) = 1./(VKC*MIN(Z(L,K),(1. - Z(L,K))))**2`<br>[aaefdc.f90:1528](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/aaefdc.f90:1528) `        ! *** Burchard et al., 2001 (simulation of an infinitely deep basin)`<br>[aaefdc.f90:1529](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/aaefdc.f90:1529) `        if( IFPROX == 4 ) FPROX(L,K) = 1./(VKC*(1. - Z(L,K)))**2` | 불일치 | ISLLIM=0·1·2의 제한 의미는 문서와 대응한다. IFPROX는 문서의 세 옵션보다 많은 0..4 분기를 가진다. RIQMAX·QQMIN·QQLMIN·DMLMIN은 C13 입력이다. |
| K08 HMD·물질 확산 스위치 | [models/EFDC/raw/manuals/confluence/spaces/EK/pages/EFDC_Explorer_12_Knowledge_Base/EFDC_Explorer_12_User_Guide/Model_Control_Form/Modules/Hydrodynamics_Module/Turbulence.md:34](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/confluence/spaces/EK/pages/EFDC_Explorer_12_Knowledge_Base/EFDC_Explorer_12_User_Guide/Model_Control_Form/Modules/Hydrodynamics_Module/Turbulence.md:34) `When Activate HMD with *Smagorinsky with Wall Drag and WC Diffusion* is selected (ISHMD=2), then the full HMD is used as well as wall effects. This option also applies diffusivities to all constituent transport, which includes salinity and temperatures. This option should be used when simulating a very uniform flow system, the constituent transport is needed. The resulting horizontal diffusivity from these options can be viewed from within *ViewPlan*provided velocities are also available. The vertical eddy viscosities and diffusivities (AVO & ABO arrays in EFDC, respectively) can also be viewed if the user configures the Internal Array Viewer (See [Appendix A](/wiki/spaces/CVLKB/pages/2818055/Appendix+A+-+Grid+Formats)) to include these arrays.` | [calhdmf.f90:121](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calhdmf.f90:121) `    if( ISHDMF == 1 .or. | K12 선형 바람 계수 표 | [T12・PDF 34쪽・인쇄 21쪽・Table 2.2](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf#page=34)<br>`Table 2.2. Values of Different Linear Wind Drag Relationships.` | [input.f90:868](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/input.f90:868) `    read(1,*,IOSTAT = ISO) ICYCLONE, RHO_A, PINF, THETAMAX, WDRAG1, WDRAG2, CDRAG1, CDRAG2`<br>[caltsxy.f90:795](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caltsxy.f90:795) `      if( U10 <= WDRAG1 )then`<br>[caltsxy.f90:796](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caltsxy.f90:796) `        CD10 = CDRAG1`<br>[caltsxy.f90:797](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caltsxy.f90:797) `      elseif( U10 >= WDRAG2 )then`<br>[caltsxy.f90:798](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caltsxy.f90:798) `        CD10 = CDRAG2`<br>[caltsxy.f90:799](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caltsxy.f90:799) `      else`<br>[caltsxy.f90:800](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caltsxy.f90:800) `        CD10 = CDRAG1 + (CDRAG2 - CDRAG1)/(WDRAG2 - WDRAG1)*(U10 - WDRAG1)` | 문서에만 | 표는 W1·W2·C1·C2의 문헌별 조합을 제공한다. C1·C2의 표 단위는 10⁻³이다. 엔진은 WDRAG1·WDRAG2·CDRAG1·CDRAG2를 읽는다. 문헌 이름으로 표 수치를 자동 대입하는 경로를 찾지 못했다. 표의 순서는 W1(m/s)·W2(m/s)·C1(10⁻³)·C2(10⁻³)이다. Francis는 1·25·1.3·32.5이다. Sheppard는 1·20·0.914·3.08이다. Wilson은 2.8·20·1.1·2.6이다. Deacon–Webb는 1·14·1.07·1.98이다. Heaps는 5·19.2·0.565·2.513이다. Smith–Banke는 6·21·1.06·2.185이다. Garratt는 4·21·1.018·2.157이다. Large–Pond는 10·26·1.14·2.18이다. Wu는 1·80·0.865·6.0이다. Anderson은 4.5·21·0.81·1.98이다. Yelland–Taylor는 6·26·1.02·2.42이다. Yelland et al.은 6·26·0.926·2.346이다. |

Run 1은 0.74·0.588·0.063이다. Composite는 0.62·0.667·0.050이다. Very Clear Water는 0.4·0.200·0.025이다. Jerlov I는 0.58·2.857·0.043이다. Jerlov I 상부 50 m는 0.68·0.833·0.036이다. IA는 0.62·1.667·0.050이다. IB는 0.67·1.000·0.059이다. II는 0.77·0.667·0.071이다. III는 0.78·0.714·0.127이다. 엔진은 FSWRATF·SWRATNF·SWRATNS를 읽는다. 물 유형 이름으로 표 값을 고르는 분기를 찾지 못했다. |

| K22 기존 퇴적물 자동 임계값·침강값 | [models/EFDC/raw/manuals/confluence/spaces/EK/pages/EFDC_Explorer_12_Knowledge_Base/EFDC_Explorer_12_User_Guide/Model_Control_Form/Modules/Sediment/Original_EFDC_Sediment_Model.md:42](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/confluence/spaces/EK/pages/EFDC_Explorer_12_Knowledge_Base/EFDC_Explorer_12_User_Guide/Model_Control_Form/Modules/Sediment/Original_EFDC_Sediment_Model.md:42) `Figure 4 shows the form with the *Non-Cohesives Suspended* tab. It is similar in function and operation to the *Cohesives* tab. Grain size for each non-cohesive class is required for the sediment transport computations. This value is also used as the grain size for the d**50** calculations. A number of options for computing equilibrium concentrations are available in the *Equilibrium Conc* frame. This includes the option of equilibrium concentrations calculated from Sedflume data with or without critical shear stress. Setting the settling velocity or the critical shear stress values to numbers <0 results in EFDC computing those parameters values using the Van Rijn equations (1984a, 1984b). Pressing F2 for help pops up information relevant to the current input field.` | [input.f90:2167](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/input.f90:2167) `        if( WSEDO(NS) < 0.0 )then`<br>[input.f90:2168](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/input.f90:2168) `          WSEDO(NS) = SETSTVEL(SEDDIA(NS),SSG(NS))`<br>[input.f90:2207](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/input.f90:2207) `        if( TAUR(NS) < 0.0 )then`<br>[input.f90:2208](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/input.f90:2208) `          call SETSHLD(TAUR(NS),TCSHIELDS(NS),SEDDIA(NS),SSG(NS),DSTR,USTR)` | 일치 | 기존 퇴적물은 음의 WSEDO에서 SETSTVEL을 호출한다. 음의 TAUR에서는 SETSHLD를 호출한다. 계급별 값을 직접 지정한 경우에는 자동 계산을 우회한다. |
| K23 SEDZLJ의 독립 물성 입력 | [models/EFDC/raw/manuals/confluence/spaces/EK/pages/EFDC_Explorer_12_Knowledge_Base/EFDC_Explorer_12_User_Guide/Model_Control_Form/Modules/Sediment/SEDZLJ_Sediment_Model.md:62](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/confluence/spaces/EK/pages/EFDC_Explorer_12_Knowledge_Base/EFDC_Explorer_12_User_Guide/Model_Control_Form/Modules/Sediment/SEDZLJ_Sediment_Model.md:62) `In *Sediment Information* frame, there is *Sediment Info* button reports the critical shear stresses and settling velocities computed based on the particle sizes entered in the *Sediment Class Properties* table. The critical shear stresses and settling velocities are calculated using Van Rijn’s equations (Van Rijn, et al 1984), and the resulting values are provided in the pop-up window. However, those values are calculated only to provide users a guideline for those transport parameters, and EFDC+ Explorer would not use the values in the pop-up window when generating the SEDZLJ model input file (bed.sdf).` | [SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedic.f90:88](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedic.f90:88) `    read(30,*) (TCRE(K),K = 1,NSEDS) `<br>[SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedic.f90:92](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedic.f90:92) `    read(30,*) (TCRSUS(K),K = 1,NSEDS)`<br>[SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedic.f90:96](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedic.f90:96) `    read(30,*) (DWSIN(K),K = 1,NSEDS)`<br>[SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedic.f90:445](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedic.f90:445) `    !ELSEIF( DWSIN(K) == -2 )then`<br>[SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedic.f90:446](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-SEDZLJ/s_sedic.f90:446) `      ! | K24 압밀 선택 | [models/EFDC/raw/manuals/confluence/spaces/EK/pages/EFDC_Explorer_12_Knowledge_Base/EFDC_Explorer_12_User_Guide/Model_Control_Form/Modules/Sediment/Original_EFDC_Sediment_Model.md:70](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/confluence/spaces/EK/pages/EFDC_Explorer_12_Knowledge_Base/EFDC_Explorer_12_User_Guide/Model_Control_Form/Modules/Sediment/Original_EFDC_Sediment_Model.md:70) `*Bed Consolidation & Mechanics Options* frame provides input to the main sediment bed consolidation options. The *Bed Mechanics* option must be 0-None, 1-Simple, or 2-Finite Strain. Option 2 is very a specialized approach and requires a detailed understanding of the of the Finite Strain implementation in the FORTRAN code. The consolidation rate is in seconds, not 1/seconds as earlier versions of EFDC.INP reported. The rate will usually be >100,000 seconds. The impact of the rate of consolidation can easily be tested using EFDC+ Explorer's *Single Column Sediment Layers* post-processing function (see [Sediment Bed Process Tools](/wiki/spaces/EEREF/pages/2380021/Sediment+Bed+Process+Tools)).` | [input.f90:2040](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/input.f90:2040) `        read(1,*,IOSTAT = ISO) SEDSTEP, SEDSTART, IBMECH, IMORPH, HBEDMAX, BEDPORC, SEDMDMX, SEDMDMN, SEDVDRD, SEDVDRM, SEDVRDT`<br>[SedTran-Original/calbed.f90:50](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calbed.f90:50) `    if( IBMECH == 0 )then`<br>[SedTran-Original/calbed.f90:86](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calbed.f90:86) `    if( IBMECH == 1 )then`<br>[SedTran-Original/calbed.f90:96](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/calbed.f90:96) `          TMPEXP = EXP(-DTSED/SEDVRDT)  ` | 코드에서 찾지 못함 | IBMECH 입력은 읽는다. CALBED는 0·1을 처리한다. 1은 DTSED/SEDVRDT의 지수 완화이다. 2의 유한 변형률 해법을 찾지 못했다. |

### 2.7 Confluence의 해당 설명

ETG의 저장된 이론 안내 본문은 PDF 연결을 제공한다. EK의 수리・난류・열・염분・염료・퇴적・파랑 설명과 ECIG의 입력 카드 설명을 대조했다. EEMS12 설명과 구형 설명의 선택 목록을 각각 표시했다.

| 항목・Confluence 원문 | 코드 원문 줄 | 판정 | 이유 |
|---|---|---|---|
| <a id="confluence-1"></a>F01 Confluence 이론 안내<br>[models/EFDC/raw/manuals/confluence/spaces/ETG/pages/EFDC_Theory_Overview.md:12](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/confluence/spaces/ETG/pages/EFDC_Theory_Overview.md:12) `The EFDC+ Theory Guide is available` | — | 문서에만 | 저장한 ETG 페이지는 PDF 이론 가이드를 연결한다. 해당 페이지 본문에는 독립적인 번호 식이 없다. 식 대조의 원문은 지정한 Ver 12 PDF이다. |

When *Smagorinsky* is selected (ISHMD=1), then EE will apply the background HMD as well as Smagorinsky’s subgrid if AHD>0. With this option, the contaminant diffusion is off and not accounted for.  If AHD=0 then the *Constant Horizontal Eddy Viscosity*option will be used.` | [calhdmf.f90:225](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calhdmf.f90:225) `    if( AHD > 0.0 )then`<br>[calhdmf.f90:226](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calhdmf.f90:226) `      ! *** CALCULATE SMAGORINSKY HORIZONTAL VISCOSITY`<br>[calhdmf.f90:227](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calhdmf.f90:227) `      do K = 1,KC`<br>[calhdmf.f90:228](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calhdmf.f90:228) `        do LP = 1,LLHDMF(K,ND)`<br>[calhdmf.f90:229](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calhdmf.f90:229) `          L = LKHDMF(LP,K,ND)`<br>[calhdmf.f90:230](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calhdmf.f90:230) `          TMPVAL = AHDXY(L)*DXP(L)*DYP(L)`<br>[calhdmf.f90:231](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calhdmf.f90:231) `          DSQR = DXU1(L,K)*DXU1(L,K) + DYV1(L,K)*DYV1(L,K) + 0.5*SXY(L,K)*SXY(L,K)`<br>[calhdmf.f90:232](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calhdmf.f90:232) `          AH(L,K) = AHOXY(L) + TMPVAL*SQRT(DSQR)`<br>[Transport/caltran.f90:162](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/caltran.f90:162) `  if( (ISHDMF == 2 .or. | <a id="confluence-19"></a>F19 가변 소산계수<br>[models/EFDC/raw/manuals/confluence/spaces/EK/pages/EFDC_Explorer_12_Knowledge_Base/EFDC_Explorer_12_User_Guide/Model_Control_Form/Modules/Temperature/Surface_Heat_Exchange.md:27](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/confluence/spaces/EK/pages/EFDC_Explorer_12_Knowledge_Base/EFDC_Explorer_12_User_Guide/Model_Control_Form/Modules/Temperature/Surface_Heat_Exchange.md:27) `The *Full Heat Balance with Variable Extinction Coefficient*option provides the user with the option of allowing light extinction to be dependent on the total suspended solids in the water column, thereby making light extinction temporally and spatially varying.  The extinction factors are determined on a cell-by-cell basis for each time step based on TSS, and if the water quality model is active, based on particulate organic matter, dissolved organic matter, and chlorophyll-a as well.` | [Transport/mod_heat.f90:1620](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:1620) `    WCKESS = WQKEB(IWQZMAP(L,K))                                     ! ## 3 코드에만 있는 처리

이 목록의 문서 범위는 대조한 PDF 장과 Confluence 본문이다. 문서가 기능을 설명하더라도 상세 수치 제한이 빠졌다면 그 제한만 별도 항목으로 적었다. 부유 얼음 수송과 Ryan–Harleman・COARE 증발은 Confluence에 있으므로 이 목록의 별도 물리 항목으로 세지 않았다.

| 항목 | 코드 원문 줄 | 확인한 처리・문서와의 관계 |
|---|---|---|
| B01 진단용 선형 염분 밀도 | [calbuoy.f90:45](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calbuoy.f90:45) `  ! *************************************************************************************`<br>[calbuoy.f90:46](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calbuoy.f90:46) `  if( IBSC == 1 )then`<br>[calbuoy.f90:47](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calbuoy.f90:47) `    ! *** DENSITY AS A LINEAR FUNCTION OF SALINITY ONLY.  FOR DIAGNOSTIC PURPOSES ONLY`<br>[calbuoy.f90:48](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calbuoy.f90:48) `    do K = 1,KC`<br>[calbuoy.f90:49](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calbuoy.f90:49) `      do L = 2,LA`<br>[calbuoy.f90:50](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calbuoy.f90:50) `        B(L,K) = 0.00075_8*SAL(L,K)`<br>[calbuoy.f90:51](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calbuoy.f90:51) `      enddo` | IBSC=1은 B=0.00075·SAL의 선형 진단식을 사용한다. PDF 식 2.12의 다항식과 확인한 Confluence Salinity 설명에는 이 선택식이 없다. |
| B02 수직 층 비율 재정규화 | [aaefdc.f90:1295](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/aaefdc.f90:1295) `  ! *** ENSURE SUM OF DZC = 1.0000 TO MACHINE PRECISION`<br>[aaefdc.f90:1296](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/aaefdc.f90:1296) `  DZPC = 0.0`<br>[aaefdc.f90:1297](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/aaefdc.f90:1297) `  do K = 1,KC`<br>[aaefdc.f90:1298](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/aaefdc.f90:1298) `    DZPC = DZPC + DZCK(K)`<br>[aaefdc.f90:1299](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/aaefdc.f90:1299) `  enddo`<br>[aaefdc.f90:1300](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/aaefdc.f90:1300) `  do K = 1,KC`<br>[aaefdc.f90:1301](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/aaefdc.f90:1301) `    DZCK(K) = DZCK(K)/DZPC`<br>[aaefdc.f90:1302](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/aaefdc.f90:1302) `  enddo` | 코드는 입력 DZCK를 실제 합으로 다시 나눈다. | B04 난류 확산의 선택별 계수와 하부 거듭제곱 | [calavb.f90:314](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calavb.f90:314) `  if( ISTOPT(0) <= 2 .or. ISSQL == 0 )then`<br>[calavb.f90:320](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calavb.f90:320) `          AQ(L,K) = 0.205*(AV(L,K-1)+AV(L,K))  `<br>[calavb.f90:336](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calavb.f90:336) `  if( ISTOPT(0) == 3 .or. ISSQL == 1 )then`<br>[calavb.f90:352](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calavb.f90:352) `            AQ(L,K) = 0.314*( DML(L,K-1)*QQSQR(L,K-1)+DML(L,K)*QQSQR(L,K) ) + AVOXYHPI(L)`<br>[calavb.f90:358](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calavb.f90:358) `          AQ(L,KSZ(L)) = 0.314*DML(L,KSZ(L))**QQSQR(L,KSZ(L)) + AVOXYHPI(L)`<br>[calqq2t.f90:99](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calqq2t.f90:99) `  SQLDSQ = 1.0`<br>[calqq2t.f90:100](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calqq2t.f90:100) `  if( ISTOPT(0) == 3 )SQLDSQ = 0.377/0.628` | 코드는 0.205·점성 합과 0.314·ql 합을 선택한다. Kantha는 q²l 확산에 0.377/0.628을 곱한다. 한 하부 경계 줄은 DML·sqrt(QQ) 대신 DML**sqrt(QQ)를 계산한다. 이 줄을 곱셈으로 고쳐 읽지 않았다. 식 2.24의 Aq 추천값과 구분했다. |
